# SP4 — Explicit, verified `pull` / `import` / `update` Implementation Plan

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** Give Secure Strata an explicit, confirmed, digest-verified way to acquire models — `strata import`/`verify`/`export` of offline bundles first, then networked `strata pull` through a confined system `curl` — with one staging/verify/publish path, a model index, manifest-composed engine launch, and model `update`; the program `update` exits 12 until SP7.

**Architecture:** Pure libraries first (SHA-256, reasons and exit classes, URL grammar, address policy, curl argv/response/exit mapping, retry schedule, manifest/index/staging/bundle/diff models, confinement planning, a fake `curl`), all testable on `main` today. Then the filesystem side built on SP1's canonical-path module and strict JSON reader (manifest parser, catalog, index, staging, publish, bundles, the pull orchestrator over an abstract `HopTransport`, the `strata` CLI's local verbs, managed engine launch). Last, the confined downloader transport on SP2's second-run launch seam, the networked CLI verbs, and the privacy lifecycle proofs.

**Tech Stack:** C++20, CMake ≥ 3.20 + Ninja, framework-free test binaries registered with `secure_frontend_add_cases`, Python 3 + pytest (dev tooling only), Linux Landlock + `PR_SET_NO_NEW_PRIVS`, Windows restricted Low-integrity token + Job Object, system `curl` as a child process (never linked).

**Spec:** [`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-10-14-sp4-acquire-design.md`](../specs/2026-10-14-sp4-acquire-design.md) (APPROVED 2026-10-14; §12 D1–D7 decided as recommended). Binding owner decisions: `owner-decisions-2026-10-14.md` section C (C1–C7) and the "SP2 second-run seam" paragraph, reproduced in Global Constraints. Interfaces consumed: SP1 spec + plan (`roots.models`, `parse_relative_path`, `open_root`/`open_beneath`/`list_directory`/`create_directory_within`, `parse_json`, `write_private_file`, the config schema table `kSchema` and `validate()`), SP2's `posix_child_launch.hpp` (`exec_child`, `kReportFd = 3`, `kLiftFloor = 10`) and Windows launcher in `engine_stdio_process.cpp`. Product surface: [`2026-10-13-product-experience-and-upstream-tracking-design.md`](../specs/2026-10-13-product-experience-and-upstream-tracking-design.md) §2, §5, §7, §11.

## Controller notes (spec-vs-code disagreements and spec silences)

1. **`strata` binary (ruling R3, accepted):** no `strata` binary exists (PX §11 gives its skeleton to SP1; the SP1 plan deferred it). SP4 creates `strata` with its own verbs — `import`, `verify`, `export`, `list`, `show`, `rm`, `pull`, `update`, `run` (to "model ready" + server hint), `doctor [--downloader]` — in library `secure_frontend_strata_cli` (Tasks 23, 27); the later CLI skeleton absorbs that dispatcher. `strata-secure-server` never links acquisition (spec §7.1).
2. **Config location (R3; a PX dependency, not an SP4 task):** PX's default config path is unimplemented and SP1 never picks one; every SP4 verb requires `strata --config <file> <verb> …` until the product-spec default config location lands.
3. **`run` (D6) has no client yet** (SP2 auto-start + SP3 streaming). SP4's `strata run <name[:tag]>` does resolve → D6 pull flow → engine gating (exit 11), then prints `model ready: <name:tag>` and the `strata-secure-server --config …` hint and exits 0; the chat client replaces that tail later.
4. **`serve --expert` does not exist (R3: stays out of SP4's `strata`).** SP1 deltas c/d need an operator escape hatch, so SP4 adds `--expert` to `strata-secure-server` only (Task 24a).
5. **Server discovery (`server.json` + status route) does not exist (R3, accepted)** (SP2 did not build it). `rm`/retire use a `LoadedVersions` seam whose production implementation `NoServerDiscovery` answers "not held"; the backstop is the OS (Windows refuses to delete an open file → version stays `retired`; Linux unlink keeps the engine's inode). `rm --force` therefore has nothing to override yet. Flagged for SP2/CLI.
6. Spec §4.4 names `load_tokenizer_pack`; SP1's real function is `load_local_tokenizer_pack(const CanonicalRoot&)` (used here).
7. Spec §4.1 `atomic_replace_within`: SP1 already exposes `write_private_file(root, file, bytes, TokenStoreMode::replace)` in `token_store.hpp`; SP4 wraps it as `replace_small_file` instead of adding a primitive.
8. Spec §4.9 names delta tests `secure_frontend_config_tests.cfg01_*`; by ruling R-3 every `cfg01_*` case lives in SP1's `secure_frontend_typed_config_tests` (`tests/typed_config_test.cpp`), so SP4's config cases go there (`typed_config.cfg01_pull_keys_table_driven`, `…cfg01_engine_profile_rejected_for_managed_model`, `…cfg01_tokenizer_pack_root_rejected_for_managed_model`).
9. Spec cites UP `v0.1.43` line numbers; `main` is at `v0.1.44.1`, so `kEngineVersion` = `0.1.44.1`. GSQ-RCO packs are still built by `tools/iq_pack.py` without `pack_args` (setup.py step 6 `elif` branch) and the PLE table is shard 2 for the original GSQ-RCO files (setup.py step 7 comment "shard 2 (original)").
10. **D5 (D) means the v1 production catalog ships EMPTY.** The four D4 entries need pinned pack digests, which only exist after the owner's determinism check ((A)) or a C++ builder ((C)). Until then developer bundles use (B′): `tools/secure/make_bundle.py` writes a third-party manifest `<quant>-local` and prints the digest for `import --trust-manifest` (Task 13). The catalog mechanism is proven with a hooks-only fixture catalog. Shard pins for all four quants were read from the Hub tree API on 2026-10-14 (LFS `oid`), not downloaded. **Owner to confirm before any release (ruling R4, accepted).**
11. **Engine version (ruling R-1):** a committed one-line file `upstream.version` at the repository root (content `v0.1.44.1`) is the default source of `kEngineVersion`. When the `upstream/` submodule has a reachable exact tag, configure checks it equals the file and fails only on a mismatch; a missing tag (preset-only jobs, depth-1 checkouts) never fails configure. No CI `-D` and no workflow env pin; the file is a pin site in PROVENANCE's runbook step 5.
12. "5 attempts with back-off 2, 4, 8, 16, 32 s" (§3.6) is read as: 5 attempts per source; 2/4/8/16 s between attempts; 32 s before switching to the next source.
13. §3.4 maps unknown curl exits to `transport_failed` → *next source*, while §8.2 lists `transport_failed` as *retried*. The plan follows §3.4 (an unknown failure is not assumed transient).
14. Landlock ABI ≥ 6 adds `scoped` rights the spec does not list; the plan handles fs/net rights through ABI 5 and leaves `scoped` unhandled (no rule needed for curl). Review with SP2.
15. **SP2 seam type names (ruling R2: authoritative).** `posix_launch::ExecOptions{std::span<const int> keep_fds; PreExecHook pre_exec; const void* ctx;}` passed as a new last parameter of `exec_child`, and Windows `win_launch::launch_in_job(const WinLaunchSpec&)` with `primary_token`, `extra_inherited_handles`, `crt_fd_block` — SP2 run-2 implements exactly these names. Tasks 25/26 keep their stop-if-missing checks.
16. `strata update` semantics: PX §7 makes `update` the program update; the spec also gives it a models part. The plan: `strata update` (no operands) = program part → exit 12; `strata update --models [name:tag …]` = models part (§6.1).
17. SP1's `roots.logs` is "unused in SP1"; SP4 is its first writer (`acquire.log`, one record per file, §7.3).
18. SP1's `CanonicalRoot` only comes from `open_root`, which demands a private root; bundles on exFAT/FAT media are world-writable by mount options and spec §5.3 says import never relies on source permissions. SP4 adds **SP1 delta f** `RootPolicy::external` (canonical + held handle + link-refusing walk, no owner/writability checks), used only for bundle source and target directories (Task 20).
19. `scripts/secure_distribution_check.py --release` (spec §10.5) also demands SP7's release manifest and SBOM, which do not exist yet. Task 28 makes the test-hooks marker scan unconditional (every mode); the CI release job (added in Task 14; marker-scan step in Task 28) runs the check without `--release` until SP7 (ruling R6, accepted; stated in the CI doc too).
20. **Facts SP3 M1 needs (ruling R-2; SP3 M1 runs after SP2 run-2 and the SP4 merge and re-anchors on the SP4 tree):** `FrontendConfig` gains a last constructor parameter and member `pull` (Task 15); `Roots::tokenizer_pack` becomes `std::optional<CanonicalRoot>` and `EngineLaunch::profile` `std::optional<EngineProfile>` (Task 24a); `kSchema` grows by 7 rows (Task 15); `secure_server_main.cpp` serves `launch.model_id` from `compose_engine_launch` and no longer has `selected`/`manifests` (Task 24b); every SP1 server-fixture launch of an inventory model carries `--expert` (Task 24a, `ServerFixture::base_flags()`); the TSan job needs no `-D` after R-1; SP4's `LD_PRELOAD` connect-audit shim (`secure_frontend_connect_audit`, Task 28) lands first and SP3 M1 reuses it for its egress checks.

## Global Constraints

**Dependency classes and order (every task is marked):**
- **NOW** — Tasks 1–24b (ruling R9; Task 24 is split into 24a/24b by ruling R-6, so 30 tasks in all). SP1 is merged on `main` (`e7f01b9e`), so the pure libraries (Tasks 1–13, run first) and the SP1-based tasks (14–24b, including SP1 deltas a–f: §4.9 plus Controller note 18) are runnable on the execution base.
- **AFTER SP2 RUN-2** — Tasks 25–29: need SP2's second-run launch seam (below).
- The execution branch `sp4-acquire` starts from current `main` (post-SP1); the controller rebases it onto `main` once SP2 run-2 merges. A task never edits SP1/SP2 files except as the named SP1 deltas.

**Binding SP2 second-run seam (owner decision, verbatim):** keep_fds lifted via `F_DUPFD_CLOEXEC` ≥ `kLiftFloor` before the 0..3 `dup2` sequence; `int pre_exec(const void*, std::span<const int> lifted) noexcept` runs after the sweep: `dup2(record,4)` → `PR_SET_NO_NEW_PRIVS` → `landlock_restrict_self` → `close(ruleset)` → `chdir`; non-zero → `'H'` marker + errno on `kReportFd(3)` → `downloader_confinement_failed`, exit 9. Windows: `primary_token`, `extra_inherited_handles`, `crt_fd_block` (n=5, packed, entry 4 = record handle).

**Owner decisions C1–C7 (binding):** C1 system curl as a confined child, we verify every byte (SHA-256) and count bytes ourselves; C2 built-in digest-pinned catalog, third-party manifests only via `--trust-manifest sha256:<hex>` per run, no remote catalog; C3 curl floor **8.4.0 Windows, 7.81.0 Linux**, `confinement: reduced` announced and logged when Landlock is unavailable; C4 catalog = Qwen3.8 GSQ-RCO `iq2_xs`, `iq3_xxs`, `iq3_s` + Coder `iq1_m` (Apache-2.0), GLM is SP6; C5 ship (D) import-from-developer-built-bundle with a seam for (A)/(C); C6 `run` on a missing model → same in-process pull flow after the same confirmation; C7 bundle trust = catalog digest or `--trust-manifest`, no signature, `strata update` (program) exits 12.

**Execution environment:**
- Worktree `/opt/projects/ai/.worktrees/sp4`, branch `sp4-acquire` (created by the controller from `main`). Never push, merge, rebase, switch branches, `rm -rf` outside a test's own scratch, or `git clean`.
- Shell prefix for every command: `cd /opt/projects/ai/.worktrees/sp4/secure_frontend && export PATH="$(git rev-parse --show-toplevel)/.venv-dev/bin:$PATH"`. The controller symlinks `.venv-dev` into the worktree (ruling R-7); if it is absent, run `scripts/dev/bootstrap-linux.sh` once from the worktree root before the first command.
- Wine: prefix direct wine runs with `WINEPREFIX=$PWD/out/wineprefix WINEDLLOVERRIDES=mscoree,mshtml=` (the `windows-llvm-mingw-cross` test preset sets `WINEPREFIX` itself).
- Presets: `linux-gcc` (hooks ON, the acceptance run), `windows-llvm-mingw-cross` (compile + link with `-Werror`; wine runs exclude `windows-runtime|release-only`), and `linux-gcc-release` (hooks OFF, added by Task 1). MSYS2 CI (`windows-msys2-clang`) is authoritative for every `windows-runtime` case; it runs after the owner pushes.
- Commits: `git -c user.name=shift-f10 -c user.email=40450442+shift-f10@users.noreply.github.com commit -m "<subject>" -m "Co-Authored-By: Claude Fable 5.1 <noreply@anthropic.com>" -m "Claude-Session: <session link>"` — the two trailer lines are written literally in every commit step (never through a shell variable). One commit per task unless a step says otherwise; the subject names the task and its spec section.

**Code and test rules:**
- No third-party dependency in the frontend; `curl` is a system executable configured by `pull.downloader`, invoked at run time, never linked. No change under `upstream/`.
- `-Werror` on every preset (`secure_frontend_set_warnings` on every new target). No `using namespace` in headers. Two-space indent, 120 columns, `[[nodiscard]]` on value-returning functions, as in `include/secure_frontend/*.hpp`.
- TDD: each task writes its failing test first and records RED evidence (the failing build or the failing case output, pasted into the task report), then GREEN.
- Tests: framework-free `main()` via `secure_frontend_test::run_cases`; registered with `secure_frontend_add_cases(<binary> <case>... [ARGS …] [LABELS …] [TIMEOUT …])`; full names `secure_frontend_<binary>_tests.<case>` (spec §10.2), e.g. `fetch.x` = `secure_frontend_fetch_tests.x`. Windows-semantics cases carry `windows-runtime` and a `_windows` suffix. Labels per §10.5: `release-only` (hooks-OFF only; excluded from every hooks-ON test preset), `release-also` (both configurations). Record-channel cases are registered only `if(SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS)`.
- The fake downloader `secure_frontend_fake_curl` is a test executable. No test touches a real network or a real model; the real-curl HTTPS check is the documented manual step (spec §13.5 item 4), never automated.
- Spec constants (verbatim): manifest ≤ 1 MiB, depth 6, ≤ 4096 files, ≤ 8 sources/file, ≤ 16 hosts, ≤ 16 redirect hosts; strings ≤ 1024 bytes except source URLs ≤ 2048 and `license.notice` ≤ 512; every manifest string printable ASCII `0x20–0x7E`; header block ≤ 16 KiB and ≤ 64 lines, ≤ 4 `1xx` blocks; `Location` ≤ 8 KiB; ≤ 3 redirect hops; staging key = first 32 hex of the manifest SHA-256; version dir `v<sequence>-<first 12 hex>`; `index.json` ≤ 4 MiB; `kFreeSpaceMarginBytes` = 1 GiB, free space re-checked every 1 GiB written; config defaults `pull.downloader` `/usr/bin/curl` | `GetSystemDirectoryW()+"\curl.exe"`, `pull.max_total_bytes` 1 099 511 627 776 (1 MiB..16 TiB), `pull.max_duration_s` 172 800 (60..604 800), `pull.stall_timeout_s` 120 (10..3600), `pull.connect_timeout_s` 30 (1..300), `pull.allow_private_addresses` false; Low integrity SID `S-1-16-4096`; TrustedInstaller SID `S-1-5-80-956008885-3418522649-1831038044-1853292631-2271478464`.
- Exit codes (§8.3): 0 success/already installed, 1 usage/config, 2 declined, 3 transport, 4 verification, 5 manifest/unknown model, 6 disk space/total budget, 7 filesystem/lock/index/bundle layout, 8 interrupted, 9 downloader, 10 license forbids bundling, 11 engine cannot run profile, 12 program update unavailable.
- Errors and logs never contain URL paths or queries, `Location` values, proxy URLs, IP addresses, tokens, config values, curl output, or OS messages naming paths outside `roots.models` (§8.1). SP4 reads no environment variable value (`doctor`'s proxy check is presence-only).
- `docs/AGENT_HANDOFF.md`: every task appends one line (task, commit subject, tests added) under `### SP4 (branch sp4-acquire)` in "What has changed" (Task 1 creates the heading); Task 29 writes the verification record, the TM amendment and the PROVENANCE runbook line.

## Review Focus

1. **Resume after preallocation.** A `.part` preallocated with `FALLOC_FL_KEEP_SIZE` / `FileAllocationInfo` must resume at its *length*, never its allocation; a reasonable person expects a crashed 39 GB pull to resume exactly where bytes stopped. Test: `acquire.resume_offset_ignores_preallocation` (Task 22).
2. **A file path that is also another file's directory prefix** (`pack` as a file next to `pack/index.txt`, or `Pack/x` beside `pack/y` after case-folding) must be `manifest_invalid`, not a publish-time collision on Windows. Test: `manifest.file_and_directory_prefix_collision_rejected` (Task 8).
3. **Confirmation answers.** `y`, `Y`, `yes`, ` yes ` accept; empty line, EOF, `n`, `yep`, `y es` decline; a reasonable person expects anything else to be No. Test: `acquire.confirmation_answers_parsed_strictly` (Task 22).
4. **File systems without `renameat2(RENAME_NOREPLACE)`** (`EINVAL`/`ENOSYS` on some FUSE/NFS mounts) must publish through the `fstatat`-then-`renameat` fallback under `.lock`, never fail or overwrite. Test: `acquire.publish_falls_back_when_noreplace_unsupported` (Task 19).
5. **Importing from read-only media** (USB stick mounted read-only, bundle dirs 0500) must work: `import`/`verify` never write into the bundle. Test: `bundle.import_from_read_only_bundle_directory` (Task 20).

Common test-file skeleton (every new test binary in this plan starts with it; tasks show only the cases and the `main` list):

```cpp
#include "support/test_support.hpp"

#include <cstdlib>
#include <iostream>
#include <string>

namespace {

int failures = 0;

void expect(const bool condition, const std::string& message) {
  if (!condition) {
    ++failures;
    std::cerr << "FAILED: " << message << '\n';
  }
}

// cases go here

}  // namespace

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
  return secure_frontend_test::run_cases(argc, argv, {
      // {"case_name", case_name},
  }, failures);
}
```

### Task 1: Test-hooks option, release preset, SHA-256 — NOW

Spec §4.2, §10.2 (`sha256` binary), §10.5 (hooks option, `linux-gcc-release`, labels).

**Files:**

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing test** — `secure_frontend/tests/sha256_test.cpp` (common skeleton plus):

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/sha256.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/test_hooks.hpp"
#include "support/test_support.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstdint>
#include <cstdlib>
#include <iostream>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>

namespace {

int failures = 0;

void expect(const bool condition, const std::string& message) {
  if (!condition) {
    ++failures;
    std::cerr << "FAILED: " << message << '\n';
  }
}

std::string hex_of(const std::string_view bytes) { return secure_frontend::to_hex(secure_frontend::sha256(bytes)); }

// FIPS 180-4 / NIST CSRC "SHA-256 examples" and the NIST long-message vectors.
void fips_180_4_short_vectors() {
  expect(hex_of("") == "e3b0c44298fc1c149afbf4c8996fb92427ae41e4649b934ca495991b7852b855", "empty message");
  expect(hex_of("abc") == "ba7816bf8f01cfea414140de5dae2223b00361a396177a9cb410ff61f20015ad", "abc (one block)");
  expect(hex_of("abcdbcdecdefdefgefghfghighijhijkijkljklmklmnlmnomnopnopq") ==
             "248d6a61d20638b8e5c026930c3e6039a33ce45964ff2167f6ecedd419db06c1",
         "448-bit message (two blocks)");
  expect(hex_of("abcdefghbcdefghicdefghijdefghijkefghijklfghijklmghijklmnhijklmnoijklmnopjklmnopqklmnopqrlmnopqrsmnopqrstnopqrstu") ==
             "cf5b16a778af8380036ce59e7b0492370b249b11e8f07a51afac45037afee9d1",
         "896-bit message");
}

void fips_180_4_million_a() {
  secure_frontend::Sha256 even;
  const std::string thousand(1000, 'a');
  for (int i = 0; i < 1000; ++i) even.update(thousand);
  expect(secure_frontend::to_hex(even.finish()) == "cdc76e5c9914fb9281a1c7e284d73e67f1809a48a497200e046d39ccc7112cd0",
         "one million 'a' in 1000-byte chunks");
  secure_frontend::Sha256 uneven;
  const std::string source(4099, 'a');
  std::size_t left = 1000000;
  std::size_t step = 1;
  while (left != 0) {
    const std::size_t take = std::min(left, step);
    uneven.update(std::string_view{source}.substr(0, take));
    left -= take;
    step = step * 7 % 4099 + 1;
  }
  expect(secure_frontend::to_hex(uneven.finish()) == "cdc76e5c9914fb9281a1c7e284d73e67f1809a48a497200e046d39ccc7112cd0",
         "one million 'a' in uneven chunks");
}

// NIST "extremely long message": the 64-byte pattern repeated 16 777 216 times (2^30 bytes, 2^33 bits).
void long_message_one_gibibyte() {
  const std::string pattern = "abcdefghbcdefghicdefghijdefghijkefghijklfghijklmghijklmnhijklmno";
  expect(pattern.size() == 64, "pattern is 64 bytes");
  std::string chunk;
  chunk.reserve(secure_frontend::kSha256ReadChunkBytes);
  while (chunk.size() < secure_frontend::kSha256ReadChunkBytes) chunk += pattern;
  secure_frontend::Sha256 hasher;
  const std::uint64_t total = std::uint64_t{1} << 30U;
  for (std::uint64_t done = 0; done < total; done += chunk.size()) hasher.update(chunk);
  expect(hasher.bytes_hashed() == total, "length counter reaches 2^30");
  expect(secure_frontend::to_hex(hasher.finish()) == "50e72a0e26442fe2552dc3938ac58658228c0cbfb1d2ca872ae435266fcd055e",
         "2^30-byte NIST long message");
}

void chunk_boundaries_do_not_change_digest() {
  std::string data(300, '\0');
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < data.size(); ++i) data[i] = static_cast<char>((i * 31U + 7U) & 0xFFU);
  for (const std::size_t n : {0U, 1U, 55U, 56U, 63U, 64U, 65U, 119U, 120U, 127U, 128U, 129U, 300U}) {
    const auto whole = secure_frontend::sha256(std::string_view{data}.substr(0, n));
    for (std::size_t split = 0; split <= n; ++split) {
      secure_frontend::Sha256 hasher;
      hasher.update(std::string_view{data}.substr(0, split));
      hasher.update(std::string_view{data}.substr(split, n - split));
      if (hasher.finish() != whole) {
        expect(false, "split at " + std::to_string(split) + " of " + std::to_string(n));
        break;
      }
    }
  }
  secure_frontend::Sha256 reused;
  reused.update("abc");
  (void)reused.finish();
  expect(secure_frontend::to_hex(reused.finish()) == hex_of(""), "finish() resets to the initial state");
}

void length_field_encodes_bits_big_endian() {
  using Field = std::array<std::uint8_t, 8>;
  expect(secure_frontend::sha256_length_field(0) == Field{0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0}, "zero length");
  expect(secure_frontend::sha256_length_field(3) == Field{0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0x18}, "3 bytes = 24 bits");
  expect(secure_frontend::sha256_length_field((std::uint64_t{1} << 32U) + 5U) == Field{0, 0, 0, 0x08, 0, 0, 0, 0x28},
         "2^32+5 bytes = 2^35+40 bits, big endian");
}

void hex_helpers_are_strict() {
  const std::string digest = hex_of("abc");
  expect(secure_frontend::is_lower_hex_digest(digest), "a digest is lowercase hex");
  std::string upper = digest;
  upper[0] = 'B';
  expect(!secure_frontend::is_lower_hex_digest(upper), "uppercase rejected");
  expect(!secure_frontend::is_lower_hex_digest(digest.substr(1)), "63 characters rejected");
  expect(!secure_frontend::is_lower_hex_digest(digest + "0"), "65 characters rejected");
}

#if defined(SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS)
void length_counter_past_2_32_bytes() {
  secure_frontend::Sha256 hasher;
  secure_frontend::sha256_add_length_for_test(hasher, std::uint64_t{1} << 32U);
  hasher.update("abc");
  expect(hasher.bytes_hashed() == (std::uint64_t{1} << 32U) + 3U, "the 64-bit counter carries past 2^32 bytes");
  const auto first = hasher.finish();
  secure_frontend::Sha256 again;
  secure_frontend::sha256_add_length_for_test(again, std::uint64_t{1} << 32U);
  again.update("abc");
  expect(again.finish() == first, "deterministic");
  expect(first != secure_frontend::sha256("abc"), "the length field participates in the digest");
}
#endif

}  // namespace

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
  return secure_frontend_test::run_cases(argc, argv, {
      {"fips_180_4_short_vectors", fips_180_4_short_vectors},
      {"fips_180_4_million_a", fips_180_4_million_a},
      {"long_message_one_gibibyte", long_message_one_gibibyte},
      {"chunk_boundaries_do_not_change_digest", chunk_boundaries_do_not_change_digest},
      {"length_field_encodes_bits_big_endian", length_field_encodes_bits_big_endian},
      {"hex_helpers_are_strict", hex_helpers_are_strict},
#if defined(SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS)
      {"length_counter_past_2_32_bytes", length_counter_past_2_32_bytes},
#endif
  }, failures);
}
```

```cpp
#if defined(SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS)
```

`secure_frontend/include/secure_frontend/sha256.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <array>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <span>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>

namespace secure_frontend {

using Sha256Digest = std::array<std::uint8_t, 32>;

// Files are hashed in reads of this size (spec §4.2).
inline constexpr std::size_t kSha256ReadChunkBytes = std::size_t{8} * 1024U * 1024U;

// FIPS 180-4 SHA-256, streaming, portable C++ (no intrinsics, no dependency).
class Sha256 final {
 public:
  Sha256() noexcept;
  void update(std::span<const std::uint8_t> bytes) noexcept;
  void update(std::string_view bytes) noexcept;
  // Pads, returns the digest, and resets to the initial state.
  [[nodiscard]] Sha256Digest finish() noexcept;
  [[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t bytes_hashed() const noexcept { return length_bytes_; }

 private:
  friend void sha256_add_length_for_test(Sha256& hasher, std::uint64_t bytes) noexcept;
  void compress(const std::uint8_t* block) noexcept;
  void reset() noexcept;
  std::array<std::uint32_t, 8> state_{};
  std::array<std::uint8_t, 64> buffer_{};
  std::size_t buffered_{0};
  std::uint64_t length_bytes_{0};
};

// The 8-byte big-endian bit count that ends the padding (FIPS 180-4 §5.1.1).
// Valid for messages below 2^61 bytes.
[[nodiscard]] std::array<std::uint8_t, 8> sha256_length_field(std::uint64_t message_bytes) noexcept;

[[nodiscard]] Sha256Digest sha256(std::string_view bytes) noexcept;
// 64 lowercase hex characters.
[[nodiscard]] std::string to_hex(const Sha256Digest& digest);
// Exactly 64 characters of [0-9a-f].
[[nodiscard]] bool is_lower_hex_digest(std::string_view text) noexcept;

#if defined(SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS)
// Spec §10.2 length-only hook: advances the length counter by `bytes` without
// hashing anything. Only at a block boundary and for whole blocks; otherwise a no-op.
void sha256_add_length_for_test(Sha256& hasher, std::uint64_t bytes) noexcept;
#endif

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`secure_frontend/src/sha256.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/sha256.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <cstring>

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {

constexpr std::array<std::uint32_t, 64> kRoundConstants{
    0x428a2f98U, 0x71374491U, 0xb5c0fbcfU, 0xe9b5dba5U, 0x3956c25bU, 0x59f111f1U, 0x923f82a4U, 0xab1c5ed5U,
    0xd807aa98U, 0x12835b01U, 0x243185beU, 0x550c7dc3U, 0x72be5d74U, 0x80deb1feU, 0x9bdc06a7U, 0xc19bf174U,
    0xe49b69c1U, 0xefbe4786U, 0x0fc19dc6U, 0x240ca1ccU, 0x2de92c6fU, 0x4a7484aaU, 0x5cb0a9dcU, 0x76f988daU,
    0x983e5152U, 0xa831c66dU, 0xb00327c8U, 0xbf597fc7U, 0xc6e00bf3U, 0xd5a79147U, 0x06ca6351U, 0x14292967U,
    0x27b70a85U, 0x2e1b2138U, 0x4d2c6dfcU, 0x53380d13U, 0x650a7354U, 0x766a0abbU, 0x81c2c92eU, 0x92722c85U,
    0xa2bfe8a1U, 0xa81a664bU, 0xc24b8b70U, 0xc76c51a3U, 0xd192e819U, 0xd6990624U, 0xf40e3585U, 0x106aa070U,
    0x19a4c116U, 0x1e376c08U, 0x2748774cU, 0x34b0bcb5U, 0x391c0cb3U, 0x4ed8aa4aU, 0x5b9cca4fU, 0x682e6ff3U,
    0x748f82eeU, 0x78a5636fU, 0x84c87814U, 0x8cc70208U, 0x90befffaU, 0xa4506cebU, 0xbef9a3f7U, 0xc67178f2U};

constexpr std::array<std::uint32_t, 8> kInitialState{0x6a09e667U, 0xbb67ae85U, 0x3c6ef372U, 0xa54ff53aU,
                                                     0x510e527fU, 0x9b05688cU, 0x1f83d9abU, 0x5be0cd19U};

constexpr std::uint32_t rotr(const std::uint32_t value, const unsigned count) noexcept {
  return (value >> count) | (value << (32U - count));
}

constexpr std::uint32_t load_be32(const std::uint8_t* p) noexcept {
  return (static_cast<std::uint32_t>(p[0]) << 24U) | (static_cast<std::uint32_t>(p[1]) << 16U) |
         (static_cast<std::uint32_t>(p[2]) << 8U) | static_cast<std::uint32_t>(p[3]);
}

}  // namespace

Sha256::Sha256() noexcept { reset(); }

void Sha256::reset() noexcept {
  state_ = kInitialState;
  buffer_.fill(0);
  buffered_ = 0;
  length_bytes_ = 0;
}

void Sha256::compress(const std::uint8_t* block) noexcept {
  std::array<std::uint32_t, 64> w{};
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 16; ++i) w[i] = load_be32(block + 4U * i);
  for (std::size_t i = 16; i < 64; ++i) {
    const std::uint32_t s0 = rotr(w[i - 15], 7) ^ rotr(w[i - 15], 18) ^ (w[i - 15] >> 3U);
    const std::uint32_t s1 = rotr(w[i - 2], 17) ^ rotr(w[i - 2], 19) ^ (w[i - 2] >> 10U);
    w[i] = w[i - 16] + s0 + w[i - 7] + s1;
  }
  std::uint32_t a = state_[0];
  std::uint32_t b = state_[1];
  std::uint32_t c = state_[2];
  std::uint32_t d = state_[3];
  std::uint32_t e = state_[4];
  std::uint32_t f = state_[5];
  std::uint32_t g = state_[6];
  std::uint32_t h = state_[7];
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 64; ++i) {
    const std::uint32_t s1 = rotr(e, 6) ^ rotr(e, 11) ^ rotr(e, 25);
    const std::uint32_t choose = (e & f) ^ (~e & g);
    const std::uint32_t t1 = h + s1 + choose + kRoundConstants[i] + w[i];
    const std::uint32_t s0 = rotr(a, 2) ^ rotr(a, 13) ^ rotr(a, 22);
    const std::uint32_t majority = (a & b) ^ (a & c) ^ (b & c);
    const std::uint32_t t2 = s0 + majority;
    h = g;
    g = f;
    f = e;
    e = d + t1;
    d = c;
    c = b;
    b = a;
    a = t1 + t2;
  }
  state_[0] += a;
  state_[1] += b;
  state_[2] += c;
  state_[3] += d;
  state_[4] += e;
  state_[5] += f;
  state_[6] += g;
  state_[7] += h;
}

void Sha256::update(const std::span<const std::uint8_t> bytes) noexcept {
  if (bytes.empty()) return;
  length_bytes_ += bytes.size();
  std::size_t offset = 0;
  if (buffered_ != 0) {
    const std::size_t take = std::min(buffer_.size() - buffered_, bytes.size());
    std::memcpy(buffer_.data() + buffered_, bytes.data(), take);
    buffered_ += take;
    offset = take;
    if (buffered_ < buffer_.size()) return;
    compress(buffer_.data());
    buffered_ = 0;
  }
  while (bytes.size() - offset >= buffer_.size()) {
    compress(bytes.data() + offset);
    offset += buffer_.size();
  }
  const std::size_t rest = bytes.size() - offset;
  if (rest != 0) {
    std::memcpy(buffer_.data(), bytes.data() + offset, rest);
    buffered_ = rest;
  }
}

void Sha256::update(const std::string_view bytes) noexcept {
  update(std::span<const std::uint8_t>{reinterpret_cast<const std::uint8_t*>(bytes.data()), bytes.size()});
}

Sha256Digest Sha256::finish() noexcept {
  const auto length = sha256_length_field(length_bytes_);
  buffer_[buffered_++] = 0x80U;
  if (buffered_ > 56) {
    std::fill(buffer_.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(buffered_), buffer_.end(), std::uint8_t{0});
    compress(buffer_.data());
    buffered_ = 0;
  }
  std::fill(buffer_.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(buffered_), buffer_.begin() + 56, std::uint8_t{0});
  std::copy(length.begin(), length.end(), buffer_.begin() + 56);
  compress(buffer_.data());
  Sha256Digest digest{};
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
    digest[4U * i] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(state_[i] >> 24U);
    digest[4U * i + 1U] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(state_[i] >> 16U);
    digest[4U * i + 2U] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(state_[i] >> 8U);
    digest[4U * i + 3U] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(state_[i]);
  }
  reset();
  return digest;
}

std::array<std::uint8_t, 8> sha256_length_field(const std::uint64_t message_bytes) noexcept {
  const std::uint64_t bits = message_bytes << 3U;
  std::array<std::uint8_t, 8> field{};
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 8; ++i) field[i] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(bits >> (56U - 8U * i));
  return field;
}

Sha256Digest sha256(const std::string_view bytes) noexcept {
  Sha256 hasher;
  hasher.update(bytes);
  return hasher.finish();
}

std::string to_hex(const Sha256Digest& digest) {
  static constexpr char kDigits[] = "0123456789abcdef";
  std::string text;
  text.reserve(64);
  for (const std::uint8_t byte : digest) {
    text.push_back(kDigits[byte >> 4U]);
    text.push_back(kDigits[byte & 0x0FU]);
  }
  return text;
}

bool is_lower_hex_digest(const std::string_view text) noexcept {
  return text.size() == 64 &&
         std::all_of(text.begin(), text.end(), [](const char c) { return (c >= '0' && c <= '9') || (c >= 'a' && c <= 'f'); });
}

#if defined(SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS)
void sha256_add_length_for_test(Sha256& hasher, const std::uint64_t bytes) noexcept {
  if (hasher.buffered_ == 0 && bytes % 64U == 0) hasher.length_bytes_ += bytes;
}
#endif

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

```cpp
void exit_classes_match_spec_table() {
  for (const auto& row : spec_table()) {
    const auto& info = secure_frontend::reason_info(row.reason);
    expect(info.name == row.name, std::string{"name of "} + row.name);
    expect(info.exit == row.exit, std::string{"exit class of "} + row.name);
    expect(info.same_source == row.same, std::string{"same-source rule of "} + row.name);
    expect(info.resumable == row.resumable, std::string{"resumable flag of "} + row.name);
    expect(secure_frontend::exit_class(row.reason) == row.exit, std::string{"exit_class of "} + row.name);
  }
  expect(secure_frontend::reason_table().size() == spec_table().size(), "every reason is in the spec table");
}

void reason_table_is_complete_and_ordered() {
  std::set<std::string_view> names;
  const auto table = secure_frontend::reason_table();
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < table.size(); ++i) {
    expect(static_cast<std::size_t>(table[i].reason) == i, "row " + std::to_string(i) + " is in enum order");
    expect(names.insert(table[i].name).second, "unique name " + std::string{table[i].name});
    for (const char c : table[i].name) expect((c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= '0' && c <= '9') || c == '_', "snake_case");
  }
  expect(secure_frontend::exit_code_value(ExitCode::program_update) == 12, "exit code values are the enum values");
}

void error_text_states_resumable() {
  expect(secure_frontend::format_error({AcquireReason::digest_mismatch, "weights/a.gguf"}) ==
             "error: digest_mismatch: weights/a.gguf (resumable: no)",
         "detail and resumable: no");
  expect(secure_frontend::format_error({AcquireReason::interrupted, ""}) == "error: interrupted (resumable: yes)",
         "no detail");
}

//   {"exit_classes_match_spec_table", exit_classes_match_spec_table},
//   {"reason_table_is_complete_and_ordered", reason_table_is_complete_and_ordered},
//   {"error_text_states_resumable", error_text_states_resumable},
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement** — `include/secure_frontend/acquire_reason.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <span>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>

namespace secure_frontend {

// Spec §8.3: exit codes of pull/import/export/verify/update/rm/run.
enum class ExitCode : int {
  success = 0, usage = 1, declined = 2, transport = 3, verification = 4, manifest = 5, space = 6,
  filesystem = 7, interrupted = 8, downloader = 9, license = 10, engine = 11, program_update = 12,
};

// Spec §8.2, in table order. usage_error and program_update_unavailable carry exit codes 1 and 12.
enum class AcquireReason : std::uint8_t {
  manifest_invalid, manifest_unsupported_version, manifest_untrusted, manifest_shadows_catalog, manifest_downgrade,
  manifest_conflict, manifest_requires_bundle, model_unknown,
  dns_failed, connect_failed, tls_failed, stalled, size_short, transport_failed,
  range_mismatch, size_mismatch_announced,
  host_not_allowed, redirect_not_allowed, redirect_limit, response_malformed, address_not_allowed, tls_untrusted,
  http_status,
  size_exceeded, digest_mismatch, pack_layout_invalid, bundle_invalid,
  bundle_layout, bundle_incomplete, bundle_target_fat32, bundle_target_not_empty,
  time_budget_exceeded, total_budget_exceeded, disk_space,
  models_busy, publish_failed, index_write_failed, index_inconsistent,
  license_forbids_bundle,
  downloader_missing, downloader_version, downloader_untrusted_path, downloader_unsupported,
  downloader_confinement_failed,
  declined, interrupted, engine_profile_unavailable, engine_too_old,
  usage_error, program_update_unavailable,
};

// Spec §8.2 "Same source": what a later attempt on the same source does.
enum class SameSource : std::uint8_t { not_applicable, retried, restart_file, next_source, next_source_file_deleted };

struct ReasonInfo final {
  AcquireReason reason;
  std::string_view name;
  ExitCode exit;
  SameSource same_source;
  bool resumable;  // §8.2 "Rerun resumes"
};

[[nodiscard]] std::span<const ReasonInfo> reason_table() noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] const ReasonInfo& reason_info(AcquireReason reason) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] std::string_view reason_name(AcquireReason reason) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] ExitCode exit_class(AcquireReason reason) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] constexpr int exit_code_value(const ExitCode code) noexcept { return static_cast<int>(code); }

// A failure: a fixed reason plus at most the §8.1 variable parts (name:tag,
// manifest-relative path, host name, HTTP status number, byte counts, digests,
// config key paths). Never a URL path or query, a Location, a proxy URL, an IP
// address, a token, a config value, curl output, or a path outside roots.models.
struct AcquireError final {
  AcquireReason reason;
  std::string detail;
};

// "error: <reason>[: <detail>] (resumable: yes|no)"
[[nodiscard]] std::string format_error(const AcquireError& error);

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

```cpp
    {R::downloader_unsupported, "downloader_unsupported", E::downloader, S::not_applicable, true},
    {R::downloader_confinement_failed, "downloader_confinement_failed", E::downloader, S::not_applicable, true},

    {R::engine_profile_unavailable, "engine_profile_unavailable", E::engine, S::not_applicable, false},
    {R::engine_too_old, "engine_too_old", E::engine, S::not_applicable, false},
    {R::usage_error, "usage_error", E::usage, S::not_applicable, false},
    {R::program_update_unavailable, "program_update_unavailable", E::program_update, S::not_applicable, false},
}};

static_assert(static_cast<std::size_t>(R::program_update_unavailable) + 1U == kReasons.size(),
              "kReasons has one row per AcquireReason, in enum order");

}  // namespace

std::span<const ReasonInfo> reason_table() noexcept { return kReasons; }
const ReasonInfo& reason_info(const AcquireReason reason) noexcept { return kReasons[static_cast<std::size_t>(reason)]; }
std::string_view reason_name(const AcquireReason reason) noexcept { return reason_info(reason).name; }
ExitCode exit_class(const AcquireReason reason) noexcept { return reason_info(reason).exit; }

std::string format_error(const AcquireError& error) {
  const auto& info = reason_info(error.reason);
  std::string text = "error: ";
  text.append(info.name);
  if (!error.detail.empty()) text.append(": ").append(error.detail);
  text.append(info.resumable ? " (resumable: yes)" : " (resumable: no)");
  return text;
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/acquire_url.hpp"

#include <variant>

// in the anonymous namespace:
bool problem_is(const std::variant<secure_frontend::HttpsUrl, secure_frontend::UrlProblem>& result,
                const secure_frontend::UrlProblem expected) {
  const auto* problem = std::get_if<secure_frontend::UrlProblem>(&result);
  return problem != nullptr && *problem == expected;
}

void source_url_grammar_matrix() {
  using P = secure_frontend::UrlProblem;
  const auto ok = secure_frontend::parse_source_url(
      "https://huggingface.co/ISTA-DASLab/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-GSQ-RCO-GGUF/resolve/ed59f92082b1e93c0e96d60a8b11aab089b52f09/"
      "IQ2_XS/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-GSQ-RCO-IQ2_XS-00001-of-00002.gguf");
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::HttpsUrl>(ok), "Hugging Face resolve URL accepted");
  if (const auto* url = std::get_if<secure_frontend::HttpsUrl>(&ok)) {
    expect(url->host == "huggingface.co", "host split");
    expect(secure_frontend::render_url(*url).starts_with("https://huggingface.co/ISTA-DASLab/"), "round trip");
  }
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::HttpsUrl>(secure_frontend::parse_source_url("https://h.example:443/a%20b")),
         "explicit :443 and %XX accepted");
  expect(problem_is(secure_frontend::parse_source_url("http://huggingface.co/a"), P::not_https), "http rejected");
  expect(problem_is(secure_frontend::parse_source_url("HTTPS://huggingface.co/a"), P::not_https), "scheme is exact");
  expect(problem_is(secure_frontend::parse_source_url("https://huggingface.co:8443/a"), P::port_not_allowed), "port 8443");
  expect(problem_is(secure_frontend::parse_source_url("https://huggingface.co:/a"), P::port_not_allowed), "empty port");
  expect(problem_is(secure_frontend::parse_source_url("https://user@huggingface.co/a"), P::userinfo), "userinfo");
  expect(problem_is(secure_frontend::parse_source_url("https://huggingface.co/a?x=1"), P::query_not_allowed), "query");
  expect(problem_is(secure_frontend::parse_source_url("https://huggingface.co/a#f"), P::fragment), "fragment");
  for (const auto* bad : {"https://h.example/a[1]", "https://h.example/a{b}", "https://h.example/a b",
                          "https://h.example/a\"b", "https://h.example/a\\b", "https://h.example/%zz",
                          "https://h.example/a%2"}) {
    expect(problem_is(secure_frontend::parse_source_url(bad), P::bad_character), std::string{"bad character: "} + bad);
  }
  expect(problem_is(secure_frontend::parse_source_url("https://h.example"), P::missing_path), "no path");
  expect(problem_is(secure_frontend::parse_source_url("https://h.example/"), P::missing_path), "root path only");
  expect(problem_is(secure_frontend::parse_source_url("https://H.example/a"), P::bad_host), "manifest hosts are lowercase");
  expect(problem_is(secure_frontend::parse_source_url("https://127.0.0.1/a"), P::bad_host), "IPv4 literal");
  expect(problem_is(secure_frontend::parse_source_url("https://[::1]/a"), P::bad_host), "IPv6 literal");
  expect(problem_is(secure_frontend::parse_source_url("https://localhost/a"), P::bad_host), "single label");
  expect(problem_is(secure_frontend::parse_source_url("https://h.example/" + std::string(2100, 'a')), P::too_long),
         "longer than 2048 bytes");
}

void dns_names_and_wildcards() {
  expect(secure_frontend::is_valid_dns_name("huggingface.co"), "two labels");
  expect(secure_frontend::is_valid_dns_name("us.aws.cdn.hf.co"), "five labels");
  for (const auto* bad : {"", "co", "-a.co", "a-.co", "a..co", "a.co.", "A.co", "a_b.co", "1.2.3.4", "a.123"}) {
    expect(!secure_frontend::is_valid_dns_name(bad), std::string{"invalid dns name: "} + bad);
  }
  expect(secure_frontend::is_valid_redirect_pattern("*.aws.cdn.hf.co"), "wildcard over 4 labels");
  expect(secure_frontend::is_valid_redirect_pattern("cas-bridge.xethub.hf.co"), "plain host");
  expect(!secure_frontend::is_valid_redirect_pattern("*.co"), "wildcard base needs two labels");
  expect(!secure_frontend::is_valid_redirect_pattern("*"), "bare star");
  expect(!secure_frontend::is_valid_redirect_pattern("a.*.co"), "star only leftmost");
  expect(secure_frontend::host_matches("*.aws.cdn.hf.co", "us.aws.cdn.hf.co"), "one extra label matches");
  expect(!secure_frontend::host_matches("*.aws.cdn.hf.co", "aws.cdn.hf.co"), "zero extra labels do not match");
  expect(!secure_frontend::host_matches("*.aws.cdn.hf.co", "a.b.aws.cdn.hf.co"), "two extra labels do not match");
  expect(!secure_frontend::host_matches("*.aws.cdn.hf.co", "evilaws.cdn.hf.co"), "label boundary");
  expect(secure_frontend::host_matches("huggingface.co", "huggingface.co"), "exact");
  expect(!secure_frontend::host_matches("huggingface.co", "x.huggingface.co"), "exact means exact");
}

void proxy_grammar() {
  const auto http = secure_frontend::parse_proxy("http://proxy.corp:3128");
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::ProxySetting>(http), "http proxy with single-label-free name");
  const auto single = secure_frontend::parse_proxy("http://proxy:8080");
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::ProxySetting>(single), "single-label proxy name");
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::ProxySetting>(secure_frontend::parse_proxy("https://10.0.0.5:443")),
         "IPv4 literal proxy (may be private)");
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::ProxySetting>(secure_frontend::parse_proxy("http://[fd00::1]:3128")),
         "IPv6 literal proxy");
  if (const auto* p = std::get_if<secure_frontend::ProxySetting>(&http)) {
    expect(secure_frontend::render_proxy(*p) == "http://proxy.corp:3128", "render");
  }
  for (const auto* bad : {"proxy:3128", "socks5://p.example:1080", "http://u:p@p.example:3128", "http://p.example",
                          "http://p.example:0", "http://p.example:65536", "http://p.example:3128/path",
                          "http://p.example:03128"}) {
    expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::UrlProblem>(secure_frontend::parse_proxy(bad)),
           std::string{"proxy rejected: "} + bad);
  }
}

// main list: source_url_grammar_matrix, dns_names_and_wildcards, proxy_grammar
```

`tests/fetch_test.cpp` (common skeleton plus):

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/acquire_url.hpp"

#include <variant>

// in the anonymous namespace:
bool location_problem(const std::string_view value, const secure_frontend::UrlProblem expected) {
  const auto result = secure_frontend::parse_location(value, "huggingface.co");
  const auto* problem = std::get_if<secure_frontend::UrlProblem>(&result);
  return problem != nullptr && *problem == expected;
}

// Spec §9 "URL globbing makes curl fetch many URLs": [ ] { } never pass a Location.
void dl03_location_with_glob_characters_rejected() {
  using P = secure_frontend::UrlProblem;
  for (const auto* bad : {"https://us.aws.cdn.hf.co/a[1-9]", "https://us.aws.cdn.hf.co/{a,b}",
                          "https://us.aws.cdn.hf.co/a?x=[1]", "https://us.aws.cdn.hf.co/a?x={y}",
                          "https://us.aws.cdn.hf.co/a b", "https://us.aws.cdn.hf.co/a?x=\"y\"",
                          "https://us.aws.cdn.hf.co/a\\b"}) {
    expect(location_problem(bad, P::bad_character), std::string{"Location rejected: "} + bad);
  }
}

void location_resolution_and_host_normalization() {
  using P = secure_frontend::UrlProblem;
  const auto relative = secure_frontend::parse_location("/api/x", "huggingface.co");
  const auto* url = std::get_if<secure_frontend::HttpsUrl>(&relative);
  expect(url != nullptr && url->host == "huggingface.co" && url->target == "/api/x", "absolute path uses current host");
  const auto upper = secure_frontend::parse_location("https://US.AWS.CDN.HF.CO/x", "huggingface.co");
  const auto* lowered = std::get_if<secure_frontend::HttpsUrl>(&upper);
  expect(lowered != nullptr && lowered->host == "us.aws.cdn.hf.co", "Location host is lowercased");
  expect(location_problem("https://us.aws.cdn.hf.co./x", P::bad_host), "trailing dot rejected");
  expect(location_problem("http://us.aws.cdn.hf.co/x", P::not_https), "http Location");
  expect(location_problem("//us.aws.cdn.hf.co/x", P::not_https), "network-path reference");
  expect(location_problem("x/y", P::not_https), "relative path");
  expect(location_problem("https://us.aws.cdn.hf.co:444/x", P::port_not_allowed), "port 444");
  expect(location_problem("https://a@us.aws.cdn.hf.co/x", P::userinfo), "userinfo");
  expect(location_problem("https://us.aws.cdn.hf.co/x#f", P::fragment), "fragment");
  expect(location_problem("https://us.aws.cdn.hf.co/" + std::string(8200, 'a'), P::too_long), "over 8 KiB");
  const auto bare = secure_frontend::parse_location("https://us.aws.cdn.hf.co", "huggingface.co");
  const auto* bare_url = std::get_if<secure_frontend::HttpsUrl>(&bare);
  expect(bare_url != nullptr && bare_url->target == "/", "no path means /");
}

void location_query_characters() {
  const auto signed_url = secure_frontend::parse_location(
      "https://us.aws.cdn.hf.co/xet-bridge-us/abc?X-Xet-Signed-Range=bytes%3D0-1&Expires=1&Policy=a,b;c:d@e!f$g'h(i)j*k+l",
      "huggingface.co");
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::HttpsUrl>(signed_url), "signed CDN query characters accepted");
  expect(location_problem("https://us.aws.cdn.hf.co/a?b?c", secure_frontend::UrlProblem::bad_character),
         "second ? rejected");
  expect(location_problem("https://us.aws.cdn.hf.co/a=b", secure_frontend::UrlProblem::bad_character),
         "query-only characters are not path characters");
}

// main list: dl03_location_with_glob_characters_rejected, location_resolution_and_host_normalization,
//            location_query_characters
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement** — `include/secure_frontend/acquire_url.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <span>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <variant>

namespace secure_frontend {

inline constexpr std::size_t kMaxSourceUrlBytes = 2048;
inline constexpr std::size_t kMaxLocationBytes = 8192;

// host: lowercase DNS name. target: path (and, for a Location only, "?query"),
// always starting with '/'. Never printed, logged or stored when it carries a query.
struct HttpsUrl final {
  std::string host;
  std::string target;
};

enum class UrlProblem {
  not_https, bad_host, port_not_allowed, userinfo, fragment, query_not_allowed, bad_character, missing_path, too_long,
};

// Lowercase LDH labels (1-63 bytes, no leading/trailing '-'), at least two
// labels, at most 253 bytes, no trailing dot, last label not all digits (so no IPv4 literal).
[[nodiscard]] bool is_valid_dns_name(std::string_view name) noexcept;
// A DNS name, or "*." followed by a DNS name (which itself has >= 2 labels).
[[nodiscard]] bool is_valid_redirect_pattern(std::string_view pattern) noexcept;
// Exact match, or "*.<base>" matching exactly one extra leftmost label.
[[nodiscard]] bool host_matches(std::string_view pattern, std::string_view host) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] bool host_in(std::span<const std::string> patterns, std::string_view host) noexcept;

// Manifest source: https://<host>[:443]/<path>; path = RFC 3986 unreserved, '/', %XX;
// no userinfo, query or fragment; host already lowercase; <= 2048 bytes.
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<HttpsUrl, UrlProblem> parse_source_url(std::string_view text);
// Location header: an absolute https URL (host lowercased) or an absolute path
// resolved against current_host. Query allowed with = & + , ; : @ ! $ ' ( ) * in
// addition to the path set; never [ ] { } space " \ ; <= 8 KiB.
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<HttpsUrl, UrlProblem> parse_location(std::string_view value, std::string_view current_host);
[[nodiscard]] std::string render_url(const HttpsUrl& url);

// pull.proxy: http://<host>:<port> or https://<host>:<port>; host = LDH name
// (one label allowed), dotted IPv4 or [IPv6]; port 1..65535 without leading zero.
struct ProxySetting final {
  bool https{false};
  std::string host;
  std::uint16_t port{0};
};
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<ProxySetting, UrlProblem> parse_proxy(std::string_view text);
[[nodiscard]] std::string render_proxy(const ProxySetting& proxy);

[[nodiscard]] std::string_view describe_url_problem(UrlProblem problem) noexcept;

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/acquire_url.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/acquire_url.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <optional>

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {

constexpr std::string_view kScheme = "https://";

constexpr bool is_digit(const char c) noexcept { return c >= '0' && c <= '9'; }
constexpr bool is_lower(const char c) noexcept { return c >= 'a' && c <= 'z'; }
constexpr bool is_upper(const char c) noexcept { return c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z'; }
constexpr bool is_hex(const char c) noexcept { return is_digit(c) || (c >= 'a' && c <= 'f') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'F'); }
constexpr bool is_unreserved(const char c) noexcept {
  return is_digit(c) || is_lower(c) || is_upper(c) || c == '-' || c == '.' || c == '_' || c == '~';
}
constexpr bool is_query_extra(const char c) noexcept { return std::string_view{"=&+,;:@!$'()*"}.find(c) != std::string_view::npos; }

// Unreserved, '/', %XX; in a query also the query-extra set.
bool valid_characters(const std::string_view text, const bool query) noexcept {
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < text.size(); ++i) {
    const char c = text[i];
    if (c == '%') {
      if (i + 2 >= text.size() || !is_hex(text[i + 1]) || !is_hex(text[i + 2])) return false;
      i += 2;
      continue;
    }
    if (is_unreserved(c) || c == '/') continue;
    if (query && is_query_extra(c)) continue;
    return false;
  }
  return true;
}

std::string lowercase(std::string_view text) {
  std::string out{text};
  for (char& c : out) {
    if (is_upper(c)) c = static_cast<char>(c - 'A' + 'a');
  }
  return out;
}

// authority = host[:443]; fills `host`.
std::variant<std::string, UrlProblem> parse_authority(const std::string_view authority, const bool lower) {
  if (authority.find('@') != std::string_view::npos) return UrlProblem::userinfo;
  if (authority.empty() || authority.front() == '[') return UrlProblem::bad_host;
  std::string_view host = authority;
  if (const auto colon = authority.rfind(':'); colon != std::string_view::npos) {
    if (authority.substr(colon + 1) != "443") return UrlProblem::port_not_allowed;
    host = authority.substr(0, colon);
  }
  std::string normalized = lower ? lowercase(host) : std::string{host};
  if (!is_valid_dns_name(normalized)) return UrlProblem::bad_host;
  return normalized;
}

// target = path[?query]; query only when allowed.
std::optional<UrlProblem> check_target(const std::string_view target, const bool allow_query) {
  if (target.find('#') != std::string_view::npos) return UrlProblem::fragment;
  const auto question = target.find('?');
  if (question != std::string_view::npos) {
    if (!allow_query) return UrlProblem::query_not_allowed;
    const auto query = target.substr(question + 1);
    if (query.find('?') != std::string_view::npos || !valid_characters(query, true)) return UrlProblem::bad_character;
  }
  if (!valid_characters(target.substr(0, question), false)) return UrlProblem::bad_character;
  return std::nullopt;
}

bool is_ldh_label(const std::string_view label) noexcept {
  if (label.empty() || label.size() > 63 || label.front() == '-' || label.back() == '-') return false;
  return std::all_of(label.begin(), label.end(), [](const char c) { return is_lower(c) || is_digit(c) || c == '-'; });
}

bool is_valid_proxy_host(const std::string_view host) noexcept {
  if (host.size() >= 2 && host.front() == '[' && host.back() == ']') {
    const auto inner = host.substr(1, host.size() - 2);
    return !inner.empty() && std::all_of(inner.begin(), inner.end(), [](const char c) {
      return is_digit(c) || (c >= 'a' && c <= 'f') || c == ':' || c == '.';
    });
  }
  if (host.empty() || host.size() > 253) return false;
  std::size_t start = 0;
  while (true) {
    const auto dot = host.find('.', start);
    if (!is_ldh_label(host.substr(start, dot == std::string_view::npos ? std::string_view::npos : dot - start))) return false;
    if (dot == std::string_view::npos) return true;
    start = dot + 1;
  }
}

}  // namespace

bool is_valid_dns_name(const std::string_view name) noexcept {
  if (name.empty() || name.size() > 253) return false;
  std::size_t labels = 0;
  std::size_t start = 0;
  std::string_view last;
  while (true) {
    const auto dot = name.find('.', start);
    const auto label = name.substr(start, dot == std::string_view::npos ? std::string_view::npos : dot - start);
    if (!is_ldh_label(label)) return false;
    ++labels;
    last = label;
    if (dot == std::string_view::npos) break;
    start = dot + 1;
  }
  return labels >= 2 && !std::all_of(last.begin(), last.end(), is_digit);
}

bool is_valid_redirect_pattern(const std::string_view pattern) noexcept {
  if (pattern.starts_with("*.")) return is_valid_dns_name(pattern.substr(2));
  return is_valid_dns_name(pattern);
}

bool host_matches(const std::string_view pattern, const std::string_view host) noexcept {
  if (!pattern.starts_with("*.")) return pattern == host;
  const auto base = pattern.substr(1);  // ".<base>"
  if (host.size() <= base.size() || !host.ends_with(base)) return false;
  const auto label = host.substr(0, host.size() - base.size());
  return !label.empty() && label.find('.') == std::string_view::npos;
}

bool host_in(const std::span<const std::string> patterns, const std::string_view host) noexcept {
  return std::any_of(patterns.begin(), patterns.end(), [host](const std::string& p) { return host_matches(p, host); });
}

std::variant<HttpsUrl, UrlProblem> parse_source_url(const std::string_view text) {
  if (text.size() > kMaxSourceUrlBytes) return UrlProblem::too_long;
  if (!text.starts_with(kScheme)) return UrlProblem::not_https;
  const auto rest = text.substr(kScheme.size());
  const auto slash = rest.find('/');
  const auto authority = rest.substr(0, slash);
  if (authority.find_first_of("?#") != std::string_view::npos) {
    return authority.find('#') != std::string_view::npos ? UrlProblem::fragment : UrlProblem::query_not_allowed;
  }
  auto host = parse_authority(authority, false);
  if (auto* problem = std::get_if<UrlProblem>(&host)) return *problem;
  if (slash == std::string_view::npos) return UrlProblem::missing_path;
  const auto target = rest.substr(slash);
  if (const auto problem = check_target(target, false)) return *problem;
  if (target == "/") return UrlProblem::missing_path;
  return HttpsUrl{std::get<std::string>(std::move(host)), std::string{target}};
}

std::variant<HttpsUrl, UrlProblem> parse_location(const std::string_view value, const std::string_view current_host) {
  if (value.size() > kMaxLocationBytes) return UrlProblem::too_long;
  std::string host;
  std::string target;
  if (value.starts_with(kScheme)) {
    const auto rest = value.substr(kScheme.size());
    const auto end = rest.find_first_of("/?#");
    auto parsed = parse_authority(rest.substr(0, end), true);
    if (auto* problem = std::get_if<UrlProblem>(&parsed)) return *problem;
    host = std::get<std::string>(std::move(parsed));
    target = end == std::string_view::npos ? std::string{"/"} : std::string{rest.substr(end)};
    if (target.front() == '?') target.insert(target.begin(), '/');
    if (target.front() == '#') return UrlProblem::fragment;
  } else if (value.starts_with('/') && !value.starts_with("//")) {
    host = std::string{current_host};
    target = std::string{value};
  } else {
    return UrlProblem::not_https;
  }
  if (const auto problem = check_target(target, true)) return *problem;
  return HttpsUrl{std::move(host), std::move(target)};
}

std::string render_url(const HttpsUrl& url) { return std::string{kScheme} + url.host + url.target; }

std::variant<ProxySetting, UrlProblem> parse_proxy(const std::string_view text) {
  ProxySetting proxy;
  std::string_view rest;
  if (text.starts_with("http://")) {
    rest = text.substr(7);
  } else if (text.starts_with("https://")) {
    proxy.https = true;
    rest = text.substr(8);
  } else {
    return UrlProblem::not_https;
  }
  if (rest.find('@') != std::string_view::npos) return UrlProblem::userinfo;
  if (rest.find_first_of("/?#") != std::string_view::npos) return UrlProblem::bad_character;
  const auto colon = rest.rfind(':');
  if (colon == std::string_view::npos || (rest.front() == '[' && colon < rest.find(']'))) return UrlProblem::port_not_allowed;
  const auto port_text = rest.substr(colon + 1);
  if (port_text.empty() || port_text.size() > 5 || port_text.front() == '0' ||
      !std::all_of(port_text.begin(), port_text.end(), is_digit)) {
    return UrlProblem::port_not_allowed;
  }
  unsigned long port = 0;
  for (const char c : port_text) port = port * 10U + static_cast<unsigned long>(c - '0');
  if (port == 0 || port > 65535U) return UrlProblem::port_not_allowed;
  const auto host = rest.substr(0, colon);
  if (!is_valid_proxy_host(host)) return UrlProblem::bad_host;
  proxy.host = std::string{host};
  proxy.port = static_cast<std::uint16_t>(port);
  return proxy;
}

std::string render_proxy(const ProxySetting& proxy) {
  return std::string{proxy.https ? "https://" : "http://"} + proxy.host + ":" + std::to_string(proxy.port);
}

std::string_view describe_url_problem(const UrlProblem problem) noexcept {
  switch (problem) {
    case UrlProblem::not_https: return "must be an https:// URL";
    case UrlProblem::bad_host: return "host must be a lowercase DNS name with at least two labels";
    case UrlProblem::port_not_allowed: return "port must be absent or 443";
    case UrlProblem::userinfo: return "must not contain userinfo";
    case UrlProblem::fragment: return "must not contain a fragment";
    case UrlProblem::query_not_allowed: return "must not contain a query";
    case UrlProblem::bad_character: return "contains a character outside the allowed set";
    case UrlProblem::missing_path: return "must name a file path";
    case UrlProblem::too_long: return "is too long";
  }
  return "is invalid";
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

```cpp
         "no answers");

void ipv6_only_answer_fails_closed() {
  const std::vector answers{make_ipv6({0x2a00, 0x1450, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1})};
  expect(secure_frontend::choose_pinned_address(answers, false).kind == secure_frontend::AddressVerdictKind::no_usable_address,
         "IPv6-only hops fail closed until the [addr] --resolve form is verified");
  const std::vector mixed{make_ipv6({0x2a00, 0x1450, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1}), make_ipv4(1, 1, 1, 1)};
  const auto verdict = secure_frontend::choose_pinned_address(mixed, false);
```

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <array>
#include <cstdint>
#include <span>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>

namespace secure_frontend {

// One getaddrinfo answer. IPv4 occupies bytes[0..3].
struct ResolvedAddress final {
  bool ipv6{false};
  std::array<std::uint8_t, 16> bytes{};
};

[[nodiscard]] ResolvedAddress make_ipv4(std::uint8_t a, std::uint8_t b, std::uint8_t c, std::uint8_t d) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] ResolvedAddress make_ipv6(const std::array<std::uint16_t, 8>& groups) noexcept;

// Spec §3.5 denied ranges (used unless pull.allow_private_addresses).
[[nodiscard]] bool is_denied_address(const ResolvedAddress& address) noexcept;

// Spec §3.5: until `--resolve host:443:[addr]` is verified on every curl at or
// above the D3 floor, an IPv6-only hop fails closed with address_not_allowed.
inline constexpr bool kIpv6ResolvePinVerified = false;

enum class AddressVerdictKind { pinned, denied, no_usable_address };
struct AddressVerdict final {
  AddressVerdictKind kind;
  ResolvedAddress pinned;
};

// Denied if ANY answer is in a denied range (unless allow_private); otherwise
// pins the first IPv4 answer (IPv6 only once kIpv6ResolvePinVerified).
[[nodiscard]] AddressVerdict choose_pinned_address(std::span<const ResolvedAddress> answers, bool allow_private) noexcept;

// Dotted IPv4, or eight lowercase hex groups without compression. For curl's
// argv only: never printed, logged or stored (spec §8.1).
[[nodiscard]] std::string format_address(const ResolvedAddress& address);
[[nodiscard]] std::string resolve_argument(std::string_view host, const ResolvedAddress& address);

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/address_policy.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/address_policy.hpp"

#include <cstdio>

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {

struct Range final {
  std::array<std::uint8_t, 16> prefix;
  unsigned bits;
};

constexpr Range v4(const std::uint8_t a, const std::uint8_t b, const std::uint8_t c, const std::uint8_t d,
                   const unsigned bits) noexcept {
  return Range{{a, b, c, d}, bits};
}

constexpr Range v6(const std::array<std::uint16_t, 8> g, const unsigned bits) noexcept {
  Range range{{}, bits};
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
    range.prefix[2 * i] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(g[i] >> 8U);
    range.prefix[2 * i + 1] = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(g[i] & 0xFFU);
  }
  return range;
}

constexpr std::array<Range, 14> kDenied4{
    v4(0, 0, 0, 0, 8),     v4(10, 0, 0, 0, 8),    v4(100, 64, 0, 0, 10), v4(127, 0, 0, 0, 8),
    v4(169, 254, 0, 0, 16), v4(172, 16, 0, 0, 12), v4(192, 0, 0, 0, 24),  v4(192, 0, 2, 0, 24),
    v4(192, 168, 0, 0, 16), v4(198, 18, 0, 0, 15), v4(198, 51, 100, 0, 24), v4(203, 0, 113, 0, 24),
    v4(224, 0, 0, 0, 4),   v4(240, 0, 0, 0, 4)};

constexpr std::array<Range, 13> kDenied6{
    v6({0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0}, 128),          v6({0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 1}, 128),
    v6({0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0}, 96),           v6({0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0xffff, 0, 0}, 96),
    v6({0x64, 0xff9b, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0}, 96),   v6({0x64, 0xff9b, 1, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0}, 48),
    v6({0x100, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0}, 64),       v6({0x2001, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0}, 32),
    v6({0x2001, 0xdb8, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0}, 32),  v6({0x2002, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0}, 16),
    v6({0xfc00, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0}, 7),       v6({0xfe80, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0}, 10),
    v6({0xff00, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0}, 8)};

bool in_range(const std::array<std::uint8_t, 16>& address, const Range& range) noexcept {
  const unsigned full = range.bits / 8U;
  for (unsigned i = 0; i < full; ++i) {
    if (address[i] != range.prefix[i]) return false;
  }
  const unsigned rest = range.bits % 8U;
  if (rest == 0) return true;
  const auto mask = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(0xFFU << (8U - rest));
  return (address[full] & mask) == (range.prefix[full] & mask);
}

}  // namespace

ResolvedAddress make_ipv4(const std::uint8_t a, const std::uint8_t b, const std::uint8_t c, const std::uint8_t d) noexcept {
  ResolvedAddress address;
  address.bytes[0] = a;
  address.bytes[1] = b;
  address.bytes[2] = c;
  address.bytes[3] = d;
  return address;
}

ResolvedAddress make_ipv6(const std::array<std::uint16_t, 8>& groups) noexcept {
  ResolvedAddress address;
  address.ipv6 = true;
  address.bytes = v6(groups, 128).prefix;
  return address;
}

bool is_denied_address(const ResolvedAddress& address) noexcept {
  if (!address.ipv6) {
    for (const auto& range : kDenied4) {
      if (in_range(address.bytes, range)) return true;
    }
    return false;
  }
  for (const auto& range : kDenied6) {
    if (in_range(address.bytes, range)) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

AddressVerdict choose_pinned_address(const std::span<const ResolvedAddress> answers, const bool allow_private) noexcept {
  if (answers.empty()) return {AddressVerdictKind::no_usable_address, {}};
  if (!allow_private) {
    for (const auto& answer : answers) {
      if (is_denied_address(answer)) return {AddressVerdictKind::denied, {}};
    }
  }
  for (const auto& answer : answers) {
    if (!answer.ipv6) return {AddressVerdictKind::pinned, answer};
  }
  if (kIpv6ResolvePinVerified) return {AddressVerdictKind::pinned, answers.front()};
  return {AddressVerdictKind::no_usable_address, {}};
}

std::string format_address(const ResolvedAddress& address) {
  char buffer[48] = {};
  if (!address.ipv6) {
    std::snprintf(buffer, sizeof buffer, "%u.%u.%u.%u", address.bytes[0], address.bytes[1], address.bytes[2],
                  address.bytes[3]);
    return buffer;
  }
  std::string text;
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 8; ++i) {
    const unsigned group = (static_cast<unsigned>(address.bytes[2 * i]) << 8U) | address.bytes[2 * i + 1];
    std::snprintf(buffer, sizeof buffer, "%x", group);
    if (i != 0) text.push_back(':');
    text += buffer;
  }
  return text;
}

std::string resolve_argument(const std::string_view host, const ResolvedAddress& address) {
  const std::string formatted = format_address(address);
  return std::string{host} + ":443:" + (address.ipv6 ? "[" + formatted + "]" : formatted);
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/downloader_command.hpp"

// in the anonymous namespace:
void transfer_argv_exact_order() {
  secure_frontend::TransferRequest request;
  request.url = "https://huggingface.co/r/resolve/abc/f.gguf";
  request.host = "huggingface.co";
  request.pin = secure_frontend::make_ipv4(1, 2, 3, 4);
  request.range_from = 1000;
  request.connect_timeout_s = 30;
  request.stall_timeout_s = 120;
  request.max_time_s = 172800;
  const std::vector<std::string> expected{
      "/usr/bin/curl", "-q", "--config", "-", "--silent", "--include", "--suppress-connect-headers", "--globoff",
      "--proto", "=https", "--max-redirs", "0", "--tlsv1.2", "--connect-timeout", "30", "--speed-limit", "1024",
      "--speed-time", "120", "--max-time", "172800", "--user-agent", "strata", "--header", "Accept-Encoding: identity",
      "--noproxy", "*", "--resolve", "huggingface.co:443:1.2.3.4", "--range", "1000-"};
  expect(secure_frontend::build_transfer_argv("/usr/bin/curl", request) == expected, "argv is exactly spec §3.3");
  for (const auto& argument : secure_frontend::build_transfer_argv("/usr/bin/curl", request)) {
    expect(argument.find("resolve/abc") == std::string::npos, "no URL in argv");
  }
  secure_frontend::TransferRequest proxied = request;
  proxied.proxy = secure_frontend::ProxySetting{false, "proxy.corp", 3128};
  proxied.pin.reset();
  proxied.range_from.reset();
  const auto with_proxy = secure_frontend::build_transfer_argv("/usr/bin/curl", proxied);
  expect(with_proxy[with_proxy.size() - 2] == "--proxy" && with_proxy.back() == "http://proxy.corp:3128",
         "--proxy replaces --noproxy and no --resolve with a proxy");
}

void config_line_is_single_quoted_url() {
  secure_frontend::TransferRequest request;
  request.url = "https://huggingface.co/a/b.gguf";
  expect(secure_frontend::build_config_stdin(request) == "url = \"https://huggingface.co/a/b.gguf\"\n", "one config line");
}

void probe_argv_is_version_only() {
  expect((secure_frontend::build_probe_argv("C:\\Windows\\System32\\curl.exe") ==
          std::vector<std::string>{"C:\\Windows\\System32\\curl.exe", "-q", "--version"}),
         "probe argv is exactly <curl> -q --version");
}

void probe_output_parsing_and_floor() {
  using secure_frontend::DownloaderPlatform;
  const auto ubuntu = secure_frontend::parse_probe_output(
      "curl 7.81.0 (x86_64-pc-linux-gnu) libcurl/7.81.0 OpenSSL/3.0.2 zlib/1.2.11 brotli/1.0.9 zstd/1.4.8\n"
      "Release-Date: 2022-01-05\nProtocols: dict file ftp ftps http https\n");
  expect(ubuntu.has_value() && ubuntu->major == 7 && ubuntu->minor == 81 && ubuntu->patch == 0, "version parsed");
  expect(ubuntu.has_value() && ubuntu->tls_backend == "OpenSSL", "TLS backend parsed");
  expect(ubuntu && secure_frontend::meets_version_floor(*ubuntu, DownloaderPlatform::linux_like), "7.81.0 meets the Linux floor");
  expect(ubuntu && !secure_frontend::meets_version_floor(*ubuntu, DownloaderPlatform::windows), "7.81.0 is below the Windows floor");
  const auto windows = secure_frontend::parse_probe_output("curl 8.4.0 (Windows) libcurl/8.4.0 Schannel WinIDN\r\n");
  expect(windows.has_value() && windows->tls_backend == "Schannel", "Schannel parsed");
  expect(windows && secure_frontend::meets_version_floor(*windows, DownloaderPlatform::windows), "8.4.0 meets the Windows floor");
  const auto old = secure_frontend::parse_probe_output("curl 7.80.0 (x86_64-pc-linux-gnu) libcurl/7.80.0 OpenSSL/1.1.1\n");
  expect(old && !secure_frontend::meets_version_floor(*old, DownloaderPlatform::linux_like), "7.80.0 below the Linux floor");
  expect(!secure_frontend::parse_probe_output("wget 1.21\n").has_value(), "not curl");
  expect(!secure_frontend::parse_probe_output("curl 8.5.0 (x) libcurl/8.5.0 zlib/1.3\n").has_value(), "no TLS backend");
  const auto dev = secure_frontend::parse_probe_output("curl 8.11.1-DEV (x) libcurl/8.11.1-DEV rustls-ffi/0.14.0\n");
  expect(dev.has_value() && dev->minor == 11 && dev->tls_backend == "rustls-ffi", "suffixes tolerated");
}

void curl_exit_codes_map_to_reasons() {
  using R = secure_frontend::AcquireReason;
  const auto map = [](const int code) { return secure_frontend::map_curl_exit(code, false); };
  expect(!map(0).has_value(), "0 is decided by the status table");
  expect(map(5) == R::dns_failed && map(6) == R::dns_failed, "5, 6");
  expect(map(7) == R::connect_failed, "7");
  for (const int code : {18, 52, 55, 56, 92}) expect(map(code) == R::size_short, "size_short " + std::to_string(code));
  expect(map(28) == R::stalled, "28 stalled");
  expect(secure_frontend::map_curl_exit(28, true) == R::time_budget_exceeded, "28 with the run budget used up");
  expect(map(35) == R::tls_failed, "35");
  for (const int code : {60, 58, 59, 77, 83, 90, 91}) expect(map(code) == R::tls_untrusted, "tls_untrusted " + std::to_string(code));
  for (const int code : {1, 2, 3, 4, 43, 48}) expect(map(code) == R::downloader_unsupported, "unsupported " + std::to_string(code));
  for (const int code : {8, 22, 47, 99}) expect(map(code) == R::transport_failed, "other " + std::to_string(code));
}
```
