```cpp
#pragma once

#include "secure_frontend/acquire_reason.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/acquire_url.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/address_policy.hpp"

#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>

namespace secure_frontend {

// One request, one hop (spec §3.3). `url` is a validated source URL or Location
// and goes only into the stdin config, never into argv.
struct TransferRequest final {
  std::string url;
  std::string host;
  std::optional<ResolvedAddress> pin;        // only without a proxy
  std::optional<std::uint64_t> range_from;   // only when resuming
  std::optional<ProxySetting> proxy;
  std::uint32_t connect_timeout_s{30};
  std::uint32_t stall_timeout_s{120};
  std::uint64_t max_time_s{172800};          // remaining run budget, >= 1
  std::string test_cacert;                   // SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS builds only; empty = absent
};

// <curl> -q --config - --silent --include --suppress-connect-headers --globoff
// --proto =https --max-redirs 0 --tlsv1.2 --connect-timeout N --speed-limit 1024
// --speed-time N --max-time N --user-agent strata --header "Accept-Encoding: identity"
// (--noproxy * | --proxy P) [--resolve h:443:a] [--range N-] [--cacert F (hooks)]
[[nodiscard]] std::vector<std::string> build_transfer_argv(std::string_view executable, const TransferRequest& request);
// Exactly: url = "<url>"\n (the URL grammar excludes '"' and '\').
[[nodiscard]] std::string build_config_stdin(const TransferRequest& request);
// Exactly: <curl> -q --version
[[nodiscard]] std::vector<std::string> build_probe_argv(std::string_view executable);

struct ProbeResult final {
  std::uint32_t major{0};
  std::uint32_t minor{0};
  std::uint32_t patch{0};
  std::string tls_backend;
};
// First line "curl X.Y.Z[...] (...) libcurl/... <TLS>/..."; nullopt if not curl or no known TLS backend.
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<ProbeResult> parse_probe_output(std::string_view text);

enum class DownloaderPlatform { linux_like, windows };
#if defined(_WIN32)
inline constexpr DownloaderPlatform kThisDownloaderPlatform = DownloaderPlatform::windows;
#else
inline constexpr DownloaderPlatform kThisDownloaderPlatform = DownloaderPlatform::linux_like;
#endif
// D3/C3: >= 8.4.0 on Windows, >= 7.81.0 on Linux.
[[nodiscard]] bool meets_version_floor(const ProbeResult& probe, DownloaderPlatform platform) noexcept;

// Spec §3.4 curl exit-code table; nullopt for 0 (the status table decides).
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireReason> map_curl_exit(int exit_code, bool run_budget_exhausted) noexcept;

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/downloader_command.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/downloader_command.hpp"

#include <array>
#include <tuple>

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {

constexpr std::array<std::string_view, 12> kTlsBackends{"OpenSSL", "LibreSSL", "BoringSSL", "quictls", "GnuTLS",
                                                        "Schannel", "wolfSSL", "mbedTLS", "rustls-ffi", "rustls",
                                                        "SecureTransport", "BearSSL"};

bool parse_number(std::string_view& text, std::uint32_t& out) {
  std::size_t used = 0;
  std::uint64_t value = 0;
  while (used < text.size() && text[used] >= '0' && text[used] <= '9' && used < 6) {
    value = value * 10U + static_cast<std::uint64_t>(text[used] - '0');
    ++used;
  }
  if (used == 0) return false;
  out = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(value);
  text.remove_prefix(used);
  return true;
}

}  // namespace

std::vector<std::string> build_transfer_argv(const std::string_view executable, const TransferRequest& request) {
  std::vector<std::string> argv{std::string{executable}, "-q", "--config", "-", "--silent", "--include",
                                "--suppress-connect-headers", "--globoff", "--proto", "=https", "--max-redirs", "0",
                                "--tlsv1.2", "--connect-timeout", std::to_string(request.connect_timeout_s),
                                "--speed-limit", "1024", "--speed-time", std::to_string(request.stall_timeout_s),
                                "--max-time", std::to_string(request.max_time_s), "--user-agent", "strata",
                                "--header", "Accept-Encoding: identity"};
  if (request.proxy.has_value()) {
    argv.emplace_back("--proxy");
    argv.push_back(render_proxy(*request.proxy));
  } else {
    argv.emplace_back("--noproxy");
    argv.emplace_back("*");
    if (request.pin.has_value()) {
      argv.emplace_back("--resolve");
      argv.push_back(resolve_argument(request.host, *request.pin));
    }
  }
  if (request.range_from.has_value()) {
    argv.emplace_back("--range");
    argv.push_back(std::to_string(*request.range_from) + "-");
  }
#if defined(SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS)
  if (!request.test_cacert.empty()) {
    argv.emplace_back("--cacert");
    argv.push_back(request.test_cacert);
  }
#endif
  return argv;
}

std::string build_config_stdin(const TransferRequest& request) { return "url = \"" + request.url + "\"\n"; }

std::vector<std::string> build_probe_argv(const std::string_view executable) {
  return {std::string{executable}, "-q", "--version"};
}

std::optional<ProbeResult> parse_probe_output(const std::string_view text) {
  const auto end = text.find_first_of("\r\n");
  std::string_view line = text.substr(0, end);
  if (!line.starts_with("curl ")) return std::nullopt;
  std::string_view version = line.substr(5);
  ProbeResult result;
  if (!parse_number(version, result.major) || !version.starts_with('.')) return std::nullopt;
  version.remove_prefix(1);
  if (!parse_number(version, result.minor) || !version.starts_with('.')) return std::nullopt;
  version.remove_prefix(1);
  if (!parse_number(version, result.patch)) return std::nullopt;
  std::size_t start = 0;
  while (start < line.size()) {
    auto stop = line.find(' ', start);
    if (stop == std::string_view::npos) stop = line.size();
    const auto token = line.substr(start, stop - start);
    const auto name = token.substr(0, token.find('/'));
    for (const auto backend : kTlsBackends) {
      if (name == backend) {
        result.tls_backend = std::string{backend};
        return result;
      }
    }
    start = stop + 1;
  }
  return std::nullopt;
}

bool meets_version_floor(const ProbeResult& probe, const DownloaderPlatform platform) noexcept {
  const auto have = std::tie(probe.major, probe.minor, probe.patch);
  if (platform == DownloaderPlatform::windows) return have >= std::make_tuple(8U, 4U, 0U);
  return have >= std::make_tuple(7U, 81U, 0U);
}

std::optional<AcquireReason> map_curl_exit(const int exit_code, const bool run_budget_exhausted) noexcept {
  switch (exit_code) {
    case 0: return std::nullopt;
    case 5: case 6: return AcquireReason::dns_failed;
    case 7: return AcquireReason::connect_failed;
    case 18: case 52: case 55: case 56: case 92: return AcquireReason::size_short;
    case 28: return run_budget_exhausted ? AcquireReason::time_budget_exceeded : AcquireReason::stalled;
    case 35: return AcquireReason::tls_failed;
    case 58: case 59: case 60: case 77: case 83: case 90: case 91: return AcquireReason::tls_untrusted;
    case 1: case 2: case 3: case 4: case 43: case 48: return AcquireReason::downloader_unsupported;
    default: return AcquireReason::transport_failed;
  }
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

Note on `"rustls-ffi"` vs `"rustls"`: the table checks `"rustls-ffi"` first because the token name before `/` is compared for equality, so order does not matter for correctness; keep both.

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/download_response.hpp"

// in the anonymous namespace:
using secure_frontend::HeadAction;
using secure_frontend::ResponseHeadParser;

secure_frontend::ResponseHead head_of(const std::string_view bytes) {
  ResponseHeadParser parser;
  const auto fed = parser.feed(bytes);
  expect(fed.state == ResponseHeadParser::State::head_ready, "head parses");
  return parser.head();
}

void informational_header_blocks_skipped() {
  ResponseHeadParser parser;
  const std::string bytes =
      "HTTP/1.1 100 Continue\r\n\r\nHTTP/2 103\r\nlink: </x>; rel=preload\r\n\r\n"
      "HTTP/2 200 \r\ncontent-length: 5\r\nX-Linked-Etag: \"abc\"\r\n\r\nhello";
  const auto fed = parser.feed(bytes);
  expect(fed.state == ResponseHeadParser::State::head_ready, "1xx blocks skipped, final head ready");
  expect(parser.head().status == 200, "final status");
  expect(bytes.substr(fed.consumed) == "hello", "consumed stops exactly at the body");
  expect(secure_frontend::header_values(parser.head(), "x-linked-etag").size() == 1, "names lowercased");
  ResponseHeadParser too_many;
  std::string five;
  for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) five += "HTTP/1.1 103 Early Hints\r\n\r\n";
  expect(too_many.feed(five + "HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\n\r\n").state == ResponseHeadParser::State::malformed,
         "a fifth 1xx block is response_malformed");
}

void header_block_limits_enforced() {
  ResponseHeadParser big;
  expect(big.feed("HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nx: " + std::string(16400, 'a') + "\r\n\r\n").state ==
             ResponseHeadParser::State::malformed,
         "block over 16 KiB");
  std::string lines = "HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\n";
  for (int i = 0; i < 64; ++i) lines += "x" + std::to_string(i) + ": v\r\n";
  ResponseHeadParser many;
  expect(many.feed(lines + "\r\n").state == ResponseHeadParser::State::malformed, "65 lines");
  for (const auto* bad : {"HTTP/1.1 20 OK\r\n\r\n", "HTTP/3 200\r\n\r\n", "ICY 200 OK\r\n\r\n",
                          "HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\n folded: x\r\n\r\n", "HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nno colon\r\n\r\n",
                          "HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nbad name: x\r\n\r\n", "HTTP/1.1 200 OK\nx: y\n\n"}) {
    ResponseHeadParser parser;
    const auto state = parser.feed(bad).state;
    expect(state != ResponseHeadParser::State::head_ready, std::string{"malformed head: "} + bad);
  }
}

void split_feed_reports_exact_body_offset() {
  const std::string bytes = "HTTP/1.1 206 Partial Content\r\nContent-Range: bytes 4-9/10\r\n\r\nbody!!";
  for (std::size_t split = 1; split < bytes.size(); ++split) {
    ResponseHeadParser parser;
    const auto first = parser.feed(std::string_view{bytes}.substr(0, split));
    std::size_t header_bytes = first.consumed;
    if (first.state == ResponseHeadParser::State::need_more) {
      const auto second = parser.feed(std::string_view{bytes}.substr(split));
      expect(second.state == ResponseHeadParser::State::head_ready, "ready after second feed");
      header_bytes += second.consumed;
    }
    expect(bytes.substr(header_bytes) == "body!!", "split at " + std::to_string(split));
  }
}

void status_table_decisions() {
  const secure_frontend::HopExpectation fresh{10, std::nullopt};
  const secure_frontend::HopExpectation resume{10, 4};
  expect(secure_frontend::classify_head(head_of("HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Length: 10\r\n\r\n"), fresh).action ==
             HeadAction::write_body, "200 without range");
  const auto announced = secure_frontend::classify_head(head_of("HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Length: 11\r\n\r\n"), fresh);
  expect(announced.action == HeadAction::fail && announced.reason == secure_frontend::AcquireReason::size_mismatch_announced,
         "Content-Length must equal the remaining size");
  expect(secure_frontend::classify_head(head_of("HTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\n\r\n"), resume).action == HeadAction::restart_from_zero,
         "200 to a range request restarts from byte 0");
  expect(secure_frontend::classify_head(head_of("HTTP/1.1 206 X\r\nContent-Range: bytes 4-9/10\r\n\r\n"), resume).action ==
             HeadAction::write_body, "exact Content-Range");
  for (const auto* range : {"bytes 0-9/10", "bytes 4-8/10", "bytes 4-9/11", "bytes 4-9/*", "bytes  4-9/10"}) {
    const auto decision = secure_frontend::classify_head(
        head_of(std::string{"HTTP/1.1 206 X\r\nContent-Range: "} + range + "\r\n\r\n"), resume);
    expect(decision.reason == secure_frontend::AcquireReason::range_mismatch, std::string{"range_mismatch: "} + range);
  }
  expect(secure_frontend::classify_head(head_of("HTTP/1.1 206 X\r\nContent-Range: bytes 0-9/10\r\n\r\n"), fresh).reason ==
             secure_frontend::AcquireReason::range_mismatch, "206 without a range request");
  const auto redirect = secure_frontend::classify_head(head_of("HTTP/1.1 302 Found\r\nLocation: /x\r\n\r\n"), fresh);
  expect(redirect.action == HeadAction::redirect && redirect.location == "/x", "302 with one Location");
  expect(secure_frontend::classify_head(head_of("HTTP/1.1 307 X\r\nLocation: /a\r\nLocation: /b\r\n\r\n"), fresh).reason ==
             secure_frontend::AcquireReason::response_malformed, "two Location headers");
  expect(secure_frontend::classify_head(head_of("HTTP/1.1 416 X\r\n\r\n"), resume).reason ==
             secure_frontend::AcquireReason::range_mismatch, "416");
  const auto busy = secure_frontend::classify_head(head_of("HTTP/1.1 503 X\r\n\r\n"), fresh);
  expect(busy.reason == secure_frontend::AcquireReason::http_status && busy.transient && busy.status == 503, "5xx transient");
  expect(secure_frontend::classify_head(head_of("HTTP/1.1 429 X\r\n\r\n"), fresh).transient, "429 transient");
  const auto missing = secure_frontend::classify_head(head_of("HTTP/1.1 404 X\r\n\r\n"), fresh);
  expect(missing.reason == secure_frontend::AcquireReason::http_status && !missing.transient && missing.status == 404,
         "404 is http_status, next source");
}
```

```cpp
#pragma once

#include "secure_frontend/acquire_reason.hpp"

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

namespace secure_frontend {

inline constexpr std::size_t kMaxHeaderBlockBytes = 16U * 1024U;
inline constexpr std::size_t kMaxHeaderLines = 64;
inline constexpr std::size_t kMaxInformationalBlocks = 4;

struct ResponseHead final {
  int status{0};
  std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>> headers;  // names lowercased, values OWS-trimmed
};

[[nodiscard]] std::vector<std::string_view> header_values(const ResponseHead& head, std::string_view lower_name);

// Parses the header blocks curl writes with --include, skipping up to four 1xx
// blocks. Lines end with CRLF; status lines are HTTP/1.0, HTTP/1.1 or HTTP/2.
class ResponseHeadParser final {
 public:
  enum class State { need_more, head_ready, malformed };
  struct Feed final {
    State state;
    std::size_t consumed;  // bytes of this call that were header bytes
  };
  [[nodiscard]] Feed feed(std::string_view bytes);
  [[nodiscard]] const ResponseHead& head() const noexcept { return head_; }

 private:
  std::string block_;
  std::size_t informational_{0};
  ResponseHead head_;
  State state_{State::need_more};
};

struct HopExpectation final {
  std::uint64_t file_size;
  std::optional<std::uint64_t> range_from;
};

enum class HeadAction { write_body, restart_from_zero, redirect, fail };

struct HeadDecision final {
  HeadAction action;
  AcquireReason reason{AcquireReason::response_malformed};
  bool transient{false};  // 429 and 5xx are retried on the same source (§3.4)
  std::string location;   // raw Location value; parse with parse_location, never print
  int status{0};
};

// Spec §3.4 status table.
[[nodiscard]] HeadDecision classify_head(const ResponseHead& head, const HopExpectation& expectation);

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/download_response.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/download_response.hpp"

#include <algorithm>

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {

constexpr bool is_digit(const char c) noexcept { return c >= '0' && c <= '9'; }
constexpr bool is_tchar(const char c) noexcept {
  return is_digit(c) || (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') ||
         std::string_view{"!#$%&'*+-.^_`|~"}.find(c) != std::string_view::npos;
}

std::string lower(std::string_view text) {
  std::string out{text};
  for (char& c : out) {
    if (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') c = static_cast<char>(c - 'A' + 'a');
  }
  return out;
}

std::string_view trim(std::string_view text) {
  while (!text.empty() && (text.front() == ' ' || text.front() == '\t')) text.remove_prefix(1);
  while (!text.empty() && (text.back() == ' ' || text.back() == '\t')) text.remove_suffix(1);
  return text;
}

std::optional<int> parse_status_line(const std::string_view line) {
  std::string_view rest;
  for (const auto* prefix : {"HTTP/1.0 ", "HTTP/1.1 ", "HTTP/2 "}) {
    if (line.starts_with(prefix)) {
      rest = line.substr(std::string_view{prefix}.size());
      break;
    }
  }
  if (rest.size() < 3 || !is_digit(rest[0]) || !is_digit(rest[1]) || !is_digit(rest[2])) return std::nullopt;
  if (rest.size() > 3 && rest[3] != ' ') return std::nullopt;
  return (rest[0] - '0') * 100 + (rest[1] - '0') * 10 + (rest[2] - '0');
}

// block ends with "\r\n\r\n".
std::optional<ResponseHead> parse_block(const std::string_view block) {
  ResponseHead head;
  std::size_t start = 0;
  std::size_t lines = 0;
  const std::size_t end = block.size() - 2;  // drop the final empty line's CRLF
  while (start < end) {
    const auto stop = block.find("\r\n", start);
    if (stop == std::string_view::npos || stop >= end + 2) return std::nullopt;
    const auto line = block.substr(start, stop - start);
    if (++lines > kMaxHeaderLines) return std::nullopt;
    if (line.find('\n') != std::string_view::npos || line.find('\r') != std::string_view::npos) return std::nullopt;
    if (lines == 1) {
      const auto status = parse_status_line(line);
      if (!status) return std::nullopt;
      head.status = *status;
    } else {
      const auto colon = line.find(':');
      if (colon == std::string_view::npos || colon == 0) return std::nullopt;
      const auto name = line.substr(0, colon);
      if (!std::all_of(name.begin(), name.end(), is_tchar)) return std::nullopt;
      const auto value = trim(line.substr(colon + 1));
      if (std::any_of(value.begin(), value.end(), [](const char c) {
            return (static_cast<unsigned char>(c) < 0x20 && c != '\t') || c == 0x7F;
          })) {
        return std::nullopt;
      }
      head.headers.emplace_back(lower(name), std::string{value});
    }
    start = stop + 2;
  }
  return lines == 0 ? std::nullopt : std::optional<ResponseHead>{std::move(head)};
}

std::optional<std::uint64_t> parse_decimal(const std::string_view text) {
  if (text.empty() || text.size() > 19 || !std::all_of(text.begin(), text.end(), is_digit)) return std::nullopt;
  std::uint64_t value = 0;
  for (const char c : text) value = value * 10U + static_cast<std::uint64_t>(c - '0');
  return value;
}

HeadDecision fail(const AcquireReason reason, const int status, const bool transient = false) {
  return HeadDecision{HeadAction::fail, reason, transient, {}, status};
}

}  // namespace

std::vector<std::string_view> header_values(const ResponseHead& head, const std::string_view lower_name) {
  std::vector<std::string_view> values;
  for (const auto& [name, value] : head.headers) {
    if (name == lower_name) values.emplace_back(value);
  }
  return values;
}

ResponseHeadParser::Feed ResponseHeadParser::feed(const std::string_view bytes) {
  if (state_ != State::need_more) return {state_, 0};
  std::size_t used = 0;
  while (used < bytes.size()) {
    block_.push_back(bytes[used++]);
    if (block_.size() > kMaxHeaderBlockBytes) {
      state_ = State::malformed;
      return {state_, used};
    }
    if (block_.size() < 4 || block_.compare(block_.size() - 4, 4, "\r\n\r\n") != 0) continue;
    auto parsed = parse_block(block_);
    block_.clear();
    if (!parsed) {
      state_ = State::malformed;
      return {state_, used};
    }
    if (parsed->status >= 100 && parsed->status < 200) {
      if (++informational_ > kMaxInformationalBlocks) {
        state_ = State::malformed;
        return {state_, used};
      }
      continue;
    }
    head_ = std::move(*parsed);
    state_ = State::head_ready;
    return {state_, used};
  }
  return {state_, used};
}

HeadDecision classify_head(const ResponseHead& head, const HopExpectation& expectation) {
  const int status = head.status;
  const auto lengths = header_values(head, "content-length");
  if (lengths.size() > 1) return fail(AcquireReason::response_malformed, status);
  std::optional<std::uint64_t> length;
  if (lengths.size() == 1) {
    length = parse_decimal(lengths.front());
    if (!length) return fail(AcquireReason::response_malformed, status);
  }
  if (status == 200) {
    if (length && *length != expectation.file_size) return fail(AcquireReason::size_mismatch_announced, status);
    return HeadDecision{expectation.range_from ? HeadAction::restart_from_zero : HeadAction::write_body,
                        AcquireReason::response_malformed, false, {}, status};
  }
  if (status == 206) {
    if (!expectation.range_from) return fail(AcquireReason::range_mismatch, status);
    const auto ranges = header_values(head, "content-range");
    const std::string expected = "bytes " + std::to_string(*expectation.range_from) + "-" +
                                 std::to_string(expectation.file_size - 1) + "/" + std::to_string(expectation.file_size);
    if (ranges.size() != 1 || ranges.front() != expected) return fail(AcquireReason::range_mismatch, status);
    if (length && *length != expectation.file_size - *expectation.range_from) {
      return fail(AcquireReason::size_mismatch_announced, status);
    }
    return HeadDecision{HeadAction::write_body, AcquireReason::response_malformed, false, {}, status};
  }
  if (status == 301 || status == 302 || status == 303 || status == 307 || status == 308) {
    const auto locations = header_values(head, "location");
    if (locations.size() != 1) return fail(AcquireReason::response_malformed, status);
    return HeadDecision{HeadAction::redirect, AcquireReason::redirect_not_allowed, false, std::string{locations.front()}, status};
  }
  if (status == 416) return fail(AcquireReason::range_mismatch, status);
  if (status == 429 || (status >= 500 && status <= 599)) return fail(AcquireReason::http_status, status, true);
  return fail(AcquireReason::http_status, status);
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`parse_block` scans lines only up to the final blank line: `end = block.size() - 2`, so the final `"\r\n"` (the empty line) is not parsed as a header. A block of only `"\r\n\r\n"` yields zero lines → malformed. A bare-LF block never ends with `"\r\n\r\n"`, so it hits the 16 KiB limit or stays `need_more` — either way never `head_ready` (the test asserts exactly that).

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/fetch_schedule.hpp"

#include <chrono>

// in the anonymous namespace:
using namespace std::chrono_literals;
using Kind = secure_frontend::SourceSchedule::Step::Kind;

void retry_backoff_follows_schedule() {
  secure_frontend::SourceSchedule schedule(2);
  const std::vector<std::chrono::seconds> waits{2s, 4s, 8s, 16s};
  for (const auto wait : waits) {
    const auto step = schedule.record_failure(secure_frontend::AcquireReason::connect_failed, true);
    expect(step.kind == Kind::retry_same_source && step.wait == wait && step.source == 0,
           "retry after " + std::to_string(wait.count()) + " s");
  }
  expect(schedule.attempt() == 5, "fifth attempt on source 0");
  const auto move = schedule.record_failure(secure_frontend::AcquireReason::connect_failed, true);
  expect(move.kind == Kind::next_source && move.wait == 32s && move.source == 1, "after 5 attempts: 32 s, next source");
  expect(schedule.attempt() == 1, "attempts restart on the new source");
}

void non_transient_moves_to_next_source() {
  secure_frontend::SourceSchedule schedule(3);
  const auto step = schedule.record_failure(secure_frontend::AcquireReason::tls_untrusted, false);
  expect(step.kind == Kind::next_source && step.wait == 0s && step.source == 1, "tls_untrusted is not retried");
  expect(secure_frontend::is_transient(secure_frontend::AcquireReason::stalled), "stalled retried");
  expect(secure_frontend::is_transient(secure_frontend::AcquireReason::range_mismatch), "range_mismatch restarts on same source");
  expect(!secure_frontend::is_transient(secure_frontend::AcquireReason::transport_failed), "unknown curl exit: next source");
  expect(!secure_frontend::is_transient(secure_frontend::AcquireReason::http_status), "http_status needs the head's transient flag");
}

void exhausted_class_follows_last_source_reason() {
  secure_frontend::SourceSchedule schedule(2);
  (void)schedule.record_failure(secure_frontend::AcquireReason::tls_untrusted, false);
  const auto last = schedule.record_failure(secure_frontend::AcquireReason::digest_mismatch, false);
  expect(last.kind == Kind::exhausted && last.wait == 0s, "no more sources");
  expect(schedule.last_reason() == secure_frontend::AcquireReason::digest_mismatch, "last reason kept");
  expect(secure_frontend::exit_class(*schedule.last_reason()) == secure_frontend::ExitCode::verification,
         "class follows the last source: 4");
}

void run_budget_remaining_seconds() {
  const auto start = std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point{} + 1000s;
  const secure_frontend::RunBudget budget(start, 60s);
  expect(budget.remaining_seconds(start) == 60U, "full budget");
  expect(budget.remaining_seconds(start + 59500ms) == 1U, "rounded up to whole seconds");
  expect(!budget.remaining_seconds(start + 60s).has_value(), "used up at the deadline");
}
```

```cpp
#pragma once

#include "secure_frontend/acquire_reason.hpp"

#include <array>
#include <chrono>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>

namespace secure_frontend {

inline constexpr std::array<std::chrono::seconds, 5> kRetryBackoff{
    std::chrono::seconds{2}, std::chrono::seconds{4}, std::chrono::seconds{8}, std::chrono::seconds{16},
    std::chrono::seconds{32}};
inline constexpr std::uint32_t kAttemptsPerSource = 5;

// Retried on the same source (§8.2 "retried" or "file restarts from 0").
[[nodiscard]] bool is_transient(AcquireReason reason) noexcept;

// Spec §3.6: up to 5 attempts per source (2/4/8/16 s between them), 32 s
// before moving on after the fifth transient failure; a non-transient reason
// moves on at once. The run's class is the last source's reason.
class SourceSchedule final {
 public:
  explicit SourceSchedule(std::size_t source_count) noexcept : count_(source_count) {}
  struct Step final {
    enum class Kind { retry_same_source, next_source, exhausted } kind;
    std::chrono::seconds wait;
    std::size_t source;
  };
  [[nodiscard]] Step record_failure(AcquireReason reason, bool transient) noexcept;
  [[nodiscard]] std::size_t source() const noexcept { return source_; }
  [[nodiscard]] std::uint32_t attempt() const noexcept { return attempt_; }
  [[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireReason> last_reason() const noexcept { return last_; }

 private:
  std::size_t count_;
  std::size_t source_{0};
  std::uint32_t attempt_{1};
  std::optional<AcquireReason> last_;
};

// pull.max_duration_s for the whole run, passed to curl as the remaining --max-time.
class RunBudget final {
 public:
  RunBudget(std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point start, std::chrono::seconds total) noexcept
      : deadline_(start + total) {}
  [[nodiscard]] std::optional<std::uint64_t> remaining_seconds(std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point now) const noexcept;

 private:
  std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point deadline_;
};

// Clock seam (§10.1): tests advance time and record sleeps.
class AcquireClock {
 public:
  virtual ~AcquireClock() = default;
  [[nodiscard]] virtual std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point now() = 0;
  virtual void sleep_for(std::chrono::seconds duration) = 0;
};

class SystemClock final : public AcquireClock {
 public:
  [[nodiscard]] std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point now() override;
  void sleep_for(std::chrono::seconds duration) override;
};

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/fetch_schedule.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/fetch_schedule.hpp"

#include <thread>

namespace secure_frontend {

bool is_transient(const AcquireReason reason) noexcept {
  const auto same = reason_info(reason).same_source;
  return same == SameSource::retried || same == SameSource::restart_file;
}

SourceSchedule::Step SourceSchedule::record_failure(const AcquireReason reason, const bool transient) noexcept {
  last_ = reason;
  if (transient && attempt_ < kAttemptsPerSource) {
    const auto wait = kRetryBackoff[attempt_ - 1U];
    ++attempt_;
    return Step{Step::Kind::retry_same_source, wait, source_};
  }
  if (source_ + 1U >= count_) return Step{Step::Kind::exhausted, std::chrono::seconds{0}, source_};
  ++source_;
  attempt_ = 1;
  return Step{Step::Kind::next_source, transient ? kRetryBackoff[4] : std::chrono::seconds{0}, source_};
}

std::optional<std::uint64_t> RunBudget::remaining_seconds(const std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point now) const noexcept {
  if (now >= deadline_) return std::nullopt;
  const auto left = std::chrono::ceil<std::chrono::seconds>(deadline_ - now).count();
  return static_cast<std::uint64_t>(left < 1 ? 1 : left);
}

std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point SystemClock::now() { return std::chrono::steady_clock::now(); }
void SystemClock::sleep_for(const std::chrono::seconds duration) { std::this_thread::sleep_for(duration); }

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

```cpp
}

secure_frontend::ManifestRules rules() { return secure_frontend::ManifestRules{}; }

void expect_issue(const AcquireManifest& m, const std::string& key, const std::string& why,
                  const AcquireReason reason = AcquireReason::manifest_invalid) {
  const auto issue = secure_frontend::validate_manifest(m, rules());
  expect(issue.has_value() && issue->reason == reason && issue->key_path == key,
         why + " (got " + (issue ? issue->key_path : std::string{"no issue"}) + ")");
}

void valid_qwen_manifest_accepted() {
  const auto issue = secure_frontend::validate_manifest(valid_qwen_manifest(), rules());
  expect(!issue.has_value(), "a complete qwen manifest is valid: " + (issue ? issue->key_path : std::string{}));
}

      {"launch.native", "native must be shard 00001", [](auto& m) { m.launch.native = "weights/m-00002-of-00002.gguf"; }},
      {"launch.pack", "pack must be a directory prefix", [](auto& m) { m.launch.pack = "pack/index.txt"; }},
      {"launch.tokenizer_pack", "tokenizer_pack must be <pack>/tokenizer", [](auto& m) { m.launch.tokenizer_pack = "pack"; }},
      {"launch.pack", "qwen requires pack", [](auto& m) { m.launch.pack.reset(); }},
  };
  for (const auto& mutation : mutations) {
    auto m = valid_qwen_manifest();
    mutation.change(m);
    expect_issue(m, mutation.key, mutation.why);
  }
}

void reserved_manifest_names_rejected() {
  for (const auto* reserved : {"manifest.json", "MANIFEST", "Strata-Bundle", "SHA256SUMS", "missing", "manifest.json/x"}) {
    auto m = valid_qwen_manifest();
    m.files.push_back(file(reserved, FileRole::pack));
    expect_issue(m, "files[10].path", std::string{"reserved top-level name: "} + reserved);
  }
  auto nested = valid_qwen_manifest();
  nested.files.push_back(file("pack/MISSING", FileRole::pack));
  expect(!secure_frontend::validate_manifest(nested, rules()).has_value(), "reserved names only at the top level");
}

void model_name_index_json_rejected() {
  auto m = valid_qwen_manifest();
  m.name = "index.json";
  expect_issue(m, "name", "index.json is a file in roots.models");
  expect(!secure_frontend::parse_model_reference("INDEX.JSON:x").has_value(), "references are case-folded first");
}

void part_suffix_path_rejected() {
  for (const auto* path : {"pack/dense.bin.part", "pack/X.PART", "pack.part/index.txt"}) {
    auto m = valid_qwen_manifest();
    m.files.push_back(file(path, FileRole::pack));
    expect_issue(m, "files[10].path", std::string{".part is the in-progress suffix: "} + path);
  }
}

void file_and_directory_prefix_collision_rejected() {
  auto file_is_dir = valid_qwen_manifest();
  file_is_dir.files.push_back(file("pack/tokenizer", FileRole::pack));
  expect_issue(file_is_dir, "files[10].path", "a file path that is another file's directory");
  auto folded = valid_qwen_manifest();
  folded.files.push_back(file("Pack/Extra.bin", FileRole::pack));
  folded.files.push_back(file("PACK", FileRole::pack));
  expect_issue(folded, "files[11].path", "case-folded file/directory collision");
}

void shard_set_must_be_complete_in_one_directory() {
  auto split = valid_qwen_manifest();
  split.files[1].path = "other/m-00002-of-00002.gguf";
  split.launch.ple_gguf = "other/m-00002-of-00002.gguf";
  expect_issue(split, "files", "shards must share one directory");
  auto three = valid_qwen_manifest();
  three.files[1].path = "weights/m-00003-of-00002.gguf";
  three.launch.ple_gguf = "weights/m-00003-of-00002.gguf";
  expect_issue(three, "files", "shard number above the count");
}

void launch_keys_follow_profile() {
  auto glm = valid_qwen_manifest();
  glm.profile = ManifestProfile::glm53;
  glm.family = secure_frontend::EngineFamily::glm;
  expect_issue(glm, "launch.ple_gguf", "glm53 has only launch.native in schema 1");
  glm.launch.pack.reset();
  glm.launch.tokenizer_pack.reset();
  glm.launch.ple_gguf.reset();
  expect(!secure_frontend::validate_manifest(glm, rules()).has_value(), "glm53 with native only is valid");
}

void wildcard_redirect_hosts_validated() {
  auto m = valid_qwen_manifest();
  m.redirect_hosts = {"*.aws.cdn.hf.co", "cas-bridge.xethub.hf.co"};
  expect(!secure_frontend::validate_manifest(m, rules()).has_value(), "valid redirect hosts");
  m.redirect_hosts.assign(17, "a.example");
  expect_issue(m, "redirect_hosts", "17 redirect hosts");
}

void total_size_above_budget_is_total_budget_exceeded() {
  auto m = valid_qwen_manifest();
  secure_frontend::ManifestRules small;
  small.max_total_bytes = 50;
  const auto issue = secure_frontend::validate_manifest(m, small);
  expect(issue && issue->reason == AcquireReason::total_budget_exceeded && issue->key_path == "files",
         "total of 100 bytes exceeds a 50-byte budget");
}

AcquireManifest raw_manifest() {
  AcquireManifest m = valid_qwen_manifest();
  m.profile = ManifestProfile::raw;
  m.family = secure_frontend::EngineFamily::none;
  m.min_version = *secure_frontend::parse_engine_version("0.0.0");
  m.files = {file("vocab.gguf", FileRole::weights)};
  m.launch = {};
  return m;
}

void raw_profile_rejected_in_release_build() {
  expect(!secure_frontend::kTestHooksEnabled, "this case runs only in the hooks-OFF configuration");
  expect_issue(raw_manifest(), "profile", "raw is rejected as manifest_invalid in release builds");
}

#if defined(SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS)
void raw_profile_accepted_with_test_hooks() {
  expect(!secure_frontend::validate_manifest(raw_manifest(), rules()).has_value(), "raw accepted in hooks builds");
  auto launched = raw_manifest();
  launched.launch.native = "vocab.gguf";
  expect_issue(launched, "launch.native", "raw has no launch keys");
}
#endif

void engine_version_parsed_with_fourth_component() {
  const auto four = secure_frontend::parse_engine_version("0.1.44.1");
  const auto three = secure_frontend::parse_engine_version("0.1.44");
  expect(four.has_value() && four->count == 4 && four->parts[3] == 1, "four components");
  expect(three && four && secure_frontend::compare_versions(*four, *three) > 0, "0.1.44.1 > 0.1.44");
  expect(three && secure_frontend::compare_versions(*three, *secure_frontend::parse_engine_version("0.1.44.0")) == 0,
         "a missing fourth part is 0");
  expect(four && secure_frontend::render_engine_version(*four) == "0.1.44.1", "render");
  for (const auto* bad : {"0.1", "0.1.44.1.2", "v0.1.44", "0.01.44", "0.1.10000", "0.1.-1", "0.1.44 "}) {
    expect(!secure_frontend::parse_engine_version(bad).has_value(), std::string{"rejected: "} + bad);
  }
}
```

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <array>
#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>

namespace secure_frontend {

// MAJOR.MINOR.PATCH with an optional fourth .N; each component 0..9999.
struct EngineVersion final {
  std::array<std::uint32_t, 4> parts{};
  std::uint8_t count{3};
};

[[nodiscard]] std::optional<EngineVersion> parse_engine_version(std::string_view text) noexcept;
// <0, 0, >0; a missing fourth component compares as 0.
[[nodiscard]] int compare_versions(const EngineVersion& a, const EngineVersion& b) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] std::string render_engine_version(const EngineVersion& version);

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/engine_version.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/engine_version.hpp"

namespace secure_frontend {

std::optional<EngineVersion> parse_engine_version(const std::string_view text) noexcept {
  EngineVersion version;
  std::size_t index = 0;
  std::size_t position = 0;
  while (true) {
    if (index == 4) return std::nullopt;
    std::size_t digits = 0;
    std::uint32_t value = 0;
    while (position < text.size() && text[position] >= '0' && text[position] <= '9') {
      if (++digits > 4) return std::nullopt;
      value = value * 10U + static_cast<std::uint32_t>(text[position] - '0');
      ++position;
    }
    if (digits == 0 || (digits > 1 && text[position - digits] == '0')) return std::nullopt;
    version.parts[index++] = value;
    if (position == text.size()) break;
    if (text[position] != '.') return std::nullopt;
    ++position;
  }
  if (index < 3) return std::nullopt;
  version.count = static_cast<std::uint8_t>(index);
  return version;
}

int compare_versions(const EngineVersion& a, const EngineVersion& b) noexcept {
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < 4; ++i) {
    if (a.parts[i] != b.parts[i]) return a.parts[i] < b.parts[i] ? -1 : 1;
  }
  return 0;
}

std::string render_engine_version(const EngineVersion& version) {
  std::string text;
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < version.count; ++i) {
    if (i != 0) text.push_back('.');
    text += std::to_string(version.parts[i]);
  }
  return text;
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`include/secure_frontend/acquire_manifest.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once

#include "secure_frontend/acquire_reason.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/engine_version.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/test_hooks.hpp"

#include <array>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>

namespace secure_frontend {

enum class ManifestProfile { qwen, glm53, raw };  // raw: SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS builds only
enum class EngineFamily { strata, glm, none };
enum class FileRole { weights, pack, tokenizer };

struct ManifestLicense final {
  std::string id;
  std::string url;
  bool bundle_allowed{false};
  std::string notice;
};

struct ManifestFile final {
  std::string path;  // '/'-separated, relative to the version directory
  FileRole role{FileRole::weights};
  std::uint64_t size{0};
  std::string sha256;
  std::vector<std::string> sources;  // empty: arrives only by import (§2.7)
  std::optional<ManifestLicense> license;
};

struct ManifestLaunch final {
  std::optional<std::string> native;
  std::optional<std::string> ple_gguf;
  std::optional<std::string> pack;
  std::optional<std::string> tokenizer_pack;
};

// Schema version 1 (spec §2.2). Identity is the SHA-256 of the exact bytes (Task 14).
struct AcquireManifest final {
  std::string name;
  std::string tag;
  std::string version;
  std::uint32_t sequence{0};
  ManifestProfile profile{ManifestProfile::qwen};
  EngineFamily family{EngineFamily::strata};
  EngineVersion min_version;
  ManifestLicense license;
  std::vector<std::string> hosts;
  std::vector<std::string> redirect_hosts;
  std::vector<ManifestFile> files;
  ManifestLaunch launch;
};

inline constexpr std::uint64_t kDefaultMaxTotalBytes = std::uint64_t{1} << 40U;  // pull.max_total_bytes default
inline constexpr std::size_t kManifestMaxFiles = 4096;
inline constexpr std::size_t kManifestMaxSources = 8;
inline constexpr std::size_t kManifestMaxHosts = 16;
inline constexpr std::size_t kManifestMaxStringBytes = 1024;
inline constexpr std::size_t kManifestMaxNoticeBytes = 512;
inline constexpr std::uint64_t kManifestMaxFileBytes = std::uint64_t{1} << 40U;
inline constexpr std::string_view kPartSuffix = ".part";
inline constexpr std::array<std::string_view, 5> kReservedTopLevelNames{"manifest.json", "manifest", "strata-bundle",
                                                                        "sha256sums", "missing"};

struct ManifestRules final {
  bool allow_raw_profile{kTestHooksEnabled};
  std::uint64_t max_total_bytes{kDefaultMaxTotalBytes};
};

// key_path names the offending key ("files[3].sources[0]"); message is fixed text.
struct ManifestIssue final {
  AcquireReason reason;
  std::string key_path;
  std::string_view message;
};

// Every struct-level rule of spec §2.1-§2.4. Returns the first issue.
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<ManifestIssue> validate_manifest(const AcquireManifest& manifest, const ManifestRules& rules);

[[nodiscard]] std::string ascii_lower(std::string_view text);
// [a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]{0,127}, not a Windows device name (with any extension),
// no trailing '.', not "index.json". Callers lowercase first.
[[nodiscard]] bool is_valid_model_name(std::string_view name) noexcept;

struct ModelReference final {
  std::string name;
  std::optional<std::string> tag;
};
// "name" or "name:tag", ASCII-lowercased, both parts valid model names.
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<ModelReference> parse_model_reference(std::string_view text);
[[nodiscard]] std::string model_ref_text(std::string_view name, std::string_view tag);

[[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t total_bytes(const AcquireManifest& manifest) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] const ManifestLicense& effective_license(const AcquireManifest& manifest, std::size_t file_index) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] bool bundle_allowed(const AcquireManifest& manifest) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] bool has_sourceless_files(const AcquireManifest& manifest) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] std::string_view profile_name(ManifestProfile profile) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] std::string_view family_name(EngineFamily family) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] std::string_view role_name(FileRole role) noexcept;

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/acquire_manifest.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/acquire_manifest.hpp"

#include "secure_frontend/acquire_url.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/sha256.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <map>
#include <set>
#include <span>

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {

bool printable(const std::string_view text) noexcept {
  return std::all_of(text.begin(), text.end(), [](const char c) { return c >= 0x20 && c <= 0x7E; });
}

bool is_device_name(const std::string_view lowered) noexcept {
  const auto stem = lowered.substr(0, lowered.find('.'));
  if (stem == "con" || stem == "prn" || stem == "aux" || stem == "nul") return true;
  return stem.size() == 4 && (stem.starts_with("com") || stem.starts_with("lpt")) && stem[3] >= '0' && stem[3] <= '9';
}

bool is_version_label(const std::string_view text) noexcept {
  // YYYY.MM.DD.N, N 1..999
  if (text.size() < 12 || text.size() > 14 || text[4] != '.' || text[7] != '.' || text[10] != '.') return false;
  const auto digits = [&](const std::size_t from, const std::size_t count) {
    return std::all_of(text.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(from),
                       text.begin() + static_cast<std::ptrdiff_t>(from + count),
                       [](const char c) { return c >= '0' && c <= '9'; });
  };
  if (!digits(0, 4) || !digits(5, 2) || !digits(8, 2) || !digits(11, text.size() - 11)) return false;
  const int month = (text[5] - '0') * 10 + (text[6] - '0');
  const int day = (text[8] - '0') * 10 + (text[9] - '0');
  if (month < 1 || month > 12 || day < 1 || day > 31) return false;
  if (text[11] == '0') return false;
  return true;  // 1..3 digits without a leading zero: 1..999
}

bool is_license_id(const std::string_view id) noexcept {
  const auto body = id.starts_with("LicenseRef-") ? id.substr(11) : id;
  if (body.empty() || id.size() > 128) return false;
  const bool ref = body.size() != id.size();
  return std::all_of(body.begin(), body.end(), [ref](const char c) {
    return (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') || (c >= '0' && c <= '9') || c == '.' || c == '-' ||
           (!ref && c == '+');
  });
}

std::optional<std::string_view> license_problem(const ManifestLicense& license, std::string& key, const std::string& prefix) {
  if (!is_license_id(license.id)) {
    key = prefix + ".id";
    return "must be an SPDX id or LicenseRef-<name>";
  }
  if (!license.url.starts_with("https://") || license.url.size() > kManifestMaxStringBytes || !printable(license.url)) {
    key = prefix + ".url";
    return "must be an https:// URL of printable ASCII";
  }
  if (license.notice.size() > kManifestMaxNoticeBytes || !printable(license.notice)) {
    key = prefix + ".notice";
    return "must be at most 512 bytes of printable ASCII";
  }
  return std::nullopt;
}

std::optional<std::string_view> path_problem(const std::string_view path) {
  if (path.empty() || path.size() > kManifestMaxStringBytes) return "must be 1-1024 bytes";
  if (!printable(path)) return "must be printable ASCII";
  if (path.find('\\') != std::string_view::npos || path.find(':') != std::string_view::npos) {
    return "must use '/' separators and no ':'";
  }
  std::size_t start = 0;
  bool first = true;
  while (true) {
    const auto slash = path.find('/', start);
    const auto component = path.substr(start, slash == std::string_view::npos ? std::string_view::npos : slash - start);
    if (component.empty() || component.front() == '.') return "components must be non-empty and not start with '.'";
    const auto lowered = ascii_lower(component);
    if (lowered.ends_with(kPartSuffix)) return "components must not end in .part";
    if (first && std::find(kReservedTopLevelNames.begin(), kReservedTopLevelNames.end(), lowered) !=
                     kReservedTopLevelNames.end()) {
      return "is a reserved top-level name";
    }
    first = false;
    if (slash == std::string_view::npos) return std::nullopt;
    start = slash + 1;
  }
}

// "<dir>/<prefix>-NNNNN-of-MMMMM.gguf" → (set key, N, M).
struct ShardName final {
  std::string key;
  unsigned number;
  unsigned count;
};
std::optional<ShardName> shard_name(const std::string_view path) {
  const auto slash = path.rfind('/');
  const auto name = path.substr(slash == std::string_view::npos ? 0 : slash + 1);
  constexpr std::size_t kSuffix = 20;  // "-00001-of-00002.gguf"
  if (name.size() <= kSuffix || !name.ends_with(".gguf")) return std::nullopt;
  const auto tail = name.substr(name.size() - kSuffix);
  const auto is_d = [](const char c) { return c >= '0' && c <= '9'; };
  if (tail[0] != '-' || tail.substr(6, 4) != "-of-") return std::nullopt;
  for (const std::size_t i : {1U, 2U, 3U, 4U, 5U, 10U, 11U, 12U, 13U, 14U}) {
    if (!is_d(tail[i])) return std::nullopt;
  }
  const auto number = static_cast<unsigned>(std::stoul(std::string{tail.substr(1, 5)}));
  const auto count = static_cast<unsigned>(std::stoul(std::string{tail.substr(11, 5)}));
  const auto dir = slash == std::string_view::npos ? std::string_view{} : path.substr(0, slash);
  return ShardName{std::string{dir} + "/" + std::string{name.substr(0, name.size() - kSuffix)} + "/" + std::to_string(count),
                   number, count};
}

const ManifestFile* find_file(const AcquireManifest& m, const std::string_view path) {
  for (const auto& f : m.files) {
    if (f.path == path) return &f;
  }
  return nullptr;
}

bool is_directory_prefix(const AcquireManifest& m, const std::string_view dir) {
  if (dir.empty() || find_file(m, dir) != nullptr) return false;
  const std::string prefix = std::string{dir} + "/";
  return std::any_of(m.files.begin(), m.files.end(), [&](const ManifestFile& f) { return f.path.starts_with(prefix); });
}

}  // namespace

std::string ascii_lower(const std::string_view text) {
  std::string out{text};
  for (char& c : out) {
    if (c >= 'A' && c <= 'Z') c = static_cast<char>(c - 'A' + 'a');
  }
  return out;
}

bool is_valid_model_name(const std::string_view name) noexcept {
  if (name.empty() || name.size() > 128) return false;
  const auto ok_first = [](const char c) { return (c >= 'a' && c <= 'z') || (c >= '0' && c <= '9'); };
  if (!ok_first(name.front()) || name.back() == '.') return false;
  if (!std::all_of(name.begin(), name.end(), [&](const char c) { return ok_first(c) || c == '.' || c == '_' || c == '-'; })) {
    return false;
  }
  return !is_device_name(name) && name != "index.json";
}

std::optional<ModelReference> parse_model_reference(const std::string_view text) {
  const auto lowered = ascii_lower(text);
  const auto colon = lowered.find(':');
  ModelReference reference{lowered.substr(0, colon), std::nullopt};
  if (colon != std::string::npos) reference.tag = lowered.substr(colon + 1);
  if (!is_valid_model_name(reference.name)) return std::nullopt;
  if (reference.tag && !is_valid_model_name(*reference.tag)) return std::nullopt;
  return reference;
}

std::string model_ref_text(const std::string_view name, const std::string_view tag) {
  return std::string{name} + ":" + std::string{tag};
}

std::uint64_t total_bytes(const AcquireManifest& manifest) noexcept {
  std::uint64_t total = 0;
  for (const auto& f : manifest.files) total += f.size;
  return total;
}

const ManifestLicense& effective_license(const AcquireManifest& manifest, const std::size_t file_index) noexcept {
  const auto& f = manifest.files[file_index];
  return f.license ? *f.license : manifest.license;
}

bool bundle_allowed(const AcquireManifest& manifest) noexcept {
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < manifest.files.size(); ++i) {
    if (!effective_license(manifest, i).bundle_allowed) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

bool has_sourceless_files(const AcquireManifest& manifest) noexcept {
  return std::any_of(manifest.files.begin(), manifest.files.end(), [](const ManifestFile& f) { return f.sources.empty(); });
}

std::string_view profile_name(const ManifestProfile profile) noexcept {
  switch (profile) {
    case ManifestProfile::qwen: return "qwen";
    case ManifestProfile::glm53: return "glm53";
    case ManifestProfile::raw: return "raw";
  }
  return "unknown";
}

std::string_view family_name(const EngineFamily family) noexcept {
  switch (family) {
    case EngineFamily::strata: return "strata";
    case EngineFamily::glm: return "glm";
    case EngineFamily::none: return "none";
  }
  return "unknown";
}

std::string_view role_name(const FileRole role) noexcept {
  switch (role) {
    case FileRole::weights: return "weights";
    case FileRole::pack: return "pack";
    case FileRole::tokenizer: return "tokenizer";
  }
  return "unknown";
}

std::optional<ManifestIssue> validate_manifest(const AcquireManifest& m, const ManifestRules& rules) {
  const auto bad = [](std::string key, const std::string_view why) {
    return ManifestIssue{AcquireReason::manifest_invalid, std::move(key), why};
  };
  if (!is_valid_model_name(m.name)) return bad("name", "must match [a-z0-9][a-z0-9._-]{0,127}, not a device name or index.json");
  if (!is_valid_model_name(m.tag) || m.tag == "default") return bad("tag", "must be a model-id; 'default' is reserved");
  if (!is_version_label(m.version)) return bad("version", "must be YYYY.MM.DD.N with N 1..999");
  if (m.sequence < 1 || m.sequence > 0x7FFFFFFFU) return bad("sequence", "must be 1..2147483647");
  if (m.profile == ManifestProfile::raw && !rules.allow_raw_profile) {
    return bad("profile", "raw is available only in SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS builds");
  }
  const EngineFamily expected_family = m.profile == ManifestProfile::qwen    ? EngineFamily::strata
                                       : m.profile == ManifestProfile::glm53 ? EngineFamily::glm
                                                                             : EngineFamily::none;
  if (m.family != expected_family) return bad("engine.family", "must be strata for qwen, glm for glm53, none for raw");
  if (m.profile == ManifestProfile::raw && compare_versions(m.min_version, EngineVersion{}) != 0) {
    return bad("engine.min_version", "must be 0.0.0 for raw");
  }
  std::string key;
  if (const auto why = license_problem(m.license, key, "license")) return bad(key, *why);
  if (m.hosts.empty() || m.hosts.size() > kManifestMaxHosts) return bad("hosts", "must list 1..16 hosts");
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < m.hosts.size(); ++i) {
    if (!is_valid_dns_name(m.hosts[i])) return bad("hosts[" + std::to_string(i) + "]", "must be a lowercase DNS name");
  }
  if (m.redirect_hosts.size() > kManifestMaxHosts) return bad("redirect_hosts", "must list at most 16 entries");
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < m.redirect_hosts.size(); ++i) {
    if (!is_valid_redirect_pattern(m.redirect_hosts[i])) {
      return bad("redirect_hosts[" + std::to_string(i) + "]", "must be a DNS name or *.<name with two labels>");
    }
  }
  if (m.files.empty() || m.files.size() > kManifestMaxFiles) return bad("files", "must list 1..4096 files");
  std::set<std::string> folded_files;
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < m.files.size(); ++i) {
    const auto& f = m.files[i];
    const std::string at = "files[" + std::to_string(i) + "]";
    if (const auto why = path_problem(f.path)) return bad(at + ".path", *why);
    if (!folded_files.insert(ascii_lower(f.path)).second) return bad(at + ".path", "must be unique after case folding");
    if (f.size < 1 || f.size > kManifestMaxFileBytes) return bad(at + ".size", "must be 1..2^40");
    if (!is_lower_hex_digest(f.sha256)) return bad(at + ".sha256", "must be 64 lowercase hex characters");
    if (f.sources.size() > kManifestMaxSources) return bad(at + ".sources", "must list at most 8 URLs");
    for (std::size_t s = 0; s < f.sources.size(); ++s) {
      const std::string source_key = at + ".sources[" + std::to_string(s) + "]";
      const auto parsed = parse_source_url(f.sources[s]);
      if (const auto* problem = std::get_if<UrlProblem>(&parsed)) return bad(source_key, describe_url_problem(*problem));
      if (std::find(m.hosts.begin(), m.hosts.end(), std::get<HttpsUrl>(parsed).host) == m.hosts.end()) {
        return bad(source_key, "host must be listed in hosts");
      }
    }
    if (f.license) {
      if (const auto why = license_problem(*f.license, key, at + ".license")) return bad(key, *why);
    }
  }
  // A file path must not equal (case-folded) a directory prefix of another file.
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < m.files.size(); ++i) {
    const std::string folded = ascii_lower(m.files[i].path) + "/";
    for (const auto& other : folded_files) {
      if (other.starts_with(folded)) return bad("files[" + std::to_string(i) + "].path", "is also a directory of another file");
    }
  }
  if (total_bytes(m) > rules.max_total_bytes) {
    return ManifestIssue{AcquireReason::total_budget_exceeded, "files", "total size exceeds pull.max_total_bytes"};
  }
  if (m.profile != ManifestProfile::raw) {
    std::map<std::string, std::set<unsigned>> sets;
    std::map<std::string, unsigned> counts;
    for (const auto& f : m.files) {
      if (f.role != FileRole::weights) continue;
      if (const auto shard = shard_name(f.path)) {
        sets[shard->key].insert(shard->number);
        counts[shard->key] = shard->count;
      }
    }
    for (const auto& [set_key, numbers] : sets) {
      const unsigned count = counts[set_key];
      if (count == 0 || numbers.size() != count || *numbers.begin() != 1 || *numbers.rbegin() != count) {
        return bad("files", "every shard set must be complete (00001..MMMMM) in one directory");
      }
    }
  }
  const auto& launch = m.launch;
  const auto weights_file = [&](const std::optional<std::string>& value) {
    const auto* f = value ? find_file(m, *value) : nullptr;
    return f != nullptr && f->role == FileRole::weights;
  };
  switch (m.profile) {
    case ManifestProfile::raw:
      if (launch.native) return bad("launch.native", "raw has no launch keys");
      if (launch.ple_gguf) return bad("launch.ple_gguf", "raw has no launch keys");
      if (launch.pack) return bad("launch.pack", "raw has no launch keys");
      if (launch.tokenizer_pack) return bad("launch.tokenizer_pack", "raw has no launch keys");
      break;
    case ManifestProfile::glm53:
      if (launch.ple_gguf) return bad("launch.ple_gguf", "glm53 has only launch.native in schema 1");
      if (launch.pack) return bad("launch.pack", "glm53 has only launch.native in schema 1");
      if (launch.tokenizer_pack) return bad("launch.tokenizer_pack", "glm53 has only launch.native in schema 1");
      [[fallthrough]];
    case ManifestProfile::qwen: {
      if (!weights_file(launch.native)) return bad("launch.native", "must name a listed weights file");
      if (const auto shard = shard_name(*launch.native); shard && shard->number != 1) {
        return bad("launch.native", "must be shard 00001");
      }
      if (m.profile == ManifestProfile::glm53) break;
      if (launch.ple_gguf && !weights_file(launch.ple_gguf)) return bad("launch.ple_gguf", "must name a listed weights file");
      if (!launch.pack || !is_directory_prefix(m, *launch.pack)) return bad("launch.pack", "must name a directory of listed files");
      if (!launch.tokenizer_pack || *launch.tokenizer_pack != *launch.pack + "/tokenizer" ||
          !is_directory_prefix(m, *launch.tokenizer_pack)) {
        return bad("launch.tokenizer_pack", "must be <pack>/tokenizer, a directory of listed files");
      }
      break;
    }
  }
  return std::nullopt;
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`secure_frontend_manifest_core` already links `secure_frontend_sha256` (for `is_lower_hex_digest`).

Checks the matrix relies on, in validation order: the case-fold collision (`files[1]` = `WEIGHTS/M-00001-OF-00002.GGUF`) is caught by the `folded_files` set before shard rules; `pack/tokenizer` as a file trips the directory-prefix rule at its own index (`files[10]`); `PACK` (index 11) collides with `Pack/Extra.bin` folded. The `launch.native` = shard 2 mutation keeps shard 2 a listed weights file, so the shard-number rule names `launch.native`.

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/model_index_model.hpp"

// in the anonymous namespace:
using secure_frontend::IndexEntry;
using secure_frontend::ModelIndex;
using secure_frontend::ModelOrigin;

IndexEntry entry(const std::string& name, const std::string& tag, const std::uint32_t sequence, const char digit) {
  const std::string digest(64, digit);
  return IndexEntry{name, tag, "2026.10.14." + std::to_string(sequence), sequence,
                    secure_frontend::version_directory_name(sequence, digest), digest, ModelOrigin::catalog};
}

void index_render_is_deterministic() {
  ModelIndex index;
  index = secure_frontend::with_installed(index, entry("zeta", "a", 1, 'b'));
  index = secure_frontend::with_installed(index, entry("alpha", "b", 2, 'c'));
  const std::string expected =
      "{\"format\":\"strata-model-index\",\"schema_version\":1,\"models\":["
      "{\"name\":\"alpha\",\"tag\":\"b\",\"version\":\"2026.10.14.2\",\"sequence\":2,\"dir\":\"v2-cccccccccccc\","
      "\"manifest_sha256\":\"" + std::string(64, 'c') + "\",\"origin\":\"catalog\"},"
      "{\"name\":\"zeta\",\"tag\":\"a\",\"version\":\"2026.10.14.1\",\"sequence\":1,\"dir\":\"v1-bbbbbbbbbbbb\","
      "\"manifest_sha256\":\"" + std::string(64, 'b') + "\",\"origin\":\"catalog\"}],\"retired\":[]}\n";
  expect(secure_frontend::render_model_index(index) == expected, "sorted, compact, one trailing newline");
}

void install_retires_previous_version() {
  ModelIndex index = secure_frontend::with_installed({}, entry("m", "t", 1, 'a'));
  index = secure_frontend::with_installed(index, entry("m", "t", 2, 'b'));
  expect(index.models.size() == 1 && index.models.front().sequence == 2, "one entry per name:tag");
  expect(index.retired.size() == 1 && index.retired.front().dir == "v1-aaaaaaaaaaaa", "old version retired");
  index = secure_frontend::without_retired(index, "m", "t", "v1-aaaaaaaaaaaa");
  expect(index.retired.empty(), "retired entry removed");
  index = secure_frontend::without_model(index, "m", "t");
  expect(index.models.empty() && secure_frontend::find_model(index, "m", "t") == nullptr, "model removed");
}

void reinstall_same_version_does_not_retire() {
  ModelIndex index = secure_frontend::with_installed({}, entry("m", "t", 1, 'a'));
  index = secure_frontend::with_installed(index, entry("m", "t", 1, 'a'));
  expect(index.models.size() == 1 && index.retired.empty(), "--verify re-publish of the same dir retires nothing");
}

// main list: index_render_is_deterministic, install_retires_previous_version, reinstall_same_version_does_not_retire
```

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/acquire_manifest.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/model_index_model.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/staging_plan.hpp"

// in the anonymous namespace:
void version_dir_name_unique_for_same_version_label() {
  const std::string first(64, 'a');
  std::string second = first;
  second[11] = 'b';
  expect(secure_frontend::version_directory_name(3, first) == "v3-aaaaaaaaaaaa", "v<sequence>-<12 hex>");
  expect(secure_frontend::version_directory_name(3, first) != secure_frontend::version_directory_name(3, second),
         "two manifests with the same version label never share a directory");
  expect(secure_frontend::staging_key(first) == std::string(32, 'a'), "staging key is 32 hex");
}

secure_frontend::AcquireManifest staging_manifest() {
  secure_frontend::AcquireManifest m;
  m.files = {secure_frontend::ManifestFile{"weights/a.gguf", secure_frontend::FileRole::weights, 100, std::string(64, 'a'), {}, {}},
             secure_frontend::ManifestFile{"pack/index.txt", secure_frontend::FileRole::pack, 10, std::string(64, 'b'), {}, {}},
             secure_frontend::ManifestFile{"pack/dense.bin", secure_frontend::FileRole::pack, 10, std::string(64, 'c'), {}, {}},
             secure_frontend::ManifestFile{"pack/extra.bin", secure_frontend::FileRole::pack, 10, std::string(64, 'd'), {}, {}}};
  return m;
}

void staging_plan_classifies_partials() {
  using secure_frontend::FileStep;
  const std::vector<secure_frontend::StagedEntry> staged{
      {"MANIFEST", 900, true},          {"weights/a.gguf.part", 40, true}, {"pack/index.txt.part", 10, true},
      {"pack/dense.bin.part", 11, true}, {"pack/extra.bin", 10, true},     {"pack/extra.bin.part", 3, true},
      {"stray.bin", 5, true},            {"weights/link", 0, false}};
  const auto plan = secure_frontend::plan_staging(staging_manifest(), staged);
  expect(plan.files.size() == 4, "one plan per manifest file");
  expect(plan.files[0].step == FileStep::rehash_then_resume && plan.files[0].existing_bytes == 40, "short .part resumes at L");
  expect(plan.files[1].step == FileStep::rehash_complete_part && plan.files[1].existing_bytes == 10, "full .part is hashed");
  expect(plan.files[2].step == FileStep::fetch_from_zero && plan.files[2].existing_bytes == 0, "oversize .part is deleted");
  expect(plan.files[3].step == FileStep::rehash_verified_candidate, "a suffix-less file is re-hashed before it is trusted");
  const std::vector<std::string> remove{"pack/dense.bin.part", "pack/extra.bin.part", "stray.bin", "weights/link"};
  expect(plan.remove == remove, "oversize part, shadowed part, strays and non-regular entries are removed");
  expect(plan.staged_bytes == 60, "40 + 10 + 10 staged bytes count toward the preflight");
}

void abandoned_keys_match_same_model_only() {
  const std::vector<secure_frontend::StagingKeyInfo> keys{
      {std::string(32, 'a'), "m", "t", 100}, {std::string(32, 'b'), "m", "t", 200}, {std::string(32, 'c'), "m", "u", 300}};
  const auto abandoned = secure_frontend::abandoned_keys(keys, std::string(32, 'a'), "m", "t");
  expect(abandoned.size() == 1 && abandoned.front().key == std::string(32, 'b'), "other manifests of the same name:tag only");
}
```

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>

namespace secure_frontend {

inline constexpr std::string_view kIndexFileName = "index.json";
inline constexpr std::string_view kLockFileName = ".lock";
inline constexpr std::string_view kStagingDirName = ".staging";
inline constexpr std::string_view kStagingCwdName = ".cwd";
inline constexpr std::string_view kStagingManifestName = "MANIFEST";
inline constexpr std::string_view kVersionManifestName = "manifest.json";
inline constexpr std::size_t kIndexMaxBytes = std::size_t{4} * 1024U * 1024U;

enum class ModelOrigin { catalog, third_party };
[[nodiscard]] std::string_view origin_name(ModelOrigin origin) noexcept;

struct IndexEntry final {
  std::string name;
  std::string tag;
  std::string version;
  std::uint32_t sequence{0};
  std::string dir;              // v<sequence>-<12 hex>, below <name>/<tag>/
  std::string manifest_sha256;  // of <dir>/manifest.json
  ModelOrigin origin{ModelOrigin::catalog};
};

struct RetiredEntry final {
  std::string name;
  std::string tag;
  std::string dir;
  std::string manifest_sha256;
};

// roots.models/index.json: the single source of truth for installed models (§4.5).
struct ModelIndex final {
  std::vector<IndexEntry> models;
  std::vector<RetiredEntry> retired;
};

// Compact strict JSON, models sorted by (name, tag), retired by (name, tag, dir), one trailing LF.
[[nodiscard]] std::string render_model_index(const ModelIndex& index);
[[nodiscard]] const IndexEntry* find_model(const ModelIndex& index, std::string_view name, std::string_view tag) noexcept;
// Names `entry`; a previous entry of the same name:tag in another dir moves to `retired`.
[[nodiscard]] ModelIndex with_installed(ModelIndex index, IndexEntry entry);
[[nodiscard]] ModelIndex without_model(ModelIndex index, std::string_view name, std::string_view tag);
[[nodiscard]] ModelIndex without_retired(ModelIndex index, std::string_view name, std::string_view tag, std::string_view dir);

// v<sequence>-<first 12 hex of the manifest digest> (§4.3); never derived from the human version.
[[nodiscard]] std::string version_directory_name(std::uint32_t sequence, std::string_view manifest_sha256);
// First 32 hex of the manifest digest (§3.7).
[[nodiscard]] std::string staging_key(std::string_view manifest_sha256);
// Exactly 32 lowercase hex: so ".cwd" and every other name is never a key.
[[nodiscard]] bool is_staging_key_name(std::string_view name) noexcept;

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/model_index_model.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/model_index_model.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <tuple>

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {

void quoted(std::string& out, const std::string_view text) {
  out.push_back('"');
  for (const char c : text) {
    if (c == '"' || c == '\\') out.push_back('\\');
    out.push_back(c);
  }
  out.push_back('"');
}

void member(std::string& out, const std::string_view key, const std::string_view value, const bool comma = true) {
  quoted(out, key);
  out.push_back(':');
  quoted(out, value);
  if (comma) out.push_back(',');
}

}  // namespace

std::string_view origin_name(const ModelOrigin origin) noexcept {
  return origin == ModelOrigin::catalog ? "catalog" : "third_party";
}

std::string render_model_index(const ModelIndex& index) {
  auto models = index.models;
  std::sort(models.begin(), models.end(), [](const IndexEntry& a, const IndexEntry& b) {
    return std::tie(a.name, a.tag) < std::tie(b.name, b.tag);
  });
  auto retired = index.retired;
  std::sort(retired.begin(), retired.end(), [](const RetiredEntry& a, const RetiredEntry& b) {
    return std::tie(a.name, a.tag, a.dir) < std::tie(b.name, b.tag, b.dir);
  });
  std::string out = "{\"format\":\"strata-model-index\",\"schema_version\":1,\"models\":[";
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < models.size(); ++i) {
    const auto& e = models[i];
    if (i != 0) out.push_back(',');
    out.push_back('{');
    member(out, "name", e.name);
    member(out, "tag", e.tag);
    member(out, "version", e.version);
    out += "\"sequence\":" + std::to_string(e.sequence) + ",";
    member(out, "dir", e.dir);
    member(out, "manifest_sha256", e.manifest_sha256);
    member(out, "origin", origin_name(e.origin), false);
    out.push_back('}');
  }
  out += "],\"retired\":[";
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < retired.size(); ++i) {
    const auto& e = retired[i];
    if (i != 0) out.push_back(',');
    out.push_back('{');
    member(out, "name", e.name);
    member(out, "tag", e.tag);
    member(out, "dir", e.dir);
    member(out, "manifest_sha256", e.manifest_sha256, false);
    out.push_back('}');
  }
  out += "]}\n";
  return out;
}

const IndexEntry* find_model(const ModelIndex& index, const std::string_view name, const std::string_view tag) noexcept {
  for (const auto& e : index.models) {
    if (e.name == name && e.tag == tag) return &e;
  }
  return nullptr;
}

ModelIndex with_installed(ModelIndex index, IndexEntry entry) {
  for (auto& e : index.models) {
    if (e.name != entry.name || e.tag != entry.tag) continue;
    if (e.dir != entry.dir) index.retired.push_back(RetiredEntry{e.name, e.tag, e.dir, e.manifest_sha256});
    e = std::move(entry);
    return index;
  }
  index.models.push_back(std::move(entry));
  return index;
}

ModelIndex without_model(ModelIndex index, const std::string_view name, const std::string_view tag) {
  std::erase_if(index.models, [&](const IndexEntry& e) { return e.name == name && e.tag == tag; });
  return index;
}

ModelIndex without_retired(ModelIndex index, const std::string_view name, const std::string_view tag,
                           const std::string_view dir) {
  std::erase_if(index.retired, [&](const RetiredEntry& e) { return e.name == name && e.tag == tag && e.dir == dir; });
  return index;
}

std::string version_directory_name(const std::uint32_t sequence, const std::string_view manifest_sha256) {
  return "v" + std::to_string(sequence) + "-" + std::string{manifest_sha256.substr(0, 12)};
}

std::string staging_key(const std::string_view manifest_sha256) { return std::string{manifest_sha256.substr(0, 32)}; }

bool is_staging_key_name(const std::string_view name) noexcept {
  return name.size() == 32 &&
         std::all_of(name.begin(), name.end(), [](const char c) { return (c >= '0' && c <= '9') || (c >= 'a' && c <= 'f'); });
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`include/secure_frontend/staging_plan.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once

#include "secure_frontend/acquire_manifest.hpp"

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <span>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>

namespace secure_frontend {

// One regular file (or other entry) found below .staging/<k>/, '/'-separated.
struct StagedEntry final {
  std::string path;
  std::uint64_t size{0};
  bool regular{true};
};

// Spec §3.7: a run boundary is a trust boundary; every staged byte is re-hashed.
enum class FileStep {
  fetch_from_zero,            // nothing usable on disk
  rehash_then_resume,         // <path>.part, L < size: hash L bytes, then --range L-
  rehash_complete_part,       // <path>.part, L == size: hash, then rename to <path>
  rehash_verified_candidate,  // <path> from an earlier run: hash before trusting
};

struct FilePlan final {
  std::size_t file_index;
  FileStep step;
  std::uint64_t existing_bytes;
};

struct StagingPlan final {
  std::vector<FilePlan> files;       // one per manifest file, manifest order
  std::vector<std::string> remove;   // entries to delete before transfer, sorted
  std::uint64_t staged_bytes{0};     // bytes kept (for the §4.6 preflight)
};

[[nodiscard]] StagingPlan plan_staging(const AcquireManifest& manifest, std::span<const StagedEntry> staged);

struct StagingKeyInfo final {
  std::string key;
  std::string name;
  std::string tag;
  std::uint64_t bytes{0};
};

// Other keys holding a manifest of the same name:tag (§3.7 "Abandoned partials").
[[nodiscard]] std::vector<StagingKeyInfo> abandoned_keys(std::span<const StagingKeyInfo> keys, std::string_view own_key,
                                                         std::string_view name, std::string_view tag);

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/staging_plan.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/staging_plan.hpp"

#include "secure_frontend/model_index_model.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <map>
#include <set>

namespace secure_frontend {

StagingPlan plan_staging(const AcquireManifest& manifest, const std::span<const StagedEntry> staged) {
  std::map<std::string, const StagedEntry*, std::less<>> by_path;
  for (const auto& entry : staged) by_path[entry.path] = &entry;
  std::set<std::string> keep{std::string{kStagingManifestName}};
  std::set<std::string> remove;
  StagingPlan plan;
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < manifest.files.size(); ++i) {
    const auto& file = manifest.files[i];
    const std::string part = file.path + std::string{kPartSuffix};
    const auto final_it = by_path.find(file.path);
    const auto part_it = by_path.find(part);
    const bool final_ok = final_it != by_path.end() && final_it->second->regular && final_it->second->size == file.size;
    if (final_it != by_path.end() && !final_ok) remove.insert(file.path);
    if (final_ok) {
      keep.insert(file.path);
      if (part_it != by_path.end()) remove.insert(part);
      plan.files.push_back({i, FileStep::rehash_verified_candidate, file.size});
      plan.staged_bytes += file.size;
      continue;
    }
    if (part_it == by_path.end()) {
      plan.files.push_back({i, FileStep::fetch_from_zero, 0});
      continue;
    }
    const auto& entry = *part_it->second;
    if (!entry.regular || entry.size > file.size) {
      remove.insert(part);
      plan.files.push_back({i, FileStep::fetch_from_zero, 0});
      continue;
    }
    keep.insert(part);
    plan.files.push_back({i, entry.size == file.size ? FileStep::rehash_complete_part : FileStep::rehash_then_resume, entry.size});
    plan.staged_bytes += entry.size;
  }
  for (const auto& entry : staged) {
    if (!keep.contains(entry.path) || !entry.regular) remove.insert(entry.path);
  }
  remove.erase(std::string{kStagingManifestName});
  plan.remove.assign(remove.begin(), remove.end());
  return plan;
}

std::vector<StagingKeyInfo> abandoned_keys(const std::span<const StagingKeyInfo> keys, const std::string_view own_key,
                                           const std::string_view name, const std::string_view tag) {
  std::vector<StagingKeyInfo> out;
  for (const auto& key : keys) {
    if (key.key != own_key && key.name == name && key.tag == tag) out.push_back(key);
  }
  return out;
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

A `.part` of length 0 is planned as `rehash_then_resume` with `existing_bytes == 0`; Task 21 sends no `--range` when the offset is 0, so that is a fresh fetch.

```cpp
void sha256sums_render_sorted_bytewise() {

void sha256sums_parse_is_strict() {

  expect(secure_frontend::parse_sha256sums(good).has_value(), "well-formed");

                                std::string(64, 'a') + "  files/x\n" + std::string(64, 'a') + "  files/x\n", std::string{}}) {
    expect(!secure_frontend::parse_sha256sums(bad).has_value(), "rejected sums text");
  }
}

void sums_agreement_rules() {
  const std::string manifest_digest(64, 'f');
  const auto lines = *secure_frontend::parse_sha256sums(secure_frontend::render_sha256sums(manifest_digest, sample()));
  expect(secure_frontend::sums_agree(lines, manifest_digest, sample()), "rendered sums agree");
  expect(!secure_frontend::sums_agree(lines, std::string(64, 'e'), sample()), "manifest digest differs");
  auto changed = sample();
  changed.files[0].sha256 = std::string(64, 'd');
  expect(!secure_frontend::sums_agree(lines, manifest_digest, changed), "a file digest differs");
  auto fewer = lines;
  fewer.pop_back();
  expect(!secure_frontend::sums_agree(fewer, manifest_digest, sample()), "a line is missing");
}

void missing_list_names_sourceless_files() {
  expect(secure_frontend::render_missing(sample()) == "Pack/Z.txt\npack/a.txt\n", "sorted sourceless paths");
  const auto parsed = secure_frontend::parse_missing("Pack/Z.txt\npack/a.txt\n");
  expect(parsed && parsed->size() == 2, "parse");
  expect(!secure_frontend::parse_missing("a\n\nb\n").has_value(), "empty line rejected");
}
```
