```cpp
#pragma once

#include "secure_frontend/acquire_manifest.hpp"

#include <optional>
#include <span>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>

namespace secure_frontend {

inline constexpr std::string_view kBundleMarkerName = "STRATA-BUNDLE";
inline constexpr std::string_view kBundleMarkerContent = "strata-bundle 1\n";
inline constexpr std::string_view kBundleManifestName = "manifest.json";
inline constexpr std::string_view kBundleSumsName = "SHA256SUMS";
inline constexpr std::string_view kBundleMissingName = "MISSING";
inline constexpr std::string_view kBundleFilesDir = "files";

struct SumsLine final {
  std::string sha256;
  std::string path;
};

// "<64 hex>  <relative path>\n", LF, sorted bytewise by path, covering
// manifest.json and every files/<path> (§5.1). For humans; never a trust source.
[[nodiscard]] std::string render_sha256sums(std::string_view manifest_sha256, const AcquireManifest& manifest);
// Strict inverse: two spaces, LF only, lowercase hex, strictly increasing paths, non-empty.
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<std::vector<SumsLine>> parse_sha256sums(std::string_view text);
// Same paths, same digests as the manifest (§5.1: else bundle_invalid).
[[nodiscard]] bool sums_agree(std::span<const SumsLine> lines, std::string_view manifest_sha256, const AcquireManifest& manifest);

// Paths of files without sources, sorted bytewise, one per line (§2.7).
[[nodiscard]] std::string render_missing(const AcquireManifest& manifest);
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<std::vector<std::string>> parse_missing(std::string_view text);

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/bundle_text.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/bundle_text.hpp"

#include "secure_frontend/sha256.hpp"

#include <algorithm>

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {

std::vector<SumsLine> expected_lines(const std::string_view manifest_sha256, const AcquireManifest& manifest) {
  std::vector<SumsLine> lines;
  lines.push_back({std::string{manifest_sha256}, std::string{kBundleManifestName}});
  for (const auto& f : manifest.files) lines.push_back({f.sha256, std::string{kBundleFilesDir} + "/" + f.path});
  std::sort(lines.begin(), lines.end(), [](const SumsLine& a, const SumsLine& b) { return a.path < b.path; });
  return lines;
}

std::optional<std::vector<std::string_view>> split_lines(const std::string_view text) {
  if (text.empty() || text.back() != '\n') return std::nullopt;
  std::vector<std::string_view> lines;
  std::size_t start = 0;
  while (start < text.size()) {
    const auto end = text.find('\n', start);
    const auto line = text.substr(start, end - start);
    if (line.empty() || line.find('\r') != std::string_view::npos) return std::nullopt;
    lines.push_back(line);
    start = end + 1;
  }
  return lines;
}

}  // namespace

std::string render_sha256sums(const std::string_view manifest_sha256, const AcquireManifest& manifest) {
  std::string text;
  for (const auto& line : expected_lines(manifest_sha256, manifest)) text += line.sha256 + "  " + line.path + "\n";
  return text;
}

std::optional<std::vector<SumsLine>> parse_sha256sums(const std::string_view text) {
  const auto lines = split_lines(text);
  if (!lines) return std::nullopt;
  std::vector<SumsLine> out;
  for (const auto line : *lines) {
    if (line.size() < 67 || line.substr(64, 2) != "  " || !is_lower_hex_digest(line.substr(0, 64))) return std::nullopt;
    SumsLine parsed{std::string{line.substr(0, 64)}, std::string{line.substr(66)}};
    if (!out.empty() && !(out.back().path < parsed.path)) return std::nullopt;
    out.push_back(std::move(parsed));
  }
  return out;
}

bool sums_agree(const std::span<const SumsLine> lines, const std::string_view manifest_sha256, const AcquireManifest& manifest) {
  const auto expected = expected_lines(manifest_sha256, manifest);
  if (expected.size() != lines.size()) return false;
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < expected.size(); ++i) {
    if (expected[i].path != lines[i].path || expected[i].sha256 != lines[i].sha256) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

std::string render_missing(const AcquireManifest& manifest) {
  std::vector<std::string> paths;
  for (const auto& f : manifest.files) {
    if (f.sources.empty()) paths.push_back(f.path);
  }
  std::sort(paths.begin(), paths.end());
  std::string text;
  for (const auto& p : paths) text += p + "\n";
  return text;
}

std::optional<std::vector<std::string>> parse_missing(const std::string_view text) {
  const auto lines = split_lines(text);
  if (!lines) return std::nullopt;
  return std::vector<std::string>(lines->begin(), lines->end());
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

```cpp
#pragma once

#include "secure_frontend/acquire_manifest.hpp"

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

namespace secure_frontend {

// Spec §6.1. Lines only use manifest data (paths, sizes, digests, hosts,
// license fields), never URLs.
struct ManifestDiff final {
  std::vector<std::string> lines;
  bool license_changed{false};          // needs an interactive y even with --yes
  std::uint64_t bytes_to_download{0};   // added or changed files only
  std::vector<std::size_t> reusable_files;  // candidate indexes with the same path and digest as an installed file
};

[[nodiscard]] ManifestDiff diff_manifests(const AcquireManifest& installed, const AcquireManifest& candidate);

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/manifest_diff.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/manifest_diff.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <map>
#include <set>

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {

std::string engine_text(const AcquireManifest& m) {
  return std::string{family_name(m.family)} + " >= " + render_engine_version(m.min_version);
}

bool same_license(const ManifestLicense& a, const ManifestLicense& b) {
  return a.id == b.id && a.url == b.url && a.bundle_allowed == b.bundle_allowed && a.notice == b.notice;
}

void list_delta(std::vector<std::string>& lines, const std::string& label, const std::vector<std::string>& before,
                const std::vector<std::string>& after) {
  const std::set<std::string> old_set(before.begin(), before.end());
  const std::set<std::string> new_set(after.begin(), after.end());
  std::string text;
  for (const auto& h : new_set) {
    if (!old_set.contains(h)) text += (text.empty() ? "" : ", ") + std::string{"+ "} + h;
  }
  for (const auto& h : old_set) {
    if (!new_set.contains(h)) text += (text.empty() ? "" : ", ") + std::string{"- "} + h;
  }
  if (!text.empty()) lines.push_back(label + ": " + text);
}

}  // namespace

ManifestDiff diff_manifests(const AcquireManifest& installed, const AcquireManifest& candidate) {
  ManifestDiff diff;
  auto& lines = diff.lines;
  if (installed.version != candidate.version || installed.sequence != candidate.sequence) {
    lines.push_back("version: " + installed.version + " (sequence " + std::to_string(installed.sequence) + ") -> " +
                    candidate.version + " (sequence " + std::to_string(candidate.sequence) + ")");
  }
  if (installed.profile != candidate.profile) {
    lines.push_back("profile: " + std::string{profile_name(installed.profile)} + " -> " +
                    std::string{profile_name(candidate.profile)});
  }
  if (engine_text(installed) != engine_text(candidate)) {
    lines.push_back("engine: " + engine_text(installed) + " -> " + engine_text(candidate));
  }
  const auto& a = installed.license;
  const auto& b = candidate.license;
  if (a.id != b.id) lines.push_back("license: " + a.id + " -> " + b.id);
  if (a.bundle_allowed != b.bundle_allowed) {
    lines.push_back(std::string{"bundle_allowed: "} + (a.bundle_allowed ? "true" : "false") + " -> " +
                    (b.bundle_allowed ? "true" : "false"));
  }
  if (a.notice != b.notice) lines.push_back("notice: changed");
  diff.license_changed = !same_license(a, b);
  list_delta(lines, "hosts", installed.hosts, candidate.hosts);
  list_delta(lines, "redirect_hosts", installed.redirect_hosts, candidate.redirect_hosts);
  std::map<std::string, std::size_t> old_files;
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < installed.files.size(); ++i) old_files[installed.files[i].path] = i;
  std::set<std::string> seen;
  std::vector<std::string> added;
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < candidate.files.size(); ++i) {
    const auto& f = candidate.files[i];
    seen.insert(f.path);
    const auto it = old_files.find(f.path);
    if (it == old_files.end()) {
      added.push_back("file added: " + f.path + " (" + std::to_string(f.size) + " bytes, sha256 " + f.sha256 + ")");
      diff.bytes_to_download += f.size;
    } else {
      const auto& old = installed.files[it->second];
      const bool old_license = old.license.has_value();
      const bool new_license = f.license.has_value();
      if (old_license != new_license || (old_license && !same_license(*old.license, *f.license))) diff.license_changed = true;
      if (old.sha256 == f.sha256 && old.size == f.size) {
        diff.reusable_files.push_back(i);
      } else {
        lines.push_back("file changed: " + f.path + " size " + std::to_string(old.size) + " -> " + std::to_string(f.size) +
                        ", sha256 " + old.sha256 + " -> " + f.sha256);
        diff.bytes_to_download += f.size;
      }
    }
  }
  for (const auto& f : installed.files) {
    if (!seen.contains(f.path)) lines.push_back("file removed: " + f.path);
  }
  lines.insert(lines.end(), added.begin(), added.end());
  lines.push_back("bytes to download: " + std::to_string(diff.bytes_to_download));
  return diff;
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

Order within the file section: changed lines in candidate order, then removed (installed order), then added — the test's expected order.

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/confinement_plan.hpp"

#include <array>
#include <set>

// in the anonymous namespace:
void landlock_rights_per_abi() {
  namespace la = secure_frontend::landlock_access;
  const auto abi1 = secure_frontend::landlock_rights_for_abi(1);
  expect(abi1.handled_fs == 0x1FFFU, "ABI 1 handles bits 0-12");
  expect(abi1.handled_net == 0, "no network rights before ABI 4");
  expect(abi1.directory_rule == (la::kFsExecute | la::kFsReadFile | la::kFsReadDir), "read and execute below system dirs");
  expect(abi1.file_rule == la::kFsReadFile, "READ_FILE on /dev/null and /dev/urandom");
  expect(secure_frontend::landlock_rights_for_abi(2).handled_fs == 0x3FFFU, "ABI 2 adds REFER");
  expect(secure_frontend::landlock_rights_for_abi(3).handled_fs == 0x7FFFU, "ABI 3 adds TRUNCATE");
  const auto abi4 = secure_frontend::landlock_rights_for_abi(4);
  expect(abi4.handled_fs == 0x7FFFU && abi4.handled_net == (la::kNetBindTcp | la::kNetConnectTcp), "ABI 4 adds TCP rights");
  expect(secure_frontend::landlock_rights_for_abi(5).handled_fs == 0xFFFFU, "ABI 5 adds IOCTL_DEV");
  expect(secure_frontend::landlock_rights_for_abi(7).handled_fs == 0xFFFFU, "later ABIs use the ABI 5 set (note 14)");
  expect((abi1.directory_rule & la::kFsWriteFile) == 0 && (abi4.directory_rule & la::kFsMakeReg) == 0, "no write right anywhere");
}

void confinement_plan_detects_readable_roots() {
  const std::set<std::string> existing{"/usr", "/lib", "/bin", "/etc", "/home/u/tools"};
  const auto exists = [&](const std::string_view d) { return existing.contains(std::string{d}); };
  const std::vector<secure_frontend::ProtectedPath> clean{
      {"config file", "/home/u/.config/strata/config.json"}, {"roots.models", "/home/u/.local/share/strata/models"}};
  const auto plan = secure_frontend::plan_linux_confinement("/usr/bin/curl", clean, exists);
  expect((plan.read_dirs == std::vector<std::string>{"/usr", "/lib", "/bin", "/etc"}), "existing system dirs; /usr/bin covered by /usr");
  expect(!plan.readable_key.has_value(), "nothing protected is readable");
  const std::vector<secure_frontend::ProtectedPath> etc_config{{"config file", "/etc/strata/config.json"}};
  expect(secure_frontend::plan_linux_confinement("/usr/bin/curl", etc_config, exists).readable_key == "config file",
         "a config under /etc is readable");
  const std::vector<secure_frontend::ProtectedPath> home_root{{"roots.models", "/home/u/tools/models"}};
  const auto home = secure_frontend::plan_linux_confinement("/home/u/tools/curl", home_root, exists);
  expect(home.readable_key == "roots.models", "a downloader parent covering a root reduces confinement");
  expect(home.read_dirs.back() == "/home/u/tools", "the downloader's parent is read-allowed");
  expect(!secure_frontend::path_is_beneath("/usr", "/usrlocal/x") && secure_frontend::path_is_beneath("/usr", "/usr"),
         "separator-boundary prefix test");
}

void confinement_label_texts() {
  expect(secure_frontend::confinement_label(true, std::nullopt) == "full", "full");
  expect(secure_frontend::confinement_label(false, std::nullopt) == "reduced:no_landlock", "no Landlock");
  expect(secure_frontend::confinement_label(true, std::string{"roots.secret"}) == "reduced:readable:roots.secret", "readable");
  expect(secure_frontend::confinement_label(false, std::string{"roots.secret"}) == "reduced:no_landlock",
         "missing Landlock is the stronger cause");
}

void crt_fd_block_layout() {
  namespace cf = secure_frontend::crt_flags;
  const std::array<std::uint64_t, 5> handles{0x11, 0x22, 0x33, secure_frontend::kInvalidHandleBits, 0x55};
  const std::array<std::uint8_t, 5> flags{cf::kOpen | cf::kPipe, cf::kOpen | cf::kPipe, cf::kOpen | cf::kDevice, 0, cf::kOpen};
  const auto block = secure_frontend::build_crt_fd_block(handles, flags, 8);
  expect(block.size() == 49, "4 + 5 * (1 + 8) = 49 bytes on x64");
  expect(block[0] == 5 && block[1] == 0 && block[2] == 0 && block[3] == 0, "little-endian int count n = 5");
  expect(block[4] == 0x09 && block[5] == 0x09 && block[6] == 0x41 && block[7] == 0 && block[8] == 0x01, "flag bytes, packed");
  expect(block[9] == 0x11 && block[17] == 0x22 && block[25] == 0x33, "handles follow the flags, no padding");
  bool all_ff = true;
  for (std::size_t i = 33; i < 41; ++i) all_ff = all_ff && block[i] == 0xFF;
  expect(all_ff, "entry 3 is INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE");
  expect(block[41] == 0x55, "entry 4 is the record handle");
  expect(secure_frontend::build_crt_fd_block(handles, flags, 4).size() == 29, "x86 layout: 4 + 5 * 5");
}
// main list additions: landlock_rights_per_abi, confinement_plan_detects_readable_roots, confinement_label_texts,
//   crt_fd_block_layout
```

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <array>
#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <functional>
#include <optional>
#include <span>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>

namespace secure_frontend {

// Landlock UAPI access bits (stable kernel ABI; defined here so the planning
// code builds and is tested on every platform).
namespace landlock_access {
inline constexpr std::uint64_t kFsExecute = 1ULL << 0U;
inline constexpr std::uint64_t kFsWriteFile = 1ULL << 1U;
inline constexpr std::uint64_t kFsReadFile = 1ULL << 2U;
inline constexpr std::uint64_t kFsReadDir = 1ULL << 3U;
inline constexpr std::uint64_t kFsRemoveDir = 1ULL << 4U;
inline constexpr std::uint64_t kFsRemoveFile = 1ULL << 5U;
inline constexpr std::uint64_t kFsMakeChar = 1ULL << 6U;
inline constexpr std::uint64_t kFsMakeDir = 1ULL << 7U;
inline constexpr std::uint64_t kFsMakeReg = 1ULL << 8U;
inline constexpr std::uint64_t kFsMakeSock = 1ULL << 9U;
inline constexpr std::uint64_t kFsMakeFifo = 1ULL << 10U;
inline constexpr std::uint64_t kFsMakeBlock = 1ULL << 11U;
inline constexpr std::uint64_t kFsMakeSym = 1ULL << 12U;
inline constexpr std::uint64_t kFsRefer = 1ULL << 13U;     // ABI 2
inline constexpr std::uint64_t kFsTruncate = 1ULL << 14U;  // ABI 3
inline constexpr std::uint64_t kFsIoctlDev = 1ULL << 15U;  // ABI 5
inline constexpr std::uint64_t kNetBindTcp = 1ULL << 0U;   // ABI 4
inline constexpr std::uint64_t kNetConnectTcp = 1ULL << 1U;
}  // namespace landlock_access

// handled_* = every right the probed ABI defines; rules grant only read/execute (§3.2).
struct LandlockRights final {
  std::uint64_t handled_fs;
  std::uint64_t handled_net;
  std::uint64_t directory_rule;  // READ_FILE | READ_DIR | EXECUTE beneath allowed dirs
  std::uint64_t file_rule;       // READ_FILE on /dev/null, /dev/urandom
};
[[nodiscard]] LandlockRights landlock_rights_for_abi(int abi) noexcept;

inline constexpr std::array<std::string_view, 7> kLandlockSystemDirs{"/usr", "/lib", "/lib64", "/lib32", "/bin", "/sbin", "/etc"};
inline constexpr std::array<std::string_view, 2> kLandlockReadableFiles{"/dev/null", "/dev/urandom"};

// A path the downloader must not read: the config file, roots.models, roots.secret.
struct ProtectedPath final {
  std::string key_path;   // "config file", "roots.models", "roots.secret"
  std::string canonical;  // canonical absolute path, '/'-separated
};

struct ConfinementPlan final {
  std::vector<std::string> read_dirs;        // existing system dirs, then the downloader's parent if not covered
  std::optional<std::string> readable_key;   // first protected path beneath a read dir
};

// Separator-boundary containment: "/usr" contains "/usr" and "/usr/x", not "/usrx".
[[nodiscard]] bool path_is_beneath(std::string_view dir, std::string_view path) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] ConfinementPlan plan_linux_confinement(std::string_view downloader_canonical,
                                                     std::span<const ProtectedPath> protected_paths,
                                                     const std::function<bool(std::string_view)>& directory_exists);
// "full", "reduced:no_landlock" or "reduced:readable:<key path>" (§7.3).
[[nodiscard]] std::string confinement_label(bool landlock_available, const std::optional<std::string>& readable_key);

inline constexpr std::string_view kLowIntegritySid = "S-1-16-4096";
inline constexpr std::string_view kTrustedInstallerSid = "S-1-5-80-956008885-3418522649-1831038044-1853292631-2271478464";

// Microsoft CRT descriptor-inheritance block (STARTUPINFO.lpReserved2), §3.2.
namespace crt_flags {
inline constexpr std::uint8_t kOpen = 0x01;
inline constexpr std::uint8_t kPipe = 0x08;
inline constexpr std::uint8_t kDevice = 0x40;
}  // namespace crt_flags
inline constexpr std::size_t kCrtBlockEntries = 5;
inline constexpr std::uint64_t kInvalidHandleBits = ~0ULL;
// int32 n = 5 (little endian), n flag bytes, n handles of handle_width bytes (little endian), packed.
[[nodiscard]] std::vector<std::uint8_t> build_crt_fd_block(std::span<const std::uint64_t, 5> handles,
                                                           std::span<const std::uint8_t, 5> flags, std::size_t handle_width);

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/confinement_plan.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/confinement_plan.hpp"

#include <algorithm>

namespace secure_frontend {

LandlockRights landlock_rights_for_abi(const int abi) noexcept {
  namespace la = landlock_access;
  std::uint64_t fs = (la::kFsMakeSym << 1U) - 1U;  // ABI 1: bits 0..12
  if (abi >= 2) fs |= la::kFsRefer;
  if (abi >= 3) fs |= la::kFsTruncate;
  if (abi >= 5) fs |= la::kFsIoctlDev;
  const std::uint64_t net = abi >= 4 ? (la::kNetBindTcp | la::kNetConnectTcp) : 0U;
  return LandlockRights{fs, net, la::kFsExecute | la::kFsReadFile | la::kFsReadDir, la::kFsReadFile};
}

bool path_is_beneath(const std::string_view dir, const std::string_view path) noexcept {
  if (!path.starts_with(dir)) return false;
  return path.size() == dir.size() || dir == "/" || path[dir.size()] == '/';
}

ConfinementPlan plan_linux_confinement(const std::string_view downloader_canonical,
                                       const std::span<const ProtectedPath> protected_paths,
                                       const std::function<bool(std::string_view)>& directory_exists) {
  ConfinementPlan plan;
  for (const auto dir : kLandlockSystemDirs) {
    if (directory_exists(dir)) plan.read_dirs.emplace_back(dir);
  }
  const auto slash = downloader_canonical.rfind('/');
  const std::string parent{downloader_canonical.substr(0, slash == 0 ? 1 : slash)};
  const bool covered = std::any_of(plan.read_dirs.begin(), plan.read_dirs.end(),
                                   [&](const std::string& d) { return path_is_beneath(d, parent); });
  if (!covered) plan.read_dirs.push_back(parent);
  for (const auto& p : protected_paths) {
    const bool readable = std::any_of(plan.read_dirs.begin(), plan.read_dirs.end(),
                                      [&](const std::string& d) { return path_is_beneath(d, p.canonical); });
    if (readable) {
      plan.readable_key = p.key_path;
      break;
    }
  }
  return plan;
}

std::string confinement_label(const bool landlock_available, const std::optional<std::string>& readable_key) {
  if (!landlock_available) return "reduced:no_landlock";
  if (readable_key) return "reduced:readable:" + *readable_key;
  return "full";
}

std::vector<std::uint8_t> build_crt_fd_block(const std::span<const std::uint64_t, 5> handles,
                                             const std::span<const std::uint8_t, 5> flags, const std::size_t handle_width) {
  std::vector<std::uint8_t> block{static_cast<std::uint8_t>(kCrtBlockEntries), 0, 0, 0};
  block.insert(block.end(), flags.begin(), flags.end());
  for (const std::uint64_t handle : handles) {
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < handle_width; ++i) block.push_back(static_cast<std::uint8_t>(handle >> (8U * i)));
  }
  return block;
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`/usr/bin` is covered by `/usr`, so the stock downloader adds no directory; the existing-dir list is in `kLandlockSystemDirs` order.

```cpp
#include "support/curl_scenario.hpp"
#include "support/fake_curl_support.hpp"

// in the anonymous namespace:
std::filesystem::path built_fake() { return secure_frontend_test::extra_arguments().at(0); }

const char* kScenario =
    "probe 8.4.0 OpenSSL/3.0.13\n"
    "response https://fixture.example/f.bin\n"
    "status 103 Early Hints\n"
    "status 200 OK\n"
    "header Content-Length: 5\n"
    "body-file f.bin\n"
    "end\n"
    "response https://fixture.example/f.bin range 2\n"
    "status 206 Partial Content\n"
    "header Content-Range: bytes 2-4/5\n"
    "body-text llo\n"
    "end\n";

void fake_curl_scenario_parses_and_renders() {
  const auto scenario = secure_frontend_test::curl::parse_scenario(kScenario);
  expect(scenario.has_value() && scenario->responses.size() == 2, "two responses");
  const auto* first = secure_frontend_test::curl::select(*scenario, "https://fixture.example/f.bin", std::nullopt);
  expect(first != nullptr && secure_frontend_test::curl::render_head(*first) ==
                                 "HTTP/1.1 103 Early Hints\r\n\r\nHTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Length: 5\r\n\r\n",
         "head render");
  const auto* resumed = secure_frontend_test::curl::select(*scenario, "https://fixture.example/f.bin", 2);
  expect(resumed != nullptr && secure_frontend_test::curl::render_body(*resumed, "") == "llo", "selected by range");
  expect(secure_frontend_test::curl::select(*scenario, "https://fixture.example/f.bin", 3) == nullptr, "no match");
  expect(!secure_frontend_test::curl::parse_scenario("response x\nstatus\nend\n").has_value(), "bad status line");
}

void fake_curl_argv_grammar_matches_builder() {
  secure_frontend::TransferRequest request;
  request.url = "https://fixture.example/f.bin";
  request.host = "fixture.example";
  request.pin = secure_frontend::make_ipv4(93, 184, 216, 34);
  request.range_from = 2;
  auto argv = secure_frontend::build_transfer_argv("/x/curl", request);
  const std::vector<std::string> args(argv.begin() + 1, argv.end());
  const auto parsed = secure_frontend_test::curl::parse_curl_argv(args);
  expect(parsed && !parsed->probe && parsed->range == 2U && parsed->resolve == "fixture.example:443:93.184.216.34",
         "the fake accepts exactly what the builder emits");
  auto with_l = args;
  with_l.insert(with_l.begin(), "-L");
  expect(!secure_frontend_test::curl::parse_curl_argv(with_l).has_value(), "-L is outside the grammar");
  const std::vector<std::string> probe{"-q", "--version"};
  expect(secure_frontend_test::curl::parse_curl_argv(probe)->probe, "probe argv");
  expect(secure_frontend_test::curl::parse_config_line("url = \"https://fixture.example/f.bin\"\n") == "https://fixture.example/f.bin",
         "config line");
  expect(!secure_frontend_test::curl::parse_config_line("url = \"a\"\nurl = \"b\"\n").has_value(), "exactly one line");
}

void fake_curl_replays_scenario_and_records() {
  const auto scratch = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  const auto fake = secure_frontend_test::install_fake_curl(scratch, built_fake(), kScenario, {{"f.bin", "hello"}});
  const auto record_path = scratch / "downloader-record.log";
  const int record = ::open(record_path.c_str(), O_WRONLY | O_APPEND | O_CREAT | O_EXCL | O_CLOEXEC, 0600);
  expect(record >= 0, "record file created by the parent");
  secure_frontend::TransferRequest request;
  request.url = "https://fixture.example/f.bin";
  request.host = "fixture.example";
  const auto result = secure_frontend_test::spawn_with_fd4(secure_frontend::build_transfer_argv(fake.executable.string(), request),
                                                           secure_frontend::build_config_stdin(request), record);
  ::close(record);
  expect(result.exit_code == 0, "exit 0");
  expect(result.stdout_bytes == "HTTP/1.1 103 Early Hints\r\n\r\nHTTP/1.1 200 OK\r\nContent-Length: 5\r\n\r\nhello", "replayed bytes");
  const auto lines = secure_frontend_test::read_record_lines(record_path);
  expect(lines.size() == 1 && lines[0].find("\"kind\":\"transfer\"") != std::string::npos, "one transfer record");
  expect(!lines.empty() && lines[0].find("\"env\":[]") != std::string::npos, "empty environment recorded");
  expect(!lines.empty() && lines[0].find("url = \\\"https://fixture.example/f.bin\\\"\\n") != std::string::npos, "stdin recorded");
}

void fake_curl_rejects_argv_outside_grammar() {
  const auto scratch = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  const auto fake = secure_frontend_test::install_fake_curl(scratch, built_fake(), kScenario);
  const auto record_path = scratch / "downloader-record.log";
  const int record = ::open(record_path.c_str(), O_WRONLY | O_APPEND | O_CREAT | O_EXCL | O_CLOEXEC, 0600);
  const auto result = secure_frontend_test::spawn_with_fd4({fake.executable.string(), "-L", "https://x.example/"}, "", record);
  ::close(record);
  expect(result.exit_code == 98, "exit 98 outside the grammar");
  const auto lines = secure_frontend_test::read_record_lines(record_path);
  expect(lines.size() == 1 && lines[0].find("\"kind\":\"invalid\"") != std::string::npos, "invalid recorded");
}

void fake_curl_answers_probe() {
  const auto scratch = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  const auto fake = secure_frontend_test::install_fake_curl(scratch, built_fake(), "probe 7.80.0 GnuTLS/3.7.3\n");
  const auto result = secure_frontend_test::spawn_with_fd4(secure_frontend::build_probe_argv(fake.executable.string()), "", -1);
  expect(result.exit_code == 0 && result.stdout_bytes == "curl 7.80.0 (fake) libcurl/7.80.0 GnuTLS/3.7.3\n", "probe text");
}
#else
void fake_curl_records_on_crt_entry_4_windows() {
  const auto scratch = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  const auto fake = secure_frontend_test::install_fake_curl(scratch, built_fake(), kScenario, {{"f.bin", "hello"}});
  const auto record_path = scratch / "downloader-record.log";
  SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES inherit{sizeof(SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES), nullptr, TRUE};
  HANDLE record = CreateFileW(record_path.wstring().c_str(), FILE_APPEND_DATA, FILE_SHARE_READ, &inherit, CREATE_NEW,
                              FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, nullptr);
  expect(record != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE, "record file created by the parent");
  secure_frontend::TransferRequest request;
  request.url = "https://fixture.example/f.bin";
  request.host = "fixture.example";
  const auto result = secure_frontend_test::spawn_with_crt_entry4(
      secure_frontend::build_transfer_argv(fake.executable.string(), request), secure_frontend::build_config_stdin(request), record);
  CloseHandle(record);
  expect(result.exit_code == 0, "exit 0");
  const auto lines = secure_frontend_test::read_record_lines(record_path);
  expect(lines.size() == 1 && lines[0].find("\"kind\":\"transfer\"") != std::string::npos, "record written to CRT entry 4");
}
```

```cpp
#pragma once
// SP4 §10.1: the fake downloader's scenario format and the exact §3.3 argv
// grammar, shared by tests/fake_curl_main.cpp and the in-process
// ScriptedTransport (tests/support/scripted_transport.hpp, Task 21).
//
// Scenario text, one directive per line ('#' comments, blank lines ignored):
//   probe <version> <tls-token>      what "-q --version" prints
//   probe-exit <code>
//   response <url> [range <N>]       starts a response; selected by URL and --range N-
//   status <code> [reason...]        starts a header block (1xx blocks first)
//   header <Name>: <value>
//   body-file <path>                 relative to the scenario directory
//   body-text <text>                 the rest of the line
//   truncate-at <N>                  emit only N body bytes
//   exceed-by <N>                    append N 'X' bytes after the body
//   exit <code>                      curl exit status (default 0)
//   write-attempt <path>             try to create this file; errno recorded
//   read-attempt <path>              try to open this file for reading; errno recorded
//   end
#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <span>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

namespace secure_frontend_test::curl {

struct Block final {
  int status{200};
  std::string reason{"OK"};
  std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>> headers;
};

struct Response final {
  std::string url;
  std::optional<std::uint64_t> range;
  std::vector<Block> blocks;
  std::string body_file;
  std::string body_text;
  std::optional<std::uint64_t> truncate_at;
  std::uint64_t exceed_by{0};
  int exit_code{0};
  std::string write_attempt;
  std::string read_attempt;
};

struct Scenario final {
  std::vector<Response> responses;
  std::string probe_version{"8.4.0"};
  std::string probe_tls{"OpenSSL/3.0.13"};
  int probe_exit{0};
};

inline std::optional<std::uint64_t> to_u64(const std::string_view text) {
  if (text.empty() || text.size() > 19) return std::nullopt;
  std::uint64_t value = 0;
  for (const char c : text) {
    if (c < '0' || c > '9') return std::nullopt;
    value = value * 10U + static_cast<std::uint64_t>(c - '0');
  }
  return value;
}

inline std::pair<std::string_view, std::string_view> split_word(const std::string_view line) {
  const auto space = line.find(' ');
  if (space == std::string_view::npos) return {line, {}};
  return {line.substr(0, space), line.substr(space + 1)};
}

inline std::optional<Scenario> parse_scenario(const std::string_view text) {
  Scenario scenario;
  std::optional<Response> current;
  std::size_t start = 0;
  while (start < text.size()) {
    auto end = text.find('\n', start);
    if (end == std::string_view::npos) end = text.size();
    std::string_view line = text.substr(start, end - start);
    start = end + 1;
    if (!line.empty() && line.back() == '\r') line.remove_suffix(1);
    if (line.empty() || line.front() == '#') continue;
    const auto [word, rest] = split_word(line);
    if (word == "probe") {
      const auto [version, tls] = split_word(rest);
      if (version.empty() || tls.empty()) return std::nullopt;
      scenario.probe_version = std::string{version};
      scenario.probe_tls = std::string{tls};
    } else if (word == "probe-exit") {
      const auto code = to_u64(rest);
      if (!code) return std::nullopt;
      scenario.probe_exit = static_cast<int>(*code);
    } else if (word == "response") {
      if (current) return std::nullopt;
      const auto [url, tail] = split_word(rest);
      current = Response{};
      current->url = std::string{url};
      if (!tail.empty()) {
        const auto [keyword, number] = split_word(tail);
        const auto n = to_u64(number);
        if (keyword != "range" || !n) return std::nullopt;
        current->range = *n;
      }
    } else if (!current) {
      return std::nullopt;
    } else if (word == "status") {
      const auto [code, reason] = split_word(rest);
      const auto n = to_u64(code);
      if (!n || code.size() != 3) return std::nullopt;
      current->blocks.push_back(Block{static_cast<int>(*n), std::string{reason}, {}});
    } else if (word == "header") {
      const auto colon = rest.find(": ");
      if (current->blocks.empty() || colon == std::string_view::npos) return std::nullopt;
      current->blocks.back().headers.emplace_back(std::string{rest.substr(0, colon)}, std::string{rest.substr(colon + 2)});
    } else if (word == "body-file") {
      current->body_file = std::string{rest};
    } else if (word == "body-text") {
      current->body_text = std::string{rest};
    } else if (word == "truncate-at") {
      current->truncate_at = to_u64(rest);
      if (!current->truncate_at) return std::nullopt;
    } else if (word == "exceed-by") {
      const auto n = to_u64(rest);
      if (!n) return std::nullopt;
      current->exceed_by = *n;
    } else if (word == "exit") {
      const auto n = to_u64(rest);
      if (!n) return std::nullopt;
      current->exit_code = static_cast<int>(*n);
    } else if (word == "write-attempt") {
      current->write_attempt = std::string{rest};
    } else if (word == "read-attempt") {
      current->read_attempt = std::string{rest};
    } else if (word == "end") {
      scenario.responses.push_back(std::move(*current));
      current.reset();
    } else {
      return std::nullopt;
    }
  }
  if (current) return std::nullopt;
  return scenario;
}

inline const Response* select(const Scenario& scenario, const std::string_view url, const std::optional<std::uint64_t> range) {
  for (const auto& response : scenario.responses) {
    if (response.url == url && response.range == range) return &response;
  }
  return nullptr;
}

inline std::string render_head(const Response& response) {
  std::string out;
  for (const auto& block : response.blocks) {
    out += "HTTP/1.1 " + std::to_string(block.status) + (block.reason.empty() ? "" : " " + block.reason) + "\r\n";
    for (const auto& [name, value] : block.headers) out += name + ": " + value + "\r\n";
    out += "\r\n";
  }
  return out;
}

inline std::string render_body(const Response& response, const std::string_view file_bytes) {
  std::string body = response.body_file.empty() ? response.body_text : std::string{file_bytes};
  if (response.truncate_at && *response.truncate_at < body.size()) body.resize(*response.truncate_at);
  body.append(response.exceed_by, 'X');
  return body;
}

struct ParsedArgv final {
  bool probe{false};
  std::uint64_t connect_timeout{0};
  std::uint64_t stall_timeout{0};
  std::uint64_t max_time{0};
  std::optional<std::string> proxy;
  std::optional<std::string> resolve;
  std::optional<std::string> cacert;
  std::optional<std::uint64_t> range;
};

// Exactly spec §3.3 (args exclude argv[0]); "#" marks a number slot.
inline std::optional<ParsedArgv> parse_curl_argv(const std::span<const std::string> args) {
  ParsedArgv parsed;
  if (args.size() == 2 && args[0] == "-q" && args[1] == "--version") {
    parsed.probe = true;
    return parsed;
  }
  static const std::vector<std::string> fixed{
      "-q", "--config", "-", "--silent", "--include", "--suppress-connect-headers", "--globoff", "--proto", "=https",
      "--max-redirs", "0", "--tlsv1.2", "--connect-timeout", "#", "--speed-limit", "1024", "--speed-time", "#",
      "--max-time", "#", "--user-agent", "strata", "--header", "Accept-Encoding: identity"};
  if (args.size() < fixed.size() + 2) return std::nullopt;
  std::vector<std::uint64_t> numbers;
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < fixed.size(); ++i) {
    if (fixed[i] == "#") {
      const auto n = to_u64(args[i]);
      if (!n || *n == 0) return std::nullopt;
      numbers.push_back(*n);
    } else if (args[i] != fixed[i]) {
      return std::nullopt;
    }
  }
  parsed.connect_timeout = numbers[0];
  parsed.stall_timeout = numbers[1];
  parsed.max_time = numbers[2];
  std::size_t i = fixed.size();
  if (args[i] == "--noproxy" && args[i + 1] == "*") {
    i += 2;
    if (i + 1 < args.size() && args[i] == "--resolve") {
      parsed.resolve = args[i + 1];
      i += 2;
    }
  } else if (args[i] == "--proxy") {
    parsed.proxy = args[i + 1];
    i += 2;
  } else {
    return std::nullopt;
  }
  if (i + 1 < args.size() && args[i] == "--range") {
    const auto& value = args[i + 1];
    if (value.empty() || value.back() != '-') return std::nullopt;
    parsed.range = to_u64(std::string_view{value}.substr(0, value.size() - 1));
    if (!parsed.range) return std::nullopt;
    i += 2;
  }
  if (i + 1 < args.size() && args[i] == "--cacert") {
    parsed.cacert = args[i + 1];
    i += 2;
  }
  if (i != args.size()) return std::nullopt;
  return parsed;
}

inline std::optional<std::string> parse_config_line(const std::string_view input) {
  constexpr std::string_view kPrefix = "url = \"";
  if (!input.starts_with(kPrefix) || !input.ends_with("\"\n")) return std::nullopt;
  const auto url = input.substr(kPrefix.size(), input.size() - kPrefix.size() - 2);
  if (url.find_first_of("\"\n\\") != std::string_view::npos) return std::nullopt;
  return std::string{url};
}

inline std::string json_escape(const std::string_view text) {
  std::string out;
  for (const char c : text) {
    switch (c) {
      case '"': out += "\\\""; break;
      case '\\': out += "\\\\"; break;
      case '\n': out += "\\n"; break;
      case '\r': out += "\\r"; break;
      case '\t': out += "\\t"; break;
      default:
        if (static_cast<unsigned char>(c) < 0x20) {
          static constexpr char kHex[] = "0123456789abcdef";
          out += "\\u00";
          out.push_back(kHex[(static_cast<unsigned char>(c) >> 4U) & 0xFU]);
          out.push_back(kHex[static_cast<unsigned char>(c) & 0xFU]);
        } else {
          out.push_back(c);
        }
    }
  }
  return out;
}

inline std::string record_line(const std::string_view kind, const std::span<const std::string> args,
                               const std::span<const std::string> env, const std::string_view stdin_bytes,
                               const std::span<const std::pair<std::string, std::string>> extras) {
  const auto list = [](const std::span<const std::string> items) {
    std::string out = "[";
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < items.size(); ++i) out += (i ? ",\"" : "\"") + json_escape(items[i]) + "\"";
    return out + "]";
  };
  std::string line = "{\"kind\":\"" + json_escape(kind) + "\",\"argv\":" + list(args) + ",\"env\":" + list(env) +
                     ",\"stdin\":\"" + json_escape(stdin_bytes) + "\"";
  for (const auto& [key, value] : extras) line += ",\"" + json_escape(key) + "\":\"" + json_escape(value) + "\"";
  return line + "}\n";
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend_test::curl
```

`tests/support/fake_curl_support.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once
// SP4 §10.1: installs the fake downloader into <scratch>/downloader/ (a
// directory that holds no root, config or secret, so the Landlock read rule
// for the downloader's parent never reduces confinement) and spawns it the way
// the frontend does, with the record descriptor as fd 4 / CRT entry 4.
#include "secure_frontend/confinement_plan.hpp"
#include "support/test_support.hpp"

#include <array>
#include <filesystem>
#include <fstream>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include "secure_frontend/win32_prelude.hpp"
#else
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <spawn.h>
#include <sys/wait.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#endif

namespace secure_frontend_test {

struct FakeCurlInstall final {
  std::filesystem::path executable;
  std::filesystem::path scenario_dir;
};

inline FakeCurlInstall install_fake_curl(const std::filesystem::path& scratch, const std::filesystem::path& built_fake,
                                         const std::string_view scenario_text,
                                         const std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>>& fixtures = {}) {
  const auto dir = scratch / "downloader";
  make_private_directory(dir);
  make_private_directory(dir / "scenario");
  const auto executable = dir / built_fake.filename();
  std::filesystem::copy_file(built_fake, executable, std::filesystem::copy_options::overwrite_existing);
#if !defined(_WIN32)
  std::filesystem::permissions(executable, std::filesystem::perms::owner_all, std::filesystem::perm_options::replace);
#endif
  write_file(dir / "scenario" / "scenario.txt", scenario_text);
  for (const auto& [name, bytes] : fixtures) write_file(dir / "scenario" / name, bytes);
  return {std::filesystem::canonical(executable), dir / "scenario"};
}

inline std::vector<std::string> read_record_lines(const std::filesystem::path& record) {
  std::vector<std::string> lines;
  std::ifstream input(record, std::ios::binary);
  for (std::string line; std::getline(input, line);) {
    if (!line.empty()) lines.push_back(line);
  }
  return lines;
}

struct SpawnResult final {
  int exit_code{-1};
  std::string stdout_bytes;
};

#if !defined(_WIN32)
// Empty environment; fd 0 = config pipe, fd 1 = output pipe, fd 2 = /dev/null, fd 4 = record_fd (if >= 0).
inline SpawnResult spawn_with_fd4(const std::vector<std::string>& argv, const std::string_view stdin_bytes, const int record_fd) {
  int in[2];
  int out[2];
  if (::pipe2(in, O_CLOEXEC) != 0 || ::pipe2(out, O_CLOEXEC) != 0) return {};
  posix_spawn_file_actions_t actions;
  posix_spawn_file_actions_init(&actions);
  posix_spawn_file_actions_adddup2(&actions, in[0], 0);
  posix_spawn_file_actions_adddup2(&actions, out[1], 1);
  posix_spawn_file_actions_addopen(&actions, 2, "/dev/null", O_WRONLY, 0);
  if (record_fd >= 0) posix_spawn_file_actions_adddup2(&actions, record_fd, 4);
  std::vector<char*> args;
  for (const auto& a : argv) args.push_back(const_cast<char*>(a.c_str()));
  args.push_back(nullptr);
  char* envp[] = {nullptr};
  pid_t pid = -1;
  const int spawned = posix_spawn(&pid, argv[0].c_str(), &actions, nullptr, args.data(), envp);
  posix_spawn_file_actions_destroy(&actions);
  ::close(in[0]);
  ::close(out[1]);
  SpawnResult result;
  if (spawned != 0) {
    ::close(in[1]);
    ::close(out[0]);
    return result;
  }
  std::size_t written = 0;
  while (written < stdin_bytes.size()) {
    const auto n = ::write(in[1], stdin_bytes.data() + written, stdin_bytes.size() - written);
    if (n <= 0) break;
    written += static_cast<std::size_t>(n);
  }
  ::close(in[1]);
  char buffer[65536];
  for (ssize_t n; (n = ::read(out[0], buffer, sizeof buffer)) > 0;) result.stdout_bytes.append(buffer, static_cast<std::size_t>(n));
  ::close(out[0]);
  int status = 0;
  ::waitpid(pid, &status, 0);
  result.exit_code = WIFEXITED(status) ? WEXITSTATUS(status) : 128 + WTERMSIG(status);
  return result;
}
#else
// MSVCRT argv quoting (backslashes double only before a quote).
inline std::wstring quote_windows_command_line(const std::vector<std::string>& argv) {
  std::wstring line;
  for (const auto& narrow : argv) {
    const std::wstring arg = std::filesystem::path(narrow).wstring();
    if (!line.empty()) line.push_back(L' ');
    if (!arg.empty() && arg.find_first_of(L" \t\"") == std::wstring::npos) {
      line += arg;
      continue;
    }
    line.push_back(L'"');
    std::size_t backslashes = 0;
    for (const wchar_t c : arg) {
      if (c == L'\\') {
        ++backslashes;
        continue;
      }
      if (c == L'"') line.append(backslashes * 2 + 1, L'\\');
      else line.append(backslashes, L'\\');
      backslashes = 0;
      line.push_back(c);
    }
    line.append(backslashes * 2, L'\\');
    line.push_back(L'"');
  }
  return line;
}

// Empty environment; handles 0/1 pipes, 2 NUL, 3 unused, 4 = record (CRT block entry 4).
inline SpawnResult spawn_with_crt_entry4(const std::vector<std::string>& argv, const std::string_view stdin_bytes,
                                         void* record_handle) {
  SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES inherit{sizeof(SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES), nullptr, TRUE};
  HANDLE in_read = nullptr, in_write = nullptr, out_read = nullptr, out_write = nullptr;
  if (!CreatePipe(&in_read, &in_write, &inherit, 0) || !CreatePipe(&out_read, &out_write, &inherit, 0)) return {};
  SetHandleInformation(in_write, HANDLE_FLAG_INHERIT, 0);
  SetHandleInformation(out_read, HANDLE_FLAG_INHERIT, 0);
  HANDLE null_device = CreateFileW(L"NUL", GENERIC_WRITE, FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE, &inherit, OPEN_EXISTING, 0, nullptr);
  namespace cf = secure_frontend::crt_flags;
  const std::array<std::uint64_t, 5> handles{reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(in_read), reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(out_write),
                                             reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(null_device), secure_frontend::kInvalidHandleBits,
                                             reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(record_handle)};
  const std::array<std::uint8_t, 5> flags{cf::kOpen | cf::kPipe, cf::kOpen | cf::kPipe, cf::kOpen | cf::kDevice, 0, cf::kOpen};
  auto block = secure_frontend::build_crt_fd_block(handles, flags, sizeof(HANDLE));
  STARTUPINFOW startup{};
  startup.cb = sizeof startup;
  startup.dwFlags = STARTF_USESTDHANDLES;
  startup.hStdInput = in_read;
  startup.hStdOutput = out_write;
  startup.hStdError = null_device;
  startup.cbReserved2 = static_cast<WORD>(block.size());
  startup.lpReserved2 = block.data();
  std::wstring command = quote_windows_command_line(argv);
  wchar_t environment[2] = {L'\0', L'\0'};
  PROCESS_INFORMATION info{};
  const BOOL created = CreateProcessW(std::filesystem::path(argv[0]).wstring().c_str(), command.data(), nullptr, nullptr, TRUE,
                                      CREATE_UNICODE_ENVIRONMENT, environment, nullptr, &startup, &info);
  CloseHandle(in_read);
  CloseHandle(out_write);
  CloseHandle(null_device);
  SpawnResult result;
  if (!created) {
    CloseHandle(in_write);
    CloseHandle(out_read);
    return result;
  }
  DWORD written = 0;
  if (!stdin_bytes.empty()) WriteFile(in_write, stdin_bytes.data(), static_cast<DWORD>(stdin_bytes.size()), &written, nullptr);
  CloseHandle(in_write);
  char buffer[65536];
  DWORD n = 0;
  while (ReadFile(out_read, buffer, sizeof buffer, &n, nullptr) && n > 0) result.stdout_bytes.append(buffer, n);
  CloseHandle(out_read);
  WaitForSingleObject(info.hProcess, INFINITE);
  DWORD code = 0;
  GetExitCodeProcess(info.hProcess, &code);
  result.exit_code = static_cast<int>(code);
  CloseHandle(info.hThread);
  CloseHandle(info.hProcess);
  return result;
}
#endif

}  // namespace secure_frontend_test
```

The fetch test binary links `secure_frontend_acquire_core`, which provides `build_crt_fd_block` for the Windows helper.

`tests/fake_curl_main.cpp`:

```cpp
// SP4 §10.1: the fake downloader. Accepts exactly the §3.3 grammar (exit 98 and
// an "invalid" record otherwise), records argv/env/stdin over the inherited
// record descriptor (fd 4 on POSIX; CRT block entry 4 on Windows) and replays
// a scripted response found in <own directory>/scenario/scenario.txt.
// It never opens a socket.
#include "support/curl_scenario.hpp"

#include <cerrno>
#include <cstdio>
#include <cstring>
#include <cwchar>
#include <filesystem>
#include <fstream>
#include <iterator>
#include <string>
#include <vector>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include "secure_frontend/win32_prelude.hpp"
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <io.h>
#include <sddl.h>
#else
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <unistd.h>
extern char** environ;
#endif

namespace {

namespace curl = secure_frontend_test::curl;

std::filesystem::path own_directory(const char* argv0) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
  (void)argv0;
  std::wstring buffer(32768, L'\0');
  const DWORD length = GetModuleFileNameW(nullptr, buffer.data(), static_cast<DWORD>(buffer.size()));
  buffer.resize(length);
  return std::filesystem::path(buffer).parent_path();
#else
  return std::filesystem::path(argv0).parent_path();
#endif
}

std::vector<std::string> environment() {
  std::vector<std::string> out;
#if defined(_WIN32)
  wchar_t* block = GetEnvironmentStringsW();
  for (const wchar_t* p = block; p != nullptr && *p != L'\0'; p += std::wcslen(p) + 1) {
    out.push_back(std::filesystem::path(p).string());
  }
  if (block != nullptr) FreeEnvironmentStringsW(block);
#else
  for (char** p = environ; p != nullptr && *p != nullptr; ++p) out.emplace_back(*p);
#endif
  return out;
}

void write_record(const std::string& line) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
  STARTUPINFOW startup{};
  startup.cb = sizeof startup;
  GetStartupInfoW(&startup);
  if (startup.lpReserved2 == nullptr || startup.cbReserved2 < 4) return;
  int count = 0;
  std::memcpy(&count, startup.lpReserved2, sizeof count);
  if (count < 5 || startup.cbReserved2 < 4U + static_cast<unsigned>(count) * (1U + sizeof(HANDLE))) return;
  const BYTE* flags = startup.lpReserved2 + 4;
  HANDLE handle = nullptr;
  std::memcpy(&handle, startup.lpReserved2 + 4 + count + 4 * sizeof(HANDLE), sizeof handle);
  if ((flags[4] & 0x01U) == 0 || handle == nullptr || handle == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return;
  DWORD written = 0;
  (void)WriteFile(handle, line.data(), static_cast<DWORD>(line.size()), &written, nullptr);
#else
  if (fcntl(4, F_GETFD) == -1) return;
  std::size_t offset = 0;
  while (offset < line.size()) {
    const ssize_t n = write(4, line.data() + offset, line.size() - offset);
    if (n < 0 && errno == EINTR) continue;
    if (n <= 0) return;
    offset += static_cast<std::size_t>(n);
  }
#endif
}

std::string read_stdin() {
  std::string input;
  char buffer[4096];
  for (std::size_t n; (n = std::fread(buffer, 1, sizeof buffer, stdin)) > 0;) input.append(buffer, n);
  return input;
}

std::string read_file(const std::filesystem::path& path) {
  std::ifstream input(path, std::ios::binary);
  return std::string{std::istreambuf_iterator<char>(input), std::istreambuf_iterator<char>()};
}

std::string attempt_write(const std::string& path) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
  HANDLE h = CreateFileW(std::filesystem::path(path).wstring().c_str(), GENERIC_WRITE, 0, nullptr, CREATE_NEW, 0, nullptr);
  if (h == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return std::to_string(GetLastError());
  CloseHandle(h);
  return "0";
#else
  const int fd = open(path.c_str(), O_WRONLY | O_CREAT | O_EXCL | O_CLOEXEC, 0600);
  if (fd < 0) return std::to_string(errno);
  close(fd);
  return "0";
#endif
}

std::string attempt_read(const std::string& path) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
  HANDLE h = CreateFileW(std::filesystem::path(path).wstring().c_str(), GENERIC_READ, FILE_SHARE_READ, nullptr, OPEN_EXISTING, 0, nullptr);
  if (h == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return std::to_string(GetLastError());
  CloseHandle(h);
  return "0";
#else
  const int fd = open(path.c_str(), O_RDONLY | O_CLOEXEC);
  if (fd < 0) return std::to_string(errno);
  close(fd);
  return "0";
#endif
}

std::string integrity_level() {
#if defined(_WIN32)
  HANDLE token = nullptr;
  if (!OpenProcessToken(GetCurrentProcess(), TOKEN_QUERY, &token)) return "unknown";
  DWORD size = 0;
  GetTokenInformation(token, TokenIntegrityLevel, nullptr, 0, &size);
  std::vector<unsigned char> buffer(size);
  std::string text = "unknown";
  if (size != 0 && GetTokenInformation(token, TokenIntegrityLevel, buffer.data(), size, &size)) {
    wchar_t* sid = nullptr;
    if (ConvertSidToStringSidW(reinterpret_cast<TOKEN_MANDATORY_LABEL*>(buffer.data())->Label.Sid, &sid)) {
      text = std::filesystem::path(sid).string();
      LocalFree(sid);
    }
  }
  CloseHandle(token);
  return text;
#else
  return "";
#endif
}

}  // namespace

int main(int argc, char** argv) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
  _setmode(_fileno(stdout), _O_BINARY);
  _setmode(_fileno(stdin), _O_BINARY);
#endif
  const std::vector<std::string> args(argv + 1, argv + argc);
  const auto env = environment();
  const auto scenario_dir = own_directory(argv[0]) / "scenario";
  const auto scenario = curl::parse_scenario(read_file(scenario_dir / "scenario.txt"));
  std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>> extras;
  if (const auto level = integrity_level(); !level.empty()) extras.emplace_back("integrity", level);
  const auto parsed = curl::parse_curl_argv(args);
  if (!parsed) {
    write_record(curl::record_line("invalid", args, env, "", extras));
    return 98;
  }
  if (parsed->probe) {
    write_record(curl::record_line("probe", args, env, "", extras));
    const curl::Scenario defaults;
    const auto& s = scenario ? *scenario : defaults;
    const std::string text = "curl " + s.probe_version + " (fake) libcurl/" + s.probe_version + " " + s.probe_tls + "\n";
    std::fwrite(text.data(), 1, text.size(), stdout);
    std::fflush(stdout);
    return s.probe_exit;
  }
  const std::string input = read_stdin();
  const auto url = curl::parse_config_line(input);
  if (!url) {
    write_record(curl::record_line("invalid", args, env, input, extras));
    return 98;
  }
  if (!scenario) {
    write_record(curl::record_line("no_scenario", args, env, input, extras));
    return 99;
  }
  const auto* response = curl::select(*scenario, *url, parsed->range);
  if (response != nullptr && !response->write_attempt.empty()) extras.emplace_back("write_errno", attempt_write(response->write_attempt));
  if (response != nullptr && !response->read_attempt.empty()) extras.emplace_back("read_errno", attempt_read(response->read_attempt));
  write_record(curl::record_line("transfer", args, env, input, extras));
  std::string out;
  if (response == nullptr) {
    out = "HTTP/1.1 404 Not Found\r\n\r\n";
  } else {
    const std::string file = response->body_file.empty() ? std::string{} : read_file(scenario_dir / response->body_file);
    out = curl::render_head(*response) + curl::render_body(*response, file);
  }
  std::fwrite(out.data(), 1, out.size(), stdout);
  std::fflush(stdout);
  return response == nullptr ? 0 : response->exit_code;
}
```

### Task 13: `make_bundle.py` (dev-only, D5 (B′)) — NOW

Spec §2.7, §5.1, D5/C5; Controller notes 9 and 10. Never shipped: `tools/` is not part of any staged distribution.

**Files:**
- Create: `tools/secure/make_bundle.py`, `tools/secure/qwen_bundle_pins.json`, `tools/secure/test_make_bundle.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the bundle format of spec §5.1 and the manifest schema of §2.2 (no C++ code).
- Produces: CLI `python3 -I tools/secure/make_bundle.py --model <name> --quant <quant> --shards-dir DIR --out DIR [--pins FILE] [--pack-tool FILE] [--python EXE] [--gguf-py DIR] [--move-shards] [--version-label YYYY.MM.DD.N] [--sequence N] [--engine-min-version X.Y.Z[.N]] [--ple-shard N]`; exit 0 on success, 2 on any `BundleError`; prints `manifest sha256: <hex>` and the `strata --config <config> import <out> --trust-manifest sha256:<hex>` line. Module functions `sha256_file(Path) -> str`, `build_bundle(args) -> str` (returns the manifest digest), `main(argv) -> int`, class `BundleError`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Failing test** — `tools/secure/test_make_bundle.py`:

```python
"""Tests for the dev-only bundle builder (SP4 Task 13). Pure local files; no network, no real model."""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import importlib.util
import json
import sys
from pathlib import Path

import pytest

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
_spec = importlib.util.spec_from_file_location("make_bundle", HERE / "make_bundle.py")
mb = importlib.util.module_from_spec(_spec)
assert _spec.loader is not None
_spec.loader.exec_module(mb)

FAKE_PACK_TOOL = '''
import argparse, pathlib
p = argparse.ArgumentParser(); p.add_argument("--gguf"); p.add_argument("--out"); a = p.parse_args()
out = pathlib.Path(a.out); (out / "tokenizer").mkdir(parents=True)
for name, text in {"index.txt": "i\\n", "dense.bin": "d", "native_experts.txt": "n\\n", "conversions.json": "{}\\n"}.items():
    if name not in SKIP:
        (out / name).write_text(text)
for name, text in {"vocab.json": '{"a":0}', "merges.txt": "", "token_type.json": "[1]", "tokenizer.json": "{}"}.items():
    (out / "tokenizer" / name).write_text(text)
'''


def sha(data: bytes) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(data).hexdigest()


def setup_inputs(tmp_path: Path, skip: tuple[str, ...] = ()) -> list[str]:
    shards = tmp_path / "shards"
    shards.mkdir()
    one, two = b"shard one", b"shard two"
    (shards / "M-IQ2_XS-00001-of-00002.gguf").write_bytes(one)
    (shards / "M-IQ2_XS-00002-of-00002.gguf").write_bytes(two)
    pins = {
        "qwen3.8-flash-next": {
            "repo": "ISTA-DASLab/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-GSQ-RCO-GGUF",
            "revision": "ed59f92082b1e93c0e96d60a8b11aab089b52f09",
            "license": {"id": "Apache-2.0", "url": "https://huggingface.co/ISTA-DASLab/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-GSQ-RCO-GGUF",
                        "bundle_allowed": True, "notice": "Test notice."},
            "quants": {"iq2_xs": {"dir": "IQ2_XS", "shards": [
                {"file": "M-IQ2_XS-00001-of-00002.gguf", "size": len(one), "sha256": sha(one)},
                {"file": "M-IQ2_XS-00002-of-00002.gguf", "size": len(two), "sha256": sha(two)}]}},
        }
    }
    (tmp_path / "pins.json").write_text(json.dumps(pins))
    (tmp_path / "pack_tool.py").write_text(f"SKIP = {skip!r}\n" + FAKE_PACK_TOOL)
    return ["--model", "qwen3.8-flash-next", "--quant", "iq2_xs", "--shards-dir", str(shards),
            "--out", str(tmp_path / "bundle"), "--pins", str(tmp_path / "pins.json"),
            "--pack-tool", str(tmp_path / "pack_tool.py"), "--python", sys.executable,
            "--version-label", "2026.10.14.1", "--engine-min-version", "0.1.44.1"]


def test_builds_complete_bundle_and_prints_trust_digest(tmp_path, capsys):
    assert mb.main(setup_inputs(tmp_path)) == 0
    bundle = tmp_path / "bundle"
    manifest_bytes = (bundle / "manifest.json").read_bytes()
    digest = sha(manifest_bytes)
    printed = capsys.readouterr().out
    assert f"manifest sha256: {digest}" in printed
    assert f"--trust-manifest sha256:{digest}" in printed
    assert (bundle / "STRATA-BUNDLE").read_bytes() == b"strata-bundle 1\n"
    manifest = json.loads(manifest_bytes)
    assert manifest["format"] == "strata-model-manifest" and manifest["schema_version"] == 1
    assert manifest["name"] == "qwen3.8-flash-next" and manifest["tag"] == "iq2_xs-local"
    assert manifest["profile"] == "qwen" and manifest["engine"] == {"family": "strata", "min_version": "0.1.44.1"}
    paths = [f["path"] for f in manifest["files"]]
    assert "weights/M-IQ2_XS-00001-of-00002.gguf" in paths and "pack/tokenizer/vocab.json" in paths
    shard = next(f for f in manifest["files"] if f["path"].endswith("00001-of-00002.gguf"))
    assert shard["sources"] == ["https://huggingface.co/ISTA-DASLab/Qwen3.8-Flash-Next-GSQ-RCO-GGUF/resolve/"
                                "ed59f92082b1e93c0e96d60a8b11aab089b52f09/IQ2_XS/M-IQ2_XS-00001-of-00002.gguf"]
    assert all(f["sources"] == [] for f in manifest["files"] if f["path"].startswith("pack/"))
    roles = {f["path"]: f["role"] for f in manifest["files"]}
    assert roles["pack/tokenizer/merges.txt"] == "tokenizer" and roles["pack/index.txt"] == "pack"
    assert manifest["launch"] == {"native": "weights/M-IQ2_XS-00001-of-00002.gguf",
                                  "ple_gguf": "weights/M-IQ2_XS-00002-of-00002.gguf",
                                  "pack": "pack", "tokenizer_pack": "pack/tokenizer"}
    sums = (bundle / "SHA256SUMS").read_text().splitlines()
    sum_paths = [line[66:] for line in sums]
    assert sum_paths == sorted(sum_paths, key=lambda p: p.encode()) and "manifest.json" in sum_paths
    for line in sums:
        target = bundle / line[66:]
        assert line[:64] == sha(target.read_bytes())
    assert not (bundle / "MISSING").exists()
    assert all(32 <= ord(c) <= 126 or c == "\n" for c in manifest_bytes.decode("ascii"))


def test_refuses_shard_digest_mismatch(tmp_path):
    args = setup_inputs(tmp_path)
    (tmp_path / "shards" / "M-IQ2_XS-00002-of-00002.gguf").write_bytes(b"tampered")
    assert mb.main(args) == 2
    assert not (tmp_path / "bundle").exists()


def test_refuses_non_empty_output(tmp_path):
    args = setup_inputs(tmp_path)
    (tmp_path / "bundle").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "bundle" / "x").write_text("x")
    assert mb.main(args) == 2


def test_refuses_incomplete_pack_layout(tmp_path):
    assert mb.main(setup_inputs(tmp_path, skip=("dense.bin",))) == 2
    assert not (tmp_path / "bundle" / "STRATA-BUNDLE").exists()


def test_pins_file_matches_spec_facts():
    pins = json.loads((HERE / "qwen_bundle_pins.json").read_text())
    gsq = pins["qwen3.8-flash-next"]
    assert gsq["revision"] == "ed59f92082b1e93c0e96d60a8b11aab089b52f09"
    one, two = gsq["quants"]["iq2_xs"]["shards"]
    assert one["size"] == 39225954592 and one["sha256"] == "92cee27ae5bbadcd732416a0f7a7f0acc092399dbbe8f5a5efa707c2ec0a49d7"
    assert two["size"] == 28800138432 and two["sha256"] == "316b46f3a2dbd68c900f43136ab9449f9dcc3725dfd8c794847c204bc161e113"
    assert set(gsq["quants"]) == {"iq2_xs", "iq3_xxs", "iq3_s"}
    coder = pins["qwen3.8-flash-next-coder"]
    assert coder["revision"] == "5348543e0147355ac9cbcb031184a3546350988e" and set(coder["quants"]) == {"iq1_m"}
    for model in pins.values():
        assert model["license"]["id"] == "Apache-2.0" and model["license"]["bundle_allowed"] is True
```

`tools/secure/make_bundle.py`:

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Dev-only, never shipped: build a Strata model bundle from verified Qwen shards.

SP4 spec §2.7 / D5 (B'). Until the owner's determinism check (D5 (A)) or a C++
pack builder (D5 (C)) gives the Qwen --pack files pinned digests, a developer
builds the pack locally with upstream's pinned tools/iq_pack.py and publishes
it to the offline box as a *third-party* bundle:

  1. download the two shards of a quant (any way; this tool re-hashes them
     against tools/secure/qwen_bundle_pins.json),
  2. python3 -I tools/secure/make_bundle.py --model qwen3.8-flash-next --quant iq2_xs \
       --shards-dir <dir> --out <usb>/qwen-iq2_xs --gguf-py <llama.cpp>/gguf-py
  3. on the offline box: strata --config <file> import <usb>/qwen-iq2_xs --trust-manifest sha256:<printed hex>

The manifest is written under tag "<quant>-local" so it never shadows a future
catalog entry. Needs the dev venv with numpy and llama.cpp's gguf-py for the
real pack tool (upstream/tools/_paths.py reads STRATA_GGUF_PY).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import argparse
import datetime
import hashlib
import json
import os
import shutil
import subprocess
import sys
from pathlib import Path

HERE = Path(__file__).resolve().parent
REPO = HERE.parent.parent
DEFAULT_PINS = HERE / "qwen_bundle_pins.json"
DEFAULT_PACK_TOOL = REPO / "upstream" / "tools" / "iq_pack.py"
PACK_REQUIRED = ("index.txt", "dense.bin", "native_experts.txt", "conversions.json")
TOKENIZER_REQUIRED = ("vocab.json", "merges.txt", "token_type.json", "tokenizer.json")
CHUNK = 8 * 1024 * 1024
MARKER = b"strata-bundle 1\n"
HOSTS = ["huggingface.co"]
REDIRECT_HOSTS = ["*.aws.cdn.hf.co", "cas-bridge.xethub.hf.co"]


class BundleError(Exception):
    """A refusal; the message never contains file contents."""


def sha256_file(path: Path) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256()
    with path.open("rb") as handle:
        while chunk := handle.read(CHUNK):
            digest.update(chunk)
    return digest.hexdigest()


def load_quant(pins_path: Path, model: str, quant: str) -> tuple[dict, dict]:
    pins = json.loads(pins_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8"))
    if model not in pins or quant not in pins[model]["quants"]:
        raise BundleError(f"no pins for {model}:{quant}")
    return pins[model], pins[model]["quants"][quant]


def verify_shards(shards_dir: Path, quant_pins: dict) -> list[Path]:
    paths = []
    for shard in quant_pins["shards"]:
        path = shards_dir / shard["file"]
        if not path.is_file() or path.is_symlink():
            raise BundleError(f"missing shard {shard['file']}")
        if path.stat().st_size != shard["size"]:
            raise BundleError(f"size mismatch for {shard['file']}")
        if sha256_file(path) != shard["sha256"]:
            raise BundleError(f"sha256 mismatch for {shard['file']}")
        paths.append(path)
    return paths


def prepare_output(out: Path) -> None:
    if out.exists():
        if not out.is_dir() or any(out.iterdir()):
            raise BundleError("output directory exists and is not empty")
    else:
        out.mkdir(mode=0o700, parents=False)
    os.chmod(out, 0o700)


def check_pack_layout(pack: Path) -> None:
    for name in PACK_REQUIRED:
        if not (pack / name).is_file():
            raise BundleError(f"pack tool did not produce {name}")
    for name in TOKENIZER_REQUIRED:
        if not (pack / "tokenizer" / name).is_file():
            raise BundleError(f"pack tool did not produce tokenizer/{name}")


def pack_files(pack: Path) -> list[tuple[str, Path]]:
    files = []
    for path in sorted(pack.rglob("*")):
        if path.is_symlink():
            raise BundleError("pack contains a symbolic link")
        if path.is_file():
            files.append(("pack/" + path.relative_to(pack).as_posix(), path))
    return files


def build_bundle(args: argparse.Namespace) -> str:
    model_pins, quant_pins = load_quant(Path(args.pins), args.model, args.quant)
    shards = verify_shards(Path(args.shards_dir), quant_pins)
    out = Path(args.out)
    prepare_output(out)
    files_dir = out / "files"
    (files_dir / "weights").mkdir(parents=True, mode=0o700)
    pack = files_dir / "pack"
    env = dict(os.environ)
    if args.gguf_py:
        env["STRATA_GGUF_PY"] = args.gguf_py
    result = subprocess.run([args.python, str(args.pack_tool), "--gguf", str(shards[0]), "--out", str(pack)],
                            env=env, check=False)
    if result.returncode != 0:
        raise BundleError(f"pack tool failed with exit code {result.returncode}")
    check_pack_layout(pack)
    entries = []
    for shard, pin in zip(shards, quant_pins["shards"]):
        target = files_dir / "weights" / shard.name
        if args.move_shards:
            shutil.move(str(shard), target)
        else:
            shutil.copyfile(shard, target)
        if sha256_file(target) != pin["sha256"]:
            raise BundleError(f"copy of {shard.name} does not match its pin")
        url = f"https://huggingface.co/{model_pins['repo']}/resolve/{model_pins['revision']}/{quant_pins['dir']}/{shard.name}"
        entries.append({"path": f"weights/{shard.name}", "role": "weights", "size": pin["size"],
                        "sha256": pin["sha256"], "sources": [url]})
    for relative, path in pack_files(pack):
        role = "tokenizer" if relative.startswith("pack/tokenizer/") else "pack"
        entries.append({"path": relative, "role": role, "size": path.stat().st_size,
                        "sha256": sha256_file(path), "sources": []})
    names = [e["path"] for e in entries if e["role"] == "weights"]
    launch = {"native": names[0]}
    ple_index = args.ple_shard - 1
    if len(names) <= 2 and 0 <= ple_index < len(names):
        launch["ple_gguf"] = names[ple_index]
    launch["pack"] = "pack"
    launch["tokenizer_pack"] = "pack/tokenizer"
    manifest = {
        "format": "strata-model-manifest",
        "schema_version": 1,
        "name": args.model,
        "tag": f"{args.quant}-local",
        "version": args.version_label or f"{datetime.date.today():%Y.%m.%d}.1",
        "sequence": args.sequence,
        "profile": "qwen",
        "engine": {"family": "strata", "min_version": args.engine_min_version},
        "license": model_pins["license"],
        "hosts": HOSTS,
        "redirect_hosts": REDIRECT_HOSTS,
        "files": entries,
        "launch": launch,
    }
    manifest_bytes = (json.dumps(manifest, indent=2, ensure_ascii=True) + "\n").encode("ascii")
    (out / "manifest.json").write_bytes(manifest_bytes)
    digest = hashlib.sha256(manifest_bytes).hexdigest()
    sums = [(digest, "manifest.json")] + [(e["sha256"], "files/" + e["path"]) for e in entries]
    sums.sort(key=lambda item: item[1].encode("ascii"))
    (out / "SHA256SUMS").write_bytes("".join(f"{h}  {p}\n" for h, p in sums).encode("ascii"))
    (out / "STRATA-BUNDLE").write_bytes(MARKER)  # written last (§5.1)
    return digest


def parse_args(argv: list[str] | None) -> argparse.Namespace:
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__, formatter_class=argparse.RawDescriptionHelpFormatter)
    parser.add_argument("--model", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--quant", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--shards-dir", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--out", required=True)
    parser.add_argument("--pins", default=str(DEFAULT_PINS))
    parser.add_argument("--pack-tool", default=str(DEFAULT_PACK_TOOL))
    parser.add_argument("--python", default=sys.executable)
    parser.add_argument("--gguf-py", default="")
    parser.add_argument("--move-shards", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--version-label", default="")
    parser.add_argument("--sequence", type=int, default=1)
    parser.add_argument("--engine-min-version", default="0.1.44.1")
    parser.add_argument("--ple-shard", type=int, default=2,
                        help="shard holding per_layer_token_embd: 2 for the original GSQ-RCO files (setup.py step 7)")
    return parser.parse_args(argv)


def main(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> int:
    args = parse_args(argv)
    try:
        digest = build_bundle(args)
    except (BundleError, OSError) as error:
        print(f"error: {error}", file=sys.stderr)
        return 2
    print(f"manifest sha256: {digest}")
    print(f"strata --config <config.json> import {args.out} --trust-manifest sha256:{digest}")
    return 0


if __name__ == "__main__":
    sys.exit(main())
```

`json.dumps(..., indent=2)` puts no trailing spaces after commas inside lines (Python 3.4+ uses `", "` only without indent), so every byte is printable ASCII or LF. `test_refuses_shard_digest_mismatch` asserts the output directory is not created: `verify_shards` runs before `prepare_output`.
