```cpp
  ],
  "launch": {"native": "weights/m-00001-of-00002.gguf", "pack": "pack", "tokenizer_pack": "pack/tokenizer"}
}
)";

std::string base_manifest() {
  std::string text = kBaseManifest;
  for (const auto& [mark, digit] : {std::pair{"SHA1", 'a'}, std::pair{"SHA2", 'b'}, std::pair{"SHA3", 'c'}, std::pair{"SHA4", 'd'}}) {
    text.replace(text.find(mark), 4, std::string(64, digit));
  }
  return text;
}

std::string replaced(std::string text, const std::string& from, const std::string& to) {
  const auto at = text.find(from);
  if (at == std::string::npos) throw std::runtime_error("fixture text not found: " + from);
  return text.replace(at, from.size(), to);
}

bool rejected(const std::string& text, const secure_frontend::AcquireReason reason = secure_frontend::AcquireReason::manifest_invalid) {
  const auto result = secure_frontend::parse_manifest(text, secure_frontend::ManifestRules{});
  const auto* issue = std::get_if<secure_frontend::ManifestIssue>(&result);
  return issue != nullptr && issue->reason == reason;
}

void base_manifest_parses() {
  const auto result = secure_frontend::parse_manifest(base_manifest(), secure_frontend::ManifestRules{});
  const auto* parsed = std::get_if<secure_frontend::ParsedManifest>(&result);
  expect(parsed != nullptr, "the base manifest parses");
  if (parsed != nullptr) {
    expect(parsed->manifest.files.size() == 4 && parsed->manifest.files[1].sources.empty(), "sourceless file kept");
    expect(parsed->bytes == base_manifest(), "exact bytes kept");
  }
}

// Spec §10.3 DL-03 matrix.
void dl03_invalid_manifest_matrix() {
  const std::string base = base_manifest();
  const std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>> cases{
      {"unknown key", replaced(base, "\"sequence\": 1,", "\"sequence\": 1, \"extra\": 1,")},
      {"duplicate key", replaced(base, "\"sequence\": 1,", "\"sequence\": 1, \"sequence\": 2,")},
      {"bad hex", replaced(base, std::string(64, 'a'), std::string(63, 'a') + "g")},
      {"size 0", replaced(base, "\"size\": 1, \"sha256\": \"" + std::string(64, 'a'), "\"size\": 0, \"sha256\": \"" + std::string(64, 'a'))},
      {"..", replaced(base, "pack/index.txt\"", "pack/../index.txt\"")},
      {"absolute", replaced(base, "\"pack/index.txt\"", "\"/pack/index.txt\"")},
      {"case-fold collision", replaced(base, "pack/tokenizer/vocab.json\", \"role", "PACK/INDEX.TXT\", \"role")},
      {"control byte", replaced(base, "\"notice\": \"n\"", "\"notice\": \"n\\u0007\"")},
      {"[ in URL", replaced(base, "https://h.example/a", "https://h.example/a[1]")},
      {"role draft", replaced(base, "\"role\": \"pack\"", "\"role\": \"draft\"")},
      {"role vision", replaced(base, "\"role\": \"pack\"", "\"role\": \"vision\"")},
      {"incomplete shard set", replaced(base, "m-00002-of-00002.gguf", "m-00002-of-00003.gguf")},
      {"launch names an unlisted file", replaced(base, "\"native\": \"weights/m-00001-of-00002.gguf\"", "\"native\": \"weights/x.gguf\"")},
      {"single-label wildcard base", replaced(base, "*.cdn.h.example", "*.example")},
      {"fraction", replaced(base, "\"sequence\": 1,", "\"sequence\": 1.0,")},
      {"string where integer", replaced(base, "\"sequence\": 1,", "\"sequence\": \"1\",")},
      {"BOM", "\xEF\xBB\xBF" + base},
      {"P1 reserved device component", replaced(base, "pack/index.txt\"", "pack/con.txt\"")},
  };
  for (const auto& [why, text] : cases) expect(rejected(text), "manifest_invalid: " + why);
  expect(rejected(replaced(base, "\"schema_version\": 1", "\"schema_version\": 2"),
                  secure_frontend::AcquireReason::manifest_unsupported_version), "schema_version 2");
  expect(rejected(std::string(secure_frontend::kManifestMaxBytes + 1, ' ')), "larger than 1 MiB");
}

void dl03_http_url_rejected() {
  expect(rejected(replaced(base_manifest(), "https://h.example/a", "http://h.example/a")), "an http source is manifest_invalid");
}

void manifest_identity_is_sha256_of_exact_bytes() {
  const std::string text = base_manifest();
  const auto parsed = std::get<secure_frontend::ParsedManifest>(secure_frontend::parse_manifest(text, {}));
  expect(parsed.sha256 == secure_frontend::to_hex(secure_frontend::sha256(text)), "digest of the exact bytes");
  const auto spaced = std::get<secure_frontend::ParsedManifest>(secure_frontend::parse_manifest(text + " ", {}));
  expect(spaced.sha256 != parsed.sha256, "nothing is re-serialized: one more byte is another manifest");
  const auto upper = replaced(text, "\"name\": \"m\"", "\"name\": \"M\"");
  const auto lowered = std::get<secure_frontend::ParsedManifest>(secure_frontend::parse_manifest(upper, {}));
  expect(lowered.manifest.name == "m", "name ASCII-lowercased on input");
}

std::string read_text(const std::filesystem::path& path) {
  std::ifstream input(path, std::ios::binary);
  return std::string{std::istreambuf_iterator<char>(input), std::istreambuf_iterator<char>()};
}

// Spec §2.5: committed CATALOG.sha256 must list exactly the embedded manifests.
void dl05_catalog_pins_match_committed_list() {
  const auto& args = secure_frontend_test::extra_arguments();
  const std::filesystem::path production = args.at(0);
  const std::filesystem::path fixtures = args.at(1);
  std::string embedded_production;
  std::string embedded_fixtures;
  for (const auto& entry : secure_frontend::catalog_entries()) {
    expect(secure_frontend::to_hex(secure_frontend::sha256(entry.bytes)) == entry.sha256, "embedded digest recomputes");
    const auto parsed = secure_frontend::parse_manifest(entry.bytes, {});
    const auto* manifest = std::get_if<secure_frontend::ParsedManifest>(&parsed);
    expect(manifest != nullptr && manifest->manifest.name == entry.name && manifest->manifest.tag == entry.tag &&
               manifest->manifest.sequence == entry.sequence,
           "catalog entry parses and matches its path");
    (entry.fixture ? embedded_fixtures : embedded_production) +=
        std::string{entry.sha256} + "  " + std::string{entry.name} + "/" + std::string{entry.tag} + ".json\n";
  }
  expect(embedded_production == read_text(production), "catalog/CATALOG.sha256 matches the embedded production catalog");
  if (secure_frontend::kTestHooksEnabled) {
    expect(embedded_fixtures == read_text(fixtures), "tests/fixtures/catalog/CATALOG.sha256 matches");
  } else {
    expect(embedded_fixtures.empty(), "release builds embed no fixture");
  }
}

void engine_version_read_from_upstream_version_file() {
  const auto& args = secure_frontend_test::extra_arguments();
  expect(secure_frontend::built_engine_version_text() == args.at(0), "kEngineVersion is upstream.version without its leading v");
  expect(secure_frontend::render_engine_version(secure_frontend::built_engine_version()) == args.at(0), "parsed");
}

void trust_rules_catalog_digest_or_typed_digest() {
  using secure_frontend::AcquireReason;
  const std::string text = base_manifest();
  const std::string digest = secure_frontend::to_hex(secure_frontend::sha256(text));
  const auto untrusted = secure_frontend::trust_manifest_bytes(text, std::nullopt, {});
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(untrusted) &&
             std::get<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(untrusted).reason == AcquireReason::manifest_untrusted, "no trust");
  const auto wrong = secure_frontend::trust_manifest_bytes(text, std::string(64, '0'), {});
  expect(std::get<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(wrong).reason == AcquireReason::manifest_untrusted, "wrong typed digest");
  const auto typed = secure_frontend::trust_manifest_bytes(text, digest, {});
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::TrustedManifest>(typed) &&
             std::get<secure_frontend::TrustedManifest>(typed).origin == secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party,
         "--trust-manifest with the exact digest");
  expect(secure_frontend::parse_trust_argument("sha256:" + digest) == digest, "argument form");
  for (const auto* bad : {"sha256:ABC", "sha512:00", "", "sha256:"}) expect(!secure_frontend::parse_trust_argument(bad), bad);
#if defined(SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS)
  const auto* fixture = secure_frontend::find_catalog_entry("fixture-qwen", "default");
  expect(fixture != nullptr && fixture->tag == "t1", "alias default -> t1");
  const auto catalog = secure_frontend::trust_manifest_bytes(fixture->bytes, std::nullopt, {});
  expect(std::get<secure_frontend::TrustedManifest>(catalog).origin == secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::catalog, "catalog digest");
  const std::string shadow = replaced(replaced(text, "\"name\": \"m\"", "\"name\": \"fixture-qwen\""), "\"tag\": \"t\"", "\"tag\": \"t1\"");
  const auto shadowed = secure_frontend::trust_manifest_bytes(shadow, secure_frontend::to_hex(secure_frontend::sha256(shadow)), {});
  expect(std::get<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(shadowed).reason == AcquireReason::manifest_shadows_catalog, "shadowing");
#endif
}

// main list additions: base_manifest_parses, dl03_invalid_manifest_matrix, dl03_http_url_rejected,
//   manifest_identity_is_sha256_of_exact_bytes, dl05_catalog_pins_match_committed_list,
//   engine_version_read_from_upstream_version_file, trust_rules_catalog_digest_or_typed_digest
```

`tools/secure/test_catalog_provenance.py`:

```python
"""DL-05 contribution (SP4 spec §2.6, §10.3): every catalog repo, revision and file digest is recorded in PROVENANCE."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import re
from pathlib import Path

REPO = Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
HF = re.compile(r"https://huggingface\.co/([A-Za-z0-9._-]+/[A-Za-z0-9._-]+)/resolve/([0-9a-f]{40})/.+")


def check(catalog_dir: Path, provenance: str) -> list[str]:
    errors = []
    for path in sorted(catalog_dir.glob("*/*.json")):
        manifest = json.loads(path.read_text(encoding="ascii"))
        for entry in manifest["files"]:
            if entry["sha256"] not in provenance:
                errors.append(f"{path.relative_to(catalog_dir)}: {entry['path']} sha256 not in PROVENANCE")
            for url in entry["sources"]:
                match = HF.fullmatch(url)
                if match is None:
                    errors.append(f"{path.relative_to(catalog_dir)}: {entry['path']} source is not a pinned Hub URL")
                    continue
                for pinned in match.groups():
                    if pinned not in provenance:
                        errors.append(f"{path.relative_to(catalog_dir)}: {pinned} not in PROVENANCE")
    return errors


def test_catalog_pins_recorded_in_provenance():
    provenance = (REPO / "docs" / "PROVENANCE.md").read_text(encoding="utf-8")
    assert check(REPO / "secure_frontend" / "catalog", provenance) == []


def test_check_reports_missing_pins(tmp_path):
    (tmp_path / "m").mkdir()
    (tmp_path / "m" / "t.json").write_text(json.dumps({"files": [{
        "path": "weights/a.gguf", "sha256": "ab" * 32,
        "sources": ["https://huggingface.co/o/r/resolve/" + "c" * 40 + "/a.gguf"]}]}), encoding="ascii")
    errors = check(tmp_path, "nothing pinned here")
    assert len(errors) == 3 and "sha256 not in PROVENANCE" in errors[0]
    assert check(tmp_path, "ab" * 32 + " o/r " + "c" * 40) == []
```

`secure_frontend/cmake/SecureFrontendCatalog.cmake`:

```cmake
# SP4 §2.5: embeds every <dir>/<name>/<tag>.json manifest (exact bytes) and
# every <dir>/<name>/default.alias into a generated C++ source. Digests are
# computed here with file(SHA256); CATALOG.sha256 is checked by
# manifest.dl05_catalog_pins_match_committed_list, not here.
function(secure_frontend_generate_catalog output)
    cmake_parse_arguments(PARSE_ARGV 1 _c "" "PRODUCTION;FIXTURE" "")
    set(_defs "")
    set(_rows "")
    set(_aliases "")
    set(_count 0)
    set(_alias_count 0)
    foreach(_kind IN ITEMS PRODUCTION FIXTURE)
        set(_dir "${_c_${_kind}}")
        if(_dir STREQUAL "")
            continue()
        endif()
        if(_kind STREQUAL "FIXTURE")
            set(_fixture true)
        else()
            set(_fixture false)
        endif()
        file(GLOB _manifests RELATIVE "${_dir}" CONFIGURE_DEPENDS "${_dir}/*/*.json")
        list(SORT _manifests)
        foreach(_rel IN LISTS _manifests)
            get_filename_component(_name "${_rel}" DIRECTORY)
            get_filename_component(_tag "${_rel}" NAME_WLE)
            file(SHA256 "${_dir}/${_rel}" _digest)
            file(READ "${_dir}/${_rel}" _hex HEX)
            file(READ "${_dir}/${_rel}" _text)
            if(_hex STREQUAL "")
                message(FATAL_ERROR "catalog manifest ${_rel} is empty")
            endif()
            string(JSON _sequence ERROR_VARIABLE _err GET "${_text}" sequence)
            if(_err)
                message(FATAL_ERROR "catalog manifest ${_rel} has no sequence")
            endif()
            string(REGEX REPLACE "([0-9a-f][0-9a-f])" "0x\\1," _bytes "${_hex}")
            string(APPEND _defs "constexpr char kCatalogBytes${_count}[] = {${_bytes}};\n")
            string(APPEND _rows "    CatalogEntry{\"${_name}\", \"${_tag}\", ${_sequence}U, \"${_digest}\", "
                "std::string_view{kCatalogBytes${_count}, sizeof kCatalogBytes${_count}}, ${_fixture}},\n")
            math(EXPR _count "${_count} + 1")
        endforeach()
        file(GLOB _alias_files RELATIVE "${_dir}" CONFIGURE_DEPENDS "${_dir}/*/default.alias")
        list(SORT _alias_files)
        foreach(_rel IN LISTS _alias_files)
            get_filename_component(_name "${_rel}" DIRECTORY)
            file(STRINGS "${_dir}/${_rel}" _real LIMIT_COUNT 1)
            string(APPEND _aliases "    CatalogAlias{\"${_name}\", \"default\", \"${_real}\"},\n")
            math(EXPR _alias_count "${_alias_count} + 1")
        endforeach()
    endforeach()
    set(_content "// Generated by cmake/SecureFrontendCatalog.cmake. Do not edit.\n"
        "#include \"secure_frontend/catalog.hpp\"\n\n#include <array>\n\nnamespace secure_frontend {\nnamespace {\n"
        "${_defs}"
        "constexpr std::array<CatalogEntry, ${_count}> kEntries{{\n${_rows}}};\n"
        "constexpr std::array<CatalogAlias, ${_alias_count}> kAliases{{\n${_aliases}}};\n"
        "}  // namespace\n\n"
        "std::span<const CatalogEntry> catalog_entries() noexcept { return kEntries; }\n"
        "std::span<const CatalogAlias> catalog_aliases() noexcept { return kAliases; }\n\n"
        "}  // namespace secure_frontend\n")
    file(WRITE "${output}.tmp" "${_content}")
    configure_file("${output}.tmp" "${output}" COPYONLY)
endfunction()
```

In `CMakeLists.txt`, after the `secure_frontend_acquire_core` block:

```cmake
# SP4 §2.3 / ruling R-1: the engine version is the committed ../upstream.version
# (one line, "v<x.y.z[.n]>"). When upstream/ has a reachable exact tag it must equal
# the file (FATAL_ERROR only on a mismatch); a missing tag never fails configure.
set(SECURE_STRATA_ENGINE_VERSION "" CACHE STRING "Override x.y.z[.n]; empty = ../upstream.version")
if(SECURE_STRATA_ENGINE_VERSION STREQUAL "")
    file(STRINGS "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/../upstream.version" _sf_pinned LIMIT_COUNT 1)
    if(NOT _sf_pinned MATCHES "^v[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?$")
        message(FATAL_ERROR "upstream.version must hold one line v<x.y.z[.n]>")
    endif()
    set_property(DIRECTORY APPEND PROPERTY CMAKE_CONFIGURE_DEPENDS "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/../upstream.version")
    set(_sf_describe_rc 1)
    # Only a checked-out submodule is asked: an empty upstream/ must never let git walk up to the superproject's tags.
    if(EXISTS "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/../upstream/.git")
        execute_process(COMMAND git -C "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/../upstream" describe --tags --exact-match
            RESULT_VARIABLE _sf_describe_rc OUTPUT_VARIABLE _sf_tag ERROR_QUIET OUTPUT_STRIP_TRAILING_WHITESPACE)
    endif()
    if(_sf_describe_rc EQUAL 0 AND NOT _sf_tag STREQUAL _sf_pinned)
        message(FATAL_ERROR "upstream/ is at ${_sf_tag} but upstream.version says ${_sf_pinned}; bump upstream.version with the submodule")
    endif()
    string(SUBSTRING "${_sf_pinned}" 1 -1 SECURE_FRONTEND_ENGINE_VERSION_TEXT)
else()
    if(NOT SECURE_STRATA_ENGINE_VERSION MATCHES "^[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+\\.[0-9]+(\\.[0-9]+)?$")
        message(FATAL_ERROR "SECURE_STRATA_ENGINE_VERSION must be x.y.z or x.y.z.n")
    endif()
    set(SECURE_FRONTEND_ENGINE_VERSION_TEXT "${SECURE_STRATA_ENGINE_VERSION}")
endif()
configure_file(cmake/engine_version.hpp.in
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/generated/secure_frontend/engine_version_generated.hpp" @ONLY)

include(cmake/SecureFrontendCatalog.cmake)
set(_sf_catalog_fixture "")
if(SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS)
    set(_sf_catalog_fixture FIXTURE "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/tests/fixtures/catalog")
endif()
secure_frontend_generate_catalog("${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/generated/catalog_data.cpp"
    PRODUCTION "${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/catalog" ${_sf_catalog_fixture})

add_library(secure_frontend_model_store src/manifest_json.cpp src/catalog.cpp src/manifest_trust.cpp
    "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/generated/catalog_data.cpp")
target_compile_features(secure_frontend_model_store PUBLIC cxx_std_20)
target_include_directories(secure_frontend_model_store PUBLIC
    $<BUILD_INTERFACE:${CMAKE_CURRENT_SOURCE_DIR}/include> $<INSTALL_INTERFACE:include>)
target_include_directories(secure_frontend_model_store PRIVATE "${CMAKE_CURRENT_BINARY_DIR}/generated")
target_link_libraries(secure_frontend_model_store PUBLIC secure_frontend_manifest_core secure_frontend_json secure_frontend_paths)
secure_frontend_set_warnings(secure_frontend_model_store)
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implement** — `include/secure_frontend/manifest_json.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once

#include "secure_frontend/acquire_manifest.hpp"

#include <cstddef>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <variant>

namespace secure_frontend {

inline constexpr std::size_t kManifestMaxBytes = std::size_t{1} << 20U;
inline constexpr std::size_t kManifestMaxDepth = 6;

// Identity = SHA-256 of `bytes`, kept verbatim (never re-serialized).
struct ParsedManifest final {
  AcquireManifest manifest;
  std::string bytes;
  std::string sha256;
};

using ManifestParseResult = std::variant<ParsedManifest, ManifestIssue>;

// Strict JSON through SP1's parse_json (UTF-8, no BOM, duplicate keys and
// non-integers rejected), then: unknown keys, types, printable ASCII, the
// format/schema_version gate, the P1 path grammar, and validate_manifest().
[[nodiscard]] ManifestParseResult parse_manifest(std::string_view bytes, const ManifestRules& rules);

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/manifest_json.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/manifest_json.hpp"

#include "secure_frontend/acquire_url.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/canonical_path.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/json.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/sha256.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <initializer_list>

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {

using Members = std::vector<JsonMember>;

std::string join(const std::string& path, const std::string_view key) {
  return path.empty() ? std::string{key} : path + "." + std::string{key};
}

bool printable(const std::string_view text) {
  return std::all_of(text.begin(), text.end(), [](const char c) { return c >= 0x20 && c <= 0x7E; });
}

class Reader final {
 public:
  std::optional<ManifestIssue> issue;

  void fail(std::string key, const std::string_view why, const AcquireReason reason = AcquireReason::manifest_invalid) {
    if (!issue) issue = ManifestIssue{reason, std::move(key), why};
  }

  static const JsonValue* find(const Members& members, const std::string_view key) {
    for (const auto& member : members) {
      if (member.key == key) return &member.value;
    }
    return nullptr;
  }

  void only(const Members& members, const std::initializer_list<std::string_view> allowed, const std::string& path) {
    for (const auto& member : members) {
      if (std::find(allowed.begin(), allowed.end(), member.key) == allowed.end()) {
        fail(join(path, printable(member.key) ? member.key : std::string{"?"}), "unknown key");
        return;
      }
    }
  }

  const Members* object(const JsonValue* value, const std::string& key, const bool required = true) {
    if (value == nullptr) {
      if (required) fail(key, "is required");
      return nullptr;
    }
    const auto* members = value->if_object();
    if (members == nullptr) fail(key, "must be an object");
    return members;
  }

  std::optional<std::string> text(const Members& members, const std::string_view k, const std::string& path,
                                  const std::size_t max, const bool required = true) {
    const std::string key = join(path, k);
    const auto* value = find(members, k);
    if (value == nullptr) {
      if (required) fail(key, "is required");
      return std::nullopt;
    }
    return string_value(*value, key, max);
  }

  std::optional<std::string> string_value(const JsonValue& value, const std::string& key, const std::size_t max) {
    const auto* s = value.if_string();
    if (s == nullptr) {
      fail(key, "must be a string");
      return std::nullopt;
    }
    if (s->size() > max || !printable(*s)) {
      fail(key, "must be printable ASCII within its length limit");
      return std::nullopt;
    }
    return *s;
  }

  std::int64_t integer(const Members& members, const std::string_view k, const std::string& path) {
    const std::string key = join(path, k);
    const auto* value = find(members, k);
    const auto* number = value != nullptr ? value->if_integer() : nullptr;
    if (number == nullptr) {
      fail(key, value == nullptr ? "is required" : "must be an integer");
      return 0;
    }
    return *number;
  }

  bool boolean(const Members& members, const std::string_view k, const std::string& path) {
    const std::string key = join(path, k);
    const auto* value = find(members, k);
    const auto* flag = value != nullptr ? value->if_boolean() : nullptr;
    if (flag == nullptr) {
      fail(key, value == nullptr ? "is required" : "must be true or false");
      return false;
    }
    return *flag;
  }

  std::vector<std::string> texts(const Members& members, const std::string_view k, const std::string& path,
                                 const std::size_t max_items, const std::size_t max_bytes, const bool required = true) {
    const std::string key = join(path, k);
    const auto* value = find(members, k);
    std::vector<std::string> out;
    if (value == nullptr) {
      if (required) fail(key, "is required");
      return out;
    }
    const auto* items = value->if_array();
    if (items == nullptr) {
      fail(key, "must be an array of strings");
      return out;
    }
    if (items->size() > max_items) {
      fail(key, "has too many entries");
      return out;
    }
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < items->size(); ++i) {
      if (auto s = string_value((*items)[i], key + "[" + std::to_string(i) + "]", max_bytes)) out.push_back(std::move(*s));
    }
    return out;
  }
};

ManifestLicense read_license(Reader& r, const Members& members, const std::string& path) {
  r.only(members, {"id", "url", "bundle_allowed", "notice"}, path);
  ManifestLicense license;
  license.id = r.text(members, "id", path, 128).value_or("");
  license.url = r.text(members, "url", path, kManifestMaxStringBytes).value_or("");
  license.bundle_allowed = r.boolean(members, "bundle_allowed", path);
  license.notice = r.text(members, "notice", path, kManifestMaxNoticeBytes, false).value_or("");
  return license;
}

}  // namespace

ManifestParseResult parse_manifest(const std::string_view bytes, const ManifestRules& rules) {
  if (bytes.size() > kManifestMaxBytes) return ManifestIssue{AcquireReason::manifest_invalid, "manifest", "is larger than 1 MiB"};
  auto parsed = parse_json(bytes, JsonLimits{kManifestMaxBytes, kManifestMaxDepth, kManifestMaxFiles, kMaxSourceUrlBytes});
  if (const auto* error = std::get_if<JsonError>(&parsed)) {
    return ManifestIssue{AcquireReason::manifest_invalid, "manifest", describe_json_error(error->code)};
  }
  const JsonValue& root = std::get<JsonValue>(parsed);
  Reader r;
  const auto* top = r.object(&root, "manifest");
  if (top == nullptr) return *r.issue;
  r.only(*top, {"format", "schema_version", "name", "tag", "version", "sequence", "profile", "engine", "license", "hosts",
                "redirect_hosts", "files", "launch"}, "");
  if (r.text(*top, "format", "", 64).value_or("") != "strata-model-manifest") r.fail("format", "must be strata-model-manifest");
  if (r.issue) return *r.issue;
  if (r.integer(*top, "schema_version", "") != 1) {
    if (!r.issue) r.fail("schema_version", "is not supported by this release", AcquireReason::manifest_unsupported_version);
    return *r.issue;
  }
  AcquireManifest m;
  m.name = ascii_lower(r.text(*top, "name", "", 128).value_or(""));
  m.tag = ascii_lower(r.text(*top, "tag", "", 128).value_or(""));
  m.version = r.text(*top, "version", "", 32).value_or("");
  const auto sequence = r.integer(*top, "sequence", "");
  m.sequence = sequence >= 1 && sequence <= 0x7FFFFFFF ? static_cast<std::uint32_t>(sequence) : 0U;
  const auto profile = r.text(*top, "profile", "", 16).value_or("");
  if (profile == "qwen") m.profile = ManifestProfile::qwen;
  else if (profile == "glm53") m.profile = ManifestProfile::glm53;
  else if (profile == "raw") m.profile = ManifestProfile::raw;
  else r.fail("profile", "must be qwen or glm53");
  if (const auto* engine = r.object(Reader::find(*top, "engine"), "engine")) {
    r.only(*engine, {"family", "min_version"}, "engine");
    const auto family = r.text(*engine, "family", "engine", 16).value_or("");
    if (family == "strata") m.family = EngineFamily::strata;
    else if (family == "glm") m.family = EngineFamily::glm;
    else if (family == "none") m.family = EngineFamily::none;
    else r.fail("engine.family", "must be strata, glm or none");
    const auto version = parse_engine_version(r.text(*engine, "min_version", "engine", 32).value_or(""));
    if (!version) r.fail("engine.min_version", "must be MAJOR.MINOR.PATCH[.N]");
    else m.min_version = *version;
  }
  if (const auto* license = r.object(Reader::find(*top, "license"), "license")) m.license = read_license(r, *license, "license");
  m.hosts = r.texts(*top, "hosts", "", kManifestMaxHosts, 253);
  m.redirect_hosts = r.texts(*top, "redirect_hosts", "", kManifestMaxHosts, 255, false);
  const auto* files = Reader::find(*top, "files");
  const auto* file_items = files != nullptr ? files->if_array() : nullptr;
  if (file_items == nullptr) r.fail("files", "must be an array");
  for (std::size_t i = 0; file_items != nullptr && i < file_items->size() && !r.issue; ++i) {
    const std::string at = "files[" + std::to_string(i) + "]";
    const auto* f = r.object(&(*file_items)[i], at);
    if (f == nullptr) break;
    r.only(*f, {"path", "role", "size", "sha256", "sources", "license"}, at);
    ManifestFile file;
    file.path = r.text(*f, "path", at, kManifestMaxStringBytes).value_or("");
    const auto role = r.text(*f, "role", at, 16).value_or("");
    if (role == "weights") file.role = FileRole::weights;
    else if (role == "pack") file.role = FileRole::pack;
    else if (role == "tokenizer") file.role = FileRole::tokenizer;
    else r.fail(at + ".role", role == "vision" || role == "draft" ? "is reserved and rejected in schema 1"
                                                                  : "must be weights, pack or tokenizer");
    const auto size = r.integer(*f, "size", at);
    file.size = size > 0 ? static_cast<std::uint64_t>(size) : 0U;
    file.sha256 = r.text(*f, "sha256", at, 64).value_or("");
    file.sources = r.texts(*f, "sources", at, kManifestMaxSources, kMaxSourceUrlBytes);
    if (const auto* license = r.object(Reader::find(*f, "license"), at + ".license", false)) {
      file.license = read_license(r, *license, at + ".license");
    }
    if (!r.issue) {
      const auto relative = parse_relative_path(file.path);
      const auto* path = std::get_if<RelativePath>(&relative);
      if (path == nullptr || path->generic() != file.path) r.fail(at + ".path", "must be a portable relative path (P1)");
    }
    m.files.push_back(std::move(file));
  }
  if (const auto* launch = r.object(Reader::find(*top, "launch"), "launch", false)) {
    r.only(*launch, {"native", "ple_gguf", "pack", "tokenizer_pack"}, "launch");
    m.launch.native = r.text(*launch, "native", "launch", kManifestMaxStringBytes, false);
    m.launch.ple_gguf = r.text(*launch, "ple_gguf", "launch", kManifestMaxStringBytes, false);
    m.launch.pack = r.text(*launch, "pack", "launch", kManifestMaxStringBytes, false);
    m.launch.tokenizer_pack = r.text(*launch, "tokenizer_pack", "launch", kManifestMaxStringBytes, false);
  }
  if (r.issue) return *r.issue;
  if (const auto issue = validate_manifest(m, rules)) return *issue;
  return ParsedManifest{std::move(m), std::string{bytes}, to_hex(sha256(bytes))};
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

```cpp
#pragma once

#include "secure_frontend/engine_version.hpp"

#include <cstdint>
#include <span>

namespace secure_frontend {

// Spec §2.5 rule 1: manifests compiled into the strata binary. `fixture`
// entries exist only in SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS builds.
struct CatalogEntry final {
  std::string_view name;
  std::string_view tag;
  std::uint32_t sequence;
  std::string_view sha256;
  std::string_view bytes;
  bool fixture;
};

struct CatalogAlias final {
  std::string_view name;
  std::string_view alias;  // "default"
  std::string_view tag;
};

[[nodiscard]] std::span<const CatalogEntry> catalog_entries() noexcept;  // generated
[[nodiscard]] std::span<const CatalogAlias> catalog_aliases() noexcept;  // generated
[[nodiscard]] const CatalogEntry* find_catalog_entry(std::string_view name, std::string_view tag) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] const CatalogEntry* find_catalog_by_digest(std::string_view sha256) noexcept;

// kEngineVersion (spec §2.3): the committed upstream.version, checked at configure time against a reachable submodule tag.
[[nodiscard]] std::string_view built_engine_version_text() noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] const EngineVersion& built_engine_version() noexcept;

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/catalog.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/catalog.hpp"

#include "secure_frontend/engine_version_generated.hpp"

namespace secure_frontend {

const CatalogEntry* find_catalog_entry(const std::string_view name, std::string_view tag) noexcept {
  if (tag == "default") {
    const CatalogAlias* alias = nullptr;
    for (const auto& a : catalog_aliases()) {
      if (a.name == name && a.alias == "default") alias = &a;
    }
    if (alias == nullptr) return nullptr;
    tag = alias->tag;
  }
  for (const auto& entry : catalog_entries()) {
    if (entry.name == name && entry.tag == tag) return &entry;
  }
  return nullptr;
}

const CatalogEntry* find_catalog_by_digest(const std::string_view sha256) noexcept {
  for (const auto& entry : catalog_entries()) {
    if (entry.sha256 == sha256) return &entry;
  }
  return nullptr;
}

std::string_view built_engine_version_text() noexcept { return generated::kEngineVersionText; }

const EngineVersion& built_engine_version() noexcept {
  // Validated by CMake's regex at configure time, so the parse cannot fail.
  static const EngineVersion version = parse_engine_version(generated::kEngineVersionText).value_or(EngineVersion{});
  return version;
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

```cpp
#pragma once

#include "secure_frontend/acquire_reason.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/manifest_json.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/model_index_model.hpp"

#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <variant>

namespace secure_frontend {

struct TrustedManifest final {
  ParsedManifest parsed;
  ModelOrigin origin;
};

// "--trust-manifest sha256:<64 lowercase hex>" → the hex, or nullopt.
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<std::string> parse_trust_argument(std::string_view text);

// Spec §2.5 / D2 / D7: trusted iff the digest is in this binary's catalog, or
// equals the operator-typed digest (then it must not shadow a catalog name:tag).
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<TrustedManifest, AcquireError> trust_manifest_bytes(
    std::string_view bytes, const std::optional<std::string>& trusted_digest, const ManifestRules& rules);

// name[:tag] (tag "default" when absent) → the catalog manifest, or model_unknown.
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<TrustedManifest, AcquireError> resolve_catalog_manifest(const ModelReference& reference,
                                                                                   const ManifestRules& rules);

[[nodiscard]] AcquireError issue_to_error(const ManifestIssue& issue);

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/manifest_trust.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/manifest_trust.hpp"

#include "secure_frontend/catalog.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/sha256.hpp"

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {

std::variant<TrustedManifest, AcquireError> parse_trusted(const std::string_view bytes, const ManifestRules& rules,
                                                          const ModelOrigin origin) {
  auto parsed = parse_manifest(bytes, rules);
  if (const auto* issue = std::get_if<ManifestIssue>(&parsed)) return issue_to_error(*issue);
  return TrustedManifest{std::get<ParsedManifest>(std::move(parsed)), origin};
}

}  // namespace

AcquireError issue_to_error(const ManifestIssue& issue) {
  return AcquireError{issue.reason, issue.key_path + ": " + std::string{issue.message}};
}

std::optional<std::string> parse_trust_argument(const std::string_view text) {
  constexpr std::string_view kPrefix = "sha256:";
  if (!text.starts_with(kPrefix) || !is_lower_hex_digest(text.substr(kPrefix.size()))) return std::nullopt;
  return std::string{text.substr(kPrefix.size())};
}

std::variant<TrustedManifest, AcquireError> trust_manifest_bytes(const std::string_view bytes,
                                                                 const std::optional<std::string>& trusted_digest,
                                                                 const ManifestRules& rules) {
  const std::string digest = to_hex(sha256(bytes));
  if (find_catalog_by_digest(digest) != nullptr) return parse_trusted(bytes, rules, ModelOrigin::catalog);
  if (!trusted_digest || *trusted_digest != digest) {
    return AcquireError{AcquireReason::manifest_untrusted, "manifest sha256 " + digest};
  }
  auto trusted = parse_trusted(bytes, rules, ModelOrigin::third_party);
  if (const auto* manifest = std::get_if<TrustedManifest>(&trusted)) {
    const auto& m = manifest->parsed.manifest;
    if (find_catalog_entry(m.name, m.tag) != nullptr) {
      return AcquireError{AcquireReason::manifest_shadows_catalog, model_ref_text(m.name, m.tag)};
    }
  }
  return trusted;
}

std::variant<TrustedManifest, AcquireError> resolve_catalog_manifest(const ModelReference& reference,
                                                                     const ManifestRules& rules) {
  const auto* entry = find_catalog_entry(reference.name, reference.tag.value_or("default"));
  if (entry == nullptr) {
    return AcquireError{AcquireReason::model_unknown, reference.name + (reference.tag ? ":" + *reference.tag : std::string{})};
  }
  return parse_trusted(entry->bytes, rules, ModelOrigin::catalog);
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/canonical_path.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/model_index_store.hpp"
#include "support/test_support.hpp"

// in the anonymous namespace:
void make_dirs(const std::filesystem::path& directory) {
  if (directory.empty() || std::filesystem::exists(directory)) return;
  make_dirs(directory.parent_path());
  secure_frontend_test::make_private_directory(directory);
}

void put(const std::filesystem::path& path, const std::string_view bytes) {
  make_dirs(path.parent_path());
  secure_frontend_test::write_file(path, bytes);
}

// Spec §4.9 delta b.
void scan_tolerates_repeated_shard_names_across_versions() {
  const auto root_path = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  put(root_path / "m" / "t" / "v1-aaaaaaaaaaaa" / "weights" / "m-00001-of-00001.gguf", "one");
  put(root_path / "m" / "t" / "v2-bbbbbbbbbbbb" / "weights" / "m-00001-of-00001.gguf", "two!");
  put(root_path / ".staging" / std::string(32, 'c') / "weights" / "m-00001-of-00001.gguf.part", "x");
  auto opened = secure_frontend::open_root(root_path);
```

In `tests/typed_config_test.cpp` (SP1's `secure_frontend_typed_config_tests`; ruling R-3). Its file already defines a three-argument `has_issue`; the helpers below are named apart from it:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/frontend_config.hpp"
#include "support/test_support.hpp"

#include <functional>

// in the anonymous namespace:
// A minimal valid draft over scratch roots; validate() opens them.
secure_frontend::FrontendConfigDraft pull_minimal_draft(const std::filesystem::path& scratch) {
  using secure_frontend::ConfigDraftEntry;
  using secure_frontend::ConfigSource;
  secure_frontend_test::make_private_directory(scratch / "models");
  secure_frontend_test::make_private_directory(scratch / "tokenizer");
  secure_frontend_test::make_private_directory(scratch / "bin");
  secure_frontend_test::write_file(scratch / "bin" / "engine", "#!/bin/sh\n", 0700);
  secure_frontend::FrontendConfigDraft draft;
  draft.entries["roots.models"] = ConfigDraftEntry{(scratch / "models").string(), ConfigSource::file};
  draft.entries["roots.tokenizer_pack"] = ConfigDraftEntry{(scratch / "tokenizer").string(), ConfigSource::file};
  draft.entries["engine.executable"] = ConfigDraftEntry{std::filesystem::canonical(scratch / "bin" / "engine").string(), ConfigSource::file};
  draft.entries["engine.model_id"] = ConfigDraftEntry{std::string{"m"}, ConfigSource::file};
  draft.entries["token.source"] = ConfigDraftEntry{std::string{"env:STRATA_TEST_TOKEN"}, ConfigSource::file};
  return draft;
}

bool has_issue_for_key(const secure_frontend::ConfigValidationResult& result, const std::string& key) {
  const auto* issues = std::get_if<secure_frontend::ConfigErrors>(&result);
  if (issues == nullptr) return false;
  for (const auto& issue : *issues) {
    if (issue.key_path == key) return true;
  }
  return false;
}

// Spec §4.9 delta e: every pull.* key, table-driven like CFG-01.
void cfg01_pull_keys_table_driven() {
  using Value = secure_frontend::ConfigDraftValue;
  struct Row final {
    std::string key;
    Value good;
    std::vector<Value> bad;
  };
  const std::vector<Row> rows{
      {"pull.downloader", Value{std::string{"/opt/curl/bin/curl"}}, {Value{std::string{"curl"}}, Value{std::int64_t{1}}}},
      {"pull.proxy", Value{std::string{"http://proxy.corp:3128"}},
       {Value{std::string{"http://u:p@proxy.corp:3128"}}, Value{std::string{"socks5://p:1"}}, Value{std::string{"http://p"}}}},
      {"pull.allow_private_addresses", Value{true}, {Value{std::string{"yes"}}}},
      {"pull.max_total_bytes", Value{std::int64_t{1} << 20}, {Value{(std::int64_t{1} << 20) - 1}, Value{(std::int64_t{1} << 44) + 1}}},
      {"pull.max_duration_s", Value{std::int64_t{60}}, {Value{std::int64_t{59}}, Value{std::int64_t{604801}}}},
      {"pull.stall_timeout_s", Value{std::int64_t{3600}}, {Value{std::int64_t{9}}, Value{std::int64_t{3601}}}},
      {"pull.connect_timeout_s", Value{std::int64_t{1}}, {Value{std::int64_t{0}}, Value{std::int64_t{301}}}},
  };
  for (const auto& row : rows) {
    const auto scratch = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
    auto draft = pull_minimal_draft(scratch);
    draft.entries[row.key] = secure_frontend::ConfigDraftEntry{row.good, secure_frontend::ConfigSource::file};
    expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::ValidatedConfig>(secure_frontend::validate(draft)), row.key + " accepts a good value");
    for (const auto& bad : row.bad) {
      draft.entries[row.key] = secure_frontend::ConfigDraftEntry{bad, secure_frontend::ConfigSource::file};
      expect(has_issue_for_key(secure_frontend::validate(draft), row.key), row.key + " rejects a bad value");
    }
  }
  const auto scratch = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  auto defaults = pull_minimal_draft(scratch);
  defaults.entries["pull.downloader"] = secure_frontend::ConfigDraftEntry{std::string{"/nonexistent/curl"}, secure_frontend::ConfigSource::file};
  const auto validated = secure_frontend::validate(defaults);
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::ValidatedConfig>(validated),
         "pull.downloader is validated lazily: a missing file does not fail validation (CFG-01 exception)");
  if (const auto* config = std::get_if<secure_frontend::ValidatedConfig>(&validated)) {
    const auto& pull = config->get().pull;
    expect(pull.max_total_bytes == (std::uint64_t{1} << 40U) && pull.max_duration.count() == 172800 &&
               pull.stall_timeout.count() == 120 && pull.connect_timeout.count() == 30 && !pull.allow_private_addresses &&
               !pull.proxy.has_value(),
           "spec §3.9 defaults");
    expect(secure_frontend::describe(config->get()).find("pull.max_total_bytes = 1099511627776\n") != std::string::npos,
           "--check lists the pull keys");
  }
}
```

```cpp
  std::uint64_t size;
};

// Spec §4.9 delta b: every regular file below roots.models with its size, for
// list --all and doctor. Derives no model IDs, so the same shard name in
// current, retired and orphaned versions is fine. Links/junctions and other
// entries are skipped and never followed. Sorted by path.
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<std::vector<ModelRootFile>, PathError> scan_model_root_files(const CanonicalRoot& root);
```

`src/model_index_store.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/model_index_store.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <optional>

namespace secure_frontend {

std::variant<std::vector<ModelRootFile>, PathError> scan_model_root_files(const CanonicalRoot& root) {
  std::vector<ModelRootFile> files;
  std::vector<std::optional<RelativePath>> pending{std::nullopt};
  while (!pending.empty()) {
    const auto directory = std::move(pending.back());
    pending.pop_back();
    auto listed = list_directory(root, directory);
    if (auto* failure = std::get_if<PathError>(&listed)) return *failure;
    for (const auto& entry : std::get<std::vector<DirectoryEntry>>(listed)) {
      const std::string text = directory ? directory->generic() + "/" + entry.name : entry.name;
      auto relative = parse_relative_path(text);
      if (!std::holds_alternative<RelativePath>(relative)) continue;  // not P1-portable: not ours
      if (entry.type == EntryType::directory) pending.emplace_back(std::get<RelativePath>(std::move(relative)));
      else if (entry.type == EntryType::regular_file) files.push_back(ModelRootFile{text, entry.size});
    }
  }
  std::sort(files.begin(), files.end(), [](const ModelRootFile& a, const ModelRootFile& b) { return a.path < b.path; });
  return files;
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`parse_relative_path` rejects a leading `.`? It does not (P1 rejects `.`/`..` only), so `.staging` is scanned, as `list --all` needs.

```cpp
std::filesystem::path default_downloader_path() {
#if defined(_WIN32)

  const UINT length = GetSystemDirectoryW(system.data(), static_cast<UINT>(system.size()));

  return std::filesystem::path(system) / L"curl.exe";
#else

#endif
}
```

```cpp
#include "acquire_cases.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/models_store.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/sha256.hpp"
#include "support/expect.hpp"
#include "support/test_support.hpp"

#include <variant>

#if !defined(_WIN32)
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#endif

namespace acquire_cases {
namespace {

using secure_frontend::must_rel;
using secure_frontend_test::expect;

secure_frontend::CanonicalRoot scratch_root() {
  auto opened = secure_frontend::open_root(secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory());
  return std::get<secure_frontend::CanonicalRoot>(std::move(opened));
}

class TestEnvironment final : public secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment {
 public:
  std::uint64_t free{std::uint64_t{1} << 40U};
  secure_frontend::FileSystemKind kind{secure_frontend::FileSystemKind::other};
  bool fallback{false};
  std::optional<std::uint64_t> fail_after;
  std::variant<std::uint64_t, secure_frontend::AcquireError> free_bytes(const secure_frontend::CanonicalRoot&) const override { return free; }
  std::variant<secure_frontend::FileSystemKind, secure_frontend::AcquireError> file_system_kind(
      const secure_frontend::CanonicalRoot&) const override { return kind; }
  bool force_rename_fallback() const override { return fallback; }
  std::optional<std::uint64_t> fail_writes_after_bytes() const override { return fail_after; }
};

}  // namespace

void concurrent_pull_same_manifest_second_is_models_busy() {
  const auto root = scratch_root();
  auto first = secure_frontend::lock_models_root(root);
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::ModelsLock>(first), "first verb takes .lock");
  auto second = secure_frontend::lock_models_root(root);
  const auto* busy = std::get_if<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(&second);
  expect(busy != nullptr && busy->reason == secure_frontend::AcquireReason::models_busy, "second verb exits 7 models_busy");
  first = secure_frontend::AcquireError{secure_frontend::AcquireReason::usage_error, ""};  // releases the lock
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::ModelsLock>(secure_frontend::lock_models_root(root)), "released on destruction");
}

void part_file_create_append_resume_truncate() {
  const auto root = scratch_root();
  const secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment env;
  expect(!secure_frontend::ensure_directory(root, must_rel(".staging/k/weights")), "directories created within the root");
  const auto part = must_rel(".staging/k/weights/a.gguf.part");
  {
    auto created = secure_frontend::open_part_file(root, part, secure_frontend::PartOpen::create_new, env);
    auto& file = std::get<secure_frontend::PartFile>(created);
    expect(!file.preallocate(1000), "preallocation succeeds or is skipped");
    expect(file.size() == 0, "KEEP_SIZE: preallocation does not change the length");
    expect(!file.append("hello") && file.size() == 5, "append");
    expect(!file.sync(), "fsync");
  }
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(
             secure_frontend::open_part_file(root, part, secure_frontend::PartOpen::create_new, env)),
         "create_new refuses an existing file");
  auto reopened = secure_frontend::open_part_file(root, part, secure_frontend::PartOpen::open_existing, env);
  auto& file = std::get<secure_frontend::PartFile>(reopened);
  expect(file.size() == 5, "resume sees the length");
  expect(!file.append(" world") && file.size() == 11, "appends at the end");
  expect(!file.truncate_to_zero() && file.size() == 0, "restart from byte 0");
  file.close();
#if !defined(_WIN32)
  struct stat info {};
  expect(::stat(root.join(part).c_str(), &info) == 0 && (info.st_mode & 0777) == 0600, "part files are 0600");
#endif
}

void rename_within_never_replaces() {
  const auto root = scratch_root();
  const secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment env;
  expect(!secure_frontend::write_new_file(root, must_rel("a"), "A") && !secure_frontend::write_new_file(root, must_rel("b"), "B"), "files");
  const auto refused = secure_frontend::rename_within(root, must_rel("a"), must_rel("b"), env);
  expect(refused && refused->reason == secure_frontend::AcquireReason::publish_failed, "an existing target is never replaced");
  expect(std::get<std::string>(secure_frontend::read_small_file(root, must_rel("b"), 10)) == "B", "target intact");
  expect(!secure_frontend::rename_within(root, must_rel("a"), must_rel("c"), env), "rename to a free name");
  expect(!secure_frontend::exists_within(root, must_rel("a")) && secure_frontend::exists_within(root, must_rel("c")), "moved");
}

void rename_fallback_never_replaces() {
  const auto root = scratch_root();
  TestEnvironment env;
  env.fallback = true;
  expect(!secure_frontend::ensure_directory(root, must_rel("src/inner")), "dir");
  expect(!secure_frontend::write_new_file(root, must_rel("dst"), "taken"), "target");
  expect(secure_frontend::rename_within(root, must_rel("src"), must_rel("dst"), env).has_value(), "fallback refuses an existing target");
  expect(!secure_frontend::rename_within(root, must_rel("src"), must_rel("moved"), env), "fallback renames a directory");
  expect(secure_frontend::exists_within(root, must_rel("moved/inner")), "directory moved with its content");
}

void remove_tree_removes_links_without_following() {
  const auto root = scratch_root();
  const auto outside = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  secure_frontend_test::write_file(outside / "keep.txt", "keep");
  expect(!secure_frontend::ensure_directory(root, must_rel("t/sub")), "dirs");
  expect(!secure_frontend::write_new_file(root, must_rel("t/sub/f"), "x"), "file");
#if !defined(_WIN32)
  std::filesystem::create_directory_symlink(outside, root.join(must_rel("t/link")));
#endif
  expect(!secure_frontend::remove_tree_within(root, must_rel("t")), "tree removed bottom-up");
  expect(!secure_frontend::exists_within(root, must_rel("t")), "gone");
  expect(std::filesystem::exists(outside / "keep.txt"), "a link's target is never touched");
  expect(!secure_frontend::remove_tree_within(root, must_rel("t")), "absent is fine");
}

void hash_into_rejects_hard_links_and_counts_bytes() {
  const auto root = scratch_root();
  expect(!secure_frontend::write_new_file(root, must_rel("f"), "abc"), "file");
  secure_frontend::Sha256 hasher;
  const auto counted = secure_frontend::hash_into(root, must_rel("f"), hasher, 10);
  expect(std::get<std::uint64_t>(counted) == 3 && hasher.finish() == secure_frontend::sha256("abc"), "bytes hashed");
  secure_frontend::Sha256 limited;
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(secure_frontend::hash_into(root, must_rel("f"), limited, 2)),
         "a file longer than max_bytes is refused");
#if !defined(_WIN32)
  ::link(root.join(must_rel("f")).c_str(), root.join(must_rel("g")).c_str());
  secure_frontend::Sha256 linked;
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(secure_frontend::hash_into(root, must_rel("g"), linked, 10)),
         "a hard-linked file is refused");
  const auto listed = std::get<std::vector<secure_frontend::StagedEntry>>(secure_frontend::list_files_recursive(root));
  expect(listed.size() == 2 && !listed[0].regular && !listed[1].regular, "hard-linked files are listed as not regular");
#endif
}

void store_environment_seams_report_space_and_fs_kind() {
  const auto root = scratch_root();
  const secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment real;
  expect(std::get<std::uint64_t>(real.free_bytes(root)) > 0, "statvfs / GetDiskFreeSpaceExW");
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::FileSystemKind>(real.file_system_kind(root)), "statfs / volume information");
  TestEnvironment env;
  env.fail_after = 4;
  expect(!secure_frontend::ensure_directory(root, must_rel("d")), "dir");
  auto created = secure_frontend::open_part_file(root, must_rel("d/x.part"), secure_frontend::PartOpen::create_new, env);
  auto& file = std::get<secure_frontend::PartFile>(created);
  expect(!file.append("1234"), "within the simulated space");
  const auto full = file.append("5");
  expect(full && full->reason == secure_frontend::AcquireReason::disk_space && file.size() == 4, "ENOSPC keeps the .part");
}

}  // namespace acquire_cases
```

```cpp
#pragma once

#include "secure_frontend/acquire_reason.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/canonical_path.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/sha256.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/staging_plan.hpp"

#include <cstddef>
#include <cstdint>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <variant>
#include <vector>

namespace secure_frontend {

inline constexpr std::uint64_t kFreeSpaceMarginBytes = std::uint64_t{1} << 30U;
inline constexpr std::uint64_t kFreeSpaceRecheckBytes = std::uint64_t{1} << 30U;

enum class FileSystemKind { other, fat };

// Spec §10.1 seams. Production code uses this class as is.
class StoreEnvironment {
 public:
  virtual ~StoreEnvironment() = default;
  [[nodiscard]] virtual std::variant<std::uint64_t, AcquireError> free_bytes(const CanonicalRoot& root) const;
  [[nodiscard]] virtual std::variant<FileSystemKind, AcquireError> file_system_kind(const CanonicalRoot& root) const;
  [[nodiscard]] virtual bool force_rename_fallback() const { return false; }
  [[nodiscard]] virtual std::optional<std::uint64_t> fail_writes_after_bytes() const { return std::nullopt; }
  [[nodiscard]] virtual std::uint64_t fat_max_file_bytes() const { return 0xFFFFFFFFULL; }
  // §4.6: free space is re-checked against kFreeSpaceMarginBytes every this many bytes written.
  [[nodiscard]] virtual std::uint64_t recheck_interval_bytes() const { return kFreeSpaceRecheckBytes; }
};

// For names this code builds itself (".staging/<key>", "<name>/<tag>/v…").
// Throws std::logic_error if `text` is not a P1 relative path.
[[nodiscard]] RelativePath must_rel(std::string_view text);
[[nodiscard]] RelativePath join_rel(const RelativePath& base, std::string_view more);

class ModelsLock final {
 public:
  explicit ModelsLock(NativeHandle handle) noexcept : handle_(handle) {}
  ModelsLock(ModelsLock&& other) noexcept : handle_(other.handle_), owned_(other.owned_) { other.owned_ = false; }
  ModelsLock& operator=(ModelsLock&& other) noexcept;
  ModelsLock(const ModelsLock&) = delete;
  ModelsLock& operator=(const ModelsLock&) = delete;
  ~ModelsLock();

 private:
  NativeHandle handle_;
  bool owned_{true};
};

// roots.models/.lock (0600 / private DACL), exclusive, non-blocking (§4.8).
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<ModelsLock, AcquireError> lock_models_root(const CanonicalRoot& root);

class PartFile final {
 public:
  PartFile(NativeHandle handle, std::uint64_t size, std::string path, std::optional<std::uint64_t> fail_after) noexcept
      : handle_(handle), size_(size), path_(std::move(path)), fail_after_(fail_after) {}
  PartFile(PartFile&& other) noexcept;
  PartFile& operator=(PartFile&& other) = delete;
  PartFile(const PartFile&) = delete;
  PartFile& operator=(const PartFile&) = delete;
  ~PartFile();
  [[nodiscard]] std::uint64_t size() const noexcept { return size_; }
  [[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireError> append(std::string_view bytes);
  [[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireError> truncate_to_zero();
  // fallocate(FALLOC_FL_KEEP_SIZE) / FileAllocationInfo: never changes size(); unsupported = success.
  [[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireError> preallocate(std::uint64_t total_size);
  [[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireError> sync();
  void close() noexcept;

 private:
  NativeHandle handle_;
  std::uint64_t size_;
  std::string path_;  // relative, for error details
  std::optional<std::uint64_t> fail_after_;
  bool open_{true};
};

enum class PartOpen { create_new, open_existing };
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<PartFile, AcquireError> open_part_file(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path,
                                                                  PartOpen mode, const StoreEnvironment& env);

[[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireError> ensure_directory(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path);
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireError> make_read_only_directory(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path);
// Never replaces; renameat2(RENAME_NOREPLACE), else fstatat-absent + renameat (caller holds .lock);
// Windows MoveFileExW(WRITE_THROUGH) with sharing-violation retries.
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireError> rename_within(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& from,
                                                        const RelativePath& to, const StoreEnvironment& env);
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireError> remove_file_within(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path);
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireError> remove_tree_within(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path);
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireError> sync_directory(const CanonicalRoot& root, const std::optional<RelativePath>& path);
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireError> write_new_file(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path, std::string_view bytes);
// §4.1 atomic_replace_within: SP1's write_private_file(…, TokenStoreMode::replace).
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireError> replace_small_file(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path,
                                                             std::string_view bytes);
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<std::uint64_t, AcquireError> hash_into(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path,
                                                                  Sha256& hasher, std::uint64_t max_bytes);
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<std::string, AcquireError> read_small_file(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path,
                                                                      std::size_t max_bytes);
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<std::vector<StagedEntry>, AcquireError> list_files_recursive(const CanonicalRoot& root,
                                                                                        const RelativePath& dir);
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<std::vector<StagedEntry>, AcquireError> list_files_recursive(const CanonicalRoot& root);
[[nodiscard]] bool exists_within(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path);

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/models_store_common.cpp` (platform-independent parts):

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/models_store.hpp"

#include "secure_frontend/token_store.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <stdexcept>

namespace secure_frontend {

RelativePath must_rel(const std::string_view text) {
  auto parsed = parse_relative_path(text);
  if (auto* path = std::get_if<RelativePath>(&parsed)) return std::move(*path);
  throw std::logic_error("internal relative path is not P1-valid");
}

RelativePath join_rel(const RelativePath& base, const std::string_view more) { return must_rel(base.generic() + "/" + std::string{more}); }

std::optional<AcquireError> replace_small_file(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path, const std::string_view bytes) {
  if (const auto failure = write_private_file(root, path, bytes, TokenStoreMode::replace)) {
    return AcquireError{AcquireReason::index_write_failed, path.generic() + " (" + failure->message + ")"};
  }
  return std::nullopt;
}

std::variant<std::uint64_t, AcquireError> hash_into(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path, Sha256& hasher,
                                                    const std::uint64_t max_bytes) {
  auto opened = open_beneath(root, path, OpenOptions{OpenKind::regular_file, FileTrust::any, true});
  if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(opened)) {
    return AcquireError{AcquireReason::publish_failed, path.generic() + ": not a single-link regular file below the root"};
  }
  const auto& file = std::get<OpenedFile>(opened);
  if (file.size() > max_bytes) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::size_exceeded, path.generic()};
  std::string buffer(kSha256ReadChunkBytes, '\0');
  std::uint64_t total = 0;
  while (true) {
    auto read = file.read_some(buffer.data(), buffer.size());
    if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(read)) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::publish_failed, path.generic() + ": read failed"};
    const auto n = std::get<std::size_t>(read);
    if (n == 0) break;
    total += n;
    if (total > max_bytes) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::size_exceeded, path.generic()};
    hasher.update(std::string_view{buffer.data(), n});
  }
  return total;
}

std::variant<std::string, AcquireError> read_small_file(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path, const std::size_t max_bytes) {
  auto opened = open_beneath(root, path, OpenOptions{OpenKind::regular_file, FileTrust::any, true});
  if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(opened)) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::index_inconsistent, path.generic() + ": cannot be opened"};
  auto bytes = std::get<OpenedFile>(opened).read_all(max_bytes);
  if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(bytes)) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::index_inconsistent, path.generic() + ": too large or unreadable"};
  return std::get<std::string>(std::move(bytes));
}

namespace {

std::variant<std::vector<StagedEntry>, AcquireError> list_from(const CanonicalRoot& root, const std::optional<RelativePath>& start) {
  std::vector<StagedEntry> out;
  std::vector<std::pair<std::optional<RelativePath>, std::string>> pending{{start, std::string{}}};
  while (!pending.empty()) {
    auto [dir, prefix] = std::move(pending.back());
    pending.pop_back();
    auto listed = list_directory(root, dir);
    if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(listed)) {
      return AcquireError{AcquireReason::publish_failed, (dir ? dir->generic() : std::string{"root"}) + ": cannot be listed"};
    }
    for (const auto& entry : std::get<std::vector<DirectoryEntry>>(listed)) {
      const std::string relative = prefix.empty() ? entry.name : prefix + "/" + entry.name;
      if (!std::holds_alternative<RelativePath>(parse_relative_path(entry.name))) {
        out.push_back(StagedEntry{relative, entry.size, false});  // not P1-portable: never ours, never descended
        continue;
      }
      if (entry.type == EntryType::directory) {
        pending.emplace_back(dir ? join_rel(*dir, entry.name) : must_rel(entry.name), relative);
        continue;
      }
      bool regular = entry.type == EntryType::regular_file;
      if (regular) {  // hard links count as not regular (§4.4 check 2, §5.2)
        const auto child = dir ? join_rel(*dir, entry.name) : must_rel(entry.name);
        regular = std::holds_alternative<OpenedFile>(open_beneath(root, child, OpenOptions{OpenKind::regular_file, FileTrust::any, true}));
      }
      out.push_back(StagedEntry{relative, entry.size, regular});
    }
  }
  std::sort(out.begin(), out.end(), [](const StagedEntry& a, const StagedEntry& b) { return a.path < b.path; });
  return out;
}

}  // namespace

std::variant<std::vector<StagedEntry>, AcquireError> list_files_recursive(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& dir) {
  return list_from(root, dir);
}

std::variant<std::vector<StagedEntry>, AcquireError> list_files_recursive(const CanonicalRoot& root) { return list_from(root, std::nullopt); }

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/models_store_posix.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/models_store.hpp"

#include <cerrno>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <sys/file.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/statvfs.h>
#include <sys/syscall.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#if defined(__linux__)
#include <linux/falloc.h>
#include <linux/magic.h>
#include <sys/vfs.h>
#endif

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {

AcquireError error(const AcquireReason reason, const std::string& detail) { return AcquireError{reason, detail}; }
AcquireError io_error(const int code, const std::string& detail) {
  return error(code == ENOSPC || code == EDQUOT ? AcquireReason::disk_space : AcquireReason::publish_failed, detail);
}

// The directory holding `path` (or the root itself), as an owned O_DIRECTORY fd.
int parent_fd(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path) {
  const auto& parts = path.components();
  if (parts.size() == 1U) return ::openat(root.handle(), ".", O_RDONLY | O_DIRECTORY | O_CLOEXEC);
  std::string parent;
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i + 1U < parts.size(); ++i) parent += (parent.empty() ? "" : "/") + parts[i];
  auto opened = open_beneath(root, must_rel(parent), OpenOptions{OpenKind::directory});
  if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(opened)) return -1;
  return ::fcntl(std::get<OpenedFile>(opened).native(), F_DUPFD_CLOEXEC, 0);
}

int dir_fd(const CanonicalRoot& root, const std::optional<RelativePath>& path) {
  if (!path) return ::openat(root.handle(), ".", O_RDONLY | O_DIRECTORY | O_CLOEXEC);
  auto opened = open_beneath(root, *path, OpenOptions{OpenKind::directory});
  if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(opened)) return -1;
  return ::fcntl(std::get<OpenedFile>(opened).native(), F_DUPFD_CLOEXEC, 0);
}

int rename_noreplace(const int from_dir, const char* from, const int to_dir, const char* to) {
#if defined(__linux__) && defined(SYS_renameat2)
  return static_cast<int>(::syscall(SYS_renameat2, from_dir, from, to_dir, to, 1U /* RENAME_NOREPLACE */));
#else
  errno = ENOSYS;
  return -1;
#endif
}

}  // namespace

std::variant<std::uint64_t, AcquireError> StoreEnvironment::free_bytes(const CanonicalRoot& root) const {
  struct statvfs info {};
  if (::fstatvfs(root.handle(), &info) != 0) return error(AcquireReason::disk_space, "free space cannot be determined");
  return static_cast<std::uint64_t>(info.f_bavail) * static_cast<std::uint64_t>(info.f_frsize);
}

std::variant<FileSystemKind, AcquireError> StoreEnvironment::file_system_kind(const CanonicalRoot& root) const {
#if defined(__linux__)
  struct statfs info {};
  if (::fstatfs(root.handle(), &info) != 0) return error(AcquireReason::bundle_layout, "file system type cannot be determined");
  return static_cast<unsigned long>(info.f_type) == MSDOS_SUPER_MAGIC ? FileSystemKind::fat : FileSystemKind::other;
#else
  (void)root;
  return FileSystemKind::other;
#endif
}

ModelsLock& ModelsLock::operator=(ModelsLock&& other) noexcept {
  if (this != &other) {
    if (owned_) ::close(handle_);
    handle_ = other.handle_;
    owned_ = other.owned_;
    other.owned_ = false;
  }
  return *this;
}

ModelsLock::~ModelsLock() {
  if (owned_) ::close(handle_);  // closing the descriptor releases the flock
}

std::variant<ModelsLock, AcquireError> lock_models_root(const CanonicalRoot& root) {
  const int fd = ::openat(root.handle(), ".lock", O_RDWR | O_CREAT | O_CLOEXEC | O_NOFOLLOW, 0600);
  if (fd < 0) return error(AcquireReason::publish_failed, ".lock cannot be opened");
  struct stat lock_info {};
  if (::fstat(fd, &lock_info) != 0 || !S_ISREG(lock_info.st_mode) || lock_info.st_uid != ::geteuid()) {
    ::close(fd);
    return error(AcquireReason::publish_failed, ".lock is not a regular file owned by you");
  }
  if (::flock(fd, LOCK_EX | LOCK_NB) != 0) {
    const int code = errno;
    ::close(fd);
    return error(code == EWOULDBLOCK ? AcquireReason::models_busy : AcquireReason::publish_failed,
                 "another pull, import, rm or update holds roots.models/.lock");
  }
  return ModelsLock{fd};
}

PartFile::PartFile(PartFile&& other) noexcept
    : handle_(other.handle_), size_(other.size_), path_(std::move(other.path_)), fail_after_(other.fail_after_), open_(other.open_) {
  other.open_ = false;
}

PartFile::~PartFile() { close(); }

void PartFile::close() noexcept {
  if (open_) ::close(handle_);
  open_ = false;
}

std::optional<AcquireError> PartFile::append(const std::string_view bytes) {
  if (fail_after_ && size_ + bytes.size() > *fail_after_) return error(AcquireReason::disk_space, path_);
  std::size_t done = 0;
  while (done < bytes.size()) {
    const ssize_t n = ::write(handle_, bytes.data() + done, bytes.size() - done);
    if (n < 0 && errno == EINTR) continue;
    if (n <= 0) return io_error(errno, path_);
    done += static_cast<std::size_t>(n);
    size_ += static_cast<std::uint64_t>(n);
  }
  return std::nullopt;
}

std::optional<AcquireError> PartFile::truncate_to_zero() {
  if (::ftruncate(handle_, 0) != 0) return io_error(errno, path_);
  size_ = 0;
  return std::nullopt;
}

std::optional<AcquireError> PartFile::preallocate(const std::uint64_t total_size) {
#if defined(__linux__)
  if (::fallocate(handle_, FALLOC_FL_KEEP_SIZE, 0, static_cast<off_t>(total_size)) != 0) {
    if (errno == EOPNOTSUPP || errno == ENOSYS || errno == EINVAL) return std::nullopt;  // unsupported FS: skipped
    return io_error(errno, path_);
  }
#else
  (void)total_size;
#endif
  return std::nullopt;
}

std::optional<AcquireError> PartFile::sync() {
  if (::fsync(handle_) != 0) return io_error(errno, path_);
  return std::nullopt;
}

std::variant<PartFile, AcquireError> open_part_file(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path, const PartOpen mode,
                                                    const StoreEnvironment& env) {
  OwnedHandle dir(parent_fd(root, path));
  if (dir.get() < 0) return error(AcquireReason::publish_failed, path.generic() + ": directory cannot be opened safely");
  const int flags = O_WRONLY | O_APPEND | O_CLOEXEC | O_NOFOLLOW | (mode == PartOpen::create_new ? O_CREAT | O_EXCL : 0);
  const int fd = ::openat(dir.get(), path.components().back().c_str(), flags, 0600);
  if (fd < 0) return io_error(errno, path.generic() + ": cannot be opened");
  struct stat info {};
  if (::fstat(fd, &info) != 0 || !S_ISREG(info.st_mode) || info.st_nlink != 1 || info.st_uid != ::geteuid()) {
    ::close(fd);
    return error(AcquireReason::publish_failed, path.generic() + ": not a private single-link regular file");
  }
  if (mode == PartOpen::create_new && ::fchmod(fd, 0600) != 0) {
    ::close(fd);
    return io_error(errno, path.generic());
  }
  return PartFile{fd, static_cast<std::uint64_t>(info.st_size), path.generic(), env.fail_writes_after_bytes()};
}

std::optional<AcquireError> ensure_directory(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path) {
  if (const auto failure = create_directory_within(root, path)) {
    return error(AcquireReason::publish_failed, path.generic() + ": " + failure->message);
  }
  return std::nullopt;
}

std::optional<AcquireError> make_read_only_directory(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path) {
  if (auto failure = ensure_directory(root, path)) return failure;
  OwnedHandle fd(dir_fd(root, path));
  if (fd.get() < 0 || ::fchmod(fd.get(), 0500) != 0) return error(AcquireReason::publish_failed, path.generic() + ": mode 0500");
  return std::nullopt;
}

std::optional<AcquireError> rename_within(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& from, const RelativePath& to,
                                          const StoreEnvironment& env) {
  OwnedHandle from_dir(parent_fd(root, from));
  OwnedHandle to_dir(parent_fd(root, to));
  if (from_dir.get() < 0 || to_dir.get() < 0) return error(AcquireReason::publish_failed, to.generic() + ": directory cannot be opened");
  const char* from_name = from.components().back().c_str();
  const char* to_name = to.components().back().c_str();
  if (!env.force_rename_fallback()) {
    if (rename_noreplace(from_dir.get(), from_name, to_dir.get(), to_name) == 0) return std::nullopt;
    if (errno == EEXIST) return error(AcquireReason::publish_failed, to.generic() + ": target exists");
    if (errno != EINVAL && errno != ENOSYS) return io_error(errno, to.generic());
  }
  // Fallback (§4.5 step 4): only under .lock, only when the target is absent.
  struct stat info {};
  if (::fstatat(to_dir.get(), to_name, &info, AT_SYMLINK_NOFOLLOW) == 0) {
    return error(AcquireReason::publish_failed, to.generic() + ": target exists");
  }
  if (errno != ENOENT) return io_error(errno, to.generic());
  if (::renameat(from_dir.get(), from_name, to_dir.get(), to_name) != 0) return io_error(errno, to.generic());
  return std::nullopt;
}

std::optional<AcquireError> remove_file_within(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path) {
  OwnedHandle dir(parent_fd(root, path));
  if (dir.get() < 0) return std::nullopt;  // parent absent: nothing to remove
  if (::unlinkat(dir.get(), path.components().back().c_str(), 0) != 0 && errno != ENOENT) return io_error(errno, path.generic());
  return std::nullopt;
}

std::optional<AcquireError> remove_tree_within(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path) {
  OwnedHandle parent(parent_fd(root, path));
  if (parent.get() < 0) return std::nullopt;
  const char* name = path.components().back().c_str();
  struct stat info {};
  if (::fstatat(parent.get(), name, &info, AT_SYMLINK_NOFOLLOW) != 0) {
    return errno == ENOENT ? std::nullopt : std::optional<AcquireError>{io_error(errno, path.generic())};
  }
  if (!S_ISDIR(info.st_mode)) {
    if (::unlinkat(parent.get(), name, 0) != 0) return io_error(errno, path.generic());
    return std::nullopt;
  }
  auto listed = list_directory(root, path);
  if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(listed)) return error(AcquireReason::publish_failed, path.generic() + ": cannot be listed");
  for (const auto& entry : std::get<std::vector<DirectoryEntry>>(listed)) {
    if (!std::holds_alternative<RelativePath>(parse_relative_path(entry.name))) {
      OwnedHandle dir(dir_fd(root, path));
      if (dir.get() < 0 || ::unlinkat(dir.get(), entry.name.c_str(), entry.type == EntryType::directory ? AT_REMOVEDIR : 0) != 0) {
        return error(AcquireReason::publish_failed, path.generic() + ": an entry cannot be removed");
      }
      continue;
    }
    if (auto failure = remove_tree_within(root, join_rel(path, entry.name))) return failure;
  }
  if (::unlinkat(parent.get(), name, AT_REMOVEDIR) != 0 && errno != ENOENT) return io_error(errno, path.generic());
  return std::nullopt;
}

std::optional<AcquireError> sync_directory(const CanonicalRoot& root, const std::optional<RelativePath>& path) {
  OwnedHandle fd(dir_fd(root, path));
  if (fd.get() < 0 || ::fsync(fd.get()) != 0) return error(AcquireReason::publish_failed, "directory fsync failed");
  return std::nullopt;
}

std::optional<AcquireError> write_new_file(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path, const std::string_view bytes) {
  const StoreEnvironment env;
  auto opened = open_part_file(root, path, PartOpen::create_new, env);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&opened)) return *failure;
  auto& file = std::get<PartFile>(opened);
  if (auto failure = file.append(bytes)) return failure;
  return file.sync();
}

bool exists_within(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path) {
  OwnedHandle parent(parent_fd(root, path));
  struct stat info {};
  return parent.get() >= 0 && ::fstatat(parent.get(), path.components().back().c_str(), &info, AT_SYMLINK_NOFOLLOW) == 0;
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`OwnedHandle` (SP1) closes on destruction and skips negative descriptors (`if (handle_ >= 0) ::close(handle_);`), so wrapping a failed `-1` is safe. `remove_tree_within` on a `0500` directory (`.cwd`) works: removing the directory needs write access on its parent only. `write_new_file` uses `O_APPEND` through `open_part_file`; that is fine for a new file.
