```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/models_store.hpp"

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include "secure_frontend/win32_prelude.hpp"
#include "windows_security.hpp"

#include <chrono>
#include <thread>

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {

namespace ws = windows_security;

AcquireError win_error(const DWORD code, const std::string& detail) {
  return AcquireError{code == ERROR_DISK_FULL || code == ERROR_HANDLE_DISK_FULL ? AcquireReason::disk_space
                                                                                 : AcquireReason::publish_failed,
                      detail};
}

std::wstring native(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path) { return ws::extended_path(root.join(path).wstring()); }

bool same_file(const HANDLE a, const HANDLE b) {
  BY_HANDLE_FILE_INFORMATION x{};
  BY_HANDLE_FILE_INFORMATION y{};
  return GetFileInformationByHandle(a, &x) != 0 && GetFileInformationByHandle(b, &y) != 0 &&
         x.dwVolumeSerialNumber == y.dwVolumeSerialNumber && x.nFileIndexHigh == y.nFileIndexHigh && x.nFileIndexLow == y.nFileIndexLow;
}

// The validated, pinned parent directory of `path` (handle without FILE_SHARE_DELETE,
// held across the path-based operation, as SP1's write_private_file does).
class PinnedParent final {
 public:
  PinnedParent(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path) {
    const auto& parts = path.components();
    std::optional<OpenedFile> validated;
    std::wstring directory = root.path().wstring();
    if (parts.size() > 1U) {
      std::string parent;
      for (std::size_t i = 0; i + 1U < parts.size(); ++i) {
        parent += (parent.empty() ? "" : "/") + parts[i];
        directory += L'\\' + path_from_utf8(parts[i]).wstring();
      }
      auto opened = open_beneath(root, must_rel(parent), OpenOptions{OpenKind::directory});
      if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(opened)) return;
      validated.emplace(std::move(std::get<OpenedFile>(opened)));
    }
    handle_ = CreateFileW(ws::extended_path(directory).c_str(), READ_CONTROL | FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES, FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE,
                          nullptr, OPEN_EXISTING, FILE_FLAG_BACKUP_SEMANTICS | FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT, nullptr);
    if (handle_ == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return;
    const HANDLE checked = validated ? static_cast<HANDLE>(validated->native()) : static_cast<HANDLE>(root.handle());
    if (ws::is_reparse_point(handle_) || !same_file(handle_, checked)) {
      CloseHandle(handle_);
      handle_ = INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE;
      return;
    }
    pinned_ = ws::final_path_for_handle(handle_);
  }
  ~PinnedParent() {
    if (handle_ != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) CloseHandle(handle_);
  }
  PinnedParent(const PinnedParent&) = delete;
  PinnedParent& operator=(const PinnedParent&) = delete;
  [[nodiscard]] bool ok() const noexcept { return handle_ != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE && !pinned_.empty(); }
  [[nodiscard]] const std::wstring& final_path() const noexcept { return pinned_; }

 private:
  HANDLE handle_{INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE};
  std::wstring pinned_;
};

bool same_path(const std::wstring& a, const std::wstring& b) {
  return !a.empty() && a.size() == b.size() &&
         CompareStringOrdinal(a.c_str(), static_cast<int>(a.size()), b.c_str(), static_cast<int>(b.size()), TRUE) == CSTR_EQUAL;
}

// Spec §11: antivirus/indexer handles → retry 10 times over 5 s.
template <typename Operation>
bool with_sharing_retries(Operation operation) {
  for (int attempt = 0; attempt < 10; ++attempt) {
    if (operation()) return true;
    const DWORD code = GetLastError();
    if (code != ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED && code != ERROR_SHARING_VIOLATION) return false;
    std::this_thread::sleep_for(std::chrono::milliseconds(500));
    SetLastError(code);
  }
  return false;
}

bool absent(const DWORD code) { return code == ERROR_FILE_NOT_FOUND || code == ERROR_PATH_NOT_FOUND; }

}  // namespace

std::variant<std::uint64_t, AcquireError> StoreEnvironment::free_bytes(const CanonicalRoot& root) const {
  ULARGE_INTEGER available{};
  if (!GetDiskFreeSpaceExW((root.path().wstring() + L"\\").c_str(), &available, nullptr, nullptr)) {
    return AcquireError{AcquireReason::disk_space, "free space cannot be determined"};
  }
  return static_cast<std::uint64_t>(available.QuadPart);
}

std::variant<FileSystemKind, AcquireError> StoreEnvironment::file_system_kind(const CanonicalRoot& root) const {
  wchar_t name[MAX_PATH + 1] = {};
  if (!GetVolumeInformationByHandleW(static_cast<HANDLE>(root.handle()), nullptr, 0, nullptr, nullptr, nullptr, name, MAX_PATH + 1)) {
    return AcquireError{AcquireReason::bundle_layout, "file system type cannot be determined"};
  }
  const std::wstring fs{name};
  return fs == L"FAT" || fs == L"FAT32" ? FileSystemKind::fat : FileSystemKind::other;
}

ModelsLock& ModelsLock::operator=(ModelsLock&& other) noexcept {
  if (this != &other) {
    if (owned_) CloseHandle(static_cast<HANDLE>(handle_));
    handle_ = other.handle_;
    owned_ = other.owned_;
    other.owned_ = false;
  }
  return *this;
}

ModelsLock::~ModelsLock() {
  if (owned_) CloseHandle(static_cast<HANDLE>(handle_));  // closing the handle releases LockFileEx
}

std::variant<ModelsLock, AcquireError> lock_models_root(const CanonicalRoot& root) {
  ws::PrivateSecurityAttributes security(false);
  const HANDLE h = CreateFileW(native(root, must_rel(".lock")).c_str(), GENERIC_READ | GENERIC_WRITE, FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE,
                               security.get(), OPEN_ALWAYS, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL | FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT, nullptr);
  if (h == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::publish_failed, ".lock cannot be opened"};
  BY_HANDLE_FILE_INFORMATION lock_info{};
  if (!GetFileInformationByHandle(h, &lock_info) ||
      (lock_info.dwFileAttributes & (FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY | FILE_ATTRIBUTE_REPARSE_POINT)) != 0) {
    CloseHandle(h);
    return AcquireError{AcquireReason::publish_failed, ".lock is not a regular file"};
  }
  OVERLAPPED overlapped{};
  if (!LockFileEx(h, LOCKFILE_EXCLUSIVE_LOCK | LOCKFILE_FAIL_IMMEDIATELY, 0, MAXDWORD, MAXDWORD, &overlapped)) {
    const DWORD code = GetLastError();
    CloseHandle(h);
    return AcquireError{code == ERROR_LOCK_VIOLATION ? AcquireReason::models_busy : AcquireReason::publish_failed,
                        "another pull, import, rm or update holds roots.models/.lock"};
  }
  return ModelsLock{h};
}

PartFile::PartFile(PartFile&& other) noexcept
    : handle_(other.handle_), size_(other.size_), path_(std::move(other.path_)), fail_after_(other.fail_after_), open_(other.open_) {
  other.open_ = false;
}

PartFile::~PartFile() { close(); }

void PartFile::close() noexcept {
  if (open_) CloseHandle(static_cast<HANDLE>(handle_));
  open_ = false;
}

std::optional<AcquireError> PartFile::append(const std::string_view bytes) {
  if (fail_after_ && size_ + bytes.size() > *fail_after_) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::disk_space, path_};
  std::size_t done = 0;
  while (done < bytes.size()) {
    const DWORD chunk = static_cast<DWORD>(std::min<std::size_t>(bytes.size() - done, 1U << 30U));
    DWORD written = 0;
    if (!WriteFile(static_cast<HANDLE>(handle_), bytes.data() + done, chunk, &written, nullptr) || written == 0) {
      return win_error(GetLastError(), path_);
    }
    done += written;
    size_ += written;
  }
  return std::nullopt;
}

std::optional<AcquireError> PartFile::truncate_to_zero() {
  LARGE_INTEGER zero{};
  if (!SetFilePointerEx(static_cast<HANDLE>(handle_), zero, nullptr, FILE_BEGIN) || !SetEndOfFile(static_cast<HANDLE>(handle_))) {
    return win_error(GetLastError(), path_);
  }
  size_ = 0;
  return std::nullopt;
}

std::optional<AcquireError> PartFile::preallocate(const std::uint64_t total_size) {
  FILE_ALLOCATION_INFO info{};
  info.AllocationSize.QuadPart = static_cast<LONGLONG>(total_size);
  if (!SetFileInformationByHandle(static_cast<HANDLE>(handle_), FileAllocationInfo, &info, sizeof info)) {
    const DWORD code = GetLastError();
    if (code == ERROR_DISK_FULL || code == ERROR_HANDLE_DISK_FULL) return win_error(code, path_);
  }
  return std::nullopt;
}

std::optional<AcquireError> PartFile::sync() {
  if (!FlushFileBuffers(static_cast<HANDLE>(handle_))) return win_error(GetLastError(), path_);
  return std::nullopt;
}

std::variant<PartFile, AcquireError> open_part_file(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path, const PartOpen mode,
                                                    const StoreEnvironment& env) {
  const PinnedParent parent(root, path);
  if (!parent.ok()) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::publish_failed, path.generic() + ": directory cannot be opened safely"};
  ws::PrivateSecurityAttributes security(false);
  const HANDLE h = CreateFileW(native(root, path).c_str(), GENERIC_WRITE | GENERIC_READ, FILE_SHARE_READ, security.get(),
                               mode == PartOpen::create_new ? CREATE_NEW : OPEN_EXISTING,
                               FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL | FILE_FLAG_SEQUENTIAL_SCAN | FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT, nullptr);
  if (h == INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE) return win_error(GetLastError(), path.generic() + ": cannot be opened");
  BY_HANDLE_FILE_INFORMATION info{};
  const bool regular = GetFileInformationByHandle(h, &info) != 0 &&
                       (info.dwFileAttributes & (FILE_ATTRIBUTE_REPARSE_POINT | FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY)) == 0 &&
                       info.nNumberOfLinks == 1;
  const bool inside = same_path(std::filesystem::path{ws::final_path_for_handle(h)}.parent_path().wstring(), parent.final_path());
  LARGE_INTEGER end{};
  LARGE_INTEGER zero{};
  if (!regular || !inside || !SetFilePointerEx(h, zero, &end, FILE_END)) {
    CloseHandle(h);
    return AcquireError{AcquireReason::publish_failed, path.generic() + ": not a private single-link regular file"};
  }
  return PartFile{h, static_cast<std::uint64_t>(end.QuadPart), path.generic(), env.fail_writes_after_bytes()};
}

std::optional<AcquireError> ensure_directory(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path) {
  if (const auto failure = create_directory_within(root, path)) {
    return AcquireError{AcquireReason::publish_failed, path.generic() + ": " + failure->message};
  }
  return std::nullopt;
}

std::optional<AcquireError> make_read_only_directory(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path) {
  if (auto failure = ensure_directory(root, path)) return failure;
  const std::wstring sddl = L"D:P(A;OICI;FRFX;;;" + ws::current_user_sid_string() + L")(A;OICI;FA;;;SY)";
  PSECURITY_DESCRIPTOR descriptor = nullptr;
  if (!ConvertStringSecurityDescriptorToSecurityDescriptorW(sddl.c_str(), SDDL_REVISION_1, &descriptor, nullptr)) {
    return AcquireError{AcquireReason::publish_failed, path.generic() + ": read-only DACL"};
  }
  const BOOL set = SetFileSecurityW(native(root, path).c_str(), DACL_SECURITY_INFORMATION | PROTECTED_DACL_SECURITY_INFORMATION, descriptor);
  LocalFree(descriptor);
  if (!set) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::publish_failed, path.generic() + ": read-only DACL"};
  return std::nullopt;
}

std::optional<AcquireError> rename_within(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& from, const RelativePath& to,
                                          const StoreEnvironment&) {
  const PinnedParent from_parent(root, from);
  const PinnedParent to_parent(root, to);
  if (!from_parent.ok() || !to_parent.ok()) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::publish_failed, to.generic() + ": directory cannot be opened"};
  const std::wstring source = native(root, from);
  const std::wstring target = native(root, to);
  if (with_sharing_retries([&] { return MoveFileExW(source.c_str(), target.c_str(), MOVEFILE_WRITE_THROUGH) != 0; })) return std::nullopt;
  const DWORD code = GetLastError();
  if (code == ERROR_ALREADY_EXISTS || code == ERROR_FILE_EXISTS) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::publish_failed, to.generic() + ": target exists"};
  return win_error(code, to.generic());
}

std::optional<AcquireError> remove_file_within(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path) {
  const PinnedParent parent(root, path);
  if (!parent.ok()) return std::nullopt;
  const std::wstring target = native(root, path);
  if (with_sharing_retries([&] { return DeleteFileW(target.c_str()) != 0; })) return std::nullopt;
  const DWORD code = GetLastError();
  if (absent(code)) return std::nullopt;
  return win_error(code, path.generic());
}

std::optional<AcquireError> remove_tree_within(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path) {
  const std::wstring target = native(root, path);
  const DWORD attributes = GetFileAttributesW(target.c_str());
  if (attributes == INVALID_FILE_ATTRIBUTES) return absent(GetLastError()) ? std::nullopt : std::optional<AcquireError>{win_error(GetLastError(), path.generic())};
  const PinnedParent parent(root, path);
  if (!parent.ok()) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::publish_failed, path.generic() + ": directory cannot be opened"};
  if ((attributes & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_REPARSE_POINT) != 0) {  // junction or link: remove the link itself, never its target
    const bool removed = (attributes & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY) != 0 ? RemoveDirectoryW(target.c_str()) != 0 : DeleteFileW(target.c_str()) != 0;
    return removed ? std::nullopt : std::optional<AcquireError>{win_error(GetLastError(), path.generic())};
  }
  if ((attributes & FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY) == 0) return remove_file_within(root, path);
  auto listed = list_directory(root, path);
  if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(listed)) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::publish_failed, path.generic() + ": cannot be listed"};
  for (const auto& entry : std::get<std::vector<DirectoryEntry>>(listed)) {
    if (!std::holds_alternative<RelativePath>(parse_relative_path(entry.name))) {
      return AcquireError{AcquireReason::publish_failed, path.generic() + ": an entry has a non-portable name"};
    }
    if (auto failure = remove_tree_within(root, join_rel(path, entry.name))) return failure;
  }
  if (with_sharing_retries([&] { return RemoveDirectoryW(target.c_str()) != 0; })) return std::nullopt;
  return win_error(GetLastError(), path.generic());
}

std::optional<AcquireError> sync_directory(const CanonicalRoot&, const std::optional<RelativePath>&) {
  return std::nullopt;  // NTFS journals metadata; renames use MOVEFILE_WRITE_THROUGH
}

std::optional<AcquireError> write_new_file(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path, const std::string_view bytes) {
  const StoreEnvironment env;
  auto opened = open_part_file(root, path, PartOpen::create_new, env);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&opened)) return *failure;
  auto& file = std::get<PartFile>(opened);
  if (auto failure = file.append(bytes)) return failure;
  return file.sync();
}

bool exists_within(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path) {
  return GetFileAttributesW(native(root, path).c_str()) != INVALID_FILE_ATTRIBUTES;
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
#endif
```

Link `advapi32` to `secure_frontend_acquire` on Windows (`if(WIN32) target_link_libraries(secure_frontend_acquire PUBLIC advapi32) endif()`). The non-portable-name branch differs from POSIX (which unlinks such entries): on Windows an entry P1 rejects (e.g. an 8.3 alias) is not something SP4 ever creates, so failing closed is correct.

### Task 18: Index on disk, installed models — NOW (SP1 on main)

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/manifest_json.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/model_index_write.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/sha256.hpp"

// in the anonymous namespace:
const char* kTinyManifest =
    "{\"format\":\"strata-model-manifest\",\"schema_version\":1,\"name\":\"m\",\"tag\":\"t\",\"version\":\"2026.10.14.1\","
    "\"sequence\":1,\"profile\":\"glm53\",\"engine\":{\"family\":\"glm\",\"min_version\":\"0.0.1\"},"
    "\"license\":{\"id\":\"MIT\",\"url\":\"https://h.example/l\",\"bundle_allowed\":true},\"hosts\":[\"h.example\"],"
    "\"files\":[{\"path\":\"w.gguf\",\"role\":\"weights\",\"size\":1,"
    "\"sha256\":\"ca978112ca1bbdcafac231b39a23dc4da786eff8147c4e72b9807785afee48bb\",\"sources\":[]}],"
    "\"launch\":{\"native\":\"w.gguf\"}}";

secure_frontend::CanonicalRoot models_root(const std::filesystem::path& path) {
  return std::get<secure_frontend::CanonicalRoot>(secure_frontend::open_root(path));
}

secure_frontend::IndexEntry install_tiny(const std::filesystem::path& root_path) {
  const std::string digest = secure_frontend::to_hex(secure_frontend::sha256(kTinyManifest));
  const std::string dir = secure_frontend::version_directory_name(1, digest);
  put(root_path / "m" / "t" / dir / "manifest.json", kTinyManifest);
  put(root_path / "m" / "t" / dir / "w.gguf", "a");
  return secure_frontend::IndexEntry{"m", "t", "2026.10.14.1", 1, dir, digest, secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party};
}

void index_round_trip_through_disk() {
  const auto root_path = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  const auto root = models_root(root_path);
  const auto empty = secure_frontend::read_model_index(root);
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::ModelIndex>(empty) && std::get<secure_frontend::ModelIndex>(empty).models.empty(),
         "a missing index.json is an empty index");
  const auto entry = install_tiny(root_path);
  const auto index = secure_frontend::with_installed({}, entry);
  expect(!secure_frontend::write_model_index(root, index), "written atomically");
  const auto read = secure_frontend::read_model_index(root);
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::ModelIndex>(read) &&
             secure_frontend::render_model_index(std::get<secure_frontend::ModelIndex>(read)) == secure_frontend::render_model_index(index),
         "round trip");
  const auto installed = secure_frontend::load_installed(root, entry);
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::InstalledModel>(installed) &&
             std::get<secure_frontend::InstalledModel>(installed).version_dir.generic() == "m/t/" + entry.dir,
         "installed model loads, manifest re-hashed");
}

void index_inconsistent_manifest_fails_closed() {
  const auto root_path = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  const auto root = models_root(root_path);
  const auto entry = install_tiny(root_path);
  secure_frontend_test::write_file(root_path / "m" / "t" / entry.dir / "manifest.json", std::string{kTinyManifest} + " ");
  const auto installed = secure_frontend::load_installed(root, entry);
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(installed) &&
             std::get<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(installed).reason == secure_frontend::AcquireReason::index_inconsistent,
         "a manifest.json that no longer hashes to the index entry fails closed");
}

void index_rejects_unknown_keys_and_bad_dirs() {
  using secure_frontend::AcquireReason;
  const std::string digest(64, 'a');
  const std::string entry = "{\"name\":\"m\",\"tag\":\"t\",\"version\":\"2026.10.14.1\",\"sequence\":1,"
                            "\"dir\":\"v1-aaaaaaaaaaaa\",\"manifest_sha256\":\"" + digest + "\",\"origin\":\"catalog\"}";
  const std::string prefix = "{\"format\":\"strata-model-index\",\"schema_version\":1,\"models\":[";
  const std::string suffix = "],\"retired\":[]}\n";
  const std::string good = prefix + entry + suffix;
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::ModelIndex>(secure_frontend::parse_model_index(good)), "good index");
  for (const auto& [from, to] : std::vector<std::pair<std::string, std::string>>{
           {"\"retired\":[]", "\"retired\":[],\"x\":1"}, {"v1-aaaaaaaaaaaa", "v2-aaaaaaaaaaaa"}, {"\"catalog\"", "\"other\""},
           {"strata-model-index", "strata-model-indexes"}, {"\"name\":\"m\"", "\"name\":\"../m\""}}) {
    std::string bad = good;
    bad.replace(bad.find(from), from.size(), to);
    const auto parsed = secure_frontend::parse_model_index(bad);
    expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(parsed) &&
               std::get<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(parsed).reason == AcquireReason::index_inconsistent,
           "index_inconsistent for " + to);
  }
  const std::string twice = prefix + entry + "," + entry + suffix;
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(secure_frontend::parse_model_index(twice)), "one entry per name:tag");
}
// main list additions: index_round_trip_through_disk, index_inconsistent_manifest_fails_closed, index_rejects_unknown_keys_and_bad_dirs
```

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/acquire_reason.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/manifest_json.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/model_index_model.hpp"

namespace secure_frontend {

// Strict JSON (§4.5): exact keys, format/schema_version, dir == v<sequence>-<12 hex>,
// names valid, one models entry per name:tag. Any failure: index_inconsistent.
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<ModelIndex, AcquireError> parse_model_index(std::string_view bytes);
// roots.models/index.json; absent = empty index.
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<ModelIndex, AcquireError> read_model_index(const CanonicalRoot& models);

struct InstalledModel final {
  IndexEntry entry;
  ParsedManifest manifest;
  RelativePath version_dir;  // <name>/<tag>/<dir>
};
// Re-hashes <dir>/manifest.json (<= 1 MiB) against the entry, parses it, and
// checks name/tag/sequence; any mismatch is index_inconsistent (fail closed).
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<InstalledModel, AcquireError> load_installed(const CanonicalRoot& models, const IndexEntry& entry);
[[nodiscard]] RelativePath version_dir_path(std::string_view name, std::string_view tag, std::string_view dir);

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

Append to `src/model_index_store.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/json.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/sha256.hpp"

#include <set>
#include <stdexcept>
#include <utility>

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {

AcquireError inconsistent(const std::string& detail) { return AcquireError{AcquireReason::index_inconsistent, detail}; }

const JsonValue* member(const std::vector<JsonMember>& members, const std::string_view key) {
  for (const auto& m : members) {
    if (m.key == key) return &m.value;
  }
  return nullptr;
}

bool exact_keys(const std::vector<JsonMember>& members, const std::initializer_list<std::string_view> keys) {
  if (members.size() != keys.size()) return false;
  for (const auto key : keys) {
    if (member(members, key) == nullptr) return false;
  }
  return true;
}

std::optional<std::string> string_of(const std::vector<JsonMember>& members, const std::string_view key) {
  const auto* value = member(members, key);
  const auto* s = value != nullptr ? value->if_string() : nullptr;
  return s != nullptr ? std::optional<std::string>{*s} : std::nullopt;
}

}  // namespace

RelativePath version_dir_path(const std::string_view name, const std::string_view tag, const std::string_view dir) {
  auto parsed = parse_relative_path(std::string{name} + "/" + std::string{tag} + "/" + std::string{dir});
  if (auto* path = std::get_if<RelativePath>(&parsed)) return std::move(*path);
  throw std::logic_error("index names are validated before use");
}

std::variant<ModelIndex, AcquireError> parse_model_index(const std::string_view bytes) {
  auto parsed = parse_json(bytes, JsonLimits{kIndexMaxBytes, 4, 65536, 1024});
  if (std::holds_alternative<JsonError>(parsed)) return inconsistent("index.json is not valid strict JSON");
  const auto* top = std::get<JsonValue>(parsed).if_object();
  if (top == nullptr || !exact_keys(*top, {"format", "schema_version", "models", "retired"}) ||
      string_of(*top, "format") != "strata-model-index") {
    return inconsistent("index.json has the wrong shape");
  }
  const auto* version = member(*top, "schema_version")->if_integer();
  const auto* models = member(*top, "models")->if_array();
  const auto* retired = member(*top, "retired")->if_array();
  if (version == nullptr || *version != 1 || models == nullptr || retired == nullptr) return inconsistent("index.json has the wrong shape");
  ModelIndex index;
  std::set<std::pair<std::string, std::string>> seen;
  for (const auto& item : *models) {
    const auto* e = item.if_object();
    if (e == nullptr || !exact_keys(*e, {"name", "tag", "version", "sequence", "dir", "manifest_sha256", "origin"})) {
      return inconsistent("index.json model entry has the wrong keys");
    }
    const auto* sequence = member(*e, "sequence")->if_integer();
    IndexEntry entry{string_of(*e, "name").value_or(""), string_of(*e, "tag").value_or(""), string_of(*e, "version").value_or(""),
                     sequence != nullptr && *sequence >= 1 && *sequence <= 0x7FFFFFFF ? static_cast<std::uint32_t>(*sequence) : 0U,
                     string_of(*e, "dir").value_or(""), string_of(*e, "manifest_sha256").value_or(""), ModelOrigin::catalog};
    const auto origin = string_of(*e, "origin").value_or("");
    if (origin == "third_party") entry.origin = ModelOrigin::third_party;
    else if (origin != "catalog") return inconsistent("index.json origin");
    if (!is_valid_model_name(entry.name) || !is_valid_model_name(entry.tag) || entry.sequence == 0 ||
        !is_lower_hex_digest(entry.manifest_sha256) || entry.dir != version_directory_name(entry.sequence, entry.manifest_sha256) ||
        !seen.insert({entry.name, entry.tag}).second) {
      return inconsistent("index.json model entry " + (is_valid_model_name(entry.name) ? entry.name : std::string{"?"}));
    }
    index.models.push_back(std::move(entry));
  }
  for (const auto& item : *retired) {
    const auto* e = item.if_object();
    if (e == nullptr || !exact_keys(*e, {"name", "tag", "dir", "manifest_sha256"})) return inconsistent("index.json retired entry");
    RetiredEntry entry{string_of(*e, "name").value_or(""), string_of(*e, "tag").value_or(""), string_of(*e, "dir").value_or(""),
                       string_of(*e, "manifest_sha256").value_or("")};
    if (!is_valid_model_name(entry.name) || !is_valid_model_name(entry.tag) || !is_lower_hex_digest(entry.manifest_sha256) ||
        !entry.dir.starts_with("v") || !entry.dir.ends_with("-" + entry.manifest_sha256.substr(0, 12))) {
      return inconsistent("index.json retired entry");
    }
    index.retired.push_back(std::move(entry));
  }
  return index;
}

std::variant<ModelIndex, AcquireError> read_model_index(const CanonicalRoot& models) {
  auto opened = open_beneath(models, std::get<RelativePath>(parse_relative_path(kIndexFileName)),
                             OpenOptions{OpenKind::regular_file, FileTrust::owner_only, true});
  if (const auto* failure = std::get_if<PathError>(&opened)) {
    if (failure->code == PathErrorCode::not_found) return ModelIndex{};
    return inconsistent("index.json cannot be opened safely");
  }
  auto bytes = std::get<OpenedFile>(opened).read_all(kIndexMaxBytes);
  if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(bytes)) return inconsistent("index.json is larger than 4 MiB or unreadable");
  return parse_model_index(std::get<std::string>(bytes));
}

std::variant<InstalledModel, AcquireError> load_installed(const CanonicalRoot& models, const IndexEntry& entry) {
  const auto dir = version_dir_path(entry.name, entry.tag, entry.dir);
  auto manifest_path = parse_relative_path(dir.generic() + "/" + std::string{kVersionManifestName});
  auto opened = open_beneath(models, std::get<RelativePath>(manifest_path), OpenOptions{OpenKind::regular_file, FileTrust::any, true});
  if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(opened)) return inconsistent(model_ref_text(entry.name, entry.tag) + ": manifest.json missing");
  auto bytes = std::get<OpenedFile>(opened).read_all(kManifestMaxBytes);
  if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(bytes)) return inconsistent(model_ref_text(entry.name, entry.tag) + ": manifest.json unreadable");
  const auto& text = std::get<std::string>(bytes);
  if (to_hex(sha256(text)) != entry.manifest_sha256) {
    return inconsistent(model_ref_text(entry.name, entry.tag) + ": manifest.json does not match the index");
  }
  ManifestRules rules;
  rules.max_total_bytes = ~std::uint64_t{0};  // already accepted; budgets apply to new transfers only
  auto parsed = parse_manifest(text, rules);
  if (std::holds_alternative<ManifestIssue>(parsed)) return inconsistent(model_ref_text(entry.name, entry.tag) + ": manifest.json invalid");
  auto& manifest = std::get<ParsedManifest>(parsed);
  if (manifest.manifest.name != entry.name || manifest.manifest.tag != entry.tag || manifest.manifest.sequence != entry.sequence) {
    return inconsistent(model_ref_text(entry.name, entry.tag) + ": manifest.json names another model");
  }
  return InstalledModel{entry, std::move(manifest), dir};
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/model_index_write.hpp"

#include "secure_frontend/models_store.hpp"

namespace secure_frontend {

std::optional<AcquireError> write_model_index(const CanonicalRoot& models, const ModelIndex& index) {
  const std::string text = render_model_index(index);
  if (text.size() > kIndexMaxBytes) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::index_write_failed, "index.json would exceed 4 MiB"};
  if (auto failure = replace_small_file(models, must_rel(kIndexFileName), text)) {
    return AcquireError{AcquireReason::index_write_failed, failure->detail};
  }
  return std::nullopt;
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

```cpp
#pragma once
// SP4 test fixtures: small qwen-profile payloads whose tokenizer passes SP1's

#include "secure_frontend/acquire_publish.hpp"

#include <set>

namespace secure_frontend_test::acquire {

struct PayloadFile final {
  std::string path;
  secure_frontend::FileRole role;
  std::string bytes;
};

inline std::vector<PayloadFile> qwen_payload(const std::string_view salt = "") {
  using secure_frontend::FileRole;
  const std::string s{salt};
  return {{"weights/fx-00001-of-00002.gguf", FileRole::weights, "shard one " + s},
          {"weights/fx-00002-of-00002.gguf", FileRole::weights, "shard two " + s},
          {"pack/index.txt", FileRole::pack, "index " + s},
          {"pack/dense.bin", FileRole::pack, "dense " + s},
          {"pack/native_experts.txt", FileRole::pack, "experts " + s},
          {"pack/conversions.json", FileRole::pack, "{}\n"},
          {"pack/tokenizer/vocab.json", FileRole::tokenizer, "{\"a\":0,\"b\":1,\"ab\":2}"},
          {"pack/tokenizer/merges.txt", FileRole::tokenizer, "a b\n"},
          {"pack/tokenizer/token_type.json", FileRole::tokenizer, "[1,1,1]"},
          {"pack/tokenizer/tokenizer.json", FileRole::tokenizer, "{}\n"}};
}

inline std::string manifest_text(const std::string_view name, const std::string_view tag, const std::uint32_t sequence,
                                 const std::vector<PayloadFile>& files, const bool with_sources = true,
                                 const bool bundle_allowed = true, const std::string_view host = "fixture.example") {
  std::string out = "{\"format\":\"strata-model-manifest\",\"schema_version\":1,\"name\":\"" + std::string{name} +
                    "\",\"tag\":\"" + std::string{tag} + "\",\"version\":\"2026.10.14." + std::to_string(sequence) +
                    "\",\"sequence\":" + std::to_string(sequence) +
                    ",\"profile\":\"qwen\",\"engine\":{\"family\":\"strata\",\"min_version\":\"0.1.43\"},"
                    "\"license\":{\"id\":\"Apache-2.0\",\"url\":\"https://" + std::string{host} + "/l\",\"bundle_allowed\":" +
                    (bundle_allowed ? "true" : "false") + ",\"notice\":\"Fixture.\"},\"hosts\":[\"" + std::string{host} +
                    "\"],\"redirect_hosts\":[\"*.cdn." + std::string{host} + "\"],\"files\":[";
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < files.size(); ++i) {
    const auto& f = files[i];
    out += (i ? "," : "") + std::string{"{\"path\":\""} + f.path + "\",\"role\":\"" + std::string{secure_frontend::role_name(f.role)} +
           "\",\"size\":" + std::to_string(f.bytes.size()) + ",\"sha256\":\"" + secure_frontend::to_hex(secure_frontend::sha256(f.bytes)) +
           "\",\"sources\":[" + (with_sources ? "\"https://" + std::string{host} + "/fx/" + f.path + "\"" : std::string{}) + "]}";
  }
  out += "],\"launch\":{\"native\":\"weights/fx-00001-of-00002.gguf\",\"ple_gguf\":\"weights/fx-00002-of-00002.gguf\","
         "\"pack\":\"pack\",\"tokenizer_pack\":\"pack/tokenizer\"}}\n";
  return out;
}

inline secure_frontend::TrustedManifest trusted(const std::string& text, const secure_frontend::ModelOrigin origin) {
  auto parsed = secure_frontend::parse_manifest(text, secure_frontend::ManifestRules{});
  if (!std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::ParsedManifest>(parsed)) throw std::runtime_error("fixture manifest invalid");
  return secure_frontend::TrustedManifest{std::get<secure_frontend::ParsedManifest>(std::move(parsed)), origin};
}

inline secure_frontend::CanonicalRoot models_root(const std::filesystem::path& path) {
  auto opened = secure_frontend::open_root(path);
  if (!std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::CanonicalRoot>(opened)) throw std::runtime_error("scratch root not private");
  return std::get<secure_frontend::CanonicalRoot>(std::move(opened));
}

// .staging/<key>/MANIFEST + every payload file under its final name.
inline void stage(const secure_frontend::CanonicalRoot& root, const secure_frontend::TrustedManifest& manifest,
                  const std::vector<PayloadFile>& files) {
  using secure_frontend::must_rel;
  const auto key = secure_frontend::staging_key(manifest.parsed.sha256);
  const auto dir = secure_frontend::staging_dir_path(key);
  if (secure_frontend::ensure_directory(root, dir)) throw std::runtime_error("stage dir");
  if (secure_frontend::write_new_file(root, secure_frontend::join_rel(dir, "MANIFEST"), manifest.parsed.bytes)) throw std::runtime_error("MANIFEST");
  for (const auto& f : files) {
    const auto path = secure_frontend::join_rel(dir, f.path);
    const auto parent = path.generic().substr(0, path.generic().rfind('/'));
    if (secure_frontend::ensure_directory(root, must_rel(parent)) || secure_frontend::write_new_file(root, path, f.bytes)) {
      throw std::runtime_error("stage file");
    }
  }
}

class FakeLoaded final : public secure_frontend::LoadedVersions {
 public:
  std::set<std::string> held;
  bool is_held(const std::string_view name, const std::string_view tag, const std::string_view dir) override {
    return held.contains(std::string{name} + "/" + std::string{tag} + "/" + std::string{dir});
  }
};

}  // namespace secure_frontend_test::acquire
```

```cpp
#include "acquire_cases.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/acquire_publish.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/model_index_store.hpp"
#include "support/acquire_fixtures.hpp"
#include "support/expect.hpp"

namespace acquire_cases {
namespace {

namespace fx = secure_frontend_test::acquire;
using secure_frontend::AcquireReason;
using secure_frontend_test::expect;

class FallbackEnvironment final : public secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment {
 public:
  bool force_rename_fallback() const override { return true; }
};

secure_frontend::ModelIndex index_of(const secure_frontend::CanonicalRoot& root) {
  return std::get<secure_frontend::ModelIndex>(secure_frontend::read_model_index(root));
}

}  // namespace

void dl04_publish_uses_noreplace_and_never_overwrites() {
  const auto path = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  const auto root = fx::models_root(path);
  const auto manifest = fx::trusted(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 1, fx::qwen_payload()), secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party);
  fx::stage(root, manifest, fx::qwen_payload());
  const std::string dir = secure_frontend::version_directory_name(1, manifest.parsed.sha256);
  expect(!secure_frontend::ensure_directory(root, secure_frontend::must_rel("m/t/" + dir)), "a squatting directory at the target");
  expect(!secure_frontend::write_new_file(root, secure_frontend::must_rel("m/t/" + dir + "/keep"), "k"), "with content");
  fx::FakeLoaded loaded;
  const secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment env;
  const auto result = secure_frontend::publish_staged(root, secure_frontend::staging_key(manifest.parsed.sha256), manifest, loaded, env, true);
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(result), "publish refuses to replace an existing version directory");
  expect(secure_frontend::exists_within(root, secure_frontend::must_rel("m/t/" + dir + "/keep")), "existing content untouched");
  expect(index_of(root).models.empty(), "nothing indexed");
}

void publish_falls_back_when_noreplace_unsupported() {
  const auto path = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  const auto root = fx::models_root(path);
  const auto manifest = fx::trusted(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 1, fx::qwen_payload()), secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::catalog);
  fx::stage(root, manifest, fx::qwen_payload());
  fx::FakeLoaded loaded;
  const FallbackEnvironment env;
  const auto result = secure_frontend::publish_staged(root, secure_frontend::staging_key(manifest.parsed.sha256), manifest, loaded, env, true);
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::PublishResult>(result), "fstatat + renameat fallback publishes");
  const auto index = index_of(root);
  expect(index.models.size() == 1 && index.models.front().origin == secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::catalog, "indexed");
  expect(!secure_frontend::exists_within(root, secure_frontend::staging_dir_path(secure_frontend::staging_key(manifest.parsed.sha256))),
         "staging key moved, not copied");
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::InstalledModel>(secure_frontend::load_installed(root, index.models.front())),
         "manifest.json in place and consistent");
}

#if defined(SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS)
void dl04_crash_between_publish_and_index_is_not_loaded() {
  const auto path = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  const auto root = fx::models_root(path);
  const auto manifest = fx::trusted(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 1, fx::qwen_payload()), secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party);
  fx::stage(root, manifest, fx::qwen_payload());
  fx::FakeLoaded loaded;
  const secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment env;
  secure_frontend::PublishOptions crash;
  crash.stop_after_version_rename = [] { return true; };
  const auto crashed = secure_frontend::publish_staged(root, secure_frontend::staging_key(manifest.parsed.sha256), manifest, loaded, env,
                                                       true, crash);
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(crashed), "fault injected between steps 4 and 5");
  expect(index_of(root).models.empty(), "the version directory is not named by the index: never loaded");
  const auto adopted = secure_frontend::adopt_orphan(root, manifest, loaded, env);
  expect(adopted.has_value() && std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::PublishResult>(*adopted) &&
             std::get<secure_frontend::PublishResult>(*adopted).adopted,
         "a later run adopts the orphan by digest (dl04_orphan_adopted_after_rehash_by_digest covers the re-hash)");
  expect(index_of(root).models.size() == 1, "adopted into the index");
}
#endif

void dl04_orphan_adopted_after_rehash_by_digest() {
  const auto path = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  const auto root = fx::models_root(path);
  const auto payload = fx::qwen_payload();
  const auto manifest = fx::trusted(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 1, payload), secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party);
  const std::string dir = "m/t/" + secure_frontend::version_directory_name(1, manifest.parsed.sha256);
  for (const auto& f : payload) {
    const auto file = secure_frontend::must_rel(dir + "/" + f.path);
    (void)secure_frontend::ensure_directory(root, secure_frontend::must_rel(file.generic().substr(0, file.generic().rfind('/'))));
    (void)secure_frontend::write_new_file(root, file, f.bytes);
  }
  (void)secure_frontend::write_new_file(root, secure_frontend::must_rel(dir + "/manifest.json"), manifest.parsed.bytes);
  secure_frontend_test::write_file(root.join(secure_frontend::must_rel(dir + "/pack/dense.bin")), "corrupt!");
  fx::FakeLoaded loaded;
  const secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment env;
  const auto corrupt = secure_frontend::adopt_orphan(root, manifest, loaded, env);
  expect(corrupt.has_value() && std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(*corrupt) &&
             std::get<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(*corrupt).reason == AcquireReason::digest_mismatch,
         "an orphan is re-hashed before adoption; a corrupt one is not adopted");
  secure_frontend_test::write_file(root.join(secure_frontend::must_rel(dir + "/pack/dense.bin")), payload[3].bytes);
  const auto good = secure_frontend::adopt_orphan(root, manifest, loaded, env);
  expect(good.has_value() && std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::PublishResult>(*good), "an intact orphan is adopted");
  expect(!secure_frontend::adopt_orphan(root, fx::trusted(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 2, payload), secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party),
                                        loaded, env).has_value(),
         "no orphan with another digest");
}

void replace_loaded_model_retires_old_version() {
  const auto path = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  const auto root = fx::models_root(path);
  const secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment env;
  fx::FakeLoaded loaded;
  const auto v1 = fx::trusted(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 1, fx::qwen_payload("1")), secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party);
  fx::stage(root, v1, fx::qwen_payload("1"));
  const auto first = std::get<secure_frontend::PublishResult>(
      secure_frontend::publish_staged(root, secure_frontend::staging_key(v1.parsed.sha256), v1, loaded, env, true));
  loaded.held.insert("m/t/" + first.entry.dir);
  const auto v2 = fx::trusted(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 2, fx::qwen_payload("2")), secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party);
  fx::stage(root, v2, fx::qwen_payload("2"));
  const auto second = std::get<secure_frontend::PublishResult>(
      secure_frontend::publish_staged(root, secure_frontend::staging_key(v2.parsed.sha256), v2, loaded, env, true));
  auto index = index_of(root);
  expect(index.models.front().sequence == 2 && index.retired.size() == 1 && second.still_retired.size() == 1,
         "the held version is retired, not deleted");
  expect(secure_frontend::exists_within(root, secure_frontend::must_rel("m/t/" + first.entry.dir)), "still on disk");
  loaded.held.clear();
  expect(!secure_frontend::delete_unheld_retired(root, loaded, env), "deleted once unheld");
  index = index_of(root);
  expect(index.retired.empty() && !secure_frontend::exists_within(root, secure_frontend::must_rel("m/t/" + first.entry.dir)), "gone");
}

void layout_check_requires_pack_files_and_valid_tokenizer() {
  const auto path = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  const auto root = fx::models_root(path);
  auto payload = fx::qwen_payload();
  payload[6].bytes = "{\"a\":0,\"b\":2}";  // vocabulary IDs not contiguous: SP1 rejects the tokenizer pack
  const auto manifest = fx::trusted(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 1, payload), secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party);
  fx::stage(root, manifest, payload);
  fx::FakeLoaded loaded;
  const secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment env;
  const auto result = secure_frontend::publish_staged(root, secure_frontend::staging_key(manifest.parsed.sha256), manifest, loaded, env, true);
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(result) &&
             std::get<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(result).reason == AcquireReason::pack_layout_invalid,
         "pack_layout_invalid (exit 4)");
  auto missing = fx::qwen_payload();
  missing.erase(missing.begin() + 3);  // no pack/dense.bin
  const auto incomplete = fx::trusted(fx::manifest_text("n", "t", 1, missing), secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party);
  fx::stage(root, incomplete, missing);
  const auto refused = secure_frontend::publish_staged(root, secure_frontend::staging_key(incomplete.parsed.sha256), incomplete, loaded, env, true);
  expect(std::get<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(refused).reason == AcquireReason::pack_layout_invalid, "required pack files");
}

void staged_tree_must_be_exactly_the_manifest() {
  const auto path = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  const auto root = fx::models_root(path);
  const auto manifest = fx::trusted(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 1, fx::qwen_payload()), secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party);
  fx::stage(root, manifest, fx::qwen_payload());
  const auto dir = secure_frontend::staging_dir_path(secure_frontend::staging_key(manifest.parsed.sha256));
  (void)secure_frontend::write_new_file(root, secure_frontend::join_rel(dir, "pack/extra.bin"), "x");
  const auto refused = secure_frontend::verify_tree(root, dir, manifest, "MANIFEST", true);
  expect(refused.has_value(), "an unlisted file in staging blocks publish");
  (void)secure_frontend::remove_file_within(root, secure_frontend::join_rel(dir, "pack/extra.bin"));
  secure_frontend_test::write_file(root.join(secure_frontend::join_rel(dir, "pack/index.txt")), "tampered");
  const auto tampered = secure_frontend::verify_tree(root, dir, manifest, "MANIFEST", true);
  expect(tampered && tampered->reason == AcquireReason::digest_mismatch, "every listed file is re-hashed when asked");
}

void install_decisions_follow_sequence_rules() {
  using secure_frontend::InstallDecisionKind;
  const auto v2 = fx::trusted(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 2, fx::qwen_payload("2")), secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party);
  secure_frontend::ModelIndex index;
  index = secure_frontend::with_installed(index, secure_frontend::IndexEntry{"m", "t", "2026.10.14.2", 2,
                                                                             secure_frontend::version_directory_name(2, v2.parsed.sha256),
                                                                             v2.parsed.sha256, secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party});
  expect(std::get<secure_frontend::InstallDecision>(secure_frontend::decide_install(index, v2, false)).kind ==
             InstallDecisionKind::already_installed, "equal digest: already installed");
  const auto v1 = fx::trusted(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 1, fx::qwen_payload("1")), secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party);
  expect(std::get<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(secure_frontend::decide_install(index, v1, false)).reason == AcquireReason::manifest_downgrade,
         "lower sequence refused");
  expect(std::get<secure_frontend::InstallDecision>(secure_frontend::decide_install(index, v1, true)).kind == InstallDecisionKind::update,
         "--allow-downgrade");
  const auto other2 = fx::trusted(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 2, fx::qwen_payload("x")), secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party);
  expect(std::get<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(secure_frontend::decide_install(index, other2, true)).reason == AcquireReason::manifest_conflict,
         "equal sequence, different digest: conflict even with --allow-downgrade");
  const auto v3 = fx::trusted(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 3, fx::qwen_payload("3")), secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party);
  expect(std::get<secure_frontend::InstallDecision>(secure_frontend::decide_install(index, v3, false)).kind == InstallDecisionKind::update, "update");
  const auto fresh = fx::trusted(fx::manifest_text("n", "t", 1, fx::qwen_payload()), secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party);
  expect(std::get<secure_frontend::InstallDecision>(secure_frontend::decide_install(index, fresh, false)).kind == InstallDecisionKind::fresh, "fresh");
}

#if defined(_WIN32)
void replace_loaded_model_retires_old_version_windows() {
  const auto path = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  const auto root = fx::models_root(path);
  const secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment env;
  secure_frontend::NoServerDiscovery nobody;  // production answer today (Controller note 5)
  const auto v1 = fx::trusted(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 1, fx::qwen_payload("1")), secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party);
  fx::stage(root, v1, fx::qwen_payload("1"));
  const auto first = std::get<secure_frontend::PublishResult>(
      secure_frontend::publish_staged(root, secure_frontend::staging_key(v1.parsed.sha256), v1, nobody, env, true));
  // An engine holds shard 1 open without FILE_SHARE_DELETE.
  const auto shard = root.join(secure_frontend::must_rel("m/t/" + first.entry.dir + "/weights/fx-00001-of-00002.gguf"));
  HANDLE held = CreateFileW(shard.wstring().c_str(), GENERIC_READ, FILE_SHARE_READ, nullptr, OPEN_EXISTING, 0, nullptr);
  const auto v2 = fx::trusted(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 2, fx::qwen_payload("2")), secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party);
  fx::stage(root, v2, fx::qwen_payload("2"));
  const auto second = secure_frontend::publish_staged(root, secure_frontend::staging_key(v2.parsed.sha256), v2, nobody, env, true);
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::PublishResult>(second), "the new version publishes");
  expect(index_of(root).retired.size() == 1, "the open version stays retired (deletion failed after retries)");
  CloseHandle(held);
  expect(!secure_frontend::delete_unheld_retired(root, nobody, env) && index_of(root).retired.empty(), "removed once released");
}
#endif

}  // namespace acquire_cases
```

```cpp
#pragma once

#include "secure_frontend/acquire_reason.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/canonical_path.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/manifest_trust.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/model_index_model.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/models_store.hpp"

#include <functional>
#include <optional>
#include <string_view>
#include <variant>
#include <vector>

namespace secure_frontend {

// §4.8 "is it loaded?". Server discovery does not exist yet (Controller note 5).
class LoadedVersions {
 public:
  virtual ~LoadedVersions() = default;
  [[nodiscard]] virtual bool is_held(std::string_view name, std::string_view tag, std::string_view dir) = 0;
};

class NoServerDiscovery final : public LoadedVersions {
 public:
  [[nodiscard]] bool is_held(std::string_view, std::string_view, std::string_view) override { return false; }
};

[[nodiscard]] RelativePath staging_dir_path(std::string_view key);

// §4.4: (2) the regular files under `dir` are exactly the manifest's files plus
// `manifest_file_name`, which holds the exact manifest bytes; no link, junction,
// hard link, device or FIFO; (1) when hash_files, every file's size and SHA-256;
// (3) qwen layout + SP1's load_local_tokenizer_pack on <pack>/tokenizer.
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireError> verify_tree(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& dir,
                                                      const TrustedManifest& manifest, std::string_view manifest_file_name,
                                                      bool hash_files);

enum class InstallDecisionKind { fresh, already_installed, update };
struct InstallDecision final {
  InstallDecisionKind kind;
  const IndexEntry* installed{nullptr};
};
// §4.7: lower sequence → manifest_downgrade unless allowed; equal sequence with
// another digest → manifest_conflict; equal digest → already installed.
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<InstallDecision, AcquireError> decide_install(const ModelIndex& index, const TrustedManifest& manifest,
                                                                         bool allow_downgrade);

struct PublishOptions final {
  // SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS builds only (§10.1 fault injection between steps 4 and 5):
  // returning true stops publish before the index is written.
  std::function<bool()> stop_after_version_rename;
};

struct PublishResult final {
  IndexEntry entry;
  bool adopted{false};
  std::vector<RetiredEntry> still_retired;  // held by the server; deleted by a later run
};

// §4.5 steps 1-6 for .staging/<key>. Caller holds .lock. hash_files=false only
// when every file was verified in this run (§3.7).
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<PublishResult, AcquireError> publish_staged(const CanonicalRoot& models, std::string_view key,
                                                                       const TrustedManifest& manifest, LoadedVersions& loaded,
                                                                       const StoreEnvironment& env, bool hash_files,
                                                                       const PublishOptions& options = {});
// §4.5 crash recovery: a version directory named for this manifest, absent from
// the index, whose manifest.json has this digest, is re-hashed and adopted.
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<std::variant<PublishResult, AcquireError>> adopt_orphan(const CanonicalRoot& models,
                                                                                    const TrustedManifest& manifest,
                                                                                    LoadedVersions& loaded,
                                                                                    const StoreEnvironment& env);
// §4.5 step 6 / rm --retired: deletes retired versions the server does not hold; rewrites the index.
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireError> delete_unheld_retired(const CanonicalRoot& models, LoadedVersions& loaded,
                                                                const StoreEnvironment& env);
// §4.8 rm: refuses a held version unless force; writes the index first, then deletes bottom-up.
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<IndexEntry, AcquireError> remove_installed(const CanonicalRoot& models, std::string_view name,
                                                                      std::string_view tag, LoadedVersions& loaded, bool force,
                                                                      const StoreEnvironment& env);

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/acquire_publish.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/acquire_publish.hpp"

#include "secure_frontend/model_index_store.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/model_index_write.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/tokenizer_pack.hpp"

#include <algorithm>
#include <array>
#include <set>

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {

constexpr std::array<std::string_view, 4> kPackRequired{"index.txt", "dense.bin", "native_experts.txt", "conversions.json"};
constexpr std::array<std::string_view, 4> kTokenizerRequired{"vocab.json", "merges.txt", "token_type.json", "tokenizer.json"};

std::string where(const AcquireManifest& m) { return model_ref_text(m.name, m.tag); }

std::optional<AcquireError> check_layout(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& dir, const AcquireManifest& m) {
  if (m.profile != ManifestProfile::qwen) return std::nullopt;  // glm53: shard-set rule only, until SP6
  std::set<std::string> listed;
  for (const auto& f : m.files) listed.insert(f.path);
  const std::string pack = *m.launch.pack;
  const std::string tokenizer = *m.launch.tokenizer_pack;
  for (const auto name : kPackRequired) {
    if (!listed.contains(pack + "/" + std::string{name})) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::pack_layout_invalid, pack + "/" + std::string{name}};
  }
  for (const auto name : kTokenizerRequired) {
    if (!listed.contains(tokenizer + "/" + std::string{name})) {
      return AcquireError{AcquireReason::pack_layout_invalid, tokenizer + "/" + std::string{name}};
    }
  }
  auto opened = open_root(root.join(join_rel(dir, tokenizer)));
  if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(opened)) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::pack_layout_invalid, tokenizer};
  if (!std::holds_alternative<TokenizerPack>(load_local_tokenizer_pack(std::get<CanonicalRoot>(opened)))) {
    return AcquireError{AcquireReason::pack_layout_invalid, tokenizer + ": tokenizer pack does not load"};
  }
  return std::nullopt;
}

std::optional<AcquireError> retire_unheld(const CanonicalRoot& models, ModelIndex& index, LoadedVersions& loaded,
                                          const StoreEnvironment& env, std::vector<RetiredEntry>* still) {
  (void)env;
  bool changed = false;
  for (const auto& retired : std::vector<RetiredEntry>(index.retired)) {
    if (loaded.is_held(retired.name, retired.tag, retired.dir)) {
      if (still) still->push_back(retired);
      continue;
    }
    if (remove_tree_within(models, version_dir_path(retired.name, retired.tag, retired.dir))) {
      if (still) still->push_back(retired);  // e.g. Windows: the engine still has a file open
      continue;
    }
    index = without_retired(std::move(index), retired.name, retired.tag, retired.dir);
    changed = true;
  }
  if (changed) return write_model_index(models, index);
  return std::nullopt;
}

}  // namespace

RelativePath staging_dir_path(const std::string_view key) { return must_rel(std::string{kStagingDirName} + "/" + std::string{key}); }

std::optional<AcquireError> verify_tree(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& dir, const TrustedManifest& manifest,
                                        const std::string_view manifest_file_name, const bool hash_files) {
  const auto& m = manifest.parsed.manifest;
  auto listed = list_files_recursive(root, dir);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&listed)) return *failure;
  std::set<std::string> expected;
  for (const auto& f : m.files) expected.insert(f.path);
  expected.insert(std::string{manifest_file_name});
  std::set<std::string> found;
  for (const auto& entry : std::get<std::vector<StagedEntry>>(listed)) {
    if (!entry.regular || !expected.contains(entry.path)) {
      return AcquireError{AcquireReason::publish_failed, where(m) + ": unexpected entry " + entry.path};
    }
    found.insert(entry.path);
  }
  if (found != expected) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::publish_failed, where(m) + ": staged files incomplete"};
  auto bytes = read_small_file(root, join_rel(dir, manifest_file_name), kManifestMaxBytes);
  if (std::holds_alternative<AcquireError>(bytes) || std::get<std::string>(bytes) != manifest.parsed.bytes) {
    return AcquireError{AcquireReason::publish_failed, where(m) + ": staged manifest differs"};
  }
  if (hash_files) {
    for (const auto& f : m.files) {
      Sha256 hasher;
      auto counted = hash_into(root, join_rel(dir, f.path), hasher, f.size);
      if (std::holds_alternative<AcquireError>(counted) || std::get<std::uint64_t>(counted) != f.size ||
          to_hex(hasher.finish()) != f.sha256) {
        return AcquireError{AcquireReason::digest_mismatch, f.path};
      }
    }
  }
  return check_layout(root, dir, m);
}

std::variant<InstallDecision, AcquireError> decide_install(const ModelIndex& index, const TrustedManifest& manifest,
                                                           const bool allow_downgrade) {
  const auto& m = manifest.parsed.manifest;
  const auto* installed = find_model(index, m.name, m.tag);
  if (installed == nullptr) return InstallDecision{InstallDecisionKind::fresh, nullptr};
  if (installed->manifest_sha256 == manifest.parsed.sha256) return InstallDecision{InstallDecisionKind::already_installed, installed};
  if (m.sequence == installed->sequence) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::manifest_conflict, where(m)};
  if (m.sequence < installed->sequence && !allow_downgrade) {
    return AcquireError{AcquireReason::manifest_downgrade,
                        where(m) + " sequence " + std::to_string(m.sequence) + " < installed " + std::to_string(installed->sequence)};
  }
  return InstallDecision{InstallDecisionKind::update, installed};
}

std::variant<PublishResult, AcquireError> publish_staged(const CanonicalRoot& models, const std::string_view key,
                                                         const TrustedManifest& manifest, LoadedVersions& loaded,
                                                         const StoreEnvironment& env, const bool hash_files,
                                                         const PublishOptions& options) {
  const auto& m = manifest.parsed.manifest;
  const auto staging = staging_dir_path(key);
  if (auto failure = verify_tree(models, staging, manifest, kStagingManifestName, hash_files)) return *failure;
  // Step 1: files were fsynced when they were completed; fsync the directories.
  (void)sync_directory(models, staging);
  // Step 2: MANIFEST -> manifest.json (same directory, no replace).
  if (auto failure = rename_within(models, join_rel(staging, kStagingManifestName), join_rel(staging, kVersionManifestName), env)) {
    return *failure;
  }
  // Steps 3-4.
  const std::string dir = version_directory_name(m.sequence, manifest.parsed.sha256);
  if (auto failure = ensure_directory(models, must_rel(m.name + "/" + m.tag))) return *failure;
  if (auto failure = rename_within(models, staging, version_dir_path(m.name, m.tag, dir), env)) return *failure;
  (void)sync_directory(models, must_rel(m.name + "/" + m.tag));
  (void)sync_directory(models, must_rel(std::string{kStagingDirName}));
#if defined(SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS)
  if (options.stop_after_version_rename && options.stop_after_version_rename()) {
    return AcquireError{AcquireReason::publish_failed, "fault injected between publish steps 4 and 5"};
  }
#else
  (void)options;
#endif
  // Step 5.
  auto read = read_model_index(models);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&read)) return *failure;
  IndexEntry entry{m.name, m.tag, m.version, m.sequence, dir, manifest.parsed.sha256, manifest.origin};
  auto index = with_installed(std::get<ModelIndex>(std::move(read)), entry);
  if (auto failure = write_model_index(models, index)) return *failure;
  // Step 6.
  PublishResult result{entry, false, {}};
  if (auto failure = retire_unheld(models, index, loaded, env, &result.still_retired)) return *failure;
  return result;
}

std::optional<std::variant<PublishResult, AcquireError>> adopt_orphan(const CanonicalRoot& models, const TrustedManifest& manifest,
                                                                      LoadedVersions& loaded, const StoreEnvironment& env) {
  const auto& m = manifest.parsed.manifest;
  const std::string dir = version_directory_name(m.sequence, manifest.parsed.sha256);
  const auto path = version_dir_path(m.name, m.tag, dir);
  if (!exists_within(models, join_rel(path, kVersionManifestName))) return std::nullopt;
  auto bytes = read_small_file(models, join_rel(path, kVersionManifestName), kManifestMaxBytes);
  if (std::holds_alternative<AcquireError>(bytes) || to_hex(sha256(std::get<std::string>(bytes))) != manifest.parsed.sha256) {
    return std::nullopt;  // compared by digest, not by directory name
  }
  auto read = read_model_index(models);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&read)) return std::variant<PublishResult, AcquireError>{*failure};
  auto index = std::get<ModelIndex>(std::move(read));
  if (const auto* current = find_model(index, m.name, m.tag); current != nullptr && current->dir == dir) return std::nullopt;
  if (auto failure = verify_tree(models, path, manifest, kVersionManifestName, true)) {
    return std::variant<PublishResult, AcquireError>{*failure};
  }
  IndexEntry entry{m.name, m.tag, m.version, m.sequence, dir, manifest.parsed.sha256, manifest.origin};
  index = with_installed(std::move(index), entry);
  if (auto failure = write_model_index(models, index)) return std::variant<PublishResult, AcquireError>{*failure};
  PublishResult result{entry, true, {}};
  if (auto failure = retire_unheld(models, index, loaded, env, &result.still_retired)) return std::variant<PublishResult, AcquireError>{*failure};
  return std::variant<PublishResult, AcquireError>{result};
}

std::optional<AcquireError> delete_unheld_retired(const CanonicalRoot& models, LoadedVersions& loaded, const StoreEnvironment& env) {
  auto read = read_model_index(models);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&read)) return *failure;
  auto index = std::get<ModelIndex>(std::move(read));
  return retire_unheld(models, index, loaded, env, nullptr);
}

std::variant<IndexEntry, AcquireError> remove_installed(const CanonicalRoot& models, const std::string_view name,
                                                        const std::string_view tag, LoadedVersions& loaded, const bool force,
                                                        const StoreEnvironment& env) {
  (void)env;
  auto read = read_model_index(models);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&read)) return *failure;
  auto index = std::get<ModelIndex>(std::move(read));
  const auto* entry = find_model(index, name, tag);
  if (entry == nullptr) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::model_unknown, model_ref_text(name, tag)};
  const IndexEntry removed = *entry;
  if (loaded.is_held(removed.name, removed.tag, removed.dir) && !force) {
    return AcquireError{AcquireReason::models_busy, model_ref_text(name, tag) + " is loaded by the running server; stop it first"};
  }
  index = without_model(std::move(index), name, tag);
  if (auto failure = write_model_index(models, index)) return *failure;
  if (auto failure = remove_tree_within(models, version_dir_path(removed.name, removed.tag, removed.dir))) {
    // Not indexed any more, so never loaded; doctor/list --all report it as orphaned.
    return *failure;
  }
  return removed;
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`TokenizerPack` and `load_local_tokenizer_pack` are SP1's (`tokenizer_pack.hpp`; the load result is a `std::variant<TokenizerPack, TokenizerPackError>`). The `stop_after_version_rename` member exists in every build (so callers compile unchanged) but is consulted only under `SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS`.

- [ ] **Step 1: SP1 delta f** (review I1). The ownership/writability checks live in `detail::open_existing_root` (POSIX and Windows), which `open_root` and `check_creatable_root` both call, and POSIX `create_directory_within` rejects every non-private directory on the way. The policy is threaded through all of them, and the root remembers it, so directory creation and SP4's file creation inside an external root (an exFAT/FAT target mounted `dmask=000`) also work.

1. `include/secure_frontend/canonical_path.hpp` — replace the `RootPolicy` comment and enum with

```cpp
// create_private: a missing last component is created 0700 / private DACL
// (reached only from serve and init, never from validation or --check).
// external (SP4 §5): a bundle directory on any local file system, including
// removable exFAT/FAT media whose modes the OS ignores: canonicalized and held
// open like any root (links below it are still refused), but its owner and
// write access are not checked, neither for the root nor for directories
// created below it. Used only for bundle source and target directories.
enum class RootPolicy { existing, create_private, external };
```

   amend the comment above `class CanonicalRoot` to end with `A root opened with RootPolicy::external is canonical and held open the same way, but its owner and write access were not checked (bundle directories only).`, and in `class CanonicalRoot` add, after `device_id()`:

```cpp
  // True only for a root opened with RootPolicy::external.
```

```cpp
void external_root_skips_ownership_checks() {
  const auto scratch = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  const auto shared = scratch / "shared";
  secure_frontend_test::make_private_directory(shared);

  ::chmod(shared.c_str(), 0700);

         "below an ordinary root a shared-writable directory on the way is still refused");

}
```
