```cpp
#include "bundle_cases.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/bundle_io.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/model_index_store.hpp"
#include "support/acquire_fixtures.hpp"
#include "support/expect.hpp"

#include <fstream>
#include <map>

#if !defined(_WIN32)
#include <sys/stat.h>
#endif

namespace bundle_cases {
namespace {

namespace fx = secure_frontend_test::acquire;
using secure_frontend::AcquireReason;
using secure_frontend::must_rel;
using secure_frontend_test::expect;

struct Installed final {
  std::filesystem::path models_path;
  secure_frontend::CanonicalRoot models;
  secure_frontend::InstalledModel model;
};

Installed install(const std::vector<fx::PayloadFile>& payload, const bool bundle_allowed = true) {
  const auto path = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  auto root = fx::models_root(path);
  const auto manifest = fx::trusted(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 1, payload, true, bundle_allowed), secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party);
  fx::stage(root, manifest, payload);
  fx::FakeLoaded loaded;
  const secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment env;
  const auto published = std::get<secure_frontend::PublishResult>(
      secure_frontend::publish_staged(root, secure_frontend::staging_key(manifest.parsed.sha256), manifest, loaded, env, true));
  auto model = std::get<secure_frontend::InstalledModel>(secure_frontend::load_installed(root, published.entry));
  return {path, std::move(root), std::move(model)};
}

std::filesystem::path exported(const Installed& installed) {
  const auto target = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory() / "bundle";
  secure_frontend::ProgressSink quiet;
  const secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment env;
  const auto failure = secure_frontend::export_model(installed.models, installed.model, target, env, quiet);
  expect(!failure.has_value(), "export: " + (failure ? secure_frontend::format_error(*failure) : std::string{}));
  return target;
}

std::optional<std::string> digest_of(const Installed& installed) { return installed.model.manifest.sha256; }

std::variant<secure_frontend::OpenedBundle, secure_frontend::AcquireError> open(const std::filesystem::path& dir,
                                                                               const std::optional<std::string>& trust) {
  secure_frontend::ProgressSink quiet;
  return secure_frontend::open_bundle(dir, trust, secure_frontend::ManifestRules{}, quiet);
}

AcquireReason reason_of(const std::variant<secure_frontend::OpenedBundle, secure_frontend::AcquireError>& result) {
  return std::get<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(result).reason;
}

std::map<std::string, std::string> snapshot(const std::filesystem::path& dir) {
  std::map<std::string, std::string> files;
  for (const auto& entry : std::filesystem::recursive_directory_iterator(dir)) {
    if (entry.is_regular_file()) files[entry.path().lexically_relative(dir).generic_string()] = secure_frontend_test::read_file(entry.path());
  }
  return files;
}

}  // namespace

void dl02_import_bundle_publishes_same_as_pull() {
  const auto source = install(fx::qwen_payload());
  const auto bundle = exported(source);
  expect(secure_frontend_test::read_file(bundle / "STRATA-BUNDLE") == "strata-bundle 1\n", "marker written last, exact");
  const auto opened = open(bundle, digest_of(source));
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::OpenedBundle>(opened), "bundle opens with the typed digest");
  const auto target_path = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  const auto target = fx::models_root(target_path);
  fx::FakeLoaded loaded;
  secure_frontend::ProgressSink quiet;
  const secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment env;
  const auto outcome = secure_frontend::import_bundle(std::get<secure_frontend::OpenedBundle>(opened), target, false, loaded, env, quiet);
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::InstallOutcome>(outcome), "import publishes");
  const auto index = std::get<secure_frontend::ModelIndex>(secure_frontend::read_model_index(target));
  expect(index.models.size() == 1 && index.models.front().dir == source.model.entry.dir &&
             index.models.front().manifest_sha256 == source.model.entry.manifest_sha256,
         "same version directory and digest as the original publish");
  const auto again = secure_frontend::import_bundle(std::get<secure_frontend::OpenedBundle>(opened), target, false, loaded, env, quiet);
  expect(std::get<secure_frontend::InstallOutcome>(again).already_installed, "re-import: already installed");
  expect(snapshot(source.models_path / "m" / "t" / source.model.entry.dir) == snapshot(target_path / "m" / "t" / source.model.entry.dir),
         "byte-identical version directories");
}

void dl03_bundle_digest_mismatch_publishes_nothing() {
  const auto source = install(fx::qwen_payload());
  const auto bundle = exported(source);
  secure_frontend_test::write_file(bundle / "files" / "pack" / "dense.bin", "dense X");  // other bytes than the manifest pins
  const auto opened = open(bundle, digest_of(source));
  secure_frontend::ProgressSink quiet;
  const auto verified = secure_frontend::verify_bundle_files(std::get<secure_frontend::OpenedBundle>(opened), quiet);
  expect(verified && verified->reason == AcquireReason::bundle_invalid, "verify reports bundle_invalid (exit 4)");
  const auto target_path = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  const auto target = fx::models_root(target_path);
  fx::FakeLoaded loaded;
  const secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment env;
  const auto outcome = secure_frontend::import_bundle(std::get<secure_frontend::OpenedBundle>(opened), target, false, loaded, env, quiet);
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(outcome) &&
             std::get<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(outcome).reason == AcquireReason::bundle_invalid, "import refuses");
  expect(std::get<secure_frontend::ModelIndex>(secure_frontend::read_model_index(target)).models.empty(), "nothing published");
  expect(!std::filesystem::exists(target_path / "m"), "no version directory");
}

void bundle_rejects_traversal_path() {
  auto payload = fx::qwen_payload();
  const auto source = install(payload);
  const auto bundle = exported(source);
  std::string manifest = secure_frontend_test::read_file(bundle / "manifest.json");
  manifest.replace(manifest.find("pack/index.txt"), 14, "../escape.txtx");
  secure_frontend_test::write_file(bundle / "manifest.json", manifest);
  const auto digest = secure_frontend::to_hex(secure_frontend::sha256(manifest));
  const auto opened = open(bundle, digest);
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(opened) && reason_of(opened) == AcquireReason::manifest_invalid,
         "a manifest path with .. never reaches the file system");
  expect(!std::filesystem::exists(bundle.parent_path() / "escape.txtx"), "nothing outside the bundle");
}

void bundle_rejects_unlisted_file_in_files_dir() {
  const auto source = install(fx::qwen_payload());
  const auto bundle = exported(source);
  secure_frontend_test::write_file(bundle / "files" / "pack" / "extra.bin", "x");
  expect(reason_of(open(bundle, digest_of(source))) == AcquireReason::bundle_layout, "unknown entry under files/");
  std::filesystem::remove(bundle / "files" / "pack" / "extra.bin");
  secure_frontend_test::write_file(bundle / "NOTES.txt", "ignored");
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::OpenedBundle>(open(bundle, digest_of(source))), "unknown top-level entries are ignored");
}

void bundle_sha256sums_must_match_manifest() {
  const auto source = install(fx::qwen_payload());
  const auto bundle = exported(source);
  std::string sums = secure_frontend_test::read_file(bundle / "SHA256SUMS");
  sums[0] = sums[0] == '0' ? '1' : '0';
  secure_frontend_test::write_file(bundle / "SHA256SUMS", sums);
  expect(reason_of(open(bundle, digest_of(source))) == AcquireReason::bundle_invalid, "SHA256SUMS disagreeing with the manifest");
}

void bundle_rejects_hardlinked_file() {
  const auto source = install(fx::qwen_payload());
  const auto bundle = exported(source);
  std::filesystem::create_hard_link(bundle / "files" / "pack" / "dense.bin", bundle.parent_path() / "outside-link");
  expect(reason_of(open(bundle, digest_of(source))) == AcquireReason::bundle_layout, "a hard-linked payload file");
}

#if defined(_WIN32)
void bundle_rejects_junction_entry_windows() {
  const auto source = install(fx::qwen_payload());
  const auto bundle = exported(source);
  const auto outside = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory();
  std::filesystem::rename(bundle / "files" / "pack" / "tokenizer", outside / "tokenizer");
  expect(secure_frontend_test::create_junction(bundle / "files" / "pack" / "tokenizer", outside / "tokenizer"), "junction");
  expect(reason_of(open(bundle, digest_of(source))) == AcquireReason::bundle_layout, "a junction under files/");
}
#else
void bundle_rejects_symlink_entry() {
  const auto source = install(fx::qwen_payload());
  const auto bundle = exported(source);
  const auto real = bundle.parent_path() / "dense.real";
  std::filesystem::rename(bundle / "files" / "pack" / "dense.bin", real);
  std::filesystem::create_symlink(real, bundle / "files" / "pack" / "dense.bin");
  expect(reason_of(open(bundle, digest_of(source))) == AcquireReason::bundle_layout, "a symlink under files/");
}
#endif

void export_refuses_bundle_forbidden_license() {
  const auto source = install(fx::qwen_payload(), false);
  const auto target = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory() / "bundle";
  secure_frontend::ProgressSink quiet;
  const secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment env;
  const auto failure = secure_frontend::export_model(source.models, source.model, target, env, quiet);
  expect(failure && failure->reason == AcquireReason::license_forbids_bundle, "exit 10");
  expect(!std::filesystem::exists(target), "before any byte is written");
}

void export_refuses_fat32_target_for_large_file() {
  class Fat final : public secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment {
   public:
    std::variant<secure_frontend::FileSystemKind, secure_frontend::AcquireError> file_system_kind(
        const secure_frontend::CanonicalRoot&) const override { return secure_frontend::FileSystemKind::fat; }
    std::uint64_t fat_max_file_bytes() const override { return 10; }  // stands in for 4 GiB - 1
  };
  const auto source = install(fx::qwen_payload());
  const auto target = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory() / "bundle";
  secure_frontend::ProgressSink quiet;
  const Fat env;
  const auto failure = secure_frontend::export_model(source.models, source.model, target, env, quiet);
  expect(failure && failure->reason == AcquireReason::bundle_target_fat32, "bundle_target_fat32");
}

void export_rehashes_and_rejects_bit_rot() {
  const auto source = install(fx::qwen_payload());
  secure_frontend_test::write_file(source.models.join(must_rel("m/t/" + source.model.entry.dir + "/pack/index.txt")), "index ROT");
  const auto target = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory() / "bundle";
  secure_frontend::ProgressSink quiet;
  const secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment env;
  const auto failure = secure_frontend::export_model(source.models, source.model, target, env, quiet);
  expect(failure && failure->reason == AcquireReason::digest_mismatch, "bit rot is not propagated");
  expect(!std::filesystem::exists(target / "STRATA-BUNDLE"), "no marker on a failed export");
}

void export_onto_shared_writable_target() {
  const auto source = install(fx::qwen_payload());
  const auto target = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory() / "fat";
  secure_frontend_test::make_private_directory(target);
#if !defined(_WIN32)
  ::chmod(target.c_str(), 0777);  // what an exFAT/FAT mount with dmask=000 reports
#endif
  secure_frontend::ProgressSink quiet;
  const secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment env;
  const auto failure = secure_frontend::export_model(source.models, source.model, target, env, quiet);
  expect(!failure.has_value(), "export onto a shared-writable (removable-media) target: " +
                                   (failure ? secure_frontend::format_error(*failure) : std::string{}));
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::OpenedBundle>(open(target, digest_of(source))), "and the bundle opens");
}

void verify_writes_nothing() {
  const auto source = install(fx::qwen_payload());
  const auto bundle = exported(source);
  const auto before = snapshot(bundle);
  const auto opened = open(bundle, digest_of(source));
  secure_frontend::ProgressSink quiet;
  expect(!secure_frontend::verify_bundle_files(std::get<secure_frontend::OpenedBundle>(opened), quiet), "verifies");
  expect(snapshot(bundle) == before, "verify writes nothing");
}

void import_from_read_only_bundle_directory() {
  const auto source = install(fx::qwen_payload());
  const auto bundle = exported(source);
#if !defined(_WIN32)
  for (const auto& entry : std::filesystem::recursive_directory_iterator(bundle)) {
    ::chmod(entry.path().c_str(), entry.is_directory() ? 0555 : 0444);
  }
  ::chmod(bundle.c_str(), 0777);  // and removable-media modes on the bundle root
#endif
  const auto opened = open(bundle, digest_of(source));
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::OpenedBundle>(opened), "a read-only (or shared-writable) bundle opens");
  const auto target = fx::models_root(secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory());
  fx::FakeLoaded loaded;
  secure_frontend::ProgressSink quiet;
  const secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment env;
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::InstallOutcome>(
             secure_frontend::import_bundle(std::get<secure_frontend::OpenedBundle>(opened), target, false, loaded, env, quiet)),
         "import never writes into the bundle");
#if !defined(_WIN32)
  for (const auto& entry : std::filesystem::recursive_directory_iterator(bundle)) ::chmod(entry.path().c_str(), 0700);
  ::chmod(bundle.c_str(), 0700);
#endif
}

void bundle_incomplete_and_marker_rules() {
  const auto source = install(fx::qwen_payload());
  const auto bundle = exported(source);
  std::filesystem::remove(bundle / "STRATA-BUNDLE");
  expect(reason_of(open(bundle, digest_of(source))) == AcquireReason::bundle_incomplete, "no marker: incomplete");
  secure_frontend_test::write_file(bundle / "STRATA-BUNDLE", "strata-bundle 2\n");
  expect(reason_of(open(bundle, digest_of(source))) == AcquireReason::bundle_layout, "unknown marker version");
  secure_frontend_test::write_file(bundle / "STRATA-BUNDLE", "strata-bundle 1\n");
  secure_frontend_test::write_file(bundle / "MISSING", "pack/dense.bin\n");
  expect(reason_of(open(bundle, digest_of(source))) == AcquireReason::bundle_incomplete, "MISSING present: incomplete");
  std::filesystem::remove(bundle / "MISSING");
  expect(reason_of(open(bundle, std::nullopt)) == AcquireReason::manifest_untrusted, "no catalog digest, no typed digest");
}

}  // namespace bundle_cases
```

```cpp
#pragma once

#include <cstdint>
#include <string_view>

namespace secure_frontend {

// Progress and notices (fixed text, relative paths, sizes, digests, hosts only — §7.3).
// The base class is the silent sink.
class ProgressSink {
 public:
  virtual ~ProgressSink() = default;
  virtual void notice(std::string_view) {}
  virtual void manifest_selected(std::string_view /*name_tag*/, std::string_view /*manifest_sha256*/) {}
  virtual void file_started(std::string_view, std::uint64_t) {}
  virtual void file_bytes(std::string_view, std::uint64_t, std::uint64_t) {}
  // §7.3 log record inputs: relative path, size, sha256, source host, result ("ok" or a reason name).
  virtual void file_finished(std::string_view, std::uint64_t, std::string_view, std::string_view, std::string_view) {}
};

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`include/secure_frontend/bundle_io.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once

#include "secure_frontend/acquire_progress.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/acquire_publish.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/model_index_store.hpp"

#include <filesystem>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <variant>

namespace secure_frontend {

struct OpenedBundle final {
  CanonicalRoot root;
  TrustedManifest manifest;
};

// §5.1-§5.2: canonicalized once (RootPolicy::external); marker exact; manifest
// trusted by catalog digest or the typed digest; SHA256SUMS agrees (never a trust
// source); files/ holds exactly the manifest's files, no link/junction/hard link/device/FIFO.
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<OpenedBundle, AcquireError> open_bundle(const std::filesystem::path& dir,
                                                                   const std::optional<std::string>& trust_digest,
                                                                   const ManifestRules& rules, ProgressSink& progress);
// Reads and hashes every file; writes nothing (strata verify).
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireError> verify_bundle_files(const OpenedBundle& bundle, ProgressSink& progress);
// Copies each file into .staging/<k>/ while hashing it, then publishes exactly as pull does.
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<InstallOutcome, AcquireError> import_bundle(const OpenedBundle& bundle, const CanonicalRoot& models,
                                                                       bool allow_downgrade, LoadedVersions& loaded,
                                                                       const StoreEnvironment& env, ProgressSink& progress);

struct BundleTarget final {
  CanonicalRoot root;
  bool resumed{false};
};
// §5.3: license first (license_forbids_bundle, nothing written), then the target must
// not exist, be empty, or (allow_resume) hold only this manifest's interrupted pull --bundle;
// FAT refused for files above env.fat_max_file_bytes(); free space checked.
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<BundleTarget, AcquireError> prepare_bundle_target(const std::filesystem::path& dir,
                                                                             const TrustedManifest& manifest, bool allow_resume,
                                                                             const StoreEnvironment& env);
// manifest.json; then MISSING (some file absent) or SHA256SUMS and STRATA-BUNDLE last; .staging removed.
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireError> finish_bundle(const CanonicalRoot& target, const TrustedManifest& manifest);
// strata export: re-hashes every installed file while copying (bit rot is not propagated).
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireError> export_model(const CanonicalRoot& models, const InstalledModel& model,
                                                       const std::filesystem::path& target, const StoreEnvironment& env,
                                                       ProgressSink& progress);
// <to>.part while hashing; renamed to <to> only when size and digest match; else on_mismatch.
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<std::uint64_t, AcquireError> copy_verified(const CanonicalRoot& from_root, const RelativePath& from,
                                                                      const CanonicalRoot& to_root, const RelativePath& to,
                                                                      const ManifestFile& file, const StoreEnvironment& env,
                                                                      ProgressSink& progress, AcquireReason on_mismatch);

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/bundle_io.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/bundle_io.hpp"

#include "secure_frontend/bundle_text.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/model_index_write.hpp"

#include <set>
#include <system_error>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include "secure_frontend/win32_prelude.hpp"
#include "windows_security.hpp"
#endif

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {

constexpr std::size_t kSumsMaxBytes = std::size_t{8} * 1024U * 1024U;

AcquireError err(const AcquireReason reason, std::string detail) { return AcquireError{reason, std::move(detail)}; }

RelativePath files_path(const std::string& manifest_path) { return must_rel(std::string{kBundleFilesDir} + "/" + manifest_path); }

std::optional<AcquireError> ensure_parent(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& path) {
  const auto& generic = path.generic();
  const auto slash = generic.rfind('/');
  if (slash == std::string::npos) return std::nullopt;
  return ensure_directory(root, must_rel(generic.substr(0, slash)));
}

bool make_private_directory(const std::filesystem::path& dir) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
  windows_security::PrivateSecurityAttributes security(true);
  return CreateDirectoryW(windows_security::extended_path(std::filesystem::absolute(dir).wstring()).c_str(), security.get()) != 0;
#else
  std::error_code ec;
  if (!std::filesystem::create_directory(dir, ec) || ec) return false;
  std::filesystem::permissions(dir, std::filesystem::perms::owner_all, std::filesystem::perm_options::replace, ec);
  return true;  // removable media may ignore modes (§5.3)
#endif
}

}  // namespace

std::variant<std::uint64_t, AcquireError> copy_verified(const CanonicalRoot& from_root, const RelativePath& from,
                                                        const CanonicalRoot& to_root, const RelativePath& to, const ManifestFile& file,
                                                        const StoreEnvironment& env, ProgressSink& progress,
                                                        const AcquireReason on_mismatch) {
  auto opened = open_beneath(from_root, from, OpenOptions{OpenKind::regular_file, FileTrust::any, true});
  if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(opened)) return err(AcquireReason::bundle_layout, file.path + ": not a single-link regular file");
  const auto& source = std::get<OpenedFile>(opened);
  if (source.size() != file.size) return err(on_mismatch, file.path + ": size differs from the manifest");
  if (auto failure = ensure_parent(to_root, to)) return *failure;
  const auto part = must_rel(to.generic() + std::string{kPartSuffix});
  (void)remove_file_within(to_root, part);
  auto created = open_part_file(to_root, part, PartOpen::create_new, env);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&created)) return *failure;
  auto& out = std::get<PartFile>(created);
  if (auto failure = out.preallocate(file.size)) return *failure;
  Sha256 hasher;
  std::string buffer(kSha256ReadChunkBytes, '\0');
  progress.file_started(file.path, file.size);
  while (true) {
    auto read = source.read_some(buffer.data(), buffer.size());
    if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(read)) return err(AcquireReason::bundle_layout, file.path + ": read failed");
    const auto n = std::get<std::size_t>(read);
    if (n == 0) break;
    if (out.size() + n > file.size) {
      out.close();
      (void)remove_file_within(to_root, part);
      return err(on_mismatch, file.path + ": longer than the manifest size");
    }
    const std::string_view chunk{buffer.data(), n};
    hasher.update(chunk);
    if (auto failure = out.append(chunk)) return *failure;
    progress.file_bytes(file.path, out.size(), file.size);
  }
  if (out.size() != file.size || to_hex(hasher.finish()) != file.sha256) {
    out.close();
    (void)remove_file_within(to_root, part);
    return err(on_mismatch, file.path);
  }
  if (auto failure = out.sync()) return *failure;
  out.close();
  if (auto failure = rename_within(to_root, part, to, env)) return *failure;
  return file.size;
}

std::variant<OpenedBundle, AcquireError> open_bundle(const std::filesystem::path& dir, const std::optional<std::string>& trust_digest,
                                                     const ManifestRules& rules, ProgressSink& progress) {
  auto opened = open_root(dir, RootPolicy::external);
  if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(opened)) return err(AcquireReason::bundle_layout, "the bundle directory cannot be opened");
  auto root = std::get<CanonicalRoot>(std::move(opened));
  auto top = list_directory(root);
  if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(top)) return err(AcquireReason::bundle_layout, "the bundle directory cannot be listed");
  std::set<std::string> names;
  for (const auto& entry : std::get<std::vector<DirectoryEntry>>(top)) {
    names.insert(entry.name);
    const bool known = entry.name == kBundleMarkerName || entry.name == kBundleManifestName || entry.name == kBundleSumsName ||
                       entry.name == kBundleMissingName || entry.name == kBundleFilesDir;
    if (!known) progress.notice("ignored top-level entry in bundle");
  }
  if (names.contains(std::string{kBundleMissingName}) || !names.contains(std::string{kBundleMarkerName})) {
    return err(AcquireReason::bundle_incomplete, "the bundle is incomplete (MISSING present or STRATA-BUNDLE absent)");
  }
  auto marker = read_small_file(root, must_rel(kBundleMarkerName), 64);
  if (std::holds_alternative<AcquireError>(marker) || std::get<std::string>(marker) != kBundleMarkerContent) {
    return err(AcquireReason::bundle_layout, "STRATA-BUNDLE is not a version 1 bundle marker");
  }
  auto manifest_bytes = read_small_file(root, must_rel(kBundleManifestName), kManifestMaxBytes);
  if (std::holds_alternative<AcquireError>(manifest_bytes)) return err(AcquireReason::bundle_layout, "manifest.json cannot be read");
  auto trusted = trust_manifest_bytes(std::get<std::string>(manifest_bytes), trust_digest, rules);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&trusted)) return *failure;
  auto manifest = std::get<TrustedManifest>(std::move(trusted));
  auto sums_text = read_small_file(root, must_rel(kBundleSumsName), kSumsMaxBytes);
  const auto sums = std::holds_alternative<std::string>(sums_text) ? parse_sha256sums(std::get<std::string>(sums_text)) : std::nullopt;
  if (!sums || !sums_agree(*sums, manifest.parsed.sha256, manifest.parsed.manifest)) {
    return err(AcquireReason::bundle_invalid, "SHA256SUMS does not agree with manifest.json");
  }
  auto listed = list_files_recursive(root, must_rel(kBundleFilesDir));
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&listed)) return err(AcquireReason::bundle_layout, failure->detail);
  std::set<std::string> expected;
  for (const auto& f : manifest.parsed.manifest.files) expected.insert(f.path);
  std::set<std::string> found;
  for (const auto& entry : std::get<std::vector<StagedEntry>>(listed)) {
    if (!entry.regular) return err(AcquireReason::bundle_layout, "files/" + entry.path + ": link, junction, hard link, device or FIFO");
    if (!expected.contains(entry.path)) return err(AcquireReason::bundle_layout, "files/" + entry.path + ": not in the manifest");
    found.insert(entry.path);
  }
  if (found != expected) return err(AcquireReason::bundle_layout, "files/ is missing manifest files");
  return OpenedBundle{std::move(root), std::move(manifest)};
}

std::optional<AcquireError> verify_bundle_files(const OpenedBundle& bundle, ProgressSink& progress) {
  for (const auto& f : bundle.manifest.parsed.manifest.files) {
    progress.file_started(f.path, f.size);
    Sha256 hasher;
    auto counted = hash_into(bundle.root, files_path(f.path), hasher, f.size);
    if (std::holds_alternative<AcquireError>(counted) || std::get<std::uint64_t>(counted) != f.size || to_hex(hasher.finish()) != f.sha256) {
      return err(AcquireReason::bundle_invalid, f.path);
    }
  }
  return std::nullopt;
}

std::variant<InstallOutcome, AcquireError> import_bundle(const OpenedBundle& bundle, const CanonicalRoot& models, const bool allow_downgrade,
                                                         LoadedVersions& loaded, const StoreEnvironment& env, ProgressSink& progress) {
  auto lock = lock_models_root(models);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&lock)) return *failure;
  auto read = read_model_index(models);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&read)) return *failure;
  const auto& manifest = bundle.manifest;
  auto decision = decide_install(std::get<ModelIndex>(read), manifest, allow_downgrade);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&decision)) return *failure;
  if (std::get<InstallDecision>(decision).kind == InstallDecisionKind::already_installed) {
    return InstallOutcome{*std::get<InstallDecision>(decision).installed, true, false, {}};
  }
  if (auto adopted = adopt_orphan(models, manifest, loaded, env)) {
    if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&*adopted)) return *failure;
    auto& result = std::get<PublishResult>(*adopted);
    return InstallOutcome{result.entry, false, true, result.still_retired};
  }
  auto space = env.free_bytes(models);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&space)) return *failure;
  if (std::get<std::uint64_t>(space) < total_bytes(manifest.parsed.manifest) + kFreeSpaceMarginBytes) {
    return err(AcquireReason::disk_space, "roots.models needs " + std::to_string(total_bytes(manifest.parsed.manifest)) + " bytes plus 1 GiB");
  }
  const auto key = staging_key(manifest.parsed.sha256);
  const auto staging = staging_dir_path(key);
  if (auto failure = remove_tree_within(models, staging)) return *failure;
  if (auto failure = ensure_directory(models, staging)) return *failure;
  if (auto failure = write_new_file(models, join_rel(staging, kStagingManifestName), manifest.parsed.bytes)) return *failure;
  for (const auto& f : manifest.parsed.manifest.files) {
    auto copied = copy_verified(bundle.root, files_path(f.path), models, join_rel(staging, f.path), f, env, progress,
                                AcquireReason::bundle_invalid);
    if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&copied)) {
      (void)remove_tree_within(models, staging);
      return *failure;
    }
  }
  auto published = publish_staged(models, key, manifest, loaded, env, false);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&published)) return *failure;
  auto& result = std::get<PublishResult>(published);
  return InstallOutcome{result.entry, false, false, result.still_retired};
}

std::variant<BundleTarget, AcquireError> prepare_bundle_target(const std::filesystem::path& dir, const TrustedManifest& manifest,
                                                               const bool allow_resume, const StoreEnvironment& env) {
  const auto& m = manifest.parsed.manifest;
  if (!bundle_allowed(m)) return err(AcquireReason::license_forbids_bundle, model_ref_text(m.name, m.tag));
  std::error_code ec;
  const bool existed = std::filesystem::exists(dir, ec);
  if (!existed && !make_private_directory(dir)) return err(AcquireReason::bundle_target_not_empty, "the bundle directory cannot be created");
  auto opened = open_root(dir, RootPolicy::external);
  if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(opened)) return err(AcquireReason::bundle_target_not_empty, "the bundle directory cannot be opened");
  BundleTarget target{std::get<CanonicalRoot>(std::move(opened)), false};
  auto listed = list_directory(target.root);
  if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(listed)) return err(AcquireReason::bundle_target_not_empty, "the bundle directory cannot be listed");
  const auto& entries = std::get<std::vector<DirectoryEntry>>(listed);
  if (!entries.empty()) {
    bool resumable = allow_resume;
    for (const auto& e : entries) {
      resumable = resumable && (e.name == ".staging" || e.name == kBundleFilesDir || e.name == kBundleManifestName || e.name == kBundleMissingName);
    }
    if (resumable && exists_within(target.root, must_rel(kBundleManifestName))) {
      auto bytes = read_small_file(target.root, must_rel(kBundleManifestName), kManifestMaxBytes);
      resumable = std::holds_alternative<std::string>(bytes) && std::get<std::string>(bytes) == manifest.parsed.bytes;
    }
    if (resumable && exists_within(target.root, must_rel(".staging"))) {
      auto keys = list_directory(target.root, must_rel(".staging"));
      if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(keys)) resumable = false;
      else {
        for (const auto& k : std::get<std::vector<DirectoryEntry>>(keys)) resumable = resumable && k.name == staging_key(manifest.parsed.sha256);
      }
    }
    if (!resumable) return err(AcquireReason::bundle_target_not_empty, "the bundle directory is not empty");
    target.resumed = true;
  }
  auto kind = env.file_system_kind(target.root);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&kind)) return *failure;
  if (std::get<FileSystemKind>(kind) == FileSystemKind::fat) {
    for (const auto& f : m.files) {
      if (f.size > env.fat_max_file_bytes()) return err(AcquireReason::bundle_target_fat32, f.path + " is 4 GiB or larger");
    }
  }
  auto space = env.free_bytes(target.root);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&space)) return *failure;
  if (std::get<std::uint64_t>(space) < total_bytes(m) + kFreeSpaceMarginBytes && !target.resumed) {
    return err(AcquireReason::disk_space, "the bundle volume needs " + std::to_string(total_bytes(m)) + " bytes plus 1 GiB");
  }
  return target;
}

std::optional<AcquireError> finish_bundle(const CanonicalRoot& target, const TrustedManifest& manifest) {
  const auto& m = manifest.parsed.manifest;
  if (!exists_within(target, must_rel(kBundleManifestName))) {
    if (auto failure = write_new_file(target, must_rel(kBundleManifestName), manifest.parsed.bytes)) return failure;
  }
  bool complete = true;
  for (const auto& f : m.files) complete = complete && exists_within(target, files_path(f.path));
  (void)remove_file_within(target, must_rel(kBundleMissingName));
  if (!complete) return write_new_file(target, must_rel(kBundleMissingName), render_missing(m));
  if (auto failure = write_new_file(target, must_rel(kBundleSumsName), render_sha256sums(manifest.parsed.sha256, m))) return failure;
  if (auto failure = remove_tree_within(target, must_rel(".staging"))) return failure;
  return write_new_file(target, must_rel(kBundleMarkerName), kBundleMarkerContent);  // written last (§5.1)
}

std::optional<AcquireError> export_model(const CanonicalRoot& models, const InstalledModel& model, const std::filesystem::path& target,
                                         const StoreEnvironment& env, ProgressSink& progress) {
  const TrustedManifest manifest{model.manifest, model.entry.origin};
  auto prepared = prepare_bundle_target(target, manifest, false, env);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&prepared)) return *failure;
  const auto& root = std::get<BundleTarget>(prepared).root;
  for (const auto& f : model.manifest.manifest.files) {
    auto copied = copy_verified(models, join_rel(model.version_dir, f.path), root, files_path(f.path), f, env, progress,
                                AcquireReason::digest_mismatch);
    if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&copied)) return *failure;
  }
  return finish_bundle(root, manifest);
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`list_directory(target.root)` relies on SP1's default `below = std::nullopt`. On Windows, the `windows_security.hpp` include path is `src/` (same directory as `bundle_io.cpp`).

### Task 21: Fetch one file over `HopTransport` — NOW (SP1 on main)

Spec §3.3–§3.7 (one request one hop, response handling, our redirects, DNS policy + `--resolve`, retries/sources/budgets, resume rules, digest-mismatch refetch-once), §4.6 (1 GiB free-space re-checks, `ENOSPC` keeps the `.part`), §8 reasons. The transport is abstract: Task 25/26 provide the confined curl; this task's tests use an in-process `ScriptedTransport` that replays the same scenario format as the fake downloader.

**Files:**
- Create: `secure_frontend/include/secure_frontend/fetch_file.hpp`, `secure_frontend/src/fetch_file.cpp`, `secure_frontend/src/system_resolver.cpp`, `secure_frontend/tests/support/scripted_transport.hpp`, `secure_frontend/tests/fetch_cases.hpp`, `secure_frontend/tests/fetch_file_test.cpp`
- Modify: `secure_frontend/CMakeLists.txt`, `secure_frontend/tests/fetch_test.cpp` (switch to `support/expect.hpp`, include `fetch_cases.hpp`, list the new cases)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: Tasks 3–7 (`parse_source_url`, `parse_location`, `host_in`, `render_url`, `choose_pinned_address`, `TransferRequest`, `map_curl_exit`, `ResponseHeadParser`, `classify_head`, `SourceSchedule`, `RunBudget`, `AcquireClock`, `is_transient`); Task 16 (`PartFile`, `rename_within`, `remove_file_within`, `StoreEnvironment`, `kFreeSpaceMarginBytes`, `kFreeSpaceRecheckBytes`); Task 20 `ProgressSink`; Task 12 `curl_scenario.hpp`.
- Produces (in `secure_frontend_acquire`): `struct HopExit {std::optional<int> curl_exit; std::optional<AcquireError> failure; bool killed{false};}`; `class HopTransport {virtual HopExit run(const TransferRequest&, const std::function<bool(std::string_view)>& on_bytes) = 0;}` (`on_bytes` returning false means "kill the downloader now"); `class Resolver {virtual std::optional<std::vector<ResolvedAddress>> resolve(std::string_view host) = 0;}`; `class SystemResolver final : public Resolver` (`getaddrinfo`, `AI_ADDRCONFIG`); `struct FetchSettings {std::optional<ProxySetting> proxy; bool allow_private_addresses{false}; std::uint32_t connect_timeout_s{30}; std::uint32_t stall_timeout_s{120}; std::string test_cacert;}`; `struct FetchContext {HopTransport& transport; Resolver& resolver; AcquireClock& clock; const RunBudget& budget; const FetchSettings& settings; const std::atomic<bool>& interrupted; ProgressSink& progress; const StoreEnvironment& store; const CanonicalRoot& root;}`; `struct FetchTarget {RelativePath part; RelativePath final_path; PartFile& file; Sha256& hasher; bool resumed_from_disk;}`; `inline constexpr int kMaxRedirectHops = 3`; `std::optional<AcquireError> fetch_file(FetchContext&, const AcquireManifest&, std::size_t file_index, FetchTarget&)` (on success the file is fsynced, closed and renamed to `final_path`); `bool is_run_fatal(AcquireReason) noexcept`.
  - Test support `tests/support/scripted_transport.hpp` (namespace `secure_frontend_test`): `class ScriptedTransport` (records `argvs`, `configs`; `forced_exits` deque; `after_first_chunk` hook; replays `curl::Scenario`), `class ScriptedResolver` (`answers` map), `class ManualClock` (records `sleeps`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Failing tests.** `tests/support/scripted_transport.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once
// In-process HopTransport replaying the fake downloader's scenario format (Task 12),
// plus a scripted resolver and a manual clock (spec §10.1 seams).
#include "secure_frontend/fetch_file.hpp"
#include "support/curl_scenario.hpp"

#include <chrono>
#include <deque>
#include <functional>
#include <map>
#include <string>
#include <utility>
#include <vector>

namespace secure_frontend_test {

class ScriptedTransport final : public secure_frontend::HopTransport {
 public:
  ScriptedTransport(curl::Scenario scenario, std::map<std::string, std::string> fixtures)
      : scenario_(std::move(scenario)), fixtures_(std::move(fixtures)) {}
  std::vector<std::vector<std::string>> argvs;
  std::vector<std::string> configs;
  std::deque<int> forced_exits;  // consumed first: that exit status, no output
  std::function<void()> after_first_chunk;  // called once, after the first chunk is delivered (interrupt tests)
  secure_frontend::HopExit run(const secure_frontend::TransferRequest& request,
                               const std::function<bool(std::string_view)>& on_bytes) override {
    argvs.push_back(secure_frontend::build_transfer_argv("/fake/curl", request));
    configs.push_back(secure_frontend::build_config_stdin(request));
    if (!forced_exits.empty()) {
      const int code = forced_exits.front();
      forced_exits.pop_front();
      return {code, std::nullopt, false};
    }
    const auto* response = curl::select(scenario_, request.url, request.range_from);
    const std::string bytes = response == nullptr ? std::string{"HTTP/1.1 404 Not Found\r\n\r\n"}
                                                  : curl::render_head(*response) + curl::render_body(*response, fixtures_[response->body_file]);
    for (std::size_t i = 0; i < bytes.size(); i += 7) {
      if (!on_bytes(std::string_view{bytes}.substr(i, 7))) return {std::nullopt, std::nullopt, true};
      if (after_first_chunk) std::exchange(after_first_chunk, nullptr)();
    }
    return {response == nullptr ? 0 : response->exit_code, std::nullopt, false};
  }

 private:
  curl::Scenario scenario_;
  std::map<std::string, std::string> fixtures_;
};

class ScriptedResolver final : public secure_frontend::Resolver {
 public:
  std::map<std::string, std::vector<secure_frontend::ResolvedAddress>, std::less<>> answers;
  std::optional<std::vector<secure_frontend::ResolvedAddress>> resolve(const std::string_view host) override {
    const auto it = answers.find(host);
    if (it == answers.end()) return std::nullopt;
    return it->second;
  }
};

class ManualClock final : public secure_frontend::AcquireClock {
 public:
  std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point now_value{std::chrono::seconds{1000}};
  std::vector<std::chrono::seconds> sleeps;
  std::chrono::steady_clock::time_point now() override { return now_value; }
  void sleep_for(const std::chrono::seconds duration) override {
    sleeps.push_back(duration);
    now_value += duration;
  }
};

}  // namespace secure_frontend_test
```

`tests/fetch_cases.hpp` declares, in `namespace fetch_cases`: `dl03_truncated_body_rejected`, `dl03_oversize_body_rejected_mid_stream`, `dl03_redirect_to_http_rejected`, `dl03_redirect_off_allowlist_rejected`, `dl03_redirect_limit`, `redirect_query_only_in_config_line`, `dl03_private_address_rejected`, `dl03_mixed_public_private_answer_rejected`, `dl03_range_ignored_restarts_from_zero`, `dl03_content_range_mismatch_rejected`, `dl03_content_encoding_gzip_fails_digest`, `dl03_stalled_source_times_out`, `time_budget_exceeded_is_resumable`, `tls_untrusted_moves_to_next_source`, `digest_mismatch_after_resume_refetches_once`, `free_space_rechecked_while_writing`. `tests/fetch_file_test.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "fetch_cases.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/fetch_file.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/sha256.hpp"
#include "support/expect.hpp"
#include "support/scripted_transport.hpp"
#include "support/test_support.hpp"

namespace fetch_cases {
namespace {

using secure_frontend::AcquireReason;
using secure_frontend::must_rel;
using secure_frontend_test::expect;

const std::string kBody = "0123456789";  // the file: 10 bytes
const std::string kUrl = "https://fixture.example/f.bin";

class SpaceEnvironment final : public secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment {
 public:
  std::uint64_t free{std::uint64_t{1} << 40U};
  std::variant<std::uint64_t, secure_frontend::AcquireError> free_bytes(const secure_frontend::CanonicalRoot&) const override { return free; }
};

struct Harness final {
  explicit Harness(const std::string& scenario_text, std::vector<std::string> sources = {"https://fixture.example/f.bin"},
                   const std::chrono::seconds budget_total = std::chrono::seconds{172800})
      : scratch(secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory()),
        root(std::get<secure_frontend::CanonicalRoot>(secure_frontend::open_root(scratch))),
        transport(*secure_frontend_test::curl::parse_scenario(scenario_text), {{"f.bin", kBody}}),
        budget(clock.now(), budget_total) {
    manifest.hosts = {"fixture.example", "mirror.example"};
    manifest.redirect_hosts = {"*.cdn.fixture.example"};
    manifest.files = {secure_frontend::ManifestFile{"f.bin", secure_frontend::FileRole::weights, kBody.size(),
                                                    secure_frontend::to_hex(secure_frontend::sha256(kBody)), std::move(sources), {}}};
    resolver.answers["fixture.example"] = {secure_frontend::make_ipv4(93, 184, 216, 34)};
    resolver.answers["mirror.example"] = {secure_frontend::make_ipv4(93, 184, 216, 35)};
    resolver.answers["us.cdn.fixture.example"] = {secure_frontend::make_ipv4(93, 184, 216, 36)};
    (void)secure_frontend::ensure_directory(root, must_rel("k"));
  }

  // Seeds the .part with `existing` bytes (a previous run), then fetches.
  std::optional<secure_frontend::AcquireError> run(const std::string& existing = "") {
    if (!existing.empty()) (void)secure_frontend::write_new_file(root, must_rel("k/f.bin.part"), existing);
    auto opened = secure_frontend::open_part_file(root, must_rel("k/f.bin.part"),
                                                  existing.empty() ? secure_frontend::PartOpen::create_new : secure_frontend::PartOpen::open_existing, env);
    auto& file = std::get<secure_frontend::PartFile>(opened);
    secure_frontend::Sha256 hasher;
    hasher.update(existing);
    secure_frontend::FetchTarget target{must_rel("k/f.bin.part"), must_rel("k/f.bin"), file, hasher, !existing.empty()};
    secure_frontend::FetchContext context{transport, resolver, clock, budget, settings, interrupted, progress, env, root};
    return secure_frontend::fetch_file(context, manifest, 0, target);
  }

  std::string final_bytes() const { return secure_frontend_test::read_file(root.join(must_rel("k/f.bin"))); }
  bool part_exists() const { return secure_frontend::exists_within(root, must_rel("k/f.bin.part")); }

  std::filesystem::path scratch;
  secure_frontend::CanonicalRoot root;
  SpaceEnvironment env;
  secure_frontend::AcquireManifest manifest;
  secure_frontend_test::ManualClock clock;
  secure_frontend_test::ScriptedResolver resolver;
  secure_frontend_test::ScriptedTransport transport;
  secure_frontend::RunBudget budget;
  secure_frontend::FetchSettings settings;
  std::atomic<bool> interrupted{false};
  secure_frontend::ProgressSink progress;
};

std::string ok_response(const std::string& url = kUrl) {
  return "response " + url + "\nstatus 200 OK\nheader Content-Length: 10\nbody-file f.bin\nend\n";
}

class RecheckEnvironment final : public secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment {
 public:
  std::variant<std::uint64_t, secure_frontend::AcquireError> free_bytes(const secure_frontend::CanonicalRoot&) const override { return std::uint64_t{0}; }
  std::uint64_t recheck_interval_bytes() const override { return 4; }
};

}  // namespace

void dl03_truncated_body_rejected() {
  Harness h("response " + kUrl + "\nstatus 200 OK\nbody-file f.bin\ntruncate-at 3\nexit 18\nend\n");
  const auto failure = h.run();
  expect(failure.has_value() && secure_frontend::exit_class(failure->reason) == secure_frontend::ExitCode::transport,
         "a truncated body is never accepted; the run ends with a transport class");
  expect(!secure_frontend::exists_within(h.root, must_rel("k/f.bin")), "nothing renamed to the final name");
  expect(h.part_exists(), "partial bytes kept for a resume");
}

void dl03_oversize_body_rejected_mid_stream() {
  Harness h("response " + kUrl + "\nstatus 200 OK\nbody-file f.bin\nexceed-by 5\nend\n");
  const auto failure = h.run();
  expect(failure && failure->reason == AcquireReason::size_exceeded, "one byte past size kills the transfer");
  expect(secure_frontend::exit_class(failure->reason) == secure_frontend::ExitCode::verification, "class 4");
  expect(!h.part_exists(), "the file's partial bytes are deleted");
}

void dl03_redirect_to_http_rejected() {
  Harness h("response " + kUrl + "\nstatus 302 Found\nheader Location: http://fixture.example/f.bin\nend\n");
  const auto failure = h.run();
  expect(failure && failure->reason == AcquireReason::redirect_not_allowed && h.transport.argvs.size() == 1, "never followed");
}

void dl03_redirect_off_allowlist_rejected() {
  Harness h("response " + kUrl + "\nstatus 302 Found\nheader Location: https://evil.example/f.bin\nend\n");
  const auto failure = h.run();
  expect(failure && failure->reason == AcquireReason::redirect_not_allowed && h.transport.argvs.size() == 1, "host outside the allowlist");
}

void dl03_redirect_limit() {
  std::string scenario;
  for (int i = 0; i < 5; ++i) {
    const std::string from = i == 0 ? kUrl : "https://us.cdn.fixture.example/" + std::to_string(i);
    scenario += "response " + from + "\nstatus 302 Found\nheader Location: https://us.cdn.fixture.example/" + std::to_string(i + 1) + "\nend\n";
  }
  Harness h(scenario);
  const auto failure = h.run();
  expect(failure && failure->reason == AcquireReason::redirect_limit, "a fourth redirect is redirect_limit");
  expect(h.transport.argvs.size() == 4, "initial request + 3 followed hops");
}

void redirect_query_only_in_config_line() {
  Harness h("response " + kUrl + "\nstatus 302 Found\nheader Location: https://us.cdn.fixture.example/x?sig=SENTINEL-QUERY\nend\n" +
            ok_response("https://us.cdn.fixture.example/x?sig=SENTINEL-QUERY"));
  expect(!h.run().has_value(), "redirected download verifies");
  expect(h.final_bytes() == kBody, "published bytes");
  for (const auto& argv : h.transport.argvs) {
    for (const auto& arg : argv) expect(arg.find("SENTINEL") == std::string::npos, "the signed query never reaches argv");
  }
  expect(h.transport.configs.size() == 2 && h.transport.configs[1].find("SENTINEL-QUERY") != std::string::npos, "only the stdin config");
  expect(h.transport.argvs[1].back() == "us.cdn.fixture.example:443:93.184.216.36", "the next hop re-runs DNS policy and is pinned");
}

void dl03_private_address_rejected() {
  Harness h(ok_response());
  h.resolver.answers["fixture.example"] = {secure_frontend::make_ipv4(10, 0, 0, 1)};
  const auto failure = h.run();
  expect(failure && failure->reason == AcquireReason::address_not_allowed && h.transport.argvs.empty(), "never spawned");
}

void dl03_mixed_public_private_answer_rejected() {
  Harness h(ok_response());
  h.resolver.answers["fixture.example"] = {secure_frontend::make_ipv4(93, 184, 216, 34), secure_frontend::make_ipv4(127, 0, 0, 1)};
  const auto failure = h.run();
  expect(failure && failure->reason == AcquireReason::address_not_allowed && h.transport.argvs.empty(), "any denied answer");
  Harness allowed(ok_response());
  allowed.settings.allow_private_addresses = true;
  allowed.resolver.answers["fixture.example"] = {secure_frontend::make_ipv4(10, 0, 0, 1)};
  expect(!allowed.run().has_value(), "pull.allow_private_addresses = true");
}

void dl03_range_ignored_restarts_from_zero() {
  Harness h("response " + kUrl + " range 3\nstatus 200 OK\nbody-file f.bin\nend\n");
  expect(!h.run("012").has_value(), "200 to a range request: restart from byte 0, once");
  expect(h.final_bytes() == kBody, "no spliced bytes");
}

void dl03_content_range_mismatch_rejected() {
  Harness h("response " + kUrl + " range 3\nstatus 206 Partial\nheader Content-Range: bytes 0-9/10\nbody-file f.bin\nend\n" + ok_response());
  expect(!h.run("012").has_value(), "the mismatched 206 is rejected; the file restarts from 0");
  expect(h.transport.argvs.size() == 2 && h.transport.argvs[1].back() != "3-", "second request has no --range");
  expect(h.final_bytes() == kBody, "verified");
}

void dl03_content_encoding_gzip_fails_digest() {
  Harness h("response " + kUrl + "\nstatus 200 OK\nheader Content-Encoding: gzip\nbody-text gzipped!!!\nend\n");
  const auto failure = h.run();
  expect(failure && failure->reason == AcquireReason::digest_mismatch, "raw bytes are hashed; encoded bytes fail the digest");
  expect(!h.part_exists(), "deleted");
}

void dl03_stalled_source_times_out() {
  Harness h(ok_response());
  h.transport.forced_exits = {28, 28, 28, 28, 28};
  const auto failure = h.run();
  expect(failure && failure->reason == AcquireReason::stalled, "curl 28 below 1024 B/s: stalled");
  expect((h.clock.sleeps == std::vector<std::chrono::seconds>{std::chrono::seconds{2}, std::chrono::seconds{4},
                                                              std::chrono::seconds{8}, std::chrono::seconds{16}}),
         "back-off 2, 4, 8, 16 between the 5 attempts");
}

void time_budget_exceeded_is_resumable() {
  Harness h(ok_response(), {"https://fixture.example/f.bin"}, std::chrono::seconds{10});
  h.transport.forced_exits = {7, 7, 7, 7, 7};
  const auto failure = h.run();
  expect(failure && failure->reason == AcquireReason::time_budget_exceeded, "pull.max_duration_s used up");
  expect(secure_frontend::reason_info(failure->reason).resumable && secure_frontend::exit_class(failure->reason) == secure_frontend::ExitCode::transport,
         "resumable, class 3");
}

void tls_untrusted_moves_to_next_source() {
  Harness h(ok_response("https://mirror.example/f.bin"), {"https://fixture.example/f.bin", "https://mirror.example/f.bin"});
  h.transport.forced_exits = {60};
  expect(!h.run().has_value(), "the mirror serves the file");
  expect(h.clock.sleeps.empty() || h.clock.sleeps.front() == std::chrono::seconds{0}, "no retry on the same source");
  expect(h.transport.argvs.size() == 2, "exactly one attempt per source");
}

void digest_mismatch_after_resume_refetches_once() {
  Harness h("response " + kUrl + " range 3\nstatus 206 Partial\nheader Content-Range: bytes 3-9/10\nbody-text 3456789\nend\n" + ok_response());
  expect(!h.run("XYZ").has_value(), "stale staged bytes: mismatch after resume, refetched once from 0");
  expect(h.transport.argvs.size() == 2 && h.final_bytes() == kBody, "second fetch from byte 0 verifies");
}

void free_space_rechecked_while_writing() {
  Harness h(ok_response());
  const RecheckEnvironment low;
  auto opened = secure_frontend::open_part_file(h.root, must_rel("k/f.bin.part"), secure_frontend::PartOpen::create_new, low);
  auto& file = std::get<secure_frontend::PartFile>(opened);
  secure_frontend::Sha256 hasher;
  secure_frontend::FetchTarget target{must_rel("k/f.bin.part"), must_rel("k/f.bin"), file, hasher, false};
  secure_frontend::FetchContext context{h.transport, h.resolver, h.clock, h.budget, h.settings, h.interrupted, h.progress, low, h.root};
  const auto failure = secure_frontend::fetch_file(context, h.manifest, 0, target);
  expect(failure && failure->reason == AcquireReason::disk_space && h.part_exists(), "ENOSPC-class failure keeps the .part (resumable)");
}

}  // namespace fetch_cases
```

```cpp
#pragma once

#include "secure_frontend/acquire_manifest.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/acquire_progress.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/address_policy.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/downloader_command.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/fetch_schedule.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/models_store.hpp"

#include <atomic>
#include <cstdint>
#include <functional>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <vector>

namespace secure_frontend {

struct HopExit final {
  std::optional<int> curl_exit;           // absent when killed or never started
  std::optional<AcquireError> failure;    // spawn/confinement failure (downloader_*)
  bool killed{false};
};

// One downloader process per hop (§3.3). on_bytes receives curl's stdout as it
// arrives; returning false kills the downloader's process group / Job at once.
class HopTransport {
 public:
  virtual ~HopTransport() = default;
  [[nodiscard]] virtual HopExit run(const TransferRequest& request, const std::function<bool(std::string_view)>& on_bytes) = 0;
};

class Resolver {
 public:
  virtual ~Resolver() = default;
  // nullopt: name resolution failed (dns_failed).
  [[nodiscard]] virtual std::optional<std::vector<ResolvedAddress>> resolve(std::string_view host) = 0;
};

class SystemResolver final : public Resolver {
 public:
  [[nodiscard]] std::optional<std::vector<ResolvedAddress>> resolve(std::string_view host) override;
};

struct FetchSettings final {
  std::optional<ProxySetting> proxy;
  bool allow_private_addresses{false};
  std::uint32_t connect_timeout_s{30};
  std::uint32_t stall_timeout_s{120};
  std::string test_cacert;  // SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS builds only
};

struct FetchContext final {
  HopTransport& transport;
  Resolver& resolver;
  AcquireClock& clock;
  const RunBudget& budget;
  const FetchSettings& settings;
  const std::atomic<bool>& interrupted;
  ProgressSink& progress;
  const StoreEnvironment& store;
  const CanonicalRoot& root;
};

// `file` is open at its current length; `hasher` already holds those bytes
// (re-hashed this run, §3.7); resumed_from_disk = the bytes came from an earlier run.
struct FetchTarget final {
  RelativePath part;
  RelativePath final_path;
  PartFile& file;
  Sha256& hasher;
  bool resumed_from_disk;
};

inline constexpr int kMaxRedirectHops = 3;

// Reasons that end the whole run at once (no next source).
[[nodiscard]] bool is_run_fatal(AcquireReason reason) noexcept;

// Spec §3.4-§3.7 for one manifest file. On success the file is fsynced, closed and
// renamed to final_path. On failure the error carries the last source's reason; a
// verification reason has deleted the .part, a transport reason has kept it.
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireError> fetch_file(FetchContext& context, const AcquireManifest& manifest, std::size_t file_index,
                                                     FetchTarget& target);

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/fetch_file.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/fetch_file.hpp"

#include "secure_frontend/acquire_url.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/download_response.hpp"

#include <variant>

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {

struct FileState final {
  bool restarted_after_ignored_range{false};
  bool refetched_after_mismatch{false};
  bool resumed{false};
  std::uint64_t written_since_check{0};
};

struct AttemptResult final {
  std::optional<AcquireError> error;
  bool transient{false};
  bool done{false};
  bool retry_now{false};
};

AttemptResult fail(const AcquireReason reason, std::string detail, const bool transient) {
  return AttemptResult{AcquireError{reason, std::move(detail)}, transient, false, false};
}

std::optional<AcquireError> reset(FetchTarget& target) {
  if (auto failure = target.file.truncate_to_zero()) return failure;
  target.hasher = Sha256{};
  return std::nullopt;
}

AttemptResult run_attempt(FetchContext& ctx, const ManifestFile& file, const std::string& source,
                          const std::vector<std::string>& allowed, FetchTarget& target, FileState& state) {
  HttpsUrl current = std::get<HttpsUrl>(parse_source_url(source));  // validated by the manifest parser
  for (int hop = 0;; ++hop) {
    const auto remaining = ctx.budget.remaining_seconds(ctx.clock.now());
    if (!remaining) return fail(AcquireReason::time_budget_exceeded, "pull.max_duration_s", false);
    TransferRequest request;
    request.url = render_url(current);
    request.host = current.host;
    request.connect_timeout_s = ctx.settings.connect_timeout_s;
    request.stall_timeout_s = ctx.settings.stall_timeout_s;
    request.max_time_s = *remaining;
#if defined(SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS)
    request.test_cacert = ctx.settings.test_cacert;
#endif
    if (ctx.settings.proxy) {
      request.proxy = *ctx.settings.proxy;  // the proxy resolves the target; §3.5 cannot see it (§13.2)
    } else {
      const auto answers = ctx.resolver.resolve(current.host);
      if (!answers || answers->empty()) return fail(AcquireReason::dns_failed, current.host, true);
      const auto verdict = choose_pinned_address(*answers, ctx.settings.allow_private_addresses);
      if (verdict.kind != AddressVerdictKind::pinned) return fail(AcquireReason::address_not_allowed, current.host, false);
      request.pin = verdict.pinned;
    }
    if (target.file.size() > 0) {
      request.range_from = target.file.size();
      state.resumed = true;
    }
    ResponseHeadParser parser;
    std::optional<HeadDecision> decision;
    std::optional<AcquireError> body_error;
    bool writing = false;
    const auto on_bytes = [&](std::string_view bytes) -> bool {
      if (ctx.interrupted.load()) {
        body_error = AcquireError{AcquireReason::interrupted, file.path};
        return false;
      }
      if (!decision) {
        const auto fed = parser.feed(bytes);
        if (fed.state == ResponseHeadParser::State::malformed) {
          body_error = AcquireError{AcquireReason::response_malformed, current.host};
          return false;
        }
        if (fed.state == ResponseHeadParser::State::need_more) return true;
        decision = classify_head(parser.head(), HopExpectation{file.size, request.range_from});
        bytes.remove_prefix(fed.consumed);
        if (decision->action == HeadAction::restart_from_zero) {
          if (state.restarted_after_ignored_range) {
            body_error = AcquireError{AcquireReason::range_mismatch, current.host};
            return false;
          }
          state.restarted_after_ignored_range = true;
          if (auto failure = reset(target)) {
            body_error = failure;
            return false;
          }
          writing = true;
        } else if (decision->action == HeadAction::write_body) {
          writing = true;
        } else {
          return false;  // redirect or failure: the body is not needed
        }
      }
      if (!writing || bytes.empty()) return true;
      if (target.file.size() + bytes.size() > file.size) {
        body_error = AcquireError{AcquireReason::size_exceeded, file.path};
        return false;
      }
      if (auto failure = target.file.append(bytes)) {
        body_error = failure;
        return false;
      }
      target.hasher.update(bytes);
      state.written_since_check += bytes.size();
      if (state.written_since_check >= ctx.store.recheck_interval_bytes()) {
        state.written_since_check = 0;
        const auto space = ctx.store.free_bytes(ctx.root);
        if (!std::holds_alternative<std::uint64_t>(space) || std::get<std::uint64_t>(space) < kFreeSpaceMarginBytes) {
          body_error = AcquireError{AcquireReason::disk_space, file.path};
          return false;
        }
      }
      ctx.progress.file_bytes(file.path, target.file.size(), file.size);
      return true;
    };
    const HopExit exit = ctx.transport.run(request, on_bytes);
    if (exit.failure) return AttemptResult{exit.failure, false, false, false};
    if (body_error) return AttemptResult{body_error, is_transient(body_error->reason), false, false};
    if (ctx.interrupted.load()) return fail(AcquireReason::interrupted, file.path, false);
    const bool budget_gone = !ctx.budget.remaining_seconds(ctx.clock.now());
    if (!decision) {
      const auto mapped = !exit.curl_exit ? std::optional<AcquireReason>{AcquireReason::transport_failed}
                          : *exit.curl_exit == 0 ? std::optional<AcquireReason>{AcquireReason::response_malformed}
                                                 : map_curl_exit(*exit.curl_exit, budget_gone);
      return fail(*mapped, current.host, is_transient(*mapped));
    }
    if (decision->action == HeadAction::redirect) {
      auto location = parse_location(decision->location, current.host);
      if (std::holds_alternative<UrlProblem>(location)) return fail(AcquireReason::redirect_not_allowed, current.host, false);
      auto next = std::get<HttpsUrl>(std::move(location));
      if (!host_in(allowed, next.host)) return fail(AcquireReason::redirect_not_allowed, next.host, false);
      if (hop + 1 > kMaxRedirectHops) return fail(AcquireReason::redirect_limit, current.host, false);
      current = std::move(next);
      continue;
    }
    if (decision->action == HeadAction::fail) {
      std::string detail = current.host;
      if (decision->reason == AcquireReason::http_status) detail += " HTTP " + std::to_string(decision->status);
      return fail(decision->reason, detail, decision->transient || is_transient(decision->reason));
    }
    if (exit.curl_exit.value_or(-1) != 0) {
      const auto mapped = exit.curl_exit ? map_curl_exit(*exit.curl_exit, budget_gone).value_or(AcquireReason::transport_failed)
                                         : AcquireReason::transport_failed;
      return fail(mapped, current.host, is_transient(mapped));
    }
    if (target.file.size() < file.size) return fail(AcquireReason::size_short, current.host, true);
    if (to_hex(target.hasher.finish()) != file.sha256) {
      if (state.resumed && !state.refetched_after_mismatch) {
        state.refetched_after_mismatch = true;
        state.resumed = false;
        if (auto failure = reset(target)) return AttemptResult{failure, false, false, false};
        return AttemptResult{std::nullopt, false, false, true};  // §3.7: refetch once from byte 0
      }
      return fail(AcquireReason::digest_mismatch, file.path, false);
    }
    if (auto failure = target.file.sync()) return AttemptResult{failure, false, false, false};
    target.file.close();
    if (auto failure = rename_within(ctx.root, target.part, target.final_path, ctx.store)) return AttemptResult{failure, false, false, false};
    return AttemptResult{std::nullopt, false, true, false};
  }
}

}  // namespace

bool is_run_fatal(const AcquireReason reason) noexcept {
  switch (reason) {
    case AcquireReason::interrupted:
    case AcquireReason::disk_space:
    case AcquireReason::time_budget_exceeded:
    case AcquireReason::publish_failed:
    case AcquireReason::downloader_missing:
    case AcquireReason::downloader_version:
    case AcquireReason::downloader_untrusted_path:
    case AcquireReason::downloader_unsupported:
    case AcquireReason::downloader_confinement_failed:
      return true;
    default:
      return false;
  }
}

std::optional<AcquireError> fetch_file(FetchContext& ctx, const AcquireManifest& manifest, const std::size_t file_index, FetchTarget& target) {
  const auto& file = manifest.files[file_index];
  if (file.sources.empty()) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::manifest_requires_bundle, file.path};
  std::vector<std::string> allowed = manifest.hosts;
  allowed.insert(allowed.end(), manifest.redirect_hosts.begin(), manifest.redirect_hosts.end());
  SourceSchedule schedule(file.sources.size());
  FileState state;
  state.resumed = target.resumed_from_disk;
  ctx.progress.file_started(file.path, file.size);
  while (true) {
    if (ctx.interrupted.load()) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::interrupted, file.path};
    const std::string source_host = std::get<HttpsUrl>(parse_source_url(file.sources[schedule.source()])).host;
    auto result = run_attempt(ctx, file, file.sources[schedule.source()], allowed, target, state);
    if (result.done) {
      ctx.progress.file_finished(file.path, file.size, file.sha256, source_host, "ok");
      return std::nullopt;
    }
    if (result.retry_now) continue;
    const AcquireError error = *result.error;
    if (is_run_fatal(error.reason)) {
      ctx.progress.file_finished(file.path, file.size, file.sha256, source_host, reason_name(error.reason));
      return AcquireError{error.reason, file.path + ": " + error.detail};
    }
    const auto same = reason_info(error.reason).same_source;
    if (same == SameSource::restart_file || same == SameSource::next_source_file_deleted) {
      if (auto failure = reset(target)) return failure;
    }
    const auto step = schedule.record_failure(error.reason, result.transient);
    if (step.kind == SourceSchedule::Step::Kind::exhausted) {
      if (exit_class(error.reason) == ExitCode::verification) {
        target.file.close();
        (void)remove_file_within(ctx.root, target.part);
      }
      ctx.progress.file_finished(file.path, file.size, file.sha256, source_host, reason_name(error.reason));
      return AcquireError{error.reason, file.path + ": " + error.detail};
    }
    ctx.clock.sleep_for(step.wait);
  }
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`state.resumed` stays true after a range-ignored restart (200 to a range), so a later digest mismatch on that file triggers the one refetch from byte 0 even though the bytes all came from offset 0; that extra refetch is harmless (still bounded to once per file).

`src/system_resolver.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/fetch_file.hpp"

#include <cstring>
#include <string>

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include "secure_frontend/win32_prelude.hpp"
#include <winsock2.h>
#include <ws2tcpip.h>
#else
#include <netdb.h>
#include <netinet/in.h>
#include <sys/socket.h>
#endif

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {
#if defined(_WIN32)
bool winsock_ready() {
  static const bool ready = [] {
    WSADATA data{};
    return WSAStartup(MAKEWORD(2, 2), &data) == 0;
  }();
  return ready;
}
#endif
}  // namespace

std::optional<std::vector<ResolvedAddress>> SystemResolver::resolve(const std::string_view host) {
#if defined(_WIN32)
  if (!winsock_ready()) return std::nullopt;
#endif
  addrinfo hints{};
  hints.ai_family = AF_UNSPEC;
  hints.ai_socktype = SOCK_STREAM;
  hints.ai_flags = AI_ADDRCONFIG;
  addrinfo* list = nullptr;
  if (getaddrinfo(std::string{host}.c_str(), "443", &hints, &list) != 0 || list == nullptr) return std::nullopt;
  std::vector<ResolvedAddress> answers;
  for (const addrinfo* item = list; item != nullptr; item = item->ai_next) {
    ResolvedAddress address;
    if (item->ai_family == AF_INET) {
      const auto* v4 = reinterpret_cast<const sockaddr_in*>(item->ai_addr);
      std::memcpy(address.bytes.data(), &v4->sin_addr, 4);
    } else if (item->ai_family == AF_INET6) {
      const auto* v6 = reinterpret_cast<const sockaddr_in6*>(item->ai_addr);
      address.ipv6 = true;
      std::memcpy(address.bytes.data(), &v6->sin6_addr, 16);
    } else {
      continue;
    }
    answers.push_back(address);
  }
  freeaddrinfo(list);
  return answers;
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

```cpp
#pragma once
#include "secure_frontend/acquire_pull.hpp"

#include "support/acquire_fixtures.hpp"
#include "support/scripted_transport.hpp"

namespace secure_frontend_test::pull {

class FakeProbe final : public secure_frontend::DownloaderProbe {
 public:
  int calls{0};
  std::optional<secure_frontend::AcquireError> result;
  std::string label{"full"};
  std::optional<secure_frontend::AcquireError> probe() override {
    ++calls;
    return result;
  }
```
