```cpp
class InterruptingProgress final : public secure_frontend::ProgressSink {
 public:
  InterruptingProgress(std::atomic<bool>& flag, const bool& armed) : flag_(flag), armed_(armed) {}
  void notice(const std::string_view line) override {
    if (armed_ && line == "verifying") flag_.store(true);
  }

 private:
  std::atomic<bool>& flag_;
  const bool& armed_;
};

class FakeConfirmer final : public secure_frontend::Confirmer {
 public:
  bool interactive{false};
  std::deque<bool> answers;
  std::vector<std::string> questions;
  std::vector<std::string> lines;
  void say(const std::string_view line) override { lines.emplace_back(line); }
  bool confirm(const std::string_view question, const bool yes_applies) override {
    questions.emplace_back(question);
    if (yes_applies) return true;
    if (!interactive || answers.empty()) return false;
    const bool answer = answers.front();
    answers.pop_front();
    return answer;
  }
  [[nodiscard]] std::string text() const {
    std::string all;
    for (const auto& line : lines) all += line + "\n";
    return all;
  }
};

struct PullHarness final {
  static secure_frontend::CanonicalRoot make_models(const std::filesystem::path& scratch_dir) {
    secure_frontend_test::make_private_directory(scratch_dir / "models");
    return fx::models_root(scratch_dir / "models");
  }

  PullHarness()
      : scratch(secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory()), models(make_models(scratch)),
        settings{secure_frontend::default_downloader_path(), std::nullopt, false, std::uint64_t{1} << 40U,
                 std::chrono::seconds{172800}, std::chrono::seconds{120}, std::chrono::seconds{30}} {
    resolver.answers["fixture.example"] = {secure_frontend::make_ipv4(93, 184, 216, 34)};
  }

  // Serves every payload file at https://fixture.example/fx/<path>; `overrides` responses win.
  void serve(const std::vector<fx::PayloadFile>& files, const std::string& overrides = "") {
    std::string scenario = overrides;
    std::map<std::string, std::string> fixtures;
    for (const auto& f : files) {
      scenario += "response https://fixture.example/fx/" + f.path + "\nstatus 200 OK\nheader Content-Length: " +
                  std::to_string(f.bytes.size()) + "\nbody-file " + f.path + "\nend\n";
      fixtures[f.path] = f.bytes;
    }
    transport = std::make_unique<secure_frontend_test::ScriptedTransport>(*secure_frontend_test::curl::parse_scenario(scenario), fixtures);
  }

  secure_frontend::PullRequest third_party(const std::string& manifest) {
    const auto path = scratch / ("manifest-" + std::to_string(++manifests) + ".json");
    secure_frontend_test::write_file(path, manifest);
    secure_frontend::PullRequest request;
    request.manifest_file = path;
    request.trust_digest = secure_frontend::to_hex(secure_frontend::sha256(manifest));
    request.yes = true;
    return request;
  }

  secure_frontend::PullOutcome pull(const secure_frontend::PullRequest& request, const secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment* override_env = nullptr) {
    secure_frontend::PullServices services{*transport, probe, resolver, clock, override_env ? *override_env : env, loaded, ui, progress, interrupted};
    return secure_frontend::run_pull(request, models, settings, services);
  }

  secure_frontend::ModelIndex index() const { return std::get<secure_frontend::ModelIndex>(secure_frontend::read_model_index(models)); }

  std::filesystem::path scratch;
  secure_frontend::CanonicalRoot models;
  secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment env;
  secure_frontend_test::ManualClock clock;
  secure_frontend_test::ScriptedResolver resolver;
  std::unique_ptr<secure_frontend_test::ScriptedTransport> transport;
  FakeProbe probe;
  FakeConfirmer ui;
  fx::FakeLoaded loaded;
  std::atomic<bool> interrupted{false};
  bool progress_interrupt{false};  // dl04_interrupt_during_verification: interrupt at the "verifying" notice
  InterruptingProgress progress{interrupted, progress_interrupt};
  secure_frontend::PullSettings settings;
  int manifests{0};
};

}  // namespace secure_frontend_test::pull
```

`tests/acquire_pull_test.cpp` (cases declared in `acquire_cases.hpp`, listed in `main`):

```cpp
#include "acquire_cases.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/model_index_store.hpp"
#include "support/expect.hpp"
#include "support/pull_harness.hpp"

#if !defined(_WIN32)
#include <sys/stat.h>
#endif

namespace acquire_cases {
namespace {

namespace fx = secure_frontend_test::acquire;
using secure_frontend::AcquireReason;
using secure_frontend::ExitCode;
using secure_frontend_test::expect;
using secure_frontend_test::pull::PullHarness;

std::string manifest(const std::uint32_t sequence = 1, const std::string_view salt = "", const bool sources = true) {
  return fx::manifest_text("m", "t", sequence, fx::qwen_payload(salt), sources);
}

}  // namespace

void dl02_correct_digest_publishes_atomically() {
  PullHarness h;
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload());
  const auto outcome = h.pull(h.third_party(manifest()));
  expect(outcome.exit == ExitCode::success && outcome.installed.has_value(), "pull succeeds");
  const auto index = h.index();
  expect(index.models.size() == 1 && index.models.front().origin == secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party, "indexed once");
  expect(!secure_frontend::exists_within(h.models, secure_frontend::must_rel(".staging/" + secure_frontend::staging_key(index.models.front().manifest_sha256))),
         "the staging key was renamed into place (atomic publish)");
  expect(h.ui.text().find("strata run m:t") != std::string::npos, "ends with the run hint");
  expect(h.probe.calls == 1, "one probe after confirmation");
}

void dl02_byte_limit_enforced_while_streaming() {
  PullHarness h;
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload(), "response https://fixture.example/fx/pack/index.txt\nstatus 200 OK\nbody-text index \nexceed-by 100\nend\n");
  const auto outcome = h.pull(h.third_party(manifest()));
  expect(outcome.error && outcome.error->reason == AcquireReason::size_exceeded && outcome.exit == ExitCode::verification, "exit 4");
  expect(h.index().models.empty(), "nothing indexed");
}

void dl02_resume_with_range_completes_and_verifies() {
  const auto payload = fx::qwen_payload();
  const auto& shard = payload[0];
  PullHarness h;
  h.serve(payload, "response https://fixture.example/fx/" + shard.path + "\nstatus 200 OK\nbody-file " + shard.path + "\ntruncate-at 5\nexit 18\nend\n");
  const auto request = h.third_party(manifest());
  const auto first = h.pull(request);
  expect(first.exit == ExitCode::transport && first.error && secure_frontend::reason_info(first.error->reason).resumable, "first run: resumable exit 3");
  h.serve(payload, "response https://fixture.example/fx/" + shard.path + " range 5\nstatus 206 Partial\nheader Content-Range: bytes 5-" +
                       std::to_string(shard.bytes.size() - 1) + "/" + std::to_string(shard.bytes.size()) + "\nbody-text " +
                       shard.bytes.substr(5) + "\nend\n");
  const auto second = h.pull(request);
  expect(second.exit == ExitCode::success, "rerun completes");
  bool ranged = false;
  for (const auto& argv : h.transport->argvs) ranged = ranged || (argv.size() >= 2 && argv[argv.size() - 2] == "--range" && argv.back() == "5-");
  expect(ranged, "resumed with --range 5- after re-hashing the 5 staged bytes");
}

void resume_offset_ignores_preallocation() {
  // Review Focus 1: the resume offset is the .part length, never its preallocated size.
  const auto payload = fx::qwen_payload();
  PullHarness h;
  h.serve(payload, "response https://fixture.example/fx/" + payload[0].path + "\nstatus 200 OK\nbody-file " + payload[0].path +
                       "\ntruncate-at 3\nexit 18\nend\n");
  const auto request = h.third_party(manifest());
  (void)h.pull(request);
  h.transport->argvs.clear();
  h.serve(payload);
  (void)h.pull(request);
  bool offset_three = false;
  for (const auto& argv : h.transport->argvs) offset_three = offset_three || argv.back() == "3-";
  expect(offset_three, "--range 3- although the .part was preallocated to the full size");
}

void dl02_published_files_are_private() {
  PullHarness h;
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload());
  expect(h.pull(h.third_party(manifest())).exit == ExitCode::success, "pull");
#if !defined(_WIN32)
  const auto entry = h.index().models.front();
  struct stat info {};
  const auto dir = h.models.join(secure_frontend::must_rel("m/t/" + entry.dir));
  expect(::stat(dir.c_str(), &info) == 0 && (info.st_mode & 0777) == 0700, "version directory 0700");
  expect(::stat((dir / "weights" / "fx-00001-of-00002.gguf").c_str(), &info) == 0 && (info.st_mode & 0777) == 0600, "files 0600");
  expect(::stat((dir / "manifest.json").c_str(), &info) == 0 && (info.st_mode & 0777) == 0600, "manifest.json 0600");
  expect(::stat(h.models.join(secure_frontend::must_rel("index.json")).c_str(), &info) == 0 && (info.st_mode & 0777) == 0600, "index 0600");
#endif
}

void dl02_preflight_refuses_without_space() {
  class Tiny final : public secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment {
   public:
    std::variant<std::uint64_t, secure_frontend::AcquireError> free_bytes(const secure_frontend::CanonicalRoot&) const override {
      return std::uint64_t{1024};
    }
  };
  PullHarness h;
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload());
  const Tiny tiny;
  const auto outcome = h.pull(h.third_party(manifest()), &tiny);
  expect(outcome.error && outcome.error->reason == AcquireReason::disk_space && outcome.exit == ExitCode::space, "exit 6");
  expect(h.transport->argvs.empty() && h.probe.calls == 0, "before any connection or probe");
}

void dl03_untrusted_manifest_digest_rejected() {
  PullHarness h;
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload());
  auto request = h.third_party(manifest());
  request.trust_digest = std::string(64, '0');
  const auto outcome = h.pull(request);
  expect(outcome.error && outcome.error->reason == AcquireReason::manifest_untrusted && outcome.exit == ExitCode::manifest, "exit 5");
  request.trust_digest.reset();
  expect(h.pull(request).error->reason == AcquireReason::manifest_untrusted, "no digest at all");
  expect(h.transport->argvs.empty(), "no network");
}

void dl03_downgrade_rejected() {
  PullHarness h;
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload("2"));
  expect(h.pull(h.third_party(manifest(2, "2"))).exit == ExitCode::success, "v2 installed");
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload("1"));
  const auto outcome = h.pull(h.third_party(manifest(1, "1")));
  expect(outcome.error && outcome.error->reason == AcquireReason::manifest_downgrade && outcome.exit == ExitCode::manifest, "refused");
  auto allowed = h.third_party(manifest(1, "1"));
  allowed.allow_downgrade = true;
  expect(h.pull(allowed).exit == ExitCode::success && h.index().models.front().sequence == 1, "--allow-downgrade");
}

void dl03_sequence_conflict_rejected() {
  PullHarness h;
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload("a"));
  expect(h.pull(h.third_party(manifest(2, "a"))).exit == ExitCode::success, "installed");
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload("b"));
  const auto outcome = h.pull(h.third_party(manifest(2, "b")));
  expect(outcome.error && outcome.error->reason == AcquireReason::manifest_conflict, "same sequence, other digest");
}

#if defined(SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS)
void dl03_third_party_cannot_shadow_catalog() {
  PullHarness h;
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload());
  const auto outcome = h.pull(h.third_party(fx::manifest_text("fixture-qwen", "t1", 9, fx::qwen_payload())));
  expect(outcome.error && outcome.error->reason == AcquireReason::manifest_shadows_catalog && outcome.exit == ExitCode::manifest, "exit 5");
}
#endif

void dl04_interrupt_during_download_never_indexed() {
  PullHarness h;
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload());
  h.transport->after_first_chunk = [&h] { h.interrupted.store(true); };
  const auto outcome = h.pull(h.third_party(manifest()));
  expect(outcome.error && outcome.error->reason == AcquireReason::interrupted && outcome.exit == ExitCode::interrupted, "exit 8");
  expect(h.index().models.empty(), "never indexed");
  expect(h.ui.text().find("again to resume") != std::string::npos, "resume hint printed");
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::ModelsLock>(secure_frontend::lock_models_root(h.models)), ".lock released");
}

void dl04_interrupt_during_verification() {
  PullHarness h;
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload());
  h.progress_interrupt = true;
  const auto outcome = h.pull(h.third_party(manifest()));
  expect(outcome.exit == ExitCode::interrupted && h.index().models.empty(), "interrupt after the last byte, before publish");
}

void dl04_stale_staging_from_other_manifest_discarded() {
  PullHarness h;
  const std::string bogus_key(32, 'a');
  (void)secure_frontend::ensure_directory(h.models, secure_frontend::must_rel(".staging/" + bogus_key));
  (void)secure_frontend::write_new_file(h.models, secure_frontend::must_rel(".staging/" + bogus_key + "/MANIFEST"), "not this key's manifest");
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload());
  expect(h.pull(h.third_party(manifest())).exit == ExitCode::success, "pull");
  expect(!secure_frontend::exists_within(h.models, secure_frontend::must_rel(".staging/" + bogus_key)), "stale key deleted");
  expect(h.ui.text().find("stale_staging_discarded") != std::string::npos, "and announced");
  expect(secure_frontend::exists_within(h.models, secure_frontend::must_rel(".staging/.cwd")), ".cwd is never treated as a key");
}

void abandoned_partials_kept_with_yes() {
  PullHarness h;
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload("1"), "response https://fixture.example/fx/weights/fx-00001-of-00002.gguf\nstatus 200 OK\nbody-text shard\nexit 18\nend\n");
  (void)h.pull(h.third_party(manifest(1, "1")));  // leaves v1 partials
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload("2"));
  const auto outcome = h.pull(h.third_party(manifest(2, "2")));  // --yes, non-interactive
  expect(outcome.exit == ExitCode::success, "v2 installs");
  expect(h.ui.text().find("strata rm --partials m:t") != std::string::npos, "--yes never deletes; the rm command is printed");
  const auto v1_key = secure_frontend::staging_key(secure_frontend::to_hex(secure_frontend::sha256(manifest(1, "1"))));
  expect(secure_frontend::exists_within(h.models, secure_frontend::must_rel(".staging/" + v1_key)), "v1 partials kept");
}

void abandoned_staging_reported_and_offered_for_deletion() {
  PullHarness h;
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload("1"), "response https://fixture.example/fx/weights/fx-00001-of-00002.gguf\nstatus 200 OK\nbody-text shard\nexit 18\nend\n");
  (void)h.pull(h.third_party(manifest(1, "1")));
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload("2"));
  h.ui.interactive = true;
  h.ui.answers = {true};
  expect(h.pull(h.third_party(manifest(2, "2"))).exit == ExitCode::success, "v2 installs");
  bool asked = false;
  for (const auto& q : h.ui.questions) asked = asked || q.find("of partial downloads for other versions of m:t? [y/N]") != std::string::npos;
  expect(asked, "the abandoned bytes are reported and offered for deletion");
  const auto v1_key = secure_frontend::staging_key(secure_frontend::to_hex(secure_frontend::sha256(manifest(1, "1"))));
  expect(!secure_frontend::exists_within(h.models, secure_frontend::must_rel(".staging/" + v1_key)), "deleted on y");
}

void all_sources_failed_exits_3_and_keeps_part() {
  PullHarness h;
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload(), "response https://fixture.example/fx/weights/fx-00001-of-00002.gguf\nstatus 503 Busy\nend\n");
  const auto outcome = h.pull(h.third_party(manifest()));
  expect(outcome.exit == ExitCode::transport && outcome.error && outcome.error->reason == AcquireReason::http_status, "exit 3");
  expect(h.clock.sleeps.size() == 4, "503 is retried 5 times on the single source");
}

void enospc_mid_stream_keeps_part_and_exits_6() {
  class Full final : public secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment {
   public:
    std::optional<std::uint64_t> fail_writes_after_bytes() const override { return 4; }
  };
  PullHarness h;
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload());
  const Full full;
  const auto outcome = h.pull(h.third_party(manifest()), &full);
  expect(outcome.error && outcome.error->reason == AcquireReason::disk_space && outcome.exit == ExitCode::space, "exit 6");
  bool part = false;
  const auto key = secure_frontend::staging_key(secure_frontend::to_hex(secure_frontend::sha256(manifest())));
  part = secure_frontend::exists_within(h.models, secure_frontend::must_rel(".staging/" + key + "/weights/fx-00001-of-00002.gguf.part"));
  expect(part, ".part kept (resumable)");
}

void update_reuses_unchanged_files() {
  PullHarness h;
  auto v1 = fx::qwen_payload();
  h.serve(v1);
  expect(h.pull(h.third_party(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 1, v1))).exit == ExitCode::success, "v1");
  auto v2 = v1;
  v2[2].bytes = "index v2";
  h.serve(v2);
  h.transport->argvs.clear();
  const auto outcome = h.pull(h.third_party(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 2, v2)));
  expect(outcome.exit == ExitCode::success && h.index().models.front().sequence == 2, "v2 published");
  expect(h.transport->argvs.size() == 1, "only the changed file is downloaded");
  expect(h.ui.text().find("file changed: pack/index.txt") != std::string::npos, "manifest diff printed");
  expect(h.index().retired.empty(), "v1 retired and, being unheld, deleted (§4.5 step 6)");
}

void license_change_requires_interactive_yes() {
  PullHarness h;
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload());
  expect(h.pull(h.third_party(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 1, fx::qwen_payload()))).exit == ExitCode::success, "v1");
  std::string v2 = fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 2, fx::qwen_payload());
  v2.replace(v2.find("\"Apache-2.0\""), 12, "\"MIT\"");
  const auto outcome = h.pull(h.third_party(v2));  // --yes but not interactive
  expect(outcome.exit == ExitCode::declined, "a license change is never answered by --yes");
}

void pull_without_bundle_refuses_sourceless_files() {
  PullHarness h;
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload());
  const auto outcome = h.pull(h.third_party(manifest(1, "", false)));
  expect(outcome.error && outcome.error->reason == AcquireReason::manifest_requires_bundle && outcome.exit == ExitCode::manifest, "exit 5");
  expect(h.ui.text().find("--bundle") != std::string::npos && h.transport->argvs.empty(), "steps printed, no connection");
}

void confirmation_answers_parsed_strictly() {
  for (const auto* yes : {"y", "Y", "yes", "YES", " yes ", "y\r"}) expect(secure_frontend::parse_confirmation(yes), std::string{"accepts "} + yes);
  for (const auto* no : {"", "n", "no", "yep", "y es", "ye", "yes please", "1"}) expect(!secure_frontend::parse_confirmation(no), std::string{"declines "} + no);
  PullHarness h;
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload());
  auto request = h.third_party(manifest());
  request.yes = false;
  const auto declined = h.pull(request);
  expect(declined.exit == ExitCode::declined && h.probe.calls == 0 && h.transport->argvs.empty(),
         "non-interactive without --yes is No: exit 2, nothing spawned");
}

void already_installed_needs_no_network() {
  PullHarness h;
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload());
  const auto request = h.third_party(manifest());
  expect(h.pull(request).exit == ExitCode::success, "installed");
  h.transport->argvs.clear();
  h.probe.calls = 0;
  const auto again = h.pull(request);
  expect(again.exit == ExitCode::success && again.already_installed && h.transport->argvs.empty() && h.probe.calls == 0, "no network");
  auto verify = request;
  verify.verify = true;
  expect(h.pull(verify).exit == ExitCode::success && h.transport->argvs.empty(), "--verify re-hashes locally");
  const auto entry = h.index().models.front();
  secure_frontend_test::write_file(h.models.join(secure_frontend::must_rel("m/t/" + entry.dir + "/pack/index.txt")), "rotten");
  expect(h.pull(verify).exit == ExitCode::verification, "--verify detects bit rot");
}

}  // namespace acquire_cases
```

`run_pull` calls `s.progress.notice("verifying")` immediately before publishing and then checks `s.interrupted`; the harness's `InterruptingProgress` sets the flag at that notice.

`tests/bundle_pull_test.cpp` (cases declared in `bundle_cases.hpp`):

```cpp
#include "bundle_cases.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/bundle_io.hpp"
#include "support/expect.hpp"
#include "support/pull_harness.hpp"

namespace bundle_cases {

namespace fx = secure_frontend_test::acquire;
using secure_frontend_test::expect;
using secure_frontend_test::pull::PullHarness;

void pull_bundle_resumes_after_interrupt() {
  PullHarness h;
  const auto payload = fx::qwen_payload();
  h.serve(payload);
  const auto text = fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 1, payload);
  auto request = h.third_party(text);
  request.bundle_dir = h.scratch / "usb" / "bundle";
  secure_frontend_test::make_private_directory(h.scratch / "usb");
  h.transport->after_first_chunk = [&h] { h.interrupted.store(true); };
  expect(h.pull(request).exit == secure_frontend::ExitCode::interrupted, "interrupted");
  expect(std::filesystem::exists(*request.bundle_dir / ".staging"), "the bundle holds only its own staging");
  h.interrupted.store(false);
  h.serve(payload);
  const auto resumed = h.pull(request);
  expect(resumed.exit == secure_frontend::ExitCode::success && resumed.bundle_complete, "the same pull --bundle resumes and completes");
  expect(secure_frontend_test::read_file(*request.bundle_dir / "STRATA-BUNDLE") == "strata-bundle 1\n", "marker last");
  expect(!std::filesystem::exists(*request.bundle_dir / ".staging"), "staging removed");
  secure_frontend::ProgressSink quiet;
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::OpenedBundle>(
             secure_frontend::open_bundle(*request.bundle_dir, *request.trust_digest, secure_frontend::ManifestRules{}, quiet)),
         "the bundle opens for import");
  expect(h.index().models.empty(), "pull --bundle installs nothing");
}

void pull_bundle_incomplete_without_pack_sources() {
  PullHarness h;
  auto payload = fx::qwen_payload();
  h.serve(payload);
  // Shards have sources; pack files do not (the §2.7 D5 (B) shape).
  std::string text = fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 1, payload);
  for (std::size_t i = 2; i < payload.size(); ++i) {
    const std::string source = "\"https://fixture.example/fx/" + payload[i].path + "\"";
    text.replace(text.find(source), source.size(), "");
  }
  auto request = h.third_party(text);
  request.bundle_dir = h.scratch / "bundle";
  const auto outcome = h.pull(request);
  expect(outcome.exit == secure_frontend::ExitCode::success && !outcome.bundle_complete, "sourced files fetched");
  expect(std::filesystem::exists(*request.bundle_dir / "MISSING") && !std::filesystem::exists(*request.bundle_dir / "SHA256SUMS") &&
             !std::filesystem::exists(*request.bundle_dir / "STRATA-BUNDLE"),
         "MISSING written; neither SHA256SUMS nor STRATA-BUNDLE");
  secure_frontend::ProgressSink quiet;
  const auto opened = secure_frontend::open_bundle(*request.bundle_dir, *request.trust_digest, secure_frontend::ManifestRules{}, quiet);
  expect(std::get<secure_frontend::AcquireError>(opened).reason == secure_frontend::AcquireReason::bundle_incomplete, "import says bundle_incomplete");
  expect(h.ui.text().find("make_bundle.py") != std::string::npos, "the developer step is printed");
}

}  // namespace bundle_cases
```

`tests/fetch_pull_test.cpp` (case declared in `fetch_cases.hpp`):

```cpp
#include "fetch_cases.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/model_index_store.hpp"
#include "support/expect.hpp"
#include "support/pull_harness.hpp"

namespace fetch_cases {

namespace fx = secure_frontend_test::acquire;
using secure_frontend_test::expect;
using secure_frontend_test::pull::PullHarness;

void dl03_wrong_digest_rejected_prior_version_intact() {
  PullHarness h;
  const auto v1 = fx::qwen_payload("1");
  h.serve(v1);
  expect(h.pull(h.third_party(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 1, v1))).exit == secure_frontend::ExitCode::success, "v1 installed");
  const auto before = h.index().models.front();
  const auto v2 = fx::qwen_payload("2");
  auto served = v2;
  served[3].bytes[0] ^= 0x20;  // a malicious mirror alters bytes (same length)
  h.serve(served);
  const auto outcome = h.pull(h.third_party(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 2, v2)));
  expect(outcome.error && outcome.error->reason == secure_frontend::AcquireReason::digest_mismatch &&
             outcome.exit == secure_frontend::ExitCode::verification, "digest_mismatch, exit 4");
  const auto index = h.index();
  expect(index.models.size() == 1 && index.models.front().dir == before.dir, "the prior version stays active");
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::InstalledModel>(secure_frontend::load_installed(h.models, before)), "and intact");
}

}  // namespace fetch_cases
```

```cpp
#pragma once

#include "secure_frontend/acquire_progress.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/acquire_publish.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/fetch_file.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/frontend_config.hpp"

#include <atomic>
#include <filesystem>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>

namespace secure_frontend {

struct PullRequest final {
  std::optional<std::string> reference;               // name[:tag] in the catalog
  std::optional<std::filesystem::path> manifest_file;  // --manifest <local file> (no URL form)
  std::optional<std::string> trust_digest;             // --trust-manifest sha256:<hex>
  bool yes{false};
  std::optional<std::filesystem::path> bundle_dir;     // --bundle <dir>
  bool allow_downgrade{false};
  bool verify{false};
  bool discard_partial{false};
};

class Confirmer {
 public:
  virtual ~Confirmer() = default;
  virtual void say(std::string_view line) = 0;
  // yes_applies: --yes was given and answers this question. Otherwise a
  // non-interactive run answers No; an interactive one reads [y/N].
  [[nodiscard]] virtual bool confirm(std::string_view question, bool yes_applies) = 0;
};

class DownloaderProbe {
 public:
  virtual ~DownloaderProbe() = default;
  // §3.2: validates pull.downloader and runs <curl> -q --version; after confirmation only.
  [[nodiscard]] virtual std::optional<AcquireError> probe() = 0;
  // "full" | "reduced:no_landlock" | "reduced:readable:<key>"; never spawns.
  [[nodiscard]] virtual std::string confinement() const = 0;
};

struct PullServices final {
  HopTransport& transport;
  DownloaderProbe& probe;
  Resolver& resolver;
  AcquireClock& clock;
  const StoreEnvironment& store;
  LoadedVersions& loaded;
  Confirmer& ui;
  ProgressSink& progress;
  const std::atomic<bool>& interrupted;
};

struct PullOutcome final {
  ExitCode exit{ExitCode::success};
  std::optional<AcquireError> error;
  std::optional<IndexEntry> installed;
  bool already_installed{false};
  bool bundle_complete{false};
};

[[nodiscard]] PullOutcome run_pull(const PullRequest& request, const CanonicalRoot& models, const PullSettings& settings,
                                   PullServices& services);
[[nodiscard]] bool parse_confirmation(std::string_view answer) noexcept;
[[nodiscard]] std::string format_gb(std::uint64_t bytes);
[[nodiscard]] FetchSettings fetch_settings_from(const PullSettings& settings);

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/acquire_pull.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/acquire_pull.hpp"

#include "secure_frontend/bundle_io.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/bundle_text.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/catalog.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/manifest_diff.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/model_index_store.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/staging_plan.hpp"

#include <cstdio>
#include <fstream>
#include <set>

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {

PullOutcome failed(AcquireError error) {
  const auto exit = exit_class(error.reason);
  return PullOutcome{exit, std::move(error), std::nullopt, false, false};
}

std::variant<TrustedManifest, AcquireError> resolve_request(const PullRequest& request, const ManifestRules& rules) {
  if (request.manifest_file) {
    std::ifstream input(*request.manifest_file, std::ios::binary);
    if (!input) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::usage_error, "--manifest file cannot be read"};
    std::string bytes(kManifestMaxBytes + 1, '\0');
    input.read(bytes.data(), static_cast<std::streamsize>(bytes.size()));
    bytes.resize(static_cast<std::size_t>(input.gcount()));
    if (bytes.size() > kManifestMaxBytes) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::manifest_invalid, "manifest: is larger than 1 MiB"};
    return trust_manifest_bytes(bytes, request.trust_digest, rules);
  }
  const auto reference = request.reference ? parse_model_reference(*request.reference) : std::nullopt;
  if (!reference) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::usage_error, "expected <name[:tag]>"};
  return resolve_catalog_manifest(*reference, rules);
}

std::string runnable_text(const AcquireManifest& m) {
  if (m.profile == ManifestProfile::raw) return "no (test profile raw)";
  if (m.profile == ManifestProfile::glm53) return "no (needs the glm engine, SP6)";
  if (compare_versions(m.min_version, built_engine_version()) > 0) return "no (needs engine strata >= " + render_engine_version(m.min_version) + ")";
  return "yes";
}

std::string hosts_text(const AcquireManifest& m) {
  std::string text;
  for (const auto& host : m.hosts) text += (text.empty() ? "" : ", ") + host;
  return text;
}

void print_summary(Confirmer& ui, const TrustedManifest& tm, const std::uint64_t free_bytes, const std::uint64_t to_fetch,
                   const std::string& confinement, const std::string_view volume) {
  const auto& m = tm.parsed.manifest;
  ui.say("model: " + model_ref_text(m.name, m.tag) + " version " + m.version + " (sequence " + std::to_string(m.sequence) + ", " +
         std::string{origin_name(tm.origin)} + ")");
  ui.say("manifest sha256: " + tm.parsed.sha256);
  ui.say("license: " + m.license.id + " " + m.license.url + (m.license.bundle_allowed ? " (bundling allowed)" : " (bundling not allowed)"));
  if (!m.license.notice.empty()) ui.say("notice: " + m.license.notice);
  ui.say("hosts: " + hosts_text(m));
  ui.say("files:");
  for (const auto& f : m.files) {
    ui.say("  " + std::to_string(f.size) + "  " + f.sha256 + "  " + f.path + (f.sources.empty() ? "  (bundle only)" : ""));
  }
  ui.say("total: " + format_gb(total_bytes(m)) + " (" + std::to_string(total_bytes(m)) + " bytes); to download: " + format_gb(to_fetch));
  ui.say("free space on " + std::string{volume} + ": " + format_gb(free_bytes));
  ui.say("The source host sees your IP address and which files you download.");
  ui.say("runnable with this release: " + runnable_text(m));
  ui.say("confinement: " + confinement);
}

std::string resume_hint(const PullRequest& request, const AcquireManifest& m) {
  if (request.manifest_file) return "run the same strata pull --manifest command again to resume";
  return "run 'strata pull " + model_ref_text(m.name, m.tag) + "' again to resume";
}

PullOutcome fail_with_hint(const PullRequest& request, const AcquireManifest& m, PullServices& s, AcquireError error) {
  if (reason_info(error.reason).resumable && error.reason != AcquireReason::declined) s.ui.say(resume_hint(request, m));
  return failed(std::move(error));
}

std::uint64_t tree_bytes(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& dir) {
  std::uint64_t total = 0;
  if (auto listed = list_files_recursive(root, dir); std::holds_alternative<std::vector<StagedEntry>>(listed)) {
    for (const auto& e : std::get<std::vector<StagedEntry>>(listed)) total += e.size;
  }
  return total;
}

// §3.7: delete keys whose MANIFEST does not hash to the key; offer to delete
// other keys of the same name:tag (never answered by --yes).
std::optional<AcquireError> tidy_staging(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& staging_root, const TrustedManifest& tm,
                                         PullServices& s) {
  const auto& m = tm.parsed.manifest;
  const std::string own = staging_key(tm.parsed.sha256);
  auto listed = list_directory(root, staging_root);
  if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(listed)) return std::nullopt;
  ManifestRules any;
  any.max_total_bytes = ~std::uint64_t{0};
  std::vector<StagingKeyInfo> infos;
  for (const auto& entry : std::get<std::vector<DirectoryEntry>>(listed)) {
    if (entry.type != EntryType::directory || !is_staging_key_name(entry.name)) continue;  // .cwd is never a key
    const auto dir = join_rel(staging_root, entry.name);
    auto bytes = read_small_file(root, join_rel(dir, kStagingManifestName), kManifestMaxBytes);
    const bool matches = std::holds_alternative<std::string>(bytes) &&
                         staging_key(to_hex(sha256(std::get<std::string>(bytes)))) == entry.name;
    auto parsed = matches ? parse_manifest(std::get<std::string>(bytes), any) : ManifestParseResult{ManifestIssue{}};
    if (!matches || !std::holds_alternative<ParsedManifest>(parsed)) {
      if (auto failure = remove_tree_within(root, dir)) return failure;
      s.ui.say("stale_staging_discarded: " + entry.name);
      continue;
    }
    if (entry.name == own) continue;
    const auto& other = std::get<ParsedManifest>(parsed).manifest;
    infos.push_back(StagingKeyInfo{entry.name, other.name, other.tag, tree_bytes(root, dir)});
  }
  const auto abandoned = abandoned_keys(infos, own, m.name, m.tag);
  if (abandoned.empty()) return std::nullopt;
  std::uint64_t bytes = 0;
  for (const auto& key : abandoned) bytes += key.bytes;
  const std::string ref = model_ref_text(m.name, m.tag);
  if (s.ui.confirm("Delete " + format_gb(bytes) + " of partial downloads for other versions of " + ref + "? [y/N]", false)) {
    for (const auto& key : abandoned) {
      if (auto failure = remove_tree_within(root, join_rel(staging_root, key.key))) return failure;
    }
  } else {
    s.ui.say("kept; delete them later with: strata rm --partials " + ref);
  }
  return std::nullopt;
}

struct Work final {
  std::size_t file_index;
  FileStep step;
  std::uint64_t existing;
  bool reuse;
};

// Brings one file of `m` to <staging>/<path> (verified): re-hash, resume, reuse or fetch.
std::optional<AcquireError> acquire_file(const CanonicalRoot& root, const RelativePath& staging, const TrustedManifest& tm, const Work& work,
                                         const std::optional<InstalledModel>& installed, const CanonicalRoot& models,
                                         FetchContext& context, PullServices& s) {
  const auto& f = tm.parsed.manifest.files[work.file_index];
  const auto final_path = join_rel(staging, f.path);
  const auto part = must_rel(final_path.generic() + std::string{kPartSuffix});
  if (const auto slash = final_path.generic().rfind('/'); slash != std::string::npos) {
    if (auto failure = ensure_directory(root, must_rel(final_path.generic().substr(0, slash)))) return failure;
  }
  FileStep step = work.step;
  if (step == FileStep::rehash_verified_candidate) {
    Sha256 hasher;
    auto counted = hash_into(root, final_path, hasher, f.size);
    if (std::holds_alternative<std::uint64_t>(counted) && std::get<std::uint64_t>(counted) == f.size && to_hex(hasher.finish()) == f.sha256) {
      return std::nullopt;
    }
    (void)remove_file_within(root, final_path);
    step = FileStep::fetch_from_zero;
  }
  Sha256 hasher;
  std::uint64_t existing = 0;
  if (step == FileStep::rehash_then_resume || step == FileStep::rehash_complete_part) {
    auto counted = hash_into(root, part, hasher, f.size);  // before the writer opens it (Windows share modes)
    existing = std::holds_alternative<std::uint64_t>(counted) ? std::get<std::uint64_t>(counted) : 0;
    if (step == FileStep::rehash_complete_part && existing == f.size) {
      if (to_hex(Sha256{hasher}.finish()) == f.sha256) return rename_within(root, part, final_path, s.store);
      (void)remove_file_within(root, part);
      hasher = Sha256{};
      existing = 0;
    }
  }
  if (existing == 0 && work.reuse && installed) {
    auto copied = copy_verified(models, join_rel(installed->version_dir, f.path), root, final_path, f, s.store, s.progress,
                                AcquireReason::digest_mismatch);
    if (std::holds_alternative<std::uint64_t>(copied)) return std::nullopt;  // §6.1: unchanged files are copied, not downloaded
  }
  if (f.sources.empty()) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::manifest_requires_bundle, f.path};
  auto opened = open_part_file(root, part, existing > 0 ? PartOpen::open_existing : PartOpen::create_new, s.store);
  if (std::holds_alternative<AcquireError>(opened) && existing == 0) {
    (void)remove_file_within(root, part);
    opened = open_part_file(root, part, PartOpen::create_new, s.store);
  }
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&opened)) return *failure;
  auto& file = std::get<PartFile>(opened);
  if (file.size() != existing) {  // the .part changed between hashing and opening: start over
    if (auto failure = file.truncate_to_zero()) return failure;
    hasher = Sha256{};
    existing = 0;
  }
  if (auto failure = file.preallocate(f.size)) return failure;
  FetchTarget target{part, final_path, file, hasher, existing > 0};
  return fetch_file(context, tm.parsed.manifest, work.file_index, target);
}

}  // namespace

bool parse_confirmation(std::string_view answer) noexcept {
  while (!answer.empty() && (answer.front() == ' ' || answer.front() == '\t' || answer.front() == '\r' || answer.front() == '\n')) answer.remove_prefix(1);
  while (!answer.empty() && (answer.back() == ' ' || answer.back() == '\t' || answer.back() == '\r' || answer.back() == '\n')) answer.remove_suffix(1);
  const auto lower = ascii_lower(answer);
  return lower == "y" || lower == "yes";
}

std::string format_gb(const std::uint64_t bytes) {
  char buffer[32];
  std::snprintf(buffer, sizeof buffer, "%.1f GB", static_cast<double>(bytes) / 1e9);
  return buffer;
}

FetchSettings fetch_settings_from(const PullSettings& settings) {
  FetchSettings fetch;
  fetch.proxy = settings.proxy;
  fetch.allow_private_addresses = settings.allow_private_addresses;
  fetch.connect_timeout_s = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(settings.connect_timeout.count());
  fetch.stall_timeout_s = static_cast<std::uint32_t>(settings.stall_timeout.count());
  return fetch;
}

PullOutcome run_pull(const PullRequest& request, const CanonicalRoot& models, const PullSettings& settings, PullServices& s) {
  ManifestRules rules;
  rules.max_total_bytes = settings.max_total_bytes;
  auto resolved = resolve_request(request, rules);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&resolved)) return failed(*failure);
  const auto tm = std::get<TrustedManifest>(std::move(resolved));
  const auto& m = tm.parsed.manifest;
  const std::string ref = model_ref_text(m.name, m.tag);
  s.progress.manifest_selected(ref, tm.parsed.sha256);
  const bool to_bundle = request.bundle_dir.has_value();
  if (!to_bundle && has_sourceless_files(m)) {
    s.ui.say("this manifest pins files that have no download source (the Qwen pack, D5); install it from a bundle:");
    s.ui.say("  1. strata pull " + ref + " --bundle <dir>   (online machine; fetches every file that has a source)");
    s.ui.say("  2. python3 -I tools/secure/make_bundle.py ...   (developer machine; builds the missing pack files)");
    s.ui.say("  3. strata import <dir>");
    return failed(AcquireError{AcquireReason::manifest_requires_bundle, ref});
  }

  // Destination: roots.models (under .lock), or the bundle directory.
  std::optional<ModelsLock> lock;
  std::optional<BundleTarget> bundle;
  std::optional<InstalledModel> installed;
  std::optional<ManifestDiff> diff;
  if (to_bundle) {
    auto prepared = prepare_bundle_target(*request.bundle_dir, tm, true, s.store);
    if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&prepared)) return failed(*failure);
    bundle.emplace(std::get<BundleTarget>(std::move(prepared)));
  } else {
    auto locked = lock_models_root(models);
    if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&locked)) return failed(*failure);
    lock.emplace(std::get<ModelsLock>(std::move(locked)));
    auto read = read_model_index(models);
    if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&read)) return failed(*failure);
    auto decision = decide_install(std::get<ModelIndex>(read), tm, request.allow_downgrade);
    if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&decision)) return failed(*failure);
    const auto& d = std::get<InstallDecision>(decision);
    if (d.kind == InstallDecisionKind::already_installed) {
      if (!request.verify) {
        s.ui.say("already installed: " + ref);
        return PullOutcome{ExitCode::success, std::nullopt, *d.installed, true, false};
      }
      auto loaded_model = load_installed(models, *d.installed);
      if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&loaded_model)) return failed(*failure);
      if (auto failure = verify_tree(models, std::get<InstalledModel>(loaded_model).version_dir, tm, kVersionManifestName, true)) {
        return failed(*failure);
      }
      s.ui.say("verified: " + ref);
      return PullOutcome{ExitCode::success, std::nullopt, *d.installed, true, false};
    }
    if (auto adopted = adopt_orphan(models, tm, s.loaded, s.store)) {
      if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&*adopted)) return failed(*failure);
      s.ui.say("adopted an earlier, interrupted publish of " + ref);
      return PullOutcome{ExitCode::success, std::nullopt, std::get<PublishResult>(*adopted).entry, false, false};
    }
    if (d.kind == InstallDecisionKind::update) {
      auto loaded_model = load_installed(models, *d.installed);
      if (auto* model = std::get_if<InstalledModel>(&loaded_model)) {
        installed.emplace(std::move(*model));
        diff = diff_manifests(installed->manifest.manifest, m);
        for (const auto& line : diff->lines) s.ui.say(line);
      }
    }
  }
  const CanonicalRoot& root = bundle ? bundle->root : models;

  // Staging (§3.7).
  const auto staging_root = must_rel(std::string{kStagingDirName});
  if (auto failure = ensure_directory(root, staging_root)) return failed(*failure);
  if (!bundle) {
    if (auto failure = make_read_only_directory(root, must_rel(std::string{kStagingDirName} + "/" + std::string{kStagingCwdName}))) {
      return failed(*failure);
    }
  }
  const std::string key = staging_key(tm.parsed.sha256);
  const auto staging = staging_dir_path(key);
  if (request.discard_partial) {
    if (auto failure = remove_tree_within(root, staging)) return failed(*failure);
  }
  if (auto failure = tidy_staging(root, staging_root, tm, s)) return failed(*failure);
  std::vector<StagedEntry> staged;
  if (exists_within(root, staging)) {
    auto listed = list_files_recursive(root, staging);
    if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&listed)) return failed(*failure);
    staged = std::get<std::vector<StagedEntry>>(std::move(listed));
  }
  auto plan = plan_staging(m, staged);
  for (const auto& path : plan.remove) (void)remove_file_within(root, join_rel(staging, path));
  std::set<std::size_t> reusable;
  if (diff) reusable.insert(diff->reusable_files.begin(), diff->reusable_files.end());
  std::vector<Work> work;
  std::uint64_t to_fetch = 0;
  std::uint64_t reused = 0;
  for (const auto& p : plan.files) {
    const auto& f = m.files[p.file_index];
    if (bundle && exists_within(root, must_rel(std::string{kBundleFilesDir} + "/" + f.path))) continue;  // done in an earlier run
    if (bundle && f.sources.empty()) continue;  // §2.7: listed in MISSING
    const bool reuse = reusable.contains(p.file_index) && p.step == FileStep::fetch_from_zero;
    if (reuse) reused += f.size;
    else if (p.step == FileStep::fetch_from_zero || p.step == FileStep::rehash_then_resume) to_fetch += f.size - p.existing_bytes;
    work.push_back(Work{p.file_index, p.step, p.existing_bytes, reuse});
  }

  // Preflight (§4.6), summary, confirmation (§1.3).
  auto space = s.store.free_bytes(root);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&space)) return failed(*failure);
  const std::uint64_t free_bytes = std::get<std::uint64_t>(space);
  const std::uint64_t needed = total_bytes(m) > plan.staged_bytes + reused ? total_bytes(m) - plan.staged_bytes - reused : 0;
  if (free_bytes < needed + kFreeSpaceMarginBytes) {
    return failed(AcquireError{AcquireReason::disk_space, "need " + format_gb(needed) + " plus 1 GiB, have " + format_gb(free_bytes)});
  }
  print_summary(s.ui, tm, free_bytes, to_fetch, s.probe.confinement(), bundle ? "the bundle volume" : "roots.models");
  if (diff && diff->license_changed &&
      !s.ui.confirm("The license of " + ref + " changes (" + installed->manifest.manifest.license.id + " -> " + m.license.id +
                        "). Continue? [y/N]", false)) {
    return failed(AcquireError{AcquireReason::declined, ref});
  }
  if (to_fetch > 0 || !work.empty()) {
    if (!s.ui.confirm("Download " + format_gb(to_fetch) + " from " + hosts_text(m) + "? [y/N]", request.yes)) {
      return failed(AcquireError{AcquireReason::declined, ref});
    }
  }
  if (to_fetch > 0) {
    if (auto failure = s.probe.probe()) return failed(*failure);  // §3.2: after confirmation, before the first transfer
  }

  // Acquire every file.
  if (auto failure = ensure_directory(root, staging)) return failed(*failure);
  if (!exists_within(root, join_rel(staging, kStagingManifestName))) {
    if (auto failure = write_new_file(root, join_rel(staging, kStagingManifestName), tm.parsed.bytes)) return failed(*failure);
  }
  const RunBudget budget(s.clock.now(), settings.max_duration);
  FetchSettings fetch = fetch_settings_from(settings);
  FetchContext context{s.transport, s.resolver, s.clock, budget, fetch, s.interrupted, s.progress, s.store, root};
  for (const auto& w : work) {
    if (s.interrupted.load()) return fail_with_hint(request, m, s, AcquireError{AcquireReason::interrupted, ref});
    if (auto failure = acquire_file(root, staging, tm, w, installed, models, context, s)) return fail_with_hint(request, m, s, *failure);
    if (bundle) {
      const auto& f = m.files[w.file_index];
      const auto into = must_rel(std::string{kBundleFilesDir} + "/" + f.path);
      if (const auto slash = into.generic().rfind('/'); slash != std::string::npos) (void)ensure_directory(root, must_rel(into.generic().substr(0, slash)));
      if (auto failure = rename_within(root, join_rel(staging, f.path), into, s.store)) return failed(*failure);
    }
  }
  s.progress.notice("verifying");
  if (s.interrupted.load()) return fail_with_hint(request, m, s, AcquireError{AcquireReason::interrupted, ref});

  if (bundle) {
    if (auto failure = finish_bundle(root, tm)) return failed(*failure);
    const bool complete = exists_within(root, must_rel(kBundleMarkerName));
    if (!complete) {
      s.ui.say("bundle incomplete: files without a source are listed in MISSING; complete it with tools/secure/make_bundle.py");
    } else {
      s.ui.say("bundle complete: strata import " + request.bundle_dir->string());
    }
    return PullOutcome{ExitCode::success, std::nullopt, std::nullopt, false, complete};
  }
  auto published = publish_staged(models, key, tm, s.loaded, s.store, false);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&published)) return failed(*failure);
  const auto& result = std::get<PublishResult>(published);
  for (const auto& retired : result.still_retired) {
    s.ui.say("retired (still loaded): " + model_ref_text(retired.name, retired.tag) + " " + retired.dir);
  }
  s.ui.say("installed " + ref + "; start it with: strata run " + ref);
  return PullOutcome{ExitCode::success, std::nullopt, result.entry, false, false};
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`Sha256{hasher}.finish()` copies the hasher (it is copyable), so the original is untouched when the complete `.part` turns out wrong.

Windows DACL case, added to `acquire_pull_test.cpp` under `#if defined(_WIN32)` and registered `LABELS windows-runtime`:

```cpp
void dl02_published_files_have_private_dacl_windows() {
  PullHarness h;
  h.serve(fx::qwen_payload());
  expect(h.pull(h.third_party(manifest())).exit == ExitCode::success, "pull");
  const auto entry = h.index().models.front();
  const auto file = h.models.join(secure_frontend::must_rel("m/t/" + entry.dir + "/weights/fx-00001-of-00002.gguf"));
  PACL dacl = nullptr;
  PSECURITY_DESCRIPTOR descriptor = nullptr;
  expect(GetNamedSecurityInfoW(file.wstring().c_str(), SE_FILE_OBJECT, DACL_SECURITY_INFORMATION, nullptr, nullptr, &dacl, nullptr,
                               &descriptor) == ERROR_SUCCESS, "DACL readable");
  SECURITY_DESCRIPTOR_CONTROL control = 0;
  DWORD revision = 0;
  GetSecurityDescriptorControl(descriptor, &control, &revision);
  expect((control & SE_DACL_PROTECTED) != 0, "protected DACL (nothing inherited)");
  PSID system = nullptr;
  ConvertStringSidToSidW(L"S-1-5-18", &system);
  HANDLE token = nullptr;
  OpenProcessToken(GetCurrentProcess(), TOKEN_QUERY, &token);
  DWORD size = 0;
  GetTokenInformation(token, TokenUser, nullptr, 0, &size);
  std::vector<unsigned char> user(size);
  GetTokenInformation(token, TokenUser, user.data(), size, &size);
  CloseHandle(token);
  const PSID self = reinterpret_cast<TOKEN_USER*>(user.data())->User.Sid;
  for (DWORD i = 0; dacl != nullptr && i < dacl->AceCount; ++i) {
    void* raw = nullptr;
    GetAce(dacl, i, &raw);
    const auto* ace = static_cast<ACCESS_ALLOWED_ACE*>(raw);
    const PSID sid = reinterpret_cast<PSID>(const_cast<DWORD*>(&ace->SidStart));
    expect(EqualSid(sid, self) || EqualSid(sid, system), "only the user and SYSTEM are granted access");
  }
  LocalFree(system);
  LocalFree(descriptor);
}
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Failing tests.** `tests/support/cli_harness.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once
#include "secure_frontend/strata_cli.hpp"
#include "support/acquire_fixtures.hpp"

#include <sstream>

namespace secure_frontend_test::cli {

// A private scratch tree with roots.models, a tokenizer root, a fake engine
// file and a config whose pull.downloader does not exist (local verbs never need it).
struct CliHarness final {
  CliHarness() : scratch(secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory()) {
    models_path = scratch / "models";
    secure_frontend_test::make_private_directory(models_path);
    secure_frontend_test::make_private_directory(scratch / "tokenizer");
    secure_frontend_test::make_private_directory(scratch / "bin");
    secure_frontend_test::write_file(scratch / "bin" / "engine", "#!/bin/sh\n", 0700);
    config = scratch / "config.json";
    write_config("");
  }

  // An absolute path that does not exist (local verbs never touch the downloader).
  std::string missing_downloader() const {
    std::string out;
    for (const char c : std::filesystem::absolute(scratch / "missing" / "curl").generic_string()) out += c == '\\' ? std::string{"\\\\"} : std::string(1, c);
    return "\"" + out + "\"";
  }

  void write_config(const std::string& extra_members) {
    const auto quote = [](const std::filesystem::path& p) {
      std::string text = std::filesystem::canonical(p).generic_string();
      std::string out;
      for (const char c : text) out += c == '\\' ? std::string{"\\\\"} : std::string(1, c);
      return "\"" + out + "\"";
    };
    secure_frontend_test::write_file(config, "{\n  \"schema_version\": 1,\n  \"roots\": {\"models\": " + quote(models_path) +
                                                 ", \"tokenizer_pack\": " + quote(scratch / "tokenizer") + "},\n"
                                                 "  \"token\": {\"source\": \"env:STRATA_TEST_TOKEN\"},\n"
                                                 "  \"engine\": {\"executable\": " + quote(scratch / "bin" / "engine") +
                                                 ", \"model_id\": \"m\"},\n"
                                                 "  \"pull\": {\"downloader\": " + missing_downloader() + extra_members + "}\n}\n");
  }

  int run(std::vector<std::string> args, const std::string& input = "", const bool interactive = false) {
    std::vector<std::string> full{"--config", config.string()};
    full.insert(full.end(), args.begin(), args.end());
    return run_raw(full, input, interactive);
  }

  int run_raw(const std::vector<std::string>& args, const std::string& input = "", const bool interactive = false) {
    std::istringstream in(input);
    std::ostringstream o;
    std::ostringstream e;
    secure_frontend::CliStreams streams{in, o, e, interactive};
    const int code = secure_frontend::run_strata(args, streams, services);
    out = o.str();
    err = e.str();
    return code;
  }

  secure_frontend::CanonicalRoot models() const { return secure_frontend_test::acquire::models_root(models_path); }

  std::filesystem::path scratch;
  std::filesystem::path models_path;
  std::filesystem::path config;
  secure_frontend::CliServices services;
  std::string out;
  std::string err;
};

// Publishes a fixture model <name>:<tag> (sequence `sequence`) straight into the harness's roots.models.
inline secure_frontend::IndexEntry install(CliHarness& h, const std::uint32_t sequence = 1, const std::string_view salt = "",
                                           const secure_frontend::ModelOrigin origin = secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party,
                                           const std::string_view name = "m", const std::string_view tag = "t") {
  namespace fx = secure_frontend_test::acquire;
  const auto root = h.models();
  const auto manifest = fx::trusted(fx::manifest_text(name, tag, sequence, fx::qwen_payload(salt)), origin);
  fx::stage(root, manifest, fx::qwen_payload(salt));
  fx::FakeLoaded nobody;
  const secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment env;
  return std::get<secure_frontend::PublishResult>(
             secure_frontend::publish_staged(root, secure_frontend::staging_key(manifest.parsed.sha256), manifest, nobody, env, true))
      .entry;
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend_test::cli
```

`tests/acquire_cli_test.cpp` (declare in `acquire_cases.hpp`, list in `main`):

```cpp
#include "acquire_cases.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/model_index_store.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/test_hooks.hpp"
#include "support/cli_harness.hpp"
#include "support/expect.hpp"

namespace acquire_cases {
namespace {

using secure_frontend_test::expect;
using secure_frontend_test::cli::CliHarness;

class HeldLoaded final : public secure_frontend::LoadedVersions {
 public:
  explicit HeldLoaded(std::string held) : held_(std::move(held)) {}
  bool is_held(const std::string_view name, const std::string_view tag, const std::string_view dir) override {
    return held_ == std::string{name} + "/" + std::string{tag} + "/" + std::string{dir};
  }

 private:
  std::string held_;
};

}  // namespace

void program_update_exits_12_without_network() {
  CliHarness h;
  const int code = h.run_raw({"update"});
  expect(code == 12, "program update exits 12");
  expect(h.out.find("program update not available in this release; install the new release manually") != std::string::npos, "message");
}

void cli_requires_config_and_known_verb() {
  CliHarness h;
  expect(h.run_raw({"list"}) == 1 && h.err.find("--config") != std::string::npos, "config is required (no default location yet)");
  expect(h.run({"frobnicate"}) == 1, "unknown verb: usage, exit 1");
  expect(h.run({"list", "--bogus"}) == 1, "unknown option: exit 1");
  if (secure_frontend::kTestHooksEnabled) expect(h.err.find("SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS_COMPILED_IN") != std::string::npos, "hooks banner");
}

void list_and_show_report_installed_models() {
  CliHarness h;
  const auto entry = secure_frontend_test::cli::install(h);
  expect(h.run({"list"}) == 0, "list");
  expect(h.out.find("m:t") != std::string::npos && h.out.find("third_party") != std::string::npos && h.out.find("runnable") != std::string::npos,
         "name:tag, origin, state");
  expect(h.run({"show", "m:t"}) == 0 && h.out.find("--native m/t/" + entry.dir + "/weights/fx-00001-of-00002.gguf") != std::string::npos,
         "show prints the engine arguments the manifest implies, relative to roots.models");
  expect(h.out.find(h.models_path.string()) == std::string::npos, "never an absolute path outside the data dir");
  expect(h.run({"show", "m:t", "--digests"}) == 0 && h.out.find("  weights/fx-00001-of-00002.gguf") != std::string::npos,
         "--digests prints sha256sum lines");
  expect(h.run({"show", "nope:t"}) == 5, "unknown model: exit 5");
}

void list_all_reports_retired_orphaned_and_staging() {
  CliHarness h;
  (void)secure_frontend_test::cli::install(h, 1, "1");
  (void)secure_frontend::ensure_directory(h.models(), secure_frontend::must_rel("x/t/v9-aaaaaaaaaaaa"));
  (void)secure_frontend::write_new_file(h.models(), secure_frontend::must_rel("x/t/v9-aaaaaaaaaaaa/w.gguf"), "orphan");
  (void)secure_frontend::ensure_directory(h.models(), secure_frontend::must_rel(".staging/" + std::string(32, 'b')));
  (void)secure_frontend::write_new_file(h.models(), secure_frontend::must_rel(".staging/" + std::string(32, 'b') + "/x.part"), "12345");
  expect(h.run({"list", "--all"}) == 0, "list --all");
  expect(h.out.find("orphaned x/t/v9-aaaaaaaaaaaa") != std::string::npos, "orphaned version reported with its size");
  expect(h.out.find("staging " + std::string(32, 'b') + " 5 bytes") != std::string::npos, "staging usage per key");
}

void rm_refuses_loaded_model() {
  CliHarness h;
  const auto entry = secure_frontend_test::cli::install(h);
  h.services.loaded = [entry](const secure_frontend::FrontendConfig&) {
    return std::make_unique<HeldLoaded>("m/t/" + entry.dir);
  };
  expect(h.run({"rm", "m:t"}) == 7 && h.err.find("models_busy") != std::string::npos, "a loaded version is refused (exit 7)");
  expect(h.run({"rm", "m:t", "--force"}) == 0, "--force after stop");
  expect(std::get<secure_frontend::ModelIndex>(secure_frontend::read_model_index(h.models())).models.empty(), "removed from the index");
  expect(!std::filesystem::exists(h.models_path / "m" / "t" / entry.dir), "deleted bottom-up");
}

void rm_retired_deletes_unheld_versions() {
  CliHarness h;
  const auto v1 = secure_frontend_test::cli::install(h, 1, "1");
  {
    // v2 publishes while v1 is held, so v1 stays retired.
    namespace fx = secure_frontend_test::acquire;
    const auto root = h.models();
    const auto manifest = fx::trusted(fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 2, fx::qwen_payload("2")), secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::third_party);
    fx::stage(root, manifest, fx::qwen_payload("2"));
    HeldLoaded held("m/t/" + v1.dir);
    const secure_frontend::StoreEnvironment env;
    (void)secure_frontend::publish_staged(root, secure_frontend::staging_key(manifest.parsed.sha256), manifest, held, env, true);
  }
  expect(std::get<secure_frontend::ModelIndex>(secure_frontend::read_model_index(h.models())).retired.size() == 1, "retired");
  expect(h.run({"rm", "--retired"}) == 0, "rm --retired");
  expect(std::get<secure_frontend::ModelIndex>(secure_frontend::read_model_index(h.models())).retired.empty() &&
             !std::filesystem::exists(h.models_path / "m" / "t" / v1.dir),
         "unheld retired versions deleted");
}

void rm_partials_deletes_other_staging() {
  CliHarness h;
  namespace fx = secure_frontend_test::acquire;
  const auto manifest = fx::manifest_text("m", "t", 3, fx::qwen_payload("p"));
  const auto key = secure_frontend::staging_key(secure_frontend::to_hex(secure_frontend::sha256(manifest)));
  (void)secure_frontend::ensure_directory(h.models(), secure_frontend::must_rel(".staging/" + key));
  (void)secure_frontend::write_new_file(h.models(), secure_frontend::must_rel(".staging/" + key + "/MANIFEST"), manifest);
  const auto other = fx::manifest_text("n", "t", 1, fx::qwen_payload("q"));
  const auto other_key = secure_frontend::staging_key(secure_frontend::to_hex(secure_frontend::sha256(other)));
  (void)secure_frontend::ensure_directory(h.models(), secure_frontend::must_rel(".staging/" + other_key));
  (void)secure_frontend::write_new_file(h.models(), secure_frontend::must_rel(".staging/" + other_key + "/MANIFEST"), other);
  expect(h.run({"rm", "--partials", "m:t"}) == 0, "rm --partials");
  expect(!std::filesystem::exists(h.models_path / ".staging" / key) && std::filesystem::exists(h.models_path / ".staging" / other_key),
         "only m:t partials deleted");
}

#if defined(SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS)
void list_outdated_uses_catalog_sequence() {
  CliHarness h;
  // An older catalog release's fixture-qwen:t1 (sequence 1); this binary's catalog has sequence 2.
  (void)secure_frontend_test::cli::install(h, 1, "", secure_frontend::ModelOrigin::catalog, "fixture-qwen", "t1");
  expect(h.run({"list", "--outdated"}) == 0, "list --outdated");
  expect(h.out.find("fixture-qwen:t1 sequence 1 -> 2") != std::string::npos, "answered offline from the embedded catalog");
}
#endif

}  // namespace acquire_cases
```

`tests/bundle_cli_test.cpp` (declare `import_works_without_downloader` and `cli_verify_export_import_round_trip` in `bundle_cases.hpp`):

```cpp
#include "bundle_cases.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/model_index_store.hpp"
#include "support/cli_harness.hpp"
#include "support/expect.hpp"

namespace bundle_cases {

using secure_frontend_test::expect;
using secure_frontend_test::cli::CliHarness;

void import_works_without_downloader() {
  CliHarness online;
  const auto entry = secure_frontend_test::cli::install(online);
  const auto bundle = online.scratch / "usb-bundle";
  expect(online.run({"export", "m:t", bundle.string()}) == 0, "export");
  CliHarness offline;  // pull.downloader = /nonexistent/curl
  expect(offline.run({"import", bundle.string(), "--trust-manifest", "sha256:" + entry.manifest_sha256, "--yes"}) == 0,
         "import needs no downloader");
  expect(std::get<secure_frontend::ModelIndex>(secure_frontend::read_model_index(offline.models())).models.size() == 1, "installed");
}

void cli_verify_export_import_round_trip() {
  CliHarness online;
  const auto entry = secure_frontend_test::cli::install(online);
  const auto bundle = online.scratch / "b";
  expect(online.run({"export", "m:t", bundle.string()}) == 0, "export");
  expect(online.run({"export", "m:t", bundle.string()}) == 7, "export into a non-empty directory: bundle_target_not_empty (exit 7)");
  CliHarness offline;
  expect(offline.run({"verify", bundle.string()}) == 5, "verify without trust: manifest_untrusted (exit 5)");
  expect(offline.run({"verify", bundle.string(), "--trust-manifest", "sha256:" + entry.manifest_sha256}) == 0, "verify: exit 0");
  expect(offline.run({"import", bundle.string(), "--trust-manifest", "sha256:" + entry.manifest_sha256}) == 2,
         "non-interactive import without --yes is declined (exit 2)");
  expect(offline.run({"import", bundle.string(), "--trust-manifest", "sha256:" + entry.manifest_sha256}, "y\n", true) == 0,
         "interactive y imports");
  expect(offline.run({"import", bundle.string(), "--trust-manifest", "sha256:" + entry.manifest_sha256, "--yes"}) == 0 &&
             offline.out.find("already installed") != std::string::npos, "re-import: already installed");
}

}  // namespace bundle_cases
```

```cpp
namespace secure_frontend {

struct CliStreams final {

  std::ostream& out;
  std::ostream& err;

};

// Seams; an empty member means the production default.
struct CliServices final {
  std::function<std::unique_ptr<HopTransport>(const FrontendConfig&)> transport;     // Task 27
  std::function<std::unique_ptr<DownloaderProbe>(const FrontendConfig&)> probe;      // Task 27
  std::function<std::unique_ptr<Resolver>()> resolver;                               // default SystemResolver
  std::function<std::unique_ptr<LoadedVersions>(const FrontendConfig&)> loaded;      // default NoServerDiscovery
  const StoreEnvironment* store{nullptr};

};

class StreamConfirmer final : public Confirmer {
 public:
  explicit StreamConfirmer(CliStreams& io) : io_(io) {}
  void say(std::string_view line) override;
  [[nodiscard]] bool confirm(std::string_view question, bool yes_applies) override;

 private:
  CliStreams& io_;
};

class StreamProgress final : public ProgressSink {
 public:
  explicit StreamProgress(CliStreams& io) : io_(io) {}
  void notice(std::string_view line) override;
  void file_started(std::string_view path, std::uint64_t total) override;
  void file_bytes(std::string_view path, std::uint64_t done, std::uint64_t total) override;

 private:
  CliStreams& io_;
  std::uint64_t last_reported_{0};
};

// strata --config <file> <verb> [arguments]; strata update; strata --help. Returns the exit code (§8.3).
[[nodiscard]] int run_strata(std::span<const std::string> args, CliStreams& streams, CliServices& services);

// The engine argv tail the manifest's launch block implies, paths relative to roots.models.
[[nodiscard]] std::string implied_engine_arguments(const InstalledModel& model);

// list --all and doctor (§3.7, §4.5): retired, orphaned and per-key staging usage with sizes.
int print_store_usage(const CanonicalRoot& models, const ModelIndex& index, CliStreams& io);

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```
