```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/strata_cli.hpp"

#include "secure_frontend/bundle_io.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/catalog.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/config_file.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/test_hooks.hpp"

#include <iostream>
#include <map>
#include <set>

namespace secure_frontend {

// Defined in strata_cli_network.cpp (Task 27); until then a stub returns a usage error.
int run_network_verb(const std::string& verb, const FrontendConfig& config, const std::filesystem::path& config_file,
                     std::span<const std::string> rest, CliStreams& io, CliServices& services);

namespace {

constexpr std::string_view kUsage =
    "strata --config <file> <verb> ...\n"
    "  import <bundle> [--trust-manifest sha256:<hex>] [--yes] [--allow-downgrade]\n"
    "  verify <bundle> [--trust-manifest sha256:<hex>]\n"
    "  export <name[:tag]> <dir>\n"
    "  list [--all] [--outdated]\n"
    "  show <name[:tag]> [--digests]\n"
    "  rm <name[:tag]> [--force] | rm --retired | rm --partials <name:tag>\n"
    "  pull <name[:tag]> | pull --manifest <file> --trust-manifest sha256:<hex>\n"
    "       [--yes] [--bundle <dir>] [--allow-downgrade] [--verify] [--discard-partial]\n"
    "  update --models [<name:tag> ...] [--yes]\n"
    "  run <name[:tag]> [--yes]\n"
    "  doctor [--downloader]\n"
    "strata update        (program update: not available until SP7, exit 12)";

struct Args final {
  std::vector<std::string> positional;
  std::set<std::string> flags;
  std::map<std::string, std::string> options;
};

// Flags are --name; options are --name <value>. Anything else starting with "--" is a usage error.
std::optional<Args> parse_args(const std::span<const std::string> args, const std::set<std::string>& flags,
                               const std::set<std::string>& options) {
  Args out;
  for (std::size_t i = 0; i < args.size(); ++i) {
    const auto& arg = args[i];
    if (flags.contains(arg)) {
      out.flags.insert(arg);
    } else if (options.contains(arg)) {
      if (i + 1 >= args.size()) return std::nullopt;
      out.options[arg] = args[++i];
    } else if (arg.starts_with("--")) {
      return std::nullopt;
    } else {
      out.positional.push_back(arg);
    }
  }
  return out;
}

int usage(CliStreams& io, const std::string_view problem) {
  io.err << "strata: " << problem << "\nusage: " << kUsage << '\n';
  return exit_code_value(ExitCode::usage);
}

int report(CliStreams& io, const AcquireError& error) {
  io.err << format_error(error) << '\n';
  return exit_code_value(exit_class(error.reason));
}

std::optional<std::string> trust_option(const Args& args, CliStreams& io, bool& bad) {
  const auto it = args.options.find("--trust-manifest");
  if (it == args.options.end()) return std::nullopt;
  auto digest = parse_trust_argument(it->second);
  if (!digest) {
    bad = true;
    io.err << "strata: --trust-manifest must be sha256:<64 lowercase hex>\n";
  }
  return digest;
}

std::unique_ptr<LoadedVersions> loaded_for(const FrontendConfig& config, CliServices& services) {
  if (services.loaded) return services.loaded(config);
  return std::make_unique<NoServerDiscovery>();
}

const StoreEnvironment& store_for(CliServices& services) {
  static const StoreEnvironment real;
  return services.store != nullptr ? *services.store : real;
}

ManifestRules rules_for(const FrontendConfig& config) {
  ManifestRules rules;
  rules.max_total_bytes = config.pull.max_total_bytes;
  return rules;
}

std::variant<IndexEntry, AcquireError> find_installed(const CanonicalRoot& models, const std::string& text) {
  const auto reference = parse_model_reference(text);
  if (!reference) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::usage_error, "expected <name[:tag]>"};
  auto read = read_model_index(models);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&read)) return *failure;
  const auto& index = std::get<ModelIndex>(read);
  std::vector<const IndexEntry*> matches;
  for (const auto& entry : index.models) {
    if (entry.name == reference->name && (!reference->tag || entry.tag == *reference->tag)) matches.push_back(&entry);
  }
  if (matches.size() != 1) {
    return AcquireError{matches.empty() ? AcquireReason::model_unknown : AcquireReason::usage_error,
                        matches.empty() ? text : "several tags installed; name one as <name:tag>"};
  }
  return *matches.front();
}

std::string runnable(const AcquireManifest& m) {
  if (m.profile != ManifestProfile::qwen) return "needs engine " + std::string{family_name(m.family)};
  if (compare_versions(m.min_version, built_engine_version()) > 0) return "needs engine strata >= " + render_engine_version(m.min_version);
  return "runnable";
}

int verb_import(const FrontendConfig& config, const Args& args, CliStreams& io, CliServices& services) {
  if (args.positional.size() != 1) return usage(io, "import takes one <bundle> directory");
  bool bad = false;
  const auto trust = trust_option(args, io, bad);
  if (bad) return exit_code_value(ExitCode::usage);
  StreamProgress progress(io);
  StreamConfirmer ui(io);
  auto opened = open_bundle(path_from_utf8(args.positional[0]), trust, rules_for(config), progress);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&opened)) return report(io, *failure);
  const auto& bundle = std::get<OpenedBundle>(opened);
  const auto& m = bundle.manifest.parsed.manifest;
  const std::string ref = model_ref_text(m.name, m.tag);
  ui.say("model: " + ref + " version " + m.version + " (sequence " + std::to_string(m.sequence) + ", " +
         std::string{origin_name(bundle.manifest.origin)} + ")");
  ui.say("manifest sha256: " + bundle.manifest.parsed.sha256);
  ui.say("license: " + m.license.id + " " + m.license.url);
  if (!m.license.notice.empty()) ui.say("notice: " + m.license.notice);
  ui.say("files: " + std::to_string(m.files.size()) + ", total " + format_gb(total_bytes(m)));
  if (!ui.confirm("Import " + ref + " into roots.models? [y/N]", args.flags.contains("--yes"))) {
    return report(io, AcquireError{AcquireReason::declined, ref});
  }
  auto loaded = loaded_for(config, services);
  auto outcome = import_bundle(bundle, config.roots.models, args.flags.contains("--allow-downgrade"), *loaded, store_for(services), progress);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&outcome)) return report(io, *failure);
  const auto& result = std::get<InstallOutcome>(outcome);
  ui.say(result.already_installed ? "already installed: " + ref : "installed " + ref + "; start it with: strata run " + ref);
  return 0;
}

int verb_verify(const FrontendConfig& config, const Args& args, CliStreams& io) {
  if (args.positional.size() != 1) return usage(io, "verify takes one <bundle> directory");
  bool bad = false;
  const auto trust = trust_option(args, io, bad);
  if (bad) return exit_code_value(ExitCode::usage);
  StreamProgress progress(io);
  auto opened = open_bundle(path_from_utf8(args.positional[0]), trust, rules_for(config), progress);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&opened)) return report(io, *failure);
  if (auto failure = verify_bundle_files(std::get<OpenedBundle>(opened), progress)) return report(io, *failure);
  io.out << "bundle verified: " << std::get<OpenedBundle>(opened).manifest.parsed.sha256 << '\n';
  return 0;
}

int verb_export(const FrontendConfig& config, const Args& args, CliStreams& io, CliServices& services) {
  if (args.positional.size() != 2) return usage(io, "export takes <name[:tag]> <dir>");
  auto entry = find_installed(config.roots.models, args.positional[0]);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&entry)) return report(io, *failure);
  auto model = load_installed(config.roots.models, std::get<IndexEntry>(entry));
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&model)) return report(io, *failure);
  StreamProgress progress(io);
  if (auto failure = export_model(config.roots.models, std::get<InstalledModel>(model), path_from_utf8(args.positional[1]),
                                  store_for(services), progress)) {
    return report(io, *failure);
  }
  io.out << "exported " << model_ref_text(std::get<IndexEntry>(entry).name, std::get<IndexEntry>(entry).tag) << '\n';
  return 0;
}

int verb_list(const FrontendConfig& config, const Args& args, CliStreams& io) {
  if (!args.positional.empty()) return usage(io, "list takes no operands");
  const auto& models = config.roots.models;
  auto read = read_model_index(models);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&read)) return report(io, *failure);
  const auto& index = std::get<ModelIndex>(read);
  for (const auto& entry : index.models) {
    auto model = load_installed(models, entry);
    if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&model)) return report(io, *failure);
    const auto& m = std::get<InstalledModel>(model).manifest.manifest;
    if (args.flags.contains("--outdated")) {
      const auto* newer = entry.origin == ModelOrigin::catalog ? find_catalog_entry(entry.name, entry.tag) : nullptr;
      if (newer != nullptr && newer->sequence > entry.sequence) {
        io.out << model_ref_text(entry.name, entry.tag) << " sequence " << entry.sequence << " -> " << newer->sequence << '\n';
      }
      continue;
    }
    io.out << model_ref_text(entry.name, entry.tag) << "  " << entry.version << "  " << format_gb(total_bytes(m)) << "  "
           << origin_name(entry.origin) << "  " << runnable(m) << '\n';
  }
  if (!args.flags.contains("--all")) return 0;
  return print_store_usage(models, index, io);
}

}  // namespace

int print_store_usage(const CanonicalRoot& models, const ModelIndex& index, CliStreams& io) {
  auto scanned = scan_model_root_files(models);
  if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(scanned)) return report(io, AcquireError{AcquireReason::publish_failed, "roots.models cannot be scanned"});
  std::set<std::string> known;
  for (const auto& e : index.models) known.insert(e.name + "/" + e.tag + "/" + e.dir);
  std::map<std::string, std::uint64_t> retired;
  for (const auto& e : index.retired) retired[e.name + "/" + e.tag + "/" + e.dir] = 0;
  std::map<std::string, std::uint64_t> orphaned;
  std::map<std::string, std::uint64_t> staging;
  for (const auto& file : std::get<std::vector<ModelRootFile>>(scanned)) {
    if (file.path.starts_with(std::string{kStagingDirName} + "/")) {
      const auto rest = file.path.substr(kStagingDirName.size() + 1);
      const auto key = rest.substr(0, rest.find('/'));
      if (is_staging_key_name(key)) staging[key] += file.size;
      continue;
    }
    const auto first = file.path.find('/');
    const auto second = first == std::string::npos ? std::string::npos : file.path.find('/', first + 1);
    const auto third = second == std::string::npos ? std::string::npos : file.path.find('/', second + 1);
    if (third == std::string::npos) continue;
    const auto version = file.path.substr(0, third);
    if (known.contains(version)) continue;
    if (retired.contains(version)) retired[version] += file.size;
    else orphaned[version] += file.size;
  }
  for (const auto& [dir, bytes] : retired) io.out << "retired " << dir << " " << bytes << " bytes\n";
  for (const auto& [dir, bytes] : orphaned) io.out << "orphaned " << dir << " " << bytes << " bytes\n";
  for (const auto& [key, bytes] : staging) io.out << "staging " << key << " " << bytes << " bytes\n";
  return 0;
}

namespace {

int verb_show(const FrontendConfig& config, const Args& args, CliStreams& io) {
  if (args.positional.size() != 1) return usage(io, "show takes <name[:tag]>");
  auto entry = find_installed(config.roots.models, args.positional[0]);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&entry)) return report(io, *failure);
  auto loaded = load_installed(config.roots.models, std::get<IndexEntry>(entry));
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&loaded)) return report(io, *failure);
  const auto& model = std::get<InstalledModel>(loaded);
  const auto& m = model.manifest.manifest;
  if (args.flags.contains("--digests")) {
    for (const auto& f : m.files) io.out << f.sha256 << "  " << f.path << '\n';
    io.out << model.manifest.sha256 << "  " << kVersionManifestName << '\n';
    return 0;
  }
  io.out << "model: " << model_ref_text(m.name, m.tag) << " version " << m.version << " sequence " << m.sequence << '\n'
         << "manifest sha256: " << model.manifest.sha256 << "\nprofile: " << profile_name(m.profile) << "; engine "
         << family_name(m.family) << " >= " << render_engine_version(m.min_version) << " (" << runnable(m) << ")\n"
         << "license: " << m.license.id << " " << m.license.url << "\nversion directory: " << model.version_dir.generic() << '\n';
  for (const auto& f : m.files) io.out << "  " << f.size << "  " << role_name(f.role) << "  " << f.path << '\n';
  io.out << "engine arguments: " << implied_engine_arguments(model) << '\n';
  return 0;
}

int verb_rm(const FrontendConfig& config, const Args& args, CliStreams& io, CliServices& services) {
  const auto& models = config.roots.models;
  auto lock = lock_models_root(models);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&lock)) return report(io, *failure);
  auto loaded = loaded_for(config, services);
  if (args.flags.contains("--retired")) {
    if (!args.positional.empty()) return usage(io, "rm --retired takes no operands");
    if (auto failure = delete_unheld_retired(models, *loaded, store_for(services))) return report(io, *failure);
    io.out << "deleted unheld retired versions\n";
    return 0;
  }
  if (const auto it = args.options.find("--partials"); it != args.options.end()) {
    const auto reference = parse_model_reference(it->second);
    if (!reference || !reference->tag || !args.positional.empty()) return usage(io, "rm --partials takes <name:tag>");
    const auto staging_root = must_rel(std::string{kStagingDirName});
    auto listed = list_directory(models, staging_root);
    if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(listed)) return 0;  // nothing staged
    ManifestRules any;
    any.max_total_bytes = ~std::uint64_t{0};
    for (const auto& entry : std::get<std::vector<DirectoryEntry>>(listed)) {
      if (entry.type != EntryType::directory || !is_staging_key_name(entry.name)) continue;
      const auto dir = join_rel(staging_root, entry.name);
      auto bytes = read_small_file(models, join_rel(dir, kStagingManifestName), kManifestMaxBytes);
      if (!std::holds_alternative<std::string>(bytes)) continue;
      auto parsed = parse_manifest(std::get<std::string>(bytes), any);
      if (const auto* manifest = std::get_if<ParsedManifest>(&parsed);
          manifest != nullptr && manifest->manifest.name == reference->name && manifest->manifest.tag == *reference->tag) {
        if (auto failure = remove_tree_within(models, dir)) return report(io, *failure);
        io.out << "deleted partial download " << entry.name << '\n';
      }
    }
    return 0;
  }
  if (args.positional.size() != 1) return usage(io, "rm takes <name[:tag]>");
  auto entry = find_installed(models, args.positional[0]);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&entry)) return report(io, *failure);
  const auto& e = std::get<IndexEntry>(entry);
  auto removed = remove_installed(models, e.name, e.tag, *loaded, args.flags.contains("--force"), store_for(services));
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&removed)) return report(io, *failure);
  io.out << "removed " << model_ref_text(e.name, e.tag) << '\n';
  return 0;
}

std::variant<ValidatedConfig, int> load_config(const std::string& file, CliStreams& io) {
  auto draft = read_config_file(path_from_utf8(file));
  if (auto* issues = std::get_if<ConfigErrors>(&draft)) {
    for (const auto& issue : *issues) io.err << "strata: config: " << format_issue(issue) << '\n';
    return exit_code_value(ExitCode::usage);
  }
  auto validated = validate(std::get<FrontendConfigDraft>(draft));
  if (auto* issues = std::get_if<ConfigErrors>(&validated)) {
    for (const auto& issue : *issues) io.err << "strata: config: " << format_issue(issue) << '\n';
    return exit_code_value(ExitCode::usage);
  }
  return std::get<ValidatedConfig>(std::move(validated));
}

}  // namespace

void StreamConfirmer::say(const std::string_view line) { io_.out << line << '\n'; }

bool StreamConfirmer::confirm(const std::string_view question, const bool yes_applies) {
  io_.out << question << ' ';
  if (yes_applies) {
    io_.out << "yes (--yes)\n";
    return true;
  }
  if (!io_.interactive) {
    io_.out << "no (not interactive; pass --yes)\n";
    return false;
  }
  io_.out.flush();
  std::string answer;
  if (!std::getline(io_.in, answer)) {
    io_.out << '\n';
    return false;  // EOF is No
  }
  return parse_confirmation(answer);
}

void StreamProgress::notice(const std::string_view line) {
  if (line != "verifying") io_.err << line << '\n';
}

void StreamProgress::file_started(const std::string_view path, const std::uint64_t total) {
  last_reported_ = 0;
  io_.err << path << ": " << total << " bytes\n";
}

void StreamProgress::file_bytes(const std::string_view path, const std::uint64_t done, const std::uint64_t total) {
  constexpr std::uint64_t kStep = std::uint64_t{1} << 30U;
  if (done == total || done / kStep != last_reported_ / kStep) io_.err << path << ": " << done << " / " << total << " bytes\n";
  last_reported_ = done;
}

std::string implied_engine_arguments(const InstalledModel& model) {
  const auto& launch = model.manifest.manifest.launch;
  const std::string base = model.version_dir.generic() + "/";
  std::string text;
  if (launch.native) text += "--native " + base + *launch.native;
  if (launch.pack) text += " --pack " + base + *launch.pack;
  if (launch.ple_gguf) text += " --ple-gguf " + base + *launch.ple_gguf;
  if (launch.tokenizer_pack) text += "; frontend --tokenizer-pack " + base + *launch.tokenizer_pack;
  return text;
}

int run_strata(const std::span<const std::string> args, CliStreams& io, CliServices& services) {
#if defined(SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS)
  io.err << "warning: test hooks compiled in (" << kTestHooksMarker << "); never ship this binary\n";
#endif
  if (args.empty() || args[0] == "--help" || args[0] == "-h") {
    io.out << "usage: " << kUsage << '\n';
    return args.empty() ? exit_code_value(ExitCode::usage) : 0;
  }
  if (args[0] == "update" && args.size() == 1) {
    // §6.2: program updates need SP7's signature verification. No network access.
    io.out << "program update not available in this release; install the new release manually\n";
    return exit_code_value(ExitCode::program_update);
  }
  if (args[0] != "--config" || args.size() < 3) return usage(io, "--config <file> is required before the verb (no default location yet)");
  auto loaded = load_config(args[1], io);
  if (auto* code = std::get_if<int>(&loaded)) return *code;
  const auto& config = std::get<ValidatedConfig>(loaded).get();
  const std::string verb = args[2];
  const auto rest = args.subspan(3);
  if (verb == "pull" || verb == "run" || verb == "doctor" || verb == "update") {
    return run_network_verb(verb, config, path_from_utf8(args[1]), rest, io, services);
  }
  struct Grammar final {
    std::set<std::string> flags;
    std::set<std::string> options;
  };
  static const std::map<std::string, Grammar> grammar{
      {"import", {{"--yes", "--allow-downgrade"}, {"--trust-manifest"}}},
      {"verify", {{}, {"--trust-manifest"}}},
      {"export", {{}, {}}},
      {"list", {{"--all", "--outdated"}, {}}},
      {"show", {{"--digests"}, {}}},
      {"rm", {{"--force", "--retired"}, {"--partials"}}},
  };
  const auto it = grammar.find(verb);
  if (it == grammar.end()) return usage(io, "unknown verb");
  const auto parsed = parse_args(rest, it->second.flags, it->second.options);
  if (!parsed) return usage(io, "unknown or incomplete option");
  if (verb == "import") return verb_import(config, *parsed, io, services);
  if (verb == "verify") return verb_verify(config, *parsed, io);
  if (verb == "export") return verb_export(config, *parsed, io, services);
  if (verb == "list") return verb_list(config, *parsed, io);
  if (verb == "show") return verb_show(config, *parsed, io);
  return verb_rm(config, *parsed, io, services);
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

```cpp
std::optional<ModelId> make_model_id(const std::string_view text) {
  const auto colon = text.find(':');
  const auto name = text.substr(0, colon);
  const bool ok = std::holds_alternative<std::string>(validate_model_id(name)) &&
                  (colon == std::string_view::npos ||
                   std::holds_alternative<std::string>(validate_model_id(text.substr(colon + 1))));
  if (!ok) return std::nullopt;
  return ModelId{std::string{text}};
}
```

```cpp
  // A managed config (SP4 §4.9 c/d): roots.tokenizer_pack removed, engine.model_id = model_ref,
  // `engine_extra` appended inside the engine object (e.g. `, "profile": "qwen"`).
  void make_managed(const std::string& model_ref, const std::string& engine_extra = "") {
    std::string text = secure_frontend_test::read_file(config);
    const auto start = text.find(", \"tokenizer_pack\"");
    text.erase(start, text.find('}', start) - start);
    const std::string from = "\"model_id\": \"m\"";
    text.replace(text.find(from), from.size(), "\"model_id\": \"" + model_ref + "\"" + engine_extra);
    secure_frontend_test::write_file(config, text);
  }

  secure_frontend::ValidatedConfig validated_config() const {
    auto draft = secure_frontend::read_config_file(config);
    return std::get<secure_frontend::ValidatedConfig>(secure_frontend::validate(std::get<secure_frontend::FrontendConfigDraft>(draft)));
  }
```

(and `#include "secure_frontend/config_file.hpp"` at the top of `cli_harness.hpp`). In `tests/engine_arguments_test.cpp` (link `secure_frontend_launch`, `secure_frontend_strata_cli`; include `support/cli_harness.hpp` and `secure_frontend/launch_composition.hpp`):

```cpp
// Spec §4.9 delta c: the managed launch comes from the manifest's launch block.
void launch_composed_from_manifest_qwen() {
  secure_frontend_test::cli::CliHarness h;
  const auto entry = secure_frontend_test::cli::install(h);
  h.make_managed("m:t");
  const auto config = h.validated_config();
  const auto composed = secure_frontend::compose_engine_launch(config.get(), false);
  const auto* launch = std::get_if<secure_frontend::ComposedLaunch>(&composed);
  expect(launch != nullptr && launch->managed, "managed launch");
  if (launch != nullptr) {
    const auto base = std::filesystem::canonical(h.models_path) / "m" / "t" / entry.dir;
    const std::vector<std::string> expected{"--native", (base / "weights" / "fx-00001-of-00002.gguf").string(), "--pack",
                                            (base / "pack").string(), "--ple-gguf", (base / "weights" / "fx-00002-of-00002.gguf").string()};
    expect(launch->engine_args == expected, "--native/--pack/--ple-gguf joined to the canonical version directory (SP1 P7)");
    expect(launch->tokenizer_root && launch->tokenizer_root->path() == base / "pack" / "tokenizer", "frontend tokenizer root from the manifest");
    expect((launch->served_models == std::vector<std::string>{"m:t"}), "/v1/models lists the managed model");
    expect(launch->model_id == "m:t" && launch->profile == secure_frontend::EngineProfile::qwen, "profile from the manifest");
  }
}

void engine_args_reject_weight_flags_for_managed_model() {
  secure_frontend_test::cli::CliHarness h;
  (void)secure_frontend_test::cli::install(h);
  h.make_managed("m:t", ", \"args\": [\"--pack=/elsewhere\"]");
  const auto config = h.validated_config();
  const auto composed = secure_frontend::compose_engine_launch(config.get(), false);
  const auto* failure = std::get_if<secure_frontend::LaunchFailure>(&composed);
  expect(failure != nullptr && failure->exit == secure_frontend::ExitCode::usage &&
             failure->messages.front().find("engine.args") != std::string::npos,
         "weight flags are refused for a managed model (--flag=value spelling too)");
  const auto expert = secure_frontend::compose_engine_launch(config.get(), true);
  expect(std::holds_alternative<secure_frontend::LaunchFailure>(expert) &&
             std::get<secure_frontend::LaunchFailure>(expert).messages.front().find("roots.tokenizer_pack") != std::string::npos,
         "--expert is the operator path and needs the operator's roots.tokenizer_pack");
}

void launch_refuses_engine_too_old() {
  secure_frontend::AcquireManifest future;
  future.profile = secure_frontend::ManifestProfile::qwen;
  future.family = secure_frontend::EngineFamily::strata;
  future.min_version = *secure_frontend::parse_engine_version("99.0.0");
  const auto gate = secure_frontend::engine_gate(future);
  expect(gate && gate->reason == secure_frontend::AcquireReason::engine_too_old &&
             secure_frontend::exit_class(gate->reason) == secure_frontend::ExitCode::engine, "exit 11");
  future.profile = secure_frontend::ManifestProfile::glm53;
  future.family = secure_frontend::EngineFamily::glm;
  expect(secure_frontend::engine_gate(future)->reason == secure_frontend::AcquireReason::engine_profile_unavailable, "glm53 until SP6");
}
// main list additions: launch_composed_from_manifest_qwen, engine_args_reject_weight_flags_for_managed_model, launch_refuses_engine_too_old
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement.** `include/secure_frontend/launch_composition.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once

#include "secure_frontend/acquire_manifest.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/frontend_config.hpp"

#include <array>
#include <filesystem>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <variant>
#include <vector>

namespace secure_frontend {

// Spec §4.9 delta c: engine weight flags a managed model's manifest owns.
inline constexpr std::array<std::string_view, 7> kWeightFlags{"--native", "--pack", "--ple-gguf", "--embd-gguf",
                                                              "--native-head-gguf", "--native-dense-gguf", "--mtp"};

struct ComposedLaunch final {
  EngineProfile profile;
  std::vector<std::string> engine_args;   // argv tail after --serve
  std::optional<CanonicalRoot> tokenizer_root;  // opened once; set on success (never re-opened by path)
  std::string model_id;                   // the model chat requests must name
  std::vector<std::string> served_models; // /v1/models: SP1's whole inventory under --expert, else {model_id}
  bool managed;                           // false only with --expert
};

struct LaunchFailure final {
  ExitCode exit;
  std::vector<std::string> messages;  // key paths and fixed reasons; never values
};

// Managed (default): engine.model_id is name[:tag] in the index; profile, weights and
// tokenizer come from the manifest; engine.profile, roots.tokenizer_pack and weight
// flags in engine.args are config errors. --expert: SP1's operator path unchanged.
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<ComposedLaunch, LaunchFailure> compose_engine_launch(const FrontendConfig& config, bool expert);

// §2.3: run/serve refuse what this release cannot run (exit 11).
[[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireError> engine_gate(const AcquireManifest& manifest);

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/launch_composition.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/launch_composition.hpp"

#include "secure_frontend/catalog.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/engine_arguments.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/model_index_store.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/model_inventory.hpp"

#include <algorithm>

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {

LaunchFailure config_failure(std::vector<std::string> messages) { return LaunchFailure{ExitCode::usage, std::move(messages)}; }

bool is_weight_flag(const std::string& argument) {
  return std::any_of(kWeightFlags.begin(), kWeightFlags.end(), [&](const std::string_view flag) {
    return argument == flag || (argument.size() > flag.size() && argument.starts_with(flag) && argument[flag.size()] == '=');
  });
}

std::variant<ComposedLaunch, LaunchFailure> expert_launch(const FrontendConfig& config) {
  if (!config.roots.tokenizer_pack) return config_failure({"roots.tokenizer_pack: required with --expert"});
  const EngineProfile profile = config.engine.profile.value_or(EngineProfile::qwen);
  auto models = enumerate_local_models(config.roots.models);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<ModelInventoryError>(&models)) {
    return config_failure({"Unable to inventory local models (roots.models): " + failure->message});
  }
  const auto& list = std::get<std::vector<ModelManifest>>(models);
  const auto selected = std::find_if(list.begin(), list.end(), [&](const ModelManifest& m) { return m.id == config.engine.model_id.value(); });
  if (selected == list.end()) return config_failure({"Selected model is not present in roots.models."});
  const auto arguments_profile = profile == EngineProfile::glm53 ? EngineArgumentsProfile::lighttransport_glm53 : EngineArgumentsProfile::strata_0143;
  auto args = build_engine_arguments(arguments_profile, *selected, config.roots.models, config.engine.args);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<EngineArgumentsError>(&args)) return config_failure({"Engine arguments rejected: " + failure->message});
  std::vector<std::string> served;  // SP1's /v1/models lists the whole inventory; --expert keeps that (SP3 decides later)
  for (const auto& model : list) served.push_back(model.id);
  return ComposedLaunch{profile, std::get<std::vector<std::string>>(std::move(args)), *config.roots.tokenizer_pack,
                        config.engine.model_id.value(), std::move(served), false};
}

}  // namespace

std::optional<AcquireError> engine_gate(const AcquireManifest& manifest) {
  if (manifest.profile != ManifestProfile::qwen || manifest.family != EngineFamily::strata) {
    return AcquireError{AcquireReason::engine_profile_unavailable, std::string{profile_name(manifest.profile)}};
  }
  if (compare_versions(manifest.min_version, built_engine_version()) > 0) {
    return AcquireError{AcquireReason::engine_too_old, "needs engine strata >= " + render_engine_version(manifest.min_version) +
                                                           ", this release has " + std::string{built_engine_version_text()}};
  }
  return std::nullopt;
}

std::variant<ComposedLaunch, LaunchFailure> compose_engine_launch(const FrontendConfig& config, const bool expert) {
  if (expert) return expert_launch(config);
  std::vector<std::string> issues;
  if (config.engine.profile) {
    issues.emplace_back("engine.profile: engine_profile_conflicts_with_manifest (the model's manifest sets the profile; only --expert takes it)");
  }
  if (config.roots.tokenizer_pack) {
    issues.emplace_back("roots.tokenizer_pack: a managed model brings its own tokenizer; only --expert takes this key");
  }
  if (std::any_of(config.engine.args.begin(), config.engine.args.end(), is_weight_flag)) {
    issues.emplace_back("engine.args: weight flags (--native, --pack, --ple-gguf, --embd-gguf, --native-head-gguf, "
                        "--native-dense-gguf, --mtp) come from the manifest; only --expert takes them");
  }
  const auto reference = parse_model_reference(config.engine.model_id.value());
  if (!reference) issues.emplace_back("engine.model_id: must be name or name:tag");
  if (!issues.empty()) return config_failure(std::move(issues));
  auto read = read_model_index(config.roots.models);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&read)) return LaunchFailure{exit_class(failure->reason), {format_error(*failure)}};
  const auto& index = std::get<ModelIndex>(read);
  const IndexEntry* entry = nullptr;
  for (const auto& e : index.models) {
    if (e.name == reference->name && (!reference->tag || e.tag == *reference->tag)) {
      if (entry != nullptr) return config_failure({"engine.model_id: several tags installed; name one as name:tag"});
      entry = &e;
    }
  }
  if (entry == nullptr) {
    return LaunchFailure{ExitCode::manifest, {format_error({AcquireReason::model_unknown, config.engine.model_id.value()}) +
                                              "; install it with strata pull or strata import"}};
  }
  auto loaded = load_installed(config.roots.models, *entry);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&loaded)) return LaunchFailure{exit_class(failure->reason), {format_error(*failure)}};
  const auto& model = std::get<InstalledModel>(loaded);
  const auto& m = model.manifest.manifest;
  if (auto gate = engine_gate(m)) return LaunchFailure{ExitCode::engine, {format_error(*gate)}};
  const auto join = [&](const std::string& relative) {
    return config.roots.models.join(std::get<RelativePath>(parse_relative_path(model.version_dir.generic() + "/" + relative))).string();
  };
  std::vector<std::string> args{"--native", join(*m.launch.native), "--pack", join(*m.launch.pack)};
  if (m.launch.ple_gguf) {
    args.emplace_back("--ple-gguf");
    args.push_back(join(*m.launch.ple_gguf));
  }
  if (const auto rejected = validate_engine_user_arguments(EngineArgumentsProfile::strata_0143, config.engine.args)) {
    return config_failure({"engine.args: " + rejected->message});
  }
  args.insert(args.end(), config.engine.args.begin(), config.engine.args.end());
  auto tokenizer = open_root(join(*m.launch.tokenizer_pack));
  if (std::holds_alternative<PathError>(tokenizer)) {
    return LaunchFailure{ExitCode::verification, {"Unable to load local tokenizer pack: the model's tokenizer directory cannot be opened safely"}};
  }
  const std::string ref = model_ref_text(entry->name, entry->tag);
  return ComposedLaunch{EngineProfile::qwen, std::move(args), std::get<CanonicalRoot>(std::move(tokenizer)), ref,
                        std::vector<std::string>{ref}, true};
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

```bash
cd /opt/projects/ai/.worktrees/sp4/secure_frontend
missing=0
# exec_child's options parameter sits on a continuation line, so it is matched as 'const ExecOptions& options'.
for name in 'ExecOptions' 'const ExecOptions& options' 'keep_fds' 'pre_exec' 'win_launch::launch_in_job' 'primary_token' \
```

**SP2 seam (binding names, ruling R2):** in `src/posix_child_launch.hpp`, namespace `secure_frontend::posix_launch`,

```cpp
using PreExecHook = int (*)(const void* ctx, std::span<const int> lifted) noexcept;
struct ExecOptions final {
  std::span<const int> keep_fds;   // lifted with F_DUPFD_CLOEXEC >= kLiftFloor before the 0..3 dup2 sequence; spared by the sweep
  PreExecHook pre_exec{nullptr};   // after the sweep, before execve; non-zero → 'H' + errno on kReportFd, _exit
  const void* ctx{nullptr};
};
inline constexpr char kHookFailureMarker = 'H';
[[noreturn]] void exec_child(const PreparedExec& prepared, int stdin_fd, int stdout_fd, bool null_stderr, int report_fd,
                             const ExecOptions& options) noexcept;
```

and `prepare(PreparedExec&, const EngineCommand&, bool insert_serve)` unchanged. Run the seam check above before anything else.

```cpp
#include "fetch_cases.hpp"

#if !defined(_WIN32)
#include "secure_frontend/downloader.hpp"
#include "support/expect.hpp"
#include "support/fake_curl_support.hpp"

#include <cerrno>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <unistd.h>

namespace fetch_cases {
namespace {

using secure_frontend::AcquireReason;
using secure_frontend_test::expect;

const char* kScenario =
    "probe 8.4.0 OpenSSL/3.0.13\n"
    "response https://fixture.example/f.bin\nstatus 200 OK\nheader Content-Length: 5\nbody-text hello\nend\n";

struct SpawnHarness final {
  explicit SpawnHarness(const std::string& scenario = kScenario)
      : scratch(secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory()),
        fake(secure_frontend_test::install_fake_curl(scratch, secure_frontend_test::extra_arguments().at(0), scenario)) {
    secure_frontend_test::make_private_directory(scratch / "models");
    secure_frontend_test::make_private_directory(scratch / "cwd");
    options.executable = fake.executable;
    options.cwd = std::filesystem::canonical(scratch / "cwd");
    options.protected_paths = {{"roots.models", std::filesystem::canonical(scratch / "models").string()}};
    options.record_file = scratch / "downloader-record.log";
  }

  secure_frontend::HopExit transfer(const std::string& url, std::string* body = nullptr) {
    secure_frontend::ConfinedDownloader downloader(options);
    secure_frontend::TransferRequest request;
    request.url = url;
    request.host = "fixture.example";
    request.pin = secure_frontend::make_ipv4(93, 184, 216, 34);
    return downloader.run(request, [body](const std::string_view bytes) {
      if (body != nullptr) body->append(bytes);
      return true;
    });
  }

  std::vector<std::string> records() const { return secure_frontend_test::read_record_lines(options.record_file); }

  std::filesystem::path scratch;
  secure_frontend_test::FakeCurlInstall fake;
  secure_frontend::DownloaderOptions options;
};

}  // namespace

void priv04_downloader_argv_exact() {
  SpawnHarness h;
  std::string body;
  const auto exit = h.transfer("https://fixture.example/f.bin", &body);
  expect(exit.curl_exit == 0 && !exit.failure && body.ends_with("hello"), "transfer through the confined child");
  const auto lines = h.records();
  expect(lines.size() == 1, "one transfer record");
  if (!lines.empty()) {
    expect(lines[0].find("\"argv\":[\"-q\",\"--config\",\"-\",\"--silent\",\"--include\",\"--suppress-connect-headers\",\"--globoff\"") !=
               std::string::npos, "-q first, --config -, --globoff");
    expect(lines[0].find("\"--user-agent\",\"strata\"") != std::string::npos && lines[0].find("\"--noproxy\",\"*\"") != std::string::npos,
           "fixed user agent, --noproxy *");
    expect(lines[0].find("\"-L\"") == std::string::npos && lines[0].find("fixture.example/f.bin\",") == std::string::npos,
           "no -L and no URL in argv");
  }
}

void priv04_downloader_env_is_empty() {
  SpawnHarness h;
  (void)h.transfer("https://fixture.example/f.bin");
  const auto lines = h.records();
  expect(!lines.empty() && lines[0].find("\"env\":[]") != std::string::npos, "empty environment");
}

void redirect_url_absent_from_downloader_argv() {
  SpawnHarness h("response https://fixture.example/f.bin?sig=SENTINEL\nstatus 200 OK\nbody-text x\nend\n");
  (void)h.transfer("https://fixture.example/f.bin?sig=SENTINEL");
  const auto lines = h.records();
  const auto argv_end = lines.empty() ? std::string::npos : lines[0].find("\"env\"");
  expect(!lines.empty() && lines[0].substr(0, argv_end).find("SENTINEL") == std::string::npos, "never in argv (/proc/<pid>/cmdline)");
  expect(!lines.empty() && lines[0].find("SENTINEL", argv_end) != std::string::npos, "only in the stdin config");
}

void dl03_downloader_untrusted_path_rejected() {
  SpawnHarness h;
  ::chmod(h.fake.executable.parent_path().c_str(), 0777);
  secure_frontend::ConfinedDownloader writable_parent(h.options);
  const auto shared = writable_parent.probe();
  expect(shared && shared->reason == AcquireReason::downloader_untrusted_path &&
             secure_frontend::exit_class(shared->reason) == secure_frontend::ExitCode::downloader, "group/other-writable parent: exit 9");
  ::chmod(h.fake.executable.parent_path().c_str(), 0700);
  auto missing_options = h.options;
  missing_options.executable = h.scratch / "downloader" / "nope";
  secure_frontend::ConfinedDownloader missing(missing_options);
  expect(missing.probe()->reason == AcquireReason::downloader_missing, "missing file: downloader_missing");
  expect(h.records().empty(), "nothing spawned");
}

void downloader_below_floor_exits_9() {
  SpawnHarness h("probe 7.80.0 OpenSSL/1.1.1\n");
  secure_frontend::ConfinedDownloader downloader(h.options);
  const auto failure = downloader.probe();
  expect(failure && failure->reason == AcquireReason::downloader_version, "below the D3 floor: downloader_version");
  const auto lines = h.records();
  expect(lines.size() == 1 && lines[0].find("\"kind\":\"probe\"") != std::string::npos &&
             lines[0].find("\"argv\":[\"-q\",\"--version\"]") != std::string::npos, "the probe is exactly -q --version");
}

void dl03_downloader_cannot_write_files() {
  const auto target = secure_frontend_test::make_scratch_directory() / "pwned";
  SpawnHarness h("response https://fixture.example/f.bin\nstatus 200 OK\nbody-text x\nwrite-attempt " + target.string() + "\nend\n");
  if (secure_frontend::landlock_abi() == 0) {  // R7: no Landlock in this kernel (< 5.13 or LSM off): skip with a fixed reason
    std::cout << "SKIPPED: Landlock is unavailable in this kernel\n";
    return;
  }
  (void)h.transfer("https://fixture.example/f.bin");
  const auto lines = h.records();
  expect(!lines.empty() && lines[0].find("\"write_errno\":\"" + std::to_string(EACCES) + "\"") != std::string::npos,
         "a write outside every allowed directory is denied");
  expect(!std::filesystem::exists(target), "no file created");
}

void dl03_downloader_cannot_read_home() {
  SpawnHarness probe_harness;
  secure_frontend_test::write_file(probe_harness.scratch / "models" / "secret.txt", "secret");
  const auto secret = (probe_harness.scratch / "models" / "secret.txt").string();
  SpawnHarness h("response https://fixture.example/f.bin\nstatus 200 OK\nbody-text x\nread-attempt " + secret + "\nend\n");
  if (secure_frontend::landlock_abi() == 0) {  // R7: no Landlock in this kernel (< 5.13 or LSM off): skip with a fixed reason
    std::cout << "SKIPPED: Landlock is unavailable in this kernel\n";
    return;
  }
  (void)h.transfer("https://fixture.example/f.bin");
  const auto lines = h.records();
  expect(!lines.empty() && lines[0].find("\"read_errno\":\"" + std::to_string(EACCES) + "\"") != std::string::npos,
         "files outside the system directories and the downloader's parent are unreadable");
}

void pre_exec_failure_fails_closed_exit_9() {
  SpawnHarness h;
  h.options.test_fail_pre_exec = true;
  secure_frontend::ConfinedDownloader downloader(h.options);
  const auto failure = downloader.probe();
  expect(failure && failure->reason == AcquireReason::downloader_confinement_failed, "'H' marker → downloader_confinement_failed");
  expect(h.records().empty(), "curl never runs unconfined");
}

void record_fd_does_not_clobber_report_pipe() {
  // Free fd 3 so the frontend's own record descriptor is opened as fd 3, the number SP2 uses for kReportFd.
  const int saved = ::fcntl(3, F_DUPFD_CLOEXEC, 100);
  ::close(3);
  SpawnHarness h;
  std::string body;
  const auto exit = h.transfer("https://fixture.example/f.bin", &body);
  if (saved >= 0) {
    ::dup2(saved, 3);
    ::close(saved);
  }
  expect(exit.curl_exit == 0 && body.ends_with("hello"), "the report pipe still reports success (EOF)");
  expect(h.records().size() == 1, "and the record reached fd 4");
}

void confinement_reduced_is_announced_without_landlock() {
  SpawnHarness h;
  h.options.test_force_no_landlock = true;
  const secure_frontend::ConfinedDownloader reduced(h.options);
  expect(reduced.confinement() == "reduced:no_landlock", "announced before confirmation");
  SpawnHarness full;
  const secure_frontend::ConfinedDownloader confined(full.options);
  expect(secure_frontend::landlock_abi() == 0 || confined.confinement() == "full", "full when Landlock is present");
  auto readable = full.options;
  readable.protected_paths = {{"roots.models", full.fake.executable.parent_path().string() + "/models-here"}};
  expect(secure_frontend::landlock_abi() == 0 ||
             secure_frontend::ConfinedDownloader(readable).confinement() == "reduced:readable:roots.models",
         "a root beneath the downloader's parent reduces confinement");
}

}  // namespace fetch_cases
#endif
```

`SpawnHarness` closing fd 3 is safe inside a CTest child (a per-case process). List the cases in `fetch_test.cpp`'s `main` under `#if !defined(_WIN32)` and `#if defined(SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS)` (all except `dl03_downloader_untrusted_path_rejected` and `confinement_reduced_is_announced_without_landlock`'s second half need the record file or a hooks switch; register all of them hooks-only for simplicity).

- [ ] **Step 4: Implement** — `include/secure_frontend/downloader.hpp`:

```cpp
#pragma once

#include "secure_frontend/acquire_pull.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/confinement_plan.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/downloader_command.hpp"

#include <atomic>
#include <cstdint>
#include <filesystem>
#include <optional>
#include <string>
#include <string_view>
#include <variant>
#include <vector>

namespace secure_frontend {

struct DownloaderOptions final {
  std::filesystem::path executable;                // pull.downloader (validated by probe(), lazily, §3.9)
  std::vector<ProtectedPath> protected_paths;      // config file, roots.models, roots.secret (canonical)
  std::filesystem::path cwd;                       // roots.models/.staging/.cwd
  std::optional<std::uint16_t> proxy_port;         // Landlock ABI >= 4 connect rule (443 otherwise)
  const std::atomic<bool>* interrupted{nullptr};   // checked while waiting for output
  // SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS builds only (ignored otherwise):
  std::filesystem::path record_file;               // §10.1 record channel → child fd 4 / CRT entry 4
  bool test_fail_pre_exec{false};                  // fault injection: the pre-exec hook returns EPERM
  bool test_force_no_landlock{false};              // behave as if Landlock were unavailable
};

// The confined system curl (§3.1-§3.3). Every spawn is a probe or a transfer.
class ConfinedDownloader final : public HopTransport, public DownloaderProbe {
 public:
  explicit ConfinedDownloader(DownloaderOptions options) : options_(std::move(options)) {}
  [[nodiscard]] HopExit run(const TransferRequest& request, const std::function<bool(std::string_view)>& on_bytes) override;
  [[nodiscard]] std::optional<AcquireError> probe() override;
  [[nodiscard]] std::string confinement() const override;
  [[nodiscard]] const std::optional<ProbeResult>& probe_result() const noexcept { return probe_result_; }

 private:
  struct SpawnOutput final {
    std::optional<int> exit;
    std::optional<AcquireError> failure;
    bool killed{false};
  };
  SpawnOutput spawn(const std::vector<std::string>& argv, std::string_view stdin_bytes,
                    const std::function<bool(std::string_view)>& on_bytes);
  DownloaderOptions options_;
  std::optional<std::filesystem::path> validated_;
  std::optional<ProbeResult> probe_result_;
};

// §3.2 "Executable" row. Linux: absolute, canonical, regular, file and parent not
// group/other-writable (SP1 open_executable). Windows (Task 26): the same, with
// TrustedInstaller also an allowed writer and INHERIT_ONLY ACEs ignored.
[[nodiscard]] std::variant<std::filesystem::path, AcquireError> validate_downloader(const std::filesystem::path& requested);
// Landlock ABI version (0: absent, disabled, or not Linux).
[[nodiscard]] int landlock_abi() noexcept;
// SP2 report pipe bytes → reason (empty input is not a failure; callers check that first).
[[nodiscard]] AcquireReason classify_spawn_report(std::string_view report) noexcept;

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/downloader_common.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/downloader.hpp"

#include <cerrno>
#include <cstring>

namespace secure_frontend {

AcquireReason classify_spawn_report(const std::string_view report) noexcept {
  if (report.size() == 1 + sizeof(int) && report.front() == 'H') return AcquireReason::downloader_confinement_failed;
  if (report.size() != sizeof(int)) return AcquireReason::downloader_unsupported;
  int code = 0;
  std::memcpy(&code, report.data(), sizeof code);
  if (code == ENOENT) return AcquireReason::downloader_missing;
  if (code == EACCES) return AcquireReason::downloader_untrusted_path;
  return AcquireReason::downloader_unsupported;
}

std::optional<AcquireError> ConfinedDownloader::probe() {
  auto valid = validate_downloader(options_.executable);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&valid)) return *failure;
  validated_ = std::get<std::filesystem::path>(valid);
  std::string output;
  const auto result = spawn(build_probe_argv(validated_->string()), {}, [&output](const std::string_view bytes) {
    output.append(bytes);
    return output.size() <= 65536;
  });
  if (result.failure) return result.failure;
  if (result.exit.value_or(-1) != 0) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::downloader_unsupported, "the probe exited non-zero"};
  const auto parsed = parse_probe_output(output);
  if (!parsed) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::downloader_unsupported, "not a curl with a TLS backend"};
  if (!meets_version_floor(*parsed, kThisDownloaderPlatform)) {
    return AcquireError{AcquireReason::downloader_version, "curl " + std::to_string(parsed->major) + "." + std::to_string(parsed->minor) +
                                                               "." + std::to_string(parsed->patch) + " is below the floor"};
  }
  probe_result_ = parsed;
  return std::nullopt;
}

HopExit ConfinedDownloader::run(const TransferRequest& request, const std::function<bool(std::string_view)>& on_bytes) {
  if (!validated_) {
    auto valid = validate_downloader(options_.executable);
    if (auto* failure = std::get_if<AcquireError>(&valid)) return HopExit{std::nullopt, *failure, false};
    validated_ = std::get<std::filesystem::path>(valid);
  }
  const auto result = spawn(build_transfer_argv(validated_->string(), request), build_config_stdin(request), on_bytes);
  return HopExit{result.exit, result.failure, result.killed};
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
```

`src/landlock_linux.hpp` (Linux-only internal header; UAPI structs defined here so old kernel headers suffice):

```cpp
#pragma once
#if defined(__linux__)
#include <cstdint>
#include <sys/syscall.h>
#include <unistd.h>

#ifndef SYS_landlock_create_ruleset
#define SYS_landlock_create_ruleset 444
#endif
#ifndef SYS_landlock_add_rule
#define SYS_landlock_add_rule 445
#endif
#ifndef SYS_landlock_restrict_self
#define SYS_landlock_restrict_self 446
#endif

namespace secure_frontend::landlock {

inline constexpr std::uint32_t kCreateRulesetVersion = 1U << 0U;
inline constexpr int kRulePathBeneath = 1;
inline constexpr int kRuleNetPort = 2;

struct RulesetAttr final {
  std::uint64_t handled_access_fs;
  std::uint64_t handled_access_net;
};

struct __attribute__((packed)) PathBeneathAttr final {
  std::uint64_t allowed_access;
  std::int32_t parent_fd;
};

struct NetPortAttr final {
  std::uint64_t allowed_access;
  std::uint64_t port;
};

inline int create_ruleset(const RulesetAttr* attr, const std::size_t size, const std::uint32_t flags) noexcept {
  return static_cast<int>(::syscall(SYS_landlock_create_ruleset, attr, size, flags));
}
inline int add_rule(const int ruleset, const int type, const void* attr) noexcept {
  return static_cast<int>(::syscall(SYS_landlock_add_rule, ruleset, type, attr, 0U));
}
inline int restrict_self(const int ruleset) noexcept { return static_cast<int>(::syscall(SYS_landlock_restrict_self, ruleset, 0U)); }

}  // namespace secure_frontend::landlock
#endif
```

`src/downloader_posix.cpp`:

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/downloader.hpp"

#if !defined(_WIN32)
#include "landlock_linux.hpp"
#include "posix_child_launch.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/canonical_path.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/engine_process.hpp"

#include <cerrno>
#include <csignal>
#include <fcntl.h>
#include <poll.h>
#include <sys/stat.h>
#include <sys/wait.h>
#include <unistd.h>
#if defined(__linux__)
#include <sys/prctl.h>
#endif

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {

// Built before fork(); read by the async-signal-safe pre-exec hook.
struct HookContext final {
  int ruleset_index;  // position in keep_fds, -1 = none (reduced: no_landlock)
  int record_index;   // position in keep_fds, -1 = none (release builds)
  const char* cwd;
  bool fail_injected;
};

// §3.2 hook, in the binding order: dup2(record, 4) → PR_SET_NO_NEW_PRIVS →
// landlock_restrict_self → close(ruleset) → chdir. Returns 0 or errno.
int downloader_pre_exec(const void* raw, const std::span<const int> lifted) noexcept {
  const auto* context = static_cast<const HookContext*>(raw);
  if (context->fail_injected) return EPERM;
  if (context->record_index >= 0) {
    const int record = lifted[static_cast<std::size_t>(context->record_index)];
    if (::dup2(record, 4) < 0) return errno;  // dup2 clears FD_CLOEXEC on 4
    ::close(record);
  }
#if defined(__linux__)
  if (::prctl(PR_SET_NO_NEW_PRIVS, 1, 0, 0, 0) != 0) return errno;
  if (context->ruleset_index >= 0) {
    const int ruleset = lifted[static_cast<std::size_t>(context->ruleset_index)];
    if (landlock::restrict_self(ruleset) != 0) return errno;
    ::close(ruleset);
  }
#endif
  if (::chdir(context->cwd) != 0) return errno;
  return 0;
}

bool directory_exists(const std::string_view path) {
  struct stat info {};
  return ::stat(std::string{path}.c_str(), &info) == 0 && S_ISDIR(info.st_mode);
}

void close_fd(int& fd) noexcept {
  if (fd >= 0) ::close(fd);
  fd = -1;
}

// Builds the Landlock ruleset for this spawn; -1 when Landlock is unavailable.
int build_ruleset(const DownloaderOptions& options, const std::string& downloader) {
#if defined(__linux__)
  const int abi = landlock_abi();
  if (abi < 1 || (kTestHooksEnabled && options.test_force_no_landlock)) return -1;
  const auto rights = landlock_rights_for_abi(abi);
  const landlock::RulesetAttr attr{rights.handled_fs, rights.handled_net};
  const int ruleset = landlock::create_ruleset(&attr, sizeof attr, 0);
  if (ruleset < 0) return -1;
  const auto plan = plan_linux_confinement(downloader, options.protected_paths, directory_exists);
  for (const auto& dir : plan.read_dirs) {
    const int fd = ::open(dir.c_str(), O_PATH | O_CLOEXEC | O_DIRECTORY);
    if (fd < 0) continue;
    const landlock::PathBeneathAttr rule{rights.directory_rule, fd};
    (void)landlock::add_rule(ruleset, landlock::kRulePathBeneath, &rule);
    ::close(fd);
  }
  for (const auto file : kLandlockReadableFiles) {
    const int fd = ::open(std::string{file}.c_str(), O_PATH | O_CLOEXEC);
    if (fd < 0) continue;
    const landlock::PathBeneathAttr rule{rights.file_rule, fd};
    (void)landlock::add_rule(ruleset, landlock::kRulePathBeneath, &rule);
    ::close(fd);
  }
  if (rights.handled_net != 0) {
    const landlock::NetPortAttr connect{landlock_access::kNetConnectTcp, options.proxy_port.value_or(443)};
    (void)landlock::add_rule(ruleset, landlock::kRuleNetPort, &connect);
  }
  return ruleset;
#else
  (void)options;
  (void)downloader;
  return -1;
#endif
}

}  // namespace

int landlock_abi() noexcept {
#if defined(__linux__)
  const int abi = landlock::create_ruleset(nullptr, 0, landlock::kCreateRulesetVersion);
  return abi > 0 ? abi : 0;
#else
  return 0;
#endif
}

std::variant<std::filesystem::path, AcquireError> validate_downloader(const std::filesystem::path& requested) {
  auto opened = open_executable(requested);
  if (auto* failure = std::get_if<PathError>(&opened)) {
    return AcquireError{failure->code == PathErrorCode::not_found ? AcquireReason::downloader_missing
                                                                  : AcquireReason::downloader_untrusted_path,
                        "pull.downloader: " + failure->message};
  }
  return std::get<CanonicalFile>(opened).path();
}

std::string ConfinedDownloader::confinement() const {
  const bool landlock = landlock_abi() >= 1 && !(kTestHooksEnabled && options_.test_force_no_landlock);
  const std::string downloader = std::filesystem::absolute(options_.executable).lexically_normal().string();
  const auto plan = plan_linux_confinement(downloader, options_.protected_paths, directory_exists);
  return confinement_label(landlock, plan.readable_key);
}

ConfinedDownloader::SpawnOutput ConfinedDownloader::spawn(const std::vector<std::string>& argv, const std::string_view stdin_bytes,
                                                          const std::function<bool(std::string_view)>& on_bytes) {
  SpawnOutput output;
  EngineCommand command{argv.front(), std::vector<std::string>(argv.begin() + 1, argv.end()), {}};
  posix_launch::PreparedExec prepared;
  posix_launch::prepare(prepared, command, false);
  int ruleset = build_ruleset(options_, argv.front());
  int record = -1;
#if defined(SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS)
  if (!options_.record_file.empty()) {
    record = ::open(options_.record_file.c_str(), O_WRONLY | O_APPEND | O_CREAT | O_CLOEXEC | O_NOFOLLOW, 0600);
  }
#endif
  std::vector<int> keep;
  HookContext context{-1, -1, options_.cwd.c_str(), kTestHooksEnabled && options_.test_fail_pre_exec};
  if (ruleset >= 0) {
    context.ruleset_index = static_cast<int>(keep.size());
    keep.push_back(ruleset);
  }
  if (record >= 0) {
    context.record_index = static_cast<int>(keep.size());
    keep.push_back(record);
  }
  int in[2] = {-1, -1};
  int out[2] = {-1, -1};
  int report[2] = {-1, -1};
  if (::pipe2(in, O_CLOEXEC) != 0 || ::pipe2(out, O_CLOEXEC) != 0 || ::pipe2(report, O_CLOEXEC) != 0) {
    close_fd(ruleset);
    close_fd(record);
    output.failure = AcquireError{AcquireReason::downloader_unsupported, "pipes cannot be created"};
    return output;
  }
  const posix_launch::ExecOptions exec_options{keep, downloader_pre_exec, &context};
  pid_t pid = -1;
  {
    const posix_launch::BlockAllSignals blocked;
    pid = ::fork();
    if (pid == 0) posix_launch::exec_child(prepared, in[0], out[1], true, report[1], exec_options);
  }
  close_fd(in[0]);
  close_fd(out[1]);
  close_fd(report[1]);
  close_fd(ruleset);
  close_fd(record);
  if (pid < 0) {
    close_fd(in[1]);
    close_fd(out[0]);
    close_fd(report[0]);
    output.failure = AcquireError{AcquireReason::downloader_unsupported, "fork failed"};
    return output;
  }
  // Pre-exec failures arrive on the report pipe; EOF means execve succeeded.
  std::string report_bytes;
  char buffer[65536];
  for (ssize_t n; (n = ::read(report[0], buffer, sizeof buffer)) != 0;) {
    if (n < 0 && errno == EINTR) continue;
    if (n < 0) break;
    report_bytes.append(buffer, static_cast<std::size_t>(n));
  }
  close_fd(report[0]);
  if (!report_bytes.empty()) {
    close_fd(in[1]);
    close_fd(out[0]);
    int status = 0;
    ::waitpid(pid, &status, 0);
    output.failure = AcquireError{classify_spawn_report(report_bytes), "pull.downloader could not be started confined"};
    return output;
  }
  std::size_t written = 0;
  while (written < stdin_bytes.size()) {
    const ssize_t n = ::write(in[1], stdin_bytes.data() + written, stdin_bytes.size() - written);  // SIGPIPE ignored by strata
    if (n < 0 && errno == EINTR) continue;
    if (n <= 0) break;
    written += static_cast<std::size_t>(n);
  }
  close_fd(in[1]);
  while (true) {
    pollfd poll_fd{out[0], POLLIN, 0};
    const int ready = ::poll(&poll_fd, 1, 250);
    if (options_.interrupted != nullptr && options_.interrupted->load()) {
      ::killpg(pid, SIGKILL);
      output.killed = true;
      break;
    }
    if (ready < 0 && errno == EINTR) continue;
    if (ready <= 0) continue;
    const ssize_t n = ::read(out[0], buffer, sizeof buffer);
    if (n < 0 && errno == EINTR) continue;
    if (n <= 0) break;
    if (!on_bytes(std::string_view{buffer, static_cast<std::size_t>(n)})) {
      ::killpg(pid, SIGKILL);  // exec_child put the child in its own process group
      output.killed = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  close_fd(out[0]);
  int status = 0;
  while (::waitpid(pid, &status, 0) < 0 && errno == EINTR) {
  }
  if (!output.killed && WIFEXITED(status)) output.exit = WEXITSTATUS(status);
  return output;
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
#endif
```

`posix_launch::BlockAllSignals` and `exec_child` come from SP2's `posix_child_launch.hpp`. Because `strata` writes to pipes whose reader can die, `src/strata_main.cpp` gains `std::signal(SIGPIPE, SIG_IGN);` (POSIX only) as its first statement in this task.

### Task 26: Confined downloader (Windows) — AFTER SP2 RUN-2

Spec §3.2 Windows bullet (restricted primary token at Low integrity, Job, explicit handle list, CRT block n=5 with entry 4 = record handle, empty block in release builds), the requirement table's Windows column (TrustedInstaller allowed as a writer, `INHERIT_ONLY` ACEs ignored, env exactly `SystemRoot` from `GetSystemWindowsDirectoryW()`, private read-only cwd, token failure fails closed), §11; Controller note 15.

**SP2 run-2 must produce** (ruling R-5; the controller hands this list to SP2 run-2 together with the binding seam names): `PreExecHook`, `kHookFailureMarker` (in `src/posix_child_launch.hpp`, namespace `secure_frontend::posix_launch`), `src/win_child_launch.hpp`, `WinLaunchSpec`, `WinLaunched`, and `launch_in_job` returning `std::variant<WinLaunched, DWORD>` (namespace `secure_frontend::win_launch`), with the shapes shown below.

**SP2 seam (binding names, ruling R2), Windows half:** `src/win_child_launch.hpp`, namespace `secure_frontend::win_launch`,

```cpp
struct WinLaunchSpec final {
  std::wstring executable;                      // absolute
  std::wstring command_line;                    // fully quoted argv
  std::wstring environment_block;               // NAME=VALUE\0...\0\0
  std::wstring cwd;
  HANDLE stdin_read;                            // inheritable
  HANDLE stdout_write;                          // inheritable
  HANDLE stderr_handle;                         // inheritable (NUL)
  HANDLE primary_token{nullptr};                // CreateProcessAsUserW when set
  std::span<const HANDLE> extra_inherited_handles;  // added to PROC_THREAD_ATTRIBUTE_HANDLE_LIST
  std::span<const std::uint8_t> crt_fd_block;       // STARTUPINFO cbReserved2/lpReserved2
};
struct WinLaunched final {
  HANDLE process;
  HANDLE job;  // KILL_ON_JOB_CLOSE, ACTIVE_PROCESS = 1, UI restrictions; created suspended, assigned, resumed
};
std::variant<WinLaunched, DWORD> launch_in_job(const WinLaunchSpec& spec);  // DWORD = GetLastError of the failing call
```

```cpp
secure_frontend::HopExit transfer(secure_frontend::ConfinedDownloader& downloader, std::string& body) {

    return true;
  });
}

void downloader_transfer_through_job_windows() {
  WindowsSpawn w;
  secure_frontend::ConfinedDownloader downloader(w.options);
  expect(!downloader.probe().has_value(), "probe through the Job at Low integrity (8.4.0 meets the Windows floor)");
  std::string body;
  const auto exit = transfer(downloader, body);
  expect(exit.curl_exit == 0 && body.ends_with("hello"), "stdout pipe readable from a Low-integrity writer");
}

void dl03_downloader_runs_at_low_integrity_windows() {
  WindowsSpawn w;
  secure_frontend::ConfinedDownloader downloader(w.options);
  std::string body;
  (void)transfer(downloader, body);
  const auto lines = w.records();
  expect(!lines.empty() && lines[0].find("\"integrity\":\"S-1-16-4096\"") != std::string::npos, "the child token is Low integrity");
}

void priv04_downloader_env_is_systemroot_only_windows() {
  WindowsSpawn w;
  secure_frontend::ConfinedDownloader downloader(w.options);
  std::string body;
  (void)transfer(downloader, body);
  std::wstring windows(MAX_PATH, L'\0');
  windows.resize(GetSystemWindowsDirectoryW(windows.data(), MAX_PATH));
  const std::string expected = "\"env\":[\"SystemRoot=" + std::filesystem::path(windows).string() + "\"]";
  std::string escaped;
  for (const char c : expected) escaped += c == '\\' ? std::string{"\\\\"} : std::string(1, c);
  const auto lines = w.records();
  expect(!lines.empty() && lines[0].find(escaped) != std::string::npos, "exactly SystemRoot, from GetSystemWindowsDirectoryW");
}

void dl03_default_windows_curl_path_accepted_windows() {
  const auto path = secure_frontend::default_downloader_path();
  if (!std::filesystem::exists(path)) {
    expect(false, "System32\\curl.exe is present on supported Windows (10 1803+) and on the CI image");
    return;
  }
  const auto validated = secure_frontend::validate_downloader(path);
  expect(std::holds_alternative<std::filesystem::path>(validated),
         "TrustedInstaller-owned System32\\curl.exe is accepted (INHERIT_ONLY ACEs ignored)");
}

}  // namespace fetch_cases
#endif
```

```cpp
#include "secure_frontend/downloader.hpp"

#if defined(_WIN32)
#include "secure_frontend/canonical_path.hpp"
#include "secure_frontend/win32_prelude.hpp"
#include "win_child_launch.hpp"
#include "windows_security.hpp"

#include <aclapi.h>
#include <sddl.h>

#include <array>
#include <chrono>
#include <thread>

namespace secure_frontend {
namespace {

class Handle final {
 public:
  explicit Handle(HANDLE h = nullptr) noexcept : h_(h) {}
  ~Handle() { reset(); }
  Handle(const Handle&) = delete;
  Handle& operator=(const Handle&) = delete;
  [[nodiscard]] HANDLE get() const noexcept { return h_; }
  [[nodiscard]] bool valid() const noexcept { return h_ != nullptr && h_ != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE; }
  void reset(HANDLE h = nullptr) noexcept {
    if (valid()) CloseHandle(h_);
    h_ = h;
  }

 private:
  HANDLE h_;
};

std::wstring quote_command_line(const std::vector<std::string>& argv) {
  std::wstring line;
  for (const auto& narrow : argv) {
    const std::wstring arg = path_from_utf8(narrow).wstring();
    if (!line.empty()) line.push_back(L' ');
    if (!arg.empty() && arg.find_first_of(L" \t\"") == std::wstring::npos) {
      line += arg;
      continue;
    }
    line.push_back(L'"');
    std::size_t backslashes = 0;
    for (const wchar_t c : arg) {
      if (c == L'\\') {
        ++backslashes;
        continue;
      }
      line.append(c == L'"' ? backslashes * 2 + 1 : backslashes, L'\\');
      backslashes = 0;
      line.push_back(c);
    }
    line.append(backslashes * 2, L'\\');
    line.push_back(L'"');
  }
  return line;
}

// §3.2 "Privilege": a restricted version of our own token at Low integrity.
HANDLE make_low_integrity_token() {
  HANDLE own = nullptr;
  if (!OpenProcessToken(GetCurrentProcess(), TOKEN_DUPLICATE | TOKEN_QUERY | TOKEN_ASSIGN_PRIMARY | TOKEN_ADJUST_DEFAULT, &own)) return nullptr;
  Handle own_guard(own);
  HANDLE restricted = nullptr;
  if (!CreateRestrictedToken(own, DISABLE_MAX_PRIVILEGE, 0, nullptr, 0, nullptr, 0, nullptr, &restricted)) return nullptr;
  PSID low = nullptr;
  if (!ConvertStringSidToSidW(L"S-1-16-4096", &low)) {
    CloseHandle(restricted);
    return nullptr;
  }
  TOKEN_MANDATORY_LABEL label{};
  label.Label.Attributes = SE_GROUP_INTEGRITY;
  label.Label.Sid = low;
  const BOOL set = SetTokenInformation(restricted, TokenIntegrityLevel, &label, sizeof label + GetLengthSid(low));
  LocalFree(low);
  if (!set) {
    CloseHandle(restricted);
    return nullptr;
  }
  return restricted;
}

bool sid_matches(const PSID sid, const wchar_t* text) {
  PSID other = nullptr;
  if (!ConvertStringSidToSidW(text, &other)) return false;
  const bool equal = EqualSid(sid, other) != 0;
  LocalFree(other);
  return equal;
}

// Owner and every write-granting allow-ACE (INHERIT_ONLY ignored) belong to the
// user, SYSTEM, Administrators or TrustedInstaller (§3.2 Windows executable rule).
bool write_access_trusted(const HANDLE handle) {
  PSID owner = nullptr;
  PACL dacl = nullptr;
  PSECURITY_DESCRIPTOR raw = nullptr;
  if (GetSecurityInfo(handle, SE_FILE_OBJECT, OWNER_SECURITY_INFORMATION | DACL_SECURITY_INFORMATION, &owner, nullptr, &dacl, nullptr,
                      &raw) != ERROR_SUCCESS) {
    return false;
  }
  std::array<std::byte, SECURITY_MAX_SID_SIZE> storage{};
  PSID user = nullptr;
  const bool have_user = windows_security::current_user_sid(storage, &user);
  const std::wstring trusted_installer = path_from_utf8(kTrustedInstallerSid).wstring();
  const auto permitted = [&](const PSID sid) {
    return (have_user && EqualSid(sid, user)) || sid_matches(sid, L"S-1-5-18") || sid_matches(sid, L"S-1-5-32-544") ||
           sid_matches(sid, trusted_installer.c_str());
  };
  bool ok = owner != nullptr && permitted(owner) && dacl != nullptr;
  constexpr ACCESS_MASK kWrite = FILE_WRITE_DATA | FILE_APPEND_DATA | FILE_WRITE_EA | FILE_WRITE_ATTRIBUTES | FILE_DELETE_CHILD |
                                 DELETE | WRITE_DAC | WRITE_OWNER | GENERIC_WRITE | GENERIC_ALL;
  for (DWORD i = 0; ok && i < dacl->AceCount; ++i) {
    void* ace = nullptr;
    if (!GetAce(dacl, i, &ace)) {
      ok = false;
      break;
    }
    const auto* header = static_cast<const ACE_HEADER*>(ace);
    if ((header->AceFlags & INHERIT_ONLY_ACE) != 0U || header->AceType == ACCESS_DENIED_ACE_TYPE) continue;
    if (header->AceType != ACCESS_ALLOWED_ACE_TYPE) {
      ok = false;
      break;
    }
    const auto* allowed = static_cast<const ACCESS_ALLOWED_ACE*>(ace);
    const PSID sid = reinterpret_cast<PSID>(const_cast<DWORD*>(&allowed->SidStart));
    if ((allowed->Mask & kWrite) != 0U && !permitted(sid)) ok = false;
  }
  LocalFree(raw);
  return ok;
}

}  // namespace

int landlock_abi() noexcept { return 0; }

std::variant<std::filesystem::path, AcquireError> validate_downloader(const std::filesystem::path& requested) {
  if (!requested.is_absolute()) return AcquireError{AcquireReason::downloader_untrusted_path, "pull.downloader: must be absolute"};
  const std::wstring path = windows_security::extended_path(requested.wstring());
  Handle file(CreateFileW(path.c_str(), READ_CONTROL | FILE_READ_ATTRIBUTES, FILE_SHARE_READ, nullptr, OPEN_EXISTING,
                          FILE_FLAG_OPEN_REPARSE_POINT, nullptr));
  if (!file.valid()) {
    const DWORD code = GetLastError();
    return AcquireError{code == ERROR_FILE_NOT_FOUND || code == ERROR_PATH_NOT_FOUND ? AcquireReason::downloader_missing
                                                                                      : AcquireReason::downloader_untrusted_path,
                        "pull.downloader: cannot be opened"};
  }
  BY_HANDLE_FILE_INFORMATION info{};
  if (!GetFileInformationByHandle(file.get(), &info) || (info.dwFileAttributes & (FILE_ATTRIBUTE_DIRECTORY | FILE_ATTRIBUTE_REPARSE_POINT)) != 0) {
    return AcquireError{AcquireReason::downloader_untrusted_path, "pull.downloader: not a regular file"};
  }
  const std::wstring final_path = windows_security::final_path_for_handle(file.get());
  Handle parent(CreateFileW(windows_security::extended_path(std::filesystem::path(final_path).parent_path().wstring()).c_str(),
                            READ_CONTROL, FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE, nullptr, OPEN_EXISTING, FILE_FLAG_BACKUP_SEMANTICS, nullptr));
  if (!parent.valid() || !write_access_trusted(file.get()) || !write_access_trusted(parent.get())) {
    return AcquireError{AcquireReason::downloader_untrusted_path, "pull.downloader: writable by another account"};
  }
  return std::filesystem::path(final_path);
}

std::string ConfinedDownloader::confinement() const { return "full"; }  // Low integrity + Job; read-up remains (§3.1)

ConfinedDownloader::SpawnOutput ConfinedDownloader::spawn(const std::vector<std::string>& argv, const std::string_view stdin_bytes,
                                                          const std::function<bool(std::string_view)>& on_bytes) {
  SpawnOutput output;
  Handle token(make_low_integrity_token());
  if (!token.valid()) {
    output.failure = AcquireError{AcquireReason::downloader_confinement_failed, "a Low-integrity token cannot be created"};
    return output;  // fail closed (§3.2 "Unavailable", Windows column)
  }
  SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES inherit{sizeof(SECURITY_ATTRIBUTES), nullptr, TRUE};
  HANDLE in_read = nullptr, in_write = nullptr, out_read = nullptr, out_write = nullptr;
  if (!CreatePipe(&in_read, &in_write, &inherit, 0) || !CreatePipe(&out_read, &out_write, &inherit, 0)) {
    output.failure = AcquireError{AcquireReason::downloader_unsupported, "pipes cannot be created"};
    return output;
  }
  Handle child_in(in_read), parent_in(in_write), parent_out(out_read), child_out(out_write);
  SetHandleInformation(parent_in.get(), HANDLE_FLAG_INHERIT, 0);
  SetHandleInformation(parent_out.get(), HANDLE_FLAG_INHERIT, 0);
  Handle null_device(CreateFileW(L"NUL", GENERIC_WRITE, FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE, &inherit, OPEN_EXISTING, 0, nullptr));
  Handle record;
  std::vector<HANDLE> extra;
  std::vector<std::uint8_t> block;
#if defined(SECURE_FRONTEND_TEST_HOOKS)
  if (!options_.record_file.empty()) {
    record.reset(CreateFileW(options_.record_file.wstring().c_str(), FILE_APPEND_DATA, FILE_SHARE_READ | FILE_SHARE_WRITE, &inherit,
                             OPEN_ALWAYS, FILE_ATTRIBUTE_NORMAL, nullptr));
  }
  if (record.valid()) {
    namespace cf = crt_flags;
    extra.push_back(record.get());
    const std::array<std::uint64_t, 5> handles{reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(child_in.get()),
                                               reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(child_out.get()),
                                               reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(null_device.get()), kInvalidHandleBits,
                                               reinterpret_cast<std::uintptr_t>(record.get())};
    const std::array<std::uint8_t, 5> flags{cf::kOpen | cf::kPipe, cf::kOpen | cf::kPipe, cf::kOpen | cf::kDevice, 0, cf::kOpen};
    block = build_crt_fd_block(handles, flags, sizeof(HANDLE));
  }
#endif
  std::wstring windows(MAX_PATH, L'\0');
  windows.resize(GetSystemWindowsDirectoryW(windows.data(), MAX_PATH));
  std::wstring environment = L"SystemRoot=" + windows;
  environment.push_back(L'\0');
  environment.push_back(L'\0');
  win_launch::WinLaunchSpec spec{path_from_utf8(argv.front()).wstring(), quote_command_line(argv), environment,
                                 options_.cwd.wstring(), child_in.get(), child_out.get(), null_device.get(), token.get(), extra, block};
  auto launched = win_launch::launch_in_job(spec);
  child_in.reset();
  child_out.reset();
  record.reset();
  if (const auto* code = std::get_if<DWORD>(&launched)) {
    output.failure = AcquireError{*code == ERROR_FILE_NOT_FOUND ? AcquireReason::downloader_missing
                                  : *code == ERROR_ACCESS_DENIED ? AcquireReason::downloader_untrusted_path
                                                                 : AcquireReason::downloader_unsupported,
                                  "pull.downloader could not be started in its Job"};
    return output;
  }
  const auto child = std::get<win_launch::WinLaunched>(launched);
  Handle process(child.process);
  Handle job(child.job);
  DWORD written = 0;
  if (!stdin_bytes.empty()) (void)WriteFile(parent_in.get(), stdin_bytes.data(), static_cast<DWORD>(stdin_bytes.size()), &written, nullptr);
  parent_in.reset();
  char buffer[65536];
  while (true) {
    if (options_.interrupted != nullptr && options_.interrupted->load()) {
      TerminateJobObject(job.get(), 1);
      output.killed = true;
      break;
    }
    DWORD available = 0;
    if (!PeekNamedPipe(parent_out.get(), nullptr, 0, nullptr, &available, nullptr)) break;  // writer closed: EOF
    if (available == 0) {
      if (WaitForSingleObject(process.get(), 50) == WAIT_OBJECT_0) {
        if (!PeekNamedPipe(parent_out.get(), nullptr, 0, nullptr, &available, nullptr) || available == 0) break;
      } else {
        continue;
      }
    }
    DWORD n = 0;
    if (!ReadFile(parent_out.get(), buffer, sizeof buffer, &n, nullptr) || n == 0) break;
    if (!on_bytes(std::string_view{buffer, n})) {
      TerminateJobObject(job.get(), 1);
      output.killed = true;
      break;
    }
  }
  WaitForSingleObject(process.get(), INFINITE);
  DWORD code = 0;
  if (!output.killed && GetExitCodeProcess(process.get(), &code)) output.exit = static_cast<int>(code);
  return output;
}

}  // namespace secure_frontend
#endif
```

In release builds `extra` is empty and `block` is empty, so `cbReserved2 = 0` (§3.2). The stdin pipe stays exactly one config line for transfers; the probe gets an empty, already-closed stdin (the `stdin_bytes.empty()` path closes `parent_in` immediately), so CRT entries never shift.
